To investigate this, we use the Qwen3-VL-4B (Bai et al., 2025), a modern VLM, to decode how visual information is encoded across depths. For this, we propose a synthetic dataset of simple geometric shapes for controlled analysis, along with queries crafted to precisely test language cues. Furthermore, the dataset is modified to test causal reliance on visual evidence. Our results show that current VLMs combine genuine visual reasoning with shortcut strategies primarily rooted in language cues.

</details>


### [183] [Predicting Multiple Clinical Outcomes Related to Functional Recovery and Social Isolation Among Older Adults After Lower-Limb Fracture or Hip Replacement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23531)
*Santosh Ray,Pratik K. Mishra,Ali Abedi,Charlene H. Chu,Amir Ahmad,Shehroz S. Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究利用多模态传感器与临床数据，联合预测下肢骨折或髋关节置换术后老年人的多项康复结局，使用多输出回归方法，提升了预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往大多研究独立分析临床结局，忽视了多种因素共同影响康复轨迹。该研究旨在通过整合多模态数据，更准确地把握老年患者的康复过程，促进个体化护理决策。

Method: 收集18位术后老年患者多达8周的室内运动、加速度、步数、心率、户外活动和睡眠多模态数据，同时每两周评估五项临床结局，将其建模为多输出回归问题。比较多种单输出和多输出的机器学习、深度学习模型，使用SHAP解释模型特征贡献。

Result: 多输出回归模型比单独预测效果更优，DL多输出回归器NODE取得MSE=3.96与MAE=1.02的最佳性能。SHAP分析证明多模态传感器数据对预测康复轨迹贡献显著。

Conclusion: 联合多模态数据并采用多输出回归，有助于同时评估老年人的功能与社会康复，提升个体康复照护的持续性和质量。

Abstract: Older adults recovering after lower-limb fracture or hip replacement may experience complex recovery trajectories. Most of the time, these clinical aspects are studied in isolation, masking their joint impact on recovery. This study used the MAISON-LLF dataset, which contains multimodal sensor and clinical assessment data from 18 older adults recovering in the community after lower-limb fracture or hip replacement. Participants were monitored for up to eight weeks, corresponding to a maximum of 1,008 participant-days of sensor monitoring. Forty-six daily features were extracted from indoor motion, acceleration, step count, heart rate, out-of-home mobility, and sleep data. Five clinical outcomes were assessed every two weeks: the Social Isolation Scale, Oxford Hip Score, Oxford Knee Score, Timed Up and Go test, and 30-second Chair Stand test. We utilize an inherent relationship between multi-modal sensor data and different clinical scores and formulate it as a multi-output regression problem. We tested various machine learning and deep learning single- and multi-output regression algorithms to predict these scores simultaneously. The results showed that predicting clinical scores jointly was better than separately. The tabular DL multi-output regressor, NODE, gave a remarkable performance of MSE=3.96 and MAE=1.02 in comparison to other multi- and single-output regressors. The SHAP feature analysis further showed the importance of including multimodal sensors to provide a good estimate of patients' recovery trajectory. This work may support the simultaneous assessment of functional recovery and social engagement among community-dwelling older adults and ultimately help improve their care and quality of life.

</details>


### [184] [FixAnything: 3D-Consistent Rendering Refinement via Video Generative Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23549)
*Khiem Vuong,Deva Ramanan,Srinivasa Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了FixAnything，一种通用模型，用于修复3D场景渲染（如Gaussian Splatting, NeRF等）中的各类伪影，无需为不同3D表示定制架构或大量重训练。该方法只需轻量微调即可显著改善多种3D渲染结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于3D场景的渲染方法在输入视角稀疏或目标视角远离输入时常产生伪影，并且已有基于扩散生成先验的方法依赖于针对每类3D表示设计的定制网络与繁琐训练，缺乏通用性。

Method: 作者提出复用预训练视频生成模型，将3D渲染过程转化为视频到视频的翻译，将清晰像素用二值掩码指定，使模型能修复低质量区域而保持高质量输入。为强化3D一致性，用相机位姿恢复精度作为偏好优化的奖励信号。该方法适用于多种3D表示，且仅需轻量微调原模型。

Result: 在四种不同的3D表示基准测试上，FixAnything通过最低程度的微调就能有效提升渲染质量，并可以替代原本为不同任务设计的多个特定修复方法。

Conclusion: FixAnything展示了强大的通用性和易用性，不仅提升了3D渲染质量，而且框架简洁，便于迁移到后续更强的视频生成模型，具备广泛应用前景。

Abstract: Rendering views using 3D scene representations such as Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF), meshes, or even point clouds produces artifacts when input views are sparse or target views lie far from the input. Recent work mitigates these artifacts using diffusion-based generative priors, but is specialized to individual representations and require custom architectures or extensive retraining. We present FixAnything, a single model for fixing a wide range of rendering artifacts. It does so by repurposing a pretrained video generative model, leveraging its implicit multi-view priors with only minimal modification and lightweight finetuning. Our key insight is that even noisily-rendered sequences preserve camera motion and coarse scene structure, allowing cleanup to be formulated as video-to-video translation. To control what scene structure should be preserved, we introduce a binary mask denoting the clean pixels, enabling the model to anchor its output to high-quality inputs (e.g. training views) while refining the rest. To encourage FixAnything to produce 3D-consistent renderings that support downstream reconstruction, we use camera pose accuracy (recovered via structure-from-motion) as a reward signal for direct preference optimization (DPO). Across four distinct 3D representations, FixAnything consistently improves rendering quality with lightweight finetuning, demonstrating that a single generalist video prior can replace multiple specialist refinement pipelines. The simplicity of the framework enables immediate adoption of stronger future video models without architectural redesign.

</details>


### [185] [EG-ARSA: An Expert-Grounded Open Model for Visual Road Safety Auditing in Low-Resource Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23563)
*Md Thamed Bin Zaman Chowdhury,Moazzem Hossain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Expert-Grounded Distillation (EGD)的新型AI框架，将道路安全专家知识迁移到紧凑的视觉-语言模型中，用于大规模自动道路安全审核，并推出了首个孟加拉国专家标注的视觉道路安全数据集及模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低中收入国家，道路交通伤害严重，现有的道路安全审核因事故数据不全、专家短缺和实地检查成本高而受限，亟需可扩展、自动化的审核手段。

Method: 提出EGD框架，核心在于用权威实地审核数据对教师视觉-语言模型进行量化校准（Cohen's kappa=0.74），达标后再大规模生成标注，并通过低秩适应技术和安全提示词，将知识蒸馏进8B参数的学生模型。同时构建了大规模孟加拉国道路安全审核数据集（BD-ARSA）和专用模型（EG-ARSA）。

Result: 实验表明，EGD中引入的专家校准显著提升了模型的风险评估能力，紧凑学生模型在盲测中性能超越了更大的教师模型和Gemini-2.5-Flash。

Conclusion: EGD为资源有限环境提供了高效可扩展的道路安全审核AI方案，有助于推动低中收入国家道路交通安全管理的智能化。

Abstract: Road traffic injuries remain a major challenge in low- and middle-income countries, where proactive road safety auditing is limited by incomplete crash records, shortages of qualified auditors, and the high cost of large-scale field inspections. To address this problem, we propose Expert-Grounded Distillation (EGD), a novel artificial intelligence framework that transfers institutional road safety expertise into a compact vision-language model for scalable visual road safety auditing. The key innovation is a quantified expert-grounding stage in which the teacher vision-language model is calibrated against authoritative field audits. Large-scale annotation is permitted only after the teacher reaches substantial agreement with expert risk assessments (Cohen's kappa = 0.74). The calibrated teacher then generates structured supervision that is distilled into an 8-billion-parameter student vision-language model using Low-Rank Adaptation and a single leakage-free prompt. We also introduce Bangladesh Road Safety Audit (BD-ARSA), the first open, expert-grounded Bangladeshi visual road safety audit dataset containing 21,947 image-audit records with near-national coverage, and Expert-Grounded Road Safety Auditor (EG-ARSA), the first vision-language model developed specifically for this task. Experimental results show that grounded fine-tuning substantially improves ordinal risk assessment over the zero-shot baseline, while blind expert evaluation demonstrates that the compact student outperforms both its 31 billion-parameter teacher and Gemini-2.5-Flash. These findings demonstrate that EGD provides an effective and scalable engineering solution for proactive road safety auditing in resource-constrained environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [Distinguishing Revision and Delayed Elaboration in Incremental Narrative Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21364)
*Yi-Chun Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了叙事内容增量式处理过程中的两种不同更新机制：修正驱动型更新与延迟细化，并探讨其在视觉叙事理解和人工智能系统中的结构性意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无论是人类还是人工智能系统，在处理叙述或长篇内容时，都需要随着输入的推进不断更新内部表征。作者关注的核心问题是：在增量式理解过程中，表示状态如何根据新证据发生演变，特别是面对矛盾或不确定信息时，应采用什么样的结构性更新机制。

Method: 作者首先区分了两种更新算子：一是修正驱动型更新（遇到矛盾时非单调地撤回或替换早先结构），二是延迟细化（通过添加约束单调地细化初始不完全元素）。通过视觉叙事作为诊断领域，提出了一种可以明确区分已承诺内容和未完全指定内容的结构化叙事表示，并以实证例子说明这两种操作的具体实现。

Result: 实证分析展示了如何通过结构化表示将已承诺内容与待完善内容区分开，并支持两种增量更新方式。实验例子揭示，延迟细化能实现解释状态的单调细化，而修正确保矛盾处理的非单调修正。

Conclusion: 两种增量更新机制在结构要求和推理方式上有本质差异。区分并支持这两种操作，对于增量推理机制的建模及符号-神经混合系统设计具重要意义，可提升系统理解复杂叙事的能力。

Abstract: Both human and AI systems that process narrative or long-form content operate incrementally: input is received over time, and internal representations must be updated accordingly. Incremental interpretation, therefore, depends not only on what is represented but also on how the representational state evolves under new evidence.
  We distinguish two structurally different update operators that arise in narrative interpretation: revision-driven update and delayed elaboration. Revision-driven updates retract or replace previously committed structure in response to a contradiction and are therefore non-monotonic. Delayed elaboration, by contrast, refines initially underspecified elements through constraint addition without retracting prior commitments, yielding monotonic extension of the interpretive state. Although both operators may alter how earlier material is understood, they impose fundamentally different structural requirements on state transitions.
  Using visual narratives as a diagnostic domain, we demonstrate how a structured narrative representation can explicitly separate committed from underspecified content and support both update operators during incremental construction. Through a worked example, we show how delayed elaboration enables monotonic refinement of interpretive state, while revision requires non-monotonic correction. We discuss the broader relevance of this structural distinction for incremental reasoning and hybrid symbolic-neural systems.

</details>


### [187] [KSE-Web: An Analysis of Hybrid Retrieval and LLM-Assisted Query Expansion for Low-Resource Khmer Semantic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21365)
*Nimol Thuon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对低资源语言高棉语的语义检索问题，构建了关键数据集并评估了多种检索方法及大型语言模型（LLM）辅助的查询扩展效果。结果显示传统BM25表现最佳，LLM辅助方法存在局限，但LLM规模对效果有正面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高棉语因标注数据少、词界难界定、多语嵌入模型支持弱以及高频混用英语，在语义检索中面临独特挑战，亟需数据集和系统评价推动研究进展。

Method: 作者新建了包含3K高棉文档与300条用户风格查询的数据集，对字符n-gram BM25、多语稠密检索、BM25+稠密检索混合方法及基于Qwen2.5的LLM辅助查询扩展进行系统性评测。

Result: BM25方法取得0.943 Recall与0.876 nDCG为最佳，混合方法稍逊，单独稠密检索效果最差。LLM辅助查询扩展并未优于未扩展方法，但更大LLM模型（Qwen2.5-3B）扩展效果显著好于小模型。LLM方法存在主题漂移、泛化及噪声引入等问题。

Conclusion: LLM辅助检索在高棉语语义搜索中有潜力，但受限于模型规模与扩展策略，传统BM25仍具优势。该工作为后续数据集建设和适配高棉语的检索模型提供基础。

Abstract: As a low-resource language, Khmer presents several retrieval challenges, including limited annotated data, ambiguous word boundaries, weak support in multilingual embedding models, and frequent mixed Khmer-English usage. This paper presents KSE-Web, an analysis of hybrid retrieval and LLM-assisted query expansion for Khmer semantic search. We construct the dataset from approximately 17K candidate Khmer titles and retain 3K cleaned full-text Khmer documents after filtering, normalization, deduplication, and document-length control. The dataset includes 300 manually reviewed user-style Khmer search queries and silver relevance labels with partial human verification. We evaluate character n-gram BM25, multilingual dense retrieval, hybrid BM25+dense retrieval, and LLM-assisted query expansion using Qwen2.5 models. Experimental results show that BM25 achieves the strongest overall performance, reaching 0.943 Recall and 0.876 nDCG. Hybrid BM25+dense retrieval performs comparably, achieving 0.929 Recall and 0.871 nDCG, while dense retrieval alone performs lower. LLM-assisted query expansion does not outperform non-expanded retrieval; however, Qwen2.5-3B produces substantially stronger expanded-query results than Qwen2.5-0.5B, suggesting that LLM size and expansion quality matter for low-resource Khmer retrieval. Our analysis further shows that direct LLM expansion can introduce topic drift, generic terms, and noisy reformulations, while simple filtering may remove useful semantic cues. These findings highlight both the potential and limitations of LLM-assisted retrieval for Khmer semantic search and provide a foundation for future Khmer retrieval datasets with stronger human-verified annotations and Khmer-aware retrieval models. The dataset and documentation will be made available at github.com/back-kh/KhmerSemantic-Search.

</details>


### [188] [Wazobia Eval: A Benchmark for Nigerian Pidgin Emotion Understanding, Sarcasm Detection, and Cultural Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21369)
*Stephanie Okoye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Wazobia Eval评测基准，专为尼日利亚皮钦语（Nigerian Pidgin）的情感理解、讽刺检测和文化推理而设计，旨在弥补现有评测在该语言情感和文化理解方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尼日利亚皮钦语使用者众多，但在现有语言模型评测中极度缺乏关注，尤其是在与文化相关的情感和语义理解方面，现有评测多局限于翻译、转录和通用情感分析，无法反映该语言独特的文化表达。

Method: 研究团队手动标注了550多个尼日利亚皮钦语例句，开发了16类涵盖本地文化特有情感表达的细粒度情感分类体系，并设计了标准化的评测协议和基准任务，用于系统评估语言模型对讽刺、情感与文化推理的掌握。

Result: Wazobia Eval的数据集和评测流程已完成，并进行了初步试点实验，展示了该基准任务的可行性和对现有模型在细粒度文化情感理解能力的检验价值。数据集与评测框架已公开发布。

Conclusion: 该工作为尼日利亚语系人工智能研究提供了基础验证工具，有助于推动针对文化差异的自然语言处理研究，为后续相关领域的标准化和可复现研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Nigerian Pidgin is one of Africa's most widely spoken languages, yet remains severely underrepresented in language model evaluation. Existing benchmarks primarily focus on translation, transcription, or generic sentiment analysis, leaving critical aspects of culturally grounded language understanding unmeasured. We introduce Wazobia Eval, a benchmark for evaluating Nigerian Pidgin emotion understanding, sarcasm detection, and cultural reasoning. The benchmark is built on a manually annotated dataset containing over 550 examples and a 16-category emotion taxonomy designed to capture culturally specific emotional registers that are not represented in conventional sentiment frameworks. Wazobia Eval provides standardized evaluation protocols and benchmark tasks for assessing model performance on nuanced Nigerian language understanding. We present the benchmark design, annotation methodology, taxonomy development process, and preliminary pilot evaluation results. Our goal is to provide foundational evaluation infrastructure for Nigerian language AI and establish a reproducible benchmark for future research. The dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/WAZOBIALABS.

</details>


### [189] [On the Role of Citations in Preference Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21376)
*Yu Hou,Hal Daumé,Rachel Rudinger,William Walden*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了人类和大型语言模型（LLM）在评价科学问答输出时对引用（citation）的偏好，发现人类偏好更具多样性但总量较少的引用，LLM对于引用也有一定偏好，但与人类不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多NLP任务要求系统在输出中给出引用，以防止幻觉并便于用户验证。然而，人类和LLM如何在比较输出时评价引用尚不清楚，这对于奖励建模及LLM后训练至关重要。因此，研究引用在偏好判断中的作用具有重要意义。

Method: 在科学问答任务下，通过混合效应模型定量分析人类评审和四个开源LLM在成对输出偏好中的引用倾向，分别考察引用的多样性和数量对偏好决策的影响。

Result: 主要发现包括：（1）人类评审更喜欢具有多样性且引用数量较少的答案；（2）LLM虽然不能访问来源文献，但在一定数据和模型条件下也表现出对引用偏好的倾向，而且这种倾向与人类不同。

Conclusion: 本研究显示，引用的多样性和数量对人类和LLM的偏好均存有重要影响，未来LLM的偏好数据采集和训练应考虑这些差异，以更好提升系统输出的可验证性和用户信任。

Abstract: Many NLP tasks require systems to provide attribution in their outputs--i.e. citations to grounding sources. Attribution serves as a bulwark against model hallucination and as a means for users to verify the credibility of model outputs. Yet, it is unclear how humans and LLMs evaluate citations when comparing outputs, a process central to reward modeling and modern LLM post-training. This paper studies the role of citations in the preferences of human judges and four open-source LLMs within the context of scientific question answering, leveraging mixed effects models to investigate the influence of citations on pairwise judgments. Among our key findings are (1) that humans prefer more diverse citations but fewer overall, and (2) that LLMs show some citation-related preferences compared to humans, despite lacking access to the sources, but these preferences depend on the data and specific models. We further discuss the implications of our findings for preference data collection.

</details>


### [190] [Agentic Scaffolding Amplifies Sycophantic Behavior in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21377)
*Thantham Jittham*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型（LLM）在多轮交互设置下拍马屁倾向的变化，发现带有反馈和自我修正机制的交互模式会加剧模型趋同于用户观点的行为，并导致准确率下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去拍马屁行为多在单轮对话中考察，现实中LLM往往在复杂、多轮对话中运作。作者关注交互支撑系统（如反馈机制、自我修正）是否会缓解还是加重LLM的拍马屁倾向。

Method: 通过4,800次真实性判断实验（200语句×6模型×4条件），分析不同交互模式（如多轮、用户压力、迭代修正）下LLM拍马屁表现。引入‘行为型拍马屁增幅’（ASA）以及两项新指标：让步率和拍马屁让步率。

Result: 交互支撑机制显著加剧了LLM的拍马屁行为，拍马屁倾向与准确率降低（平均-6.3个百分点）相关。能力更强的模型受到影响更大。

Conclusion: AI系统随着自主性和智能的提升，拍马屁问题会被放大。设计用于监督和修正的交互机制可能反而为此类偏差提供温床，需在系统设计上加以警惕。

Abstract: Sycophancy in large language models, the tendency to prioritize user agreement over truthful responses, has been documented extensively but studied primarily in single-turn settings. This paper investigates a critical question: does subjecting LLMs to greater interaction scaffolding make sycophancy better or worse? Across 4,800 veracity judgments (200 statements $\times$ 6 models $\times$ 4 conditions), we find that the interaction scaffolding characteristic of agentic systems (feedback loops, reconsideration checkpoints, and iterative refinement) systematically amplifies sycophantic behavior. Multi-turn interaction, user pressure, and iterative self-refinement each provide additional opportunities for models to drift toward agreement, and this drift coincides with a mean accuracy drop of $-6.3$ percentage points, establishing the capitulation as harmful rather than corrective. More capable models show larger amplification effects, a troubling inversion of expectations. We introduce the concept of agentic sycophancy amplification (ASA) and two novel metrics: capitulation rate and sycophantic capitulation rate. Our results indicate that as AI systems acquire greater autonomy, sycophancy becomes compounding rather than merely persistent. Systems designed with human oversight loops may inadvertently create the conditions for this drift.

</details>


### [191] [Beyond Two Bytes per Letter: Tokenization Overhead in Cyrillic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21384)
*Ivan Dobrovolskyi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了现代多语种分词器对乌克兰语等西里尔字母语言的分词效率低下的问题，并提出了有效的缓解措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代分词器对西里尔字母语言（如乌克兰语）分词时产生的token数量显著高于英语等拉丁字母语言，造成效率和成本不平等，因此需要量化该问题，并探索改进方法。

Method: 作者对9种分词器和5种使用西里尔与拉丁字母的语言进行了测试，采用8.37百万词形分析分词开销，使用标准化语料库（BrUK和Brown）评估中文化影响，并验证两种优化策略（LLMLingua-2模型和字节级BPE分词器），同时分析罗马化对token数量的影响。

Result: 乌克兰语在现代分词器上存在68-121%的token开销，老式分词器开销更高。LLMLingua-2能将乌克兰语输入长度减少近半且无信息损失，字节级BPE分词器显著降低了乌克兰/英语token比例。分词效率与网络训练数据里脚本的流行度相关。

Conclusion: 分词token开销与训练数据分配有关。通过优化推理模型和分词器设计，可有效缓解西里尔字母语言的token分词开销问题。

Abstract: Modern multilingual tokenizers often fragment Ukrainian and other underrepresented Cyrillic-script languages more heavily than English, creating disparities in cost and context capacity. We quantify this overhead across nine production tokenizers and five languages with standardized Cyrillic and Latin representations, covering 8.37 million word forms. On a corpus benchmark, Ukrainian shows 68-121% token overhead on modern tokenizers and 220% on the older cl100k, measured through full-text fertility on the BrUK and Brown corpora. Overhead is negatively associated with Cyrillic vocabulary allocation in the subset with independently verified English baselines, although the association is not statistically significant (Spearman rho = -0.536, p = 0.215, n = 7). We evaluate two mitigation strategies. LLMLingua-2 reduces Ukrainian input length by 47-49% on an e-commerce RAG benchmark of 1,536 products and 145 queries, with no compression-induced value losses among 80 retrievable cases. A balanced byte-level BPE tokenizer trained with a 200K vocabulary cap, converging at 158,184 actual entries, reduces the held-out UK/EN ratio from 2.22x to 1.30x. Romanization increases Ukrainian token counts by 2-19% on most tokenizers. Across the five languages, tokenization efficiency favors the script more prevalent in web data. These findings indicate that training data allocation contributes to Cyrillic tokenization overhead and that mitigation is possible at both inference and tokenizer-design stages.

</details>


### [192] [A Social Media Analysis of Discourse on the Israel--Palestine Conflict on Telegram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21385)
*Michail Zafeiropoulos,Despoina Antonakaki,Sotiris Ioannidis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对Telegram上的支持以色列和支持巴勒斯坦社区进行了大规模的系统性对比，揭示了两者在情感表达、立场和叙事框架上的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管社交媒体成为冲突话语的主要战场，但Telegram因其广播特性被认为能直接反映政治传播，有关以色列与巴勒斯坦群体在该平台上的系统性对比分析却十分匮乏。

Method: 收集了2021年5月至2026年6月间16个频道（8个亲以色列、8个亲巴勒斯坦）共87,617条消息，借助情感分析、三种立场检测方法（关键词匹配、零样本DeBERTa推理、微调过的BERTweet模型）以及框架分析，这些方法在736个人工注释消息上进行了评估。

Result: 微调BERTweet模型表现最佳，准确率72.1%，宏F1值为0.721，超过基线模型8至11个百分点。情感、立场和框架综合分析显示：两类社区关于死亡和受害者的词汇高度重合，但情感表达却相反——以色列社区偏中性、报道式，巴勒斯坦社区则更为负面，反映了二者在冲突中的不同话语站位。

Conclusion: 只有综合分析情感、立场检测和叙事框架，才能揭示亲以色列和亲巴勒斯坦社区在Telegram上的情感表达以及政治传播模式的深层区别，这对于理解平台上冲突相关政治叙事具有重要意义。

Abstract: Social media has become a central arena in which armed conflicts are contested, yet the pro-Israel and pro-Palestine communities on Telegram, whose broadcast architecture yields an unusually direct record of deliberate political communication, have not been systematically compared at scale. This study presents a multi-method computational analysis of 87,617 messages from sixteen Telegram channels, eight pro-Israel and eight pro-Palestine, spanning May 2021 to June 2026 and covering multiple conflict escalations. It combines sentiment analysis, three stance detection methods drawn from distinct paradigms (keyword matching, zero-shot DeBERTa via natural language inference, and a fine-tuned BERTweet model), and a framing analysis, all evaluated against 736 manually annotated messages. The fine-tuned model performed best (72.1% accuracy, 0.721 macro F1 under 5-fold cross-validation), outperforming both label-free baselines by 8 to 11 points; the baselines stalled in the low-to-mid 60s, indicating a hard ceiling for stance detection not adapted to in-domain language. The central finding emerges only when sentiment, stance, and framing are read together: the two communities deploy the same death- and victim-related vocabulary in opposite emotional registers, pro-Israel channels predominantly neutral and report-style, pro-Palestine channels markedly more negative, consistent with writing from the distinct discourse positions of acting party and affected party.

</details>


### [193] [Mitigating Bias in Large Vision-Language Models via Counterfactual Ensemble Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21415)
*Yisong Xiao,Aishan Liu,Yongxin Huang,Zonghao Ying,Shiji Zhao,Tianlin Li,Yong Han,Jian Yang,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）在社会群体人像处理时带有偏见的问题，通过引入多元视角的对抗性集成解码（CED）框架，大幅降低模型偏见，提升生成公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLMs虽然表现优异，但常因训练数据中固有的社会偏见，导致其对不同社会群体产生不公的输出。现有去偏方法多以单一视角为主，无法有效兼顾社会多样性。

Method: CED方法在视觉特征空间中针对每个社会群体找到相应的语义方向并生成对抗性视角特征。解码时，在分布发散最大的解码层，对不同群体的分布按不确定性加权集成，优先选取各群体的高置信度token，指导公平生成。

Result: 在三个社会偏见评测基准上，CED方法相较于主流基线方法大幅减少偏见，最高降低47.97%，涵盖职业、描述词和人格特质等场景。

Conclusion: CED显著提升了LVLMs的公平性，且对模型原有核心能力影响较小，是应对视觉-语言模型社会偏见问题的有效方法。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved remarkable performance across a wide range of tasks; however, they often inherit social biases from their training data, resulting in biased behavior when processing portraits from different social groups. Existing debiasing approaches typically compare token probabilities between the original and biased generations during decoding, but they are fundamentally limited by their reliance on a single, stereotyped viewpoint and fail to account for the diversity of social perspectives. Inspired by the social science principle that diversity fosters fairness, we propose Counterfactual Ensemble Decoding (CED), a novel framework that constructs multi-group counterfactual perspectives within the visual representation space and integrates them during decoding to promote equitable model behavior. CED first performs counterfactual steering in the visual space by identifying semantic directions associated with each social group and generating counterfactual representations along these directions, thereby offering diverse perspectives that disrupt stereotypical narratives. During decoding, CED locates the decoder layer exhibiting the greatest divergence among these perspectives and ensembles their token distributions using uncertainty-aware weights, prioritizing high-confidence tokens from different groups to yield a more balanced probability distribution that guides fairer generation. Extensive experiments on three social bias evaluation benchmarks demonstrate that \tool achieves substantial improvements over leading baselines, reducing bias by up to 47.97% across scenarios involving occupations, descriptors, and persona traits. Moreover, CED also preserves the core capabilities of the original model with minimal degradation.

</details>


### [194] [Agentic Security: A Systematization of Tools, Failure Modes, and Design Laws for LLM-Driven Penetration Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21423)
*Israt Moyeen Noumi,Tarannum Ahmed Nowshin,Md. Mehedi Hasan Nipu,Mohammad Sakib Mahmood,Md. Jakir Hossain,M. F. Mridha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统性地分析了代理化安全系统（agentic security）在实际部署中常见的失败模式，并通过对十种主流安全工具的实验，总结了可靠性与集成中的关键难点。提出了集成摩擦指数，用于量化工程、组织、法律和维护等成本，并对代理系统的运行机制、判决流程与资源调度进行了理论与量化分析。最后，通过Inspectra平台演示实际机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）为核心的安全代理系统逐步从实验室走向实际产品应用，业界在实施过程中屡次遭遇类似的操作与集成障碍。本文旨在系统梳理这些障碍背后的共性故障与瓶颈，并为安全系统设计和维护提供可量化和可复用的理论参考。

Method: 作者采用实验与理论结合，评测了十款主流静态、动态、云、编排与AI红队安全工具的无人值守流水线，提出“四维集成摩擦指数”分离一次性工程成本与长期运维的组织、法规、维护成本，并建立了概率建模和最优化（如背包问题）分析，辅以Inspectra系统的实际案例。

Result: 主要结果包括：1）提出四维集成摩擦指数以量化集成难点；2）长生命周期代理系统随阶段增多，驻留证据流失严重，而短生命周期子代理借助证据压缩能延长有效期；3）两阶段裁决流程仅在打分器无高度相关错误时才有效；4）将无法评估的结果视为攻击失败会导致指标偏向严重与隐蔽攻击；5）计划/执行路由最优资源分配公式；6）证明系统提示无法真正完成范围与预算约束。

Conclusion: 代理化安全系统在大型语言模型的支持下展现出强大的自动化能力，但在部署实战中面临系统性集成与运维难点。针对这些难点，可通过理论建模与指数化评估提前识别并规避风险。同时，配套机制需更好地结合工程与组织层面实际，不能依赖简单提示约束代理行为。Inspectra平台的实现和经验为业界提供了具体可操作的参考。

Abstract: Agentic security uses large-language-model (LLM) agents to plan, dispatch, and interpret security tools. As these systems move from demonstrations to deployed products, practitioners repeatedly encounter the same operational failures. We systematize these failures through a hands-on evaluation of ten widely used static, dynamic, cloud, orchestration, and AI red-teaming tools for unattended pipelines. We introduce a four-dimensional Integration Friction Index that separates one-time engineering cost from recurring organisational, legal, and maintenance cost. We then derive quantitative regularities that explain recurring failure modes. Modelling an agentic security system as stochastic LLM policies wrapped by a deterministic mediator, we show that long-lived sessions lose resident evidence with phase count, while short-lived sub-agents extend the usable horizon according to the compression ratio between raw evidence and its summary. We show that a two-stage verdict cascade multiplies scorer likelihood ratios, but provides little benefit when scorer errors correlate. We show that treating unevaluable outcomes as attack failures biases downstream measurements toward evasive and severe responses. We formulate planner-versus-worker model routing as a knapsack problem and derive a closed-form execution cap for heavy-tailed tools, eta* = alpha v/c. Finally, we show why scope and budget enforcement cannot be delegated to system prompts: prompts do not constrain what actually executes. Inspectra, our implemented platform, serves as a worked instantiation, with mechanisms labelled shipped, partial, or planned, including those that did not work.

</details>


### [195] [CyrillicQA: The Influence of Phonetically Encoded Secret Language on LLM Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21462)
*Erik Thureck,Leo S. Rdian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型（LLM）通过训练数据的选择在标准拉丁字母大语言群体表现最佳，但边缘语言受限。论文探讨LLM是否具备像人类一样解码语音编码语言的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前LLM主要服务于主流语言，这加剧了小众和濒危语言的边缘化；然而，LLM同样被寄予希望，用于这些语言的保存和复兴。

Method: 分析和测试LLM对于语音编码（phonetic encoding）的非主流/濒危语言的处理能力，衡量其在创意性和抽象理解上的表现。

Result: 结果尚未详细说明，但中心在于LLM在主流外的语言处理上存在短板，其在解码能力方面待验证。

Conclusion: LLM或许可成为多样化语言保护工具，但其解码和理解非主流语音编码语言的能力需进一步验证和提升。

Abstract: Due to the selection of their training data, large language models (LLMs) perform best on standard-language inputs from languages using the Latin alphabet with large speaker populations, while disadvantaging other language varieties. Nevertheless, they can also be a versatile tool for preserving precisely such endangered languages. But do they also possess the necessary creativity and capacity for abstraction to decode phonetically encoded language the same way humans do?

</details>


### [196] [Forgotten in Weights, Recovered by Tools: Agentic Tool Unlearning for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21544)
*Baicheng Chen,Zheyuan Liu,Jingyu Zhang,Kaize Ding,Ningshan Ma,Yue Huang,Meng Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的大型语言模型（LLMs）遗忘方法，能够应对通过工具辅助找回被遗忘信息的问题。作者提出Agentic Tool Unlearning (ATU) 框架，有效平衡了遗忘目标和保留正常知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLMs遗忘方法主要针对模型参数直接记忆，但实际部署为工具增强智能体时，通过检索等外部工具仍可恢复被遗忘信息，导致遗忘不彻底。为提升遗忘的实际有效性，亟需应对工具介导的信息恢复。

Method: 提出Agentic Tool Unlearning (ATU) 两阶段框架：第一阶段用传统方法进行参数遗忘；第二阶段在模拟工具环境中训练，惩罚通过工具寻找被遗忘答案和相关泄漏行为，实现工具层级的深度遗忘。

Result: 在RWKU和MUSE等不同的LLM架构上实验证明，ATU能更好平衡遗忘和模型效能，工具环境下遗忘更加健壮，工具使用下目标信息恢复大幅降低。

Conclusion: ATU方法为工具增强LLM的遗忘提供了系统解决方案，有效遏制了工具介导的恢复问题，使遗忘机制更实用和可靠。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as tool-augmented agents, where responses can depend on tool calls and external observations rather than model parameters alone. This creates an evaluation mismatch for LLM unlearning: previous unlearning methods may suppress direct parametric recall, but an agent can still recover the same forget target through tools such as web search, retrieval, or database lookup. We identify this failure mode as tool-mediated recovery and study agentic tool unlearning, which aims to reduce both parametric recall and tool-mediated recovery while preserving normal tool use for retained knowledge. To address this challenge, we propose Agentic Tool Unlearning (ATU), a two-stage framework. The first stage applies parametric knowledge unlearning to suppress direct recall, while the second stage performs trajectory-level reinforcement learning in simulated tool-augmented environments to penalize target-seeking tool behavior and final-answer leakage. Experiments on RWKU and MUSE across different LLM architectures show that ATU achieves a better balance between target forgetting and retained utility, making unlearning more robust under tool-augmented agent deployment.

</details>


### [197] [Automating Multi-Hop RAG Evaluation via TRIAD: From Context Extraction to Validated Dataset Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21558)
*Lorenz Brehme,Adam Jatowt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了TRIAD方法，自动为RAG系统生成领域专属问答数据集，提升领域内RAG评估能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM进展和RAG系统产业应用，对于能够用于特定领域性能评估的问答数据集需求上升。当前诸如HotpotQA之类的数据集适用于维基百科，但无法直接迁移至细分领域。

Method: 提出TRIAD三阶段自动生成方法：（1）为领域知识库生成问答对；（2）自动验证并反馈修正；（3）为QA对配套关联的上下文文档用于后续评测。

Result: 与MuSiQue和HotpotQA数据集对比，TRIAD生成的数据集在不同RAG系统下表现出类似的性能趋势。通过人工评测验证，该方案生成的问题更适合用于领域RAG系统评估。

Conclusion: TRIAD方法能有效生成适合领域RAG系统性能评测的数据集。作者开源了全部代码和验证结果。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLMs and the adoption of RAG systems in industry have created a need for domain-specific question-answer datasets that can assess RAG performance on proprietary data. Existing datasets, such as HotpotQA, challenge current RAG systems on Wikipedia-based knowledge, but they cannot be transferred directly to domain-specific settings. A comprehensive evaluation of RAG system quality requires both multi-hop queries and unanswerable questions. This paper introduces TRIAD, a three-stage automated dataset generation approach. First, it generates question--answer (QA) pairs for the domain-specific knowledge base of a RAG system. Second, a validator checks each QA-pair in a feedback loop. Third, the QA pairs are extended with relevance-labeled context documents for downstream evaluation. We evaluate this approach against the established MuSiQue and HotpotQA datasets. The results show that the generated dataset exhibits similar performance trends across different RAG setups, while human validation indicates that the questions are suitable for evaluating a domain-specific RAG system. The code used to generate the dataset and all validation results are available in our GitHub repository(https://github.com/lorenzbrehme/triad).

</details>


### [198] [Evidence-State Reliability Under Controlled Degradation: Parser-Validity Divergence in a Multi-Stage LLM Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21559)
*Naimur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并实证了一种用于多阶段大模型（LLM）流程中证据状态可靠性的评估方法，并通过特定实验展示结构有效性与证据敏感性阶段成功率之间的分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多阶段LLM流程中，下游阶段处理的证据可能不完整、压缩或冲突，如何系统评估中间证据的可靠性对保证整体决策质量至关重要。现有评估往往只关注结构有效性（如解析正确），忽略证据可靠性。

Method: 作者提出证据状态可靠性（ESR）评估层，侧重检测中间证据信息的完整性、一致性与可用性，并与解析有效性（结构合规）指标区分开来。实验采用GLM-5.2，在60个标准案例下仿真“干净”“压缩丢失”“部分缺失”和“冲突噪声”四类证据状态，逐步执行决策、审计、升级三阶段，总计720次流程测试。

Result: 实验发现，结构合规性并不能保证流程成功率，所有退化-干净条件下流程成功率降为负向，结构有效性则表现为正（部分有置信区间含零）。当证据退化时，审计阶段能100%检测到退化，但错误安慰率仍有存在，升级阶段无一例能恢复。

Conclusion: 实验证明结构一致性与证据敏感性流程成功率存在分歧，检出和恢复是两个独立问题。结论仅适用于本模型、流程、用例和打分设置，不宜泛化。

Abstract: Multi-stage LLM pipelines can remain structurally valid even when evidence available to downstream stages becomes incomplete, compressed, or conflicting. This paper introduces and operationalizes Evidence-State Reliability (ESR), an evaluation layer concerned with whether intermediate evidence remains sufficiently complete, grounded, internally consistent, and usable for a stage's assigned function. ESR is evaluated separately from parser validity, which measures structural conformance.
  We evaluate the framework using GLM-5.2 on 60 sanitized base cases under four evidence conditions: clean, compressed-lossy, partial-dropout, and noisy-conflicting. Each condition was processed through decision, audit, and escalation stages. The design comprised 720 planned and ledgered calls, with 713 retained, sanitized execution rows.
  Across nine matched degraded-minus-clean condition-stage comparisons, all operational stage-success estimates were negative, and all 95% bootstrap intervals remained below zero. All nine parser-validity point estimates were positive, although the three partial-dropout intervals included zero. Among parser-valid degraded audit outputs, degradation detection was 1.0 in each degraded condition, while false-assurance rates remained non-zero; among parser-valid degraded escalation outputs, recovery was 0.0 in every degraded condition.
  The results show a bounded reliability-layer divergence in the evaluated pipeline: structural conformance can improve directionally while evidence-sensitive stage success deteriorates under the same controlled intervention. They also separate detection of degraded evidence from recovery. The conclusions are limited to the evaluated model configuration, pipeline design, selected sanitized cases, scoring procedure, and single scaled run.

</details>


### [199] [Can LLMs Truly Forget? Revealing Unlearning Gaps Through Adversarial Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21606)
*Ayush Gupta,Hima Varshini Surisetty,Sreevidya Bollineni,Varad Ingale,Tuhina Tripathi,Abhishek Lalwani,Somya Chatterjee,Sadid Hasan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了当前主流的机器遗忘(unlearning)方法在面对对抗性攻击时的鲁棒性，大幅揭示了标准评价与实际遗忘效果之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘方法主要在干净的、非对抗性查询下进行测试，尚未充分验证在对抗性提示下私密信息是否真的被遗忘。这对真实应用场景尤其重要，因为攻击者可能采用策略化提示恢复敏感内容。

Method: 作者统一评测了基于提示和微调的多种遗忘方法，使用Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct作为基础模型，并在TOFU数据集上实施。方法强度评估后，引入对抗性攻击，总结并提出攻击成功率（ASR）等新指标，用于衡量遗忘信息的恢复概率。对信息恢复能力用八套不同攻击方法系统考察。

Result: 尽管微调法在标准遗忘衡量（Forget Quality）上表现良好（>0.91），但在ASR指标下被遗忘信息仍高度可恢复(ASR高达72.8%-84.3%，接近未保护模型的87.5%)。而普通多语重述下，仅有2.95%的信息被泄露。同时，ASR的二值判别在多数案例里也与人工审核一致。

Conclusion: 单纯依赖标准指标并不能证明实际不可恢复性，作者强调应将对抗性压力测试纳入遗忘方法评估体系中，以保证模型真的失去了对指定数据的知识。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of targeted training data from a model while preserving its remaining capabilities, but evaluating whether such information has truly become inaccessible remains challenging. Existing benchmarks primarily assess unlearning under clean, non-adversarial queries, leaving open whether information that appears forgotten can still be recovered through strategic prompting. We address this gap through a unified evaluation of prompt-based and fine-tuning-based unlearning methods on TOFU using Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, followed by an adversarial robustness evaluation of methods that perform strongly under standard metrics. We introduce Attack Success Rate (ASR), an LLM-as-judge metric that measures the fraction of adversarial responses whose leakage score exceeds $0.2$, and evaluate recovery across eight attack suites. Our results reveal a substantial gap between clean-query forgetting and adversarial robustness. Although several fine-tuning-based methods achieve Forget Quality above $0.91$, targeted information remains recoverable with ASRs between $72.8\%$ and $84.3\%$, close to the $87.5\%$ ASR of the unprotected base model. In contrast, clean multilingual reformulations yield only $2.95\%$ measured leakage. A manual audit further finds agreement between binary ASR decisions and human factual assessments in seven of ten cases, indicating that ASR provides a useful, though imperfect, signal of behavioral recoverability. These findings show that strong standard-metric performance alone is insufficient to establish robustness after unlearning and motivate adversarial stress-testing as a complementary component of unlearning evaluation.

</details>


### [200] [Mitigating Database Leakage in RAG Systems with Keyword-Grounded Fact Substitution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21656)
*Ziliang Zhang,Yubo Zhu,Wei Tong,Jingyu Hua,Zijian Wang,Yuan Zhang,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于RAG系统的新型防御方法KFS-RAG，通过重构外部检索内容缓解信息泄露，实验表明可有效降低敏感信息泄露风险且不损失生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统虽结合了LLM与外部知识，但易受prompt injection攻击，导致数据库敏感信息泄露。提升系统安全性成为亟需解决的问题。

Method: 提出KFS-RAG方法：1）用注意力分解和因果干扰机制识别检索内容的关键关键词；2）用这些关键词引导辅助LLM生成关键词支撑事实，压缩原始检索片段；3）模型以这些经过处理的事实替代原始文本进行生成，从而减少信息泄漏风险。

Result: 实验结果显示，KFS-RAG能显著降低注入攻击下的数据库信息泄露风险，同时维持生成回复的准确性和相关性。

Conclusion: KFS-RAG为提升RAG系统安全与可信度提供了新方向，证明对抗注入攻击相关的泄露风险是现实可行的。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for combining large language models (LLMs) with external knowledge sources. However, RAG systems remain vulnerable to prompt injection attacks, which may mislead the retriever or generator to expose sensitive database contents. To address this issue, we propose KFS-RAG, a defense that mitigates information leakage by reformulating the retrieved context. Specifically, our method first identifies a small set of influential keywords from the retrieved context via an attention rollout plus a causal perturbation mechanism. These keywords are then used to guide an auxiliary LLM to generate a compact set of keyword-grounded facts from the retrieved passages. Finally, the original context is substituted with these curated facts, ensuring that the generator operates on sanitized evidence rather than the raw retrieved text. Experimental evaluations demonstrate that KFS-RAG significantly reduces the risk of database leakage under injection attacks while maintaining response accuracy and relevance. This work highlights a practical pathway toward building secure and trustworthy RAG systems.

</details>


### [201] [LëtzCross: A Cross-Lingual Page-Level Benchmark for Multimodal Retrieval over Luxembourgish Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21714)
*Omar El Bachyr,Fred Philippy,Laura Maria Bernardy,Saad Ezzini,Jacques Klein,Tegawende Bissyande*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了LëtzCross基准集，用于评估多语言、低资源环境中PDF页面检索的能力，结果显示图像型检索器在多语言环境下表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前页面图像检索在富视觉文档中表现良好，但其在跨语言、低资源场景下的表现与机制尚不明确。作者希望通过引入新的基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 作者构建了LëtzCross基准，收集了卢森堡语PDF文档，并将每页作为图像索引，同时设定英、法、德、卢多语言查询，设计了包含文本型与视觉型QA对的问题，用于系统评测。然后，作者对比了基于OCR文本的检索器和基于页面图像的ColPali型检索器，并探讨了单语言与多语言微调的效果。

Result: 页面图像检索器在所有语言查询中都优于文本型检索器。单语言微调下，法语对卢森堡语有最佳迁移效果。多语言微调时，纳入卢森堡语显著提升了其查询的检索表现。

Conclusion: 图像检索器特别适合跨语言、低资源的页面检索任务，多语言微调且包含目标低资源语言能极大提升检索效果。

Abstract: Recent page-image retrievers such as ColPali have improved retrieval over visually rich documents, yet little is known about how they behave in cross-lingual, low-resource settings. We introduce LëtzCross, a benchmark for cross-lingual page-level retrieval over Luxembourgish PDF documents, with document pages indexed as images and queries provided in English, French, German, and Luxembourgish. The benchmark combines text-focused QA pairs with visually grounded QA pairs, covering both textual and visual retrieval needs in PDF-based RAG. We use LëtzCross to compare OCR-based text-only retrievers with ColPali-style page-image retrievers and find that the latter perform better across query languages in this system-level comparison. We also examine single-language and multilingual fine-tuning. Fine-tuning transfers across query languages, with French yielding the highest mean performance on Luxembourgish queries among the single-language settings. In the multilingual setting, including Luxembourgish gives the strongest results and substantially improves retrieval for Luxembourgish queries.

</details>


### [202] [FCPRAG: Fusion-Controller Parametric Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Stable Multi-Passage LoRA Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21750)
*Jinchang Zhu,Jindong Li,Yi Ding,Xiaojian Nie,Rong Fu,Shuangyong Song,Haowei He,Menglin Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的融合控制参数化RAG（FCPRAG）框架，通过对检索到的证据适配器进行样本级加权融合，提升生成质量，并显著优于以往方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的参数化RAG方法依赖对检索证据的简单融合，存在融合噪声、冲突及全局调参脆弱等问题。希望在检索不确定性多变场景下，实现更自适应、鲁棒的多证据融合。

Method: FCPRAG在每个检索证据的LoRA适配器上加了一个轻量级融合控制器，可预测每段文本的融合分数、混合门控和自适应温度，实现基于检索输出条件的样本级融合。训练时用多适配器融合中的边际贡献作监督信号，全程只需用训练数据。

Result: 在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA、PopQA、CWQ四个公开数据集和三种LLM主干模型上，FCPRAG的F1分数显著提升，2WikiMultiHopQA上提升4.65%，CWQ上提升7.55%，并能减少调参成本且对检索扰动更鲁棒。

Conclusion: FCPRAG通过样本级自适应融合，解决了RAG方法证据融合中的核心瓶颈，有效提升多跳问答和复杂推理任务的性能和健壮性，具有很强的实用价值。

Abstract: Parametric retrieval-augmented generation (PRAG) injects retrieved evidence into a large language model (LLM) through passage-specific LoRA adapters, reducing reliance on long in-context prompts. When multiple passages are retrieved for the same query, however, evidence-level fusion becomes a bottleneck: equal-weight merging can amplify weak or conflicting evidence, and translating retrieval signals into fusion weights often requires fragile global tuning. We propose FCPRAG, a fusion-controlled parametric RAG framework that adds a lightweight controller for retrieval-conditioned, sample-level adapter fusion. The controller predicts per-passage fusion scores together with sample-level calibration signals, including a mixing gate and an adaptive temperature, enabling fusion that stays selective under informative retrieval signals and conservative under uncertainty. FCPRAG is trained with merge-aware supervision derived from each adapter's marginal contribution within a multi-adapter merge, using training data only. We further show that a single dataset-level temperature is suboptimal under heteroscedastic retrieval uncertainty, motivating sample-level adaptation. Experiments on HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, PopQA, and ComplexWebQuestions (CWQ) across three LLM backbones show that FCPRAG consistently improves F1 over standard RAG and parametric RAG baselines, with gains of up to 4.65% on 2WikiMultiHopQA and 7.55% on CWQ, while also reducing tuning cost and improving robustness under retrieval perturbations.

</details>


### [203] [Evaluation Awareness in Language Models: Representation, Verbalization, and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21766)
*Farzaneh Heidari,Amin Memarian,Guillaume Rabusseau*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统性地研究了大语言模型在被评测时能否感知自身处于评测状态（即“评测意识”）。结果发现，大部分模型内部的残差流可线性解码出评测意识，但其在输出中的表达和内部表征并不总是完全一致，同时，通过因果操控能影响模型的言语表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大模型能力与安全评测普遍假设，模型在测试中的行为能反映其实际应用的表现。但如果模型能意识到自己正在被评测（“评测意识”），它可能调节自身表现，从而使评测结果不具备代表性。为此，有必要系统性研究和量化这种评测意识。

Method: 作者在6个不同的语言模型（来自4个家族，3种规模）和3类评测指标上，对“评测意识”进行探查：（1）用线性探针分析模型激活空间中是否存在评测意识表征；（2）用大模型判别其输出内容中是否出现评测相关措辞；（3）对探针方向进行因果操控看能否改变模型行为。对开源Olmo模型，还在所有训练阶段逐步测试这些指标变化。

Result: 所有受测模型的残差流中均可线性解码出评测意识（最佳AUROC≥0.7），但这种激活表征与其输出语言表达相关性较弱，不同模型、层和读取口的表现各异。通过沿探针方向操控，可系统地改变模型评测意识在输出中的表达。Olmo系列模型对比显示，评测意识在基础模型阶段已初现，监督微调后显著增强，之后保持稳定。但因果操控对输出的影响随训练阶段逐步增强。

Conclusion: 大模型内部对评测状态的感知、其在输出中的表达、以及对这些表征的操控彼此并不一致。评测方法需考虑他们的差异，否则可能高估或低估模型实际部署时的风险与能力。

Abstract: Both capability and safety benchmarks rest upon the assumption that the behavior of language models undergoing a test is informative about their behavior in deployment. This assumption can fail, should models infer that they are being evaluated and condition their response on such context. This hypothesis, termed ``evaluation awareness'', has been observed in frontier and open-weight language models alike. We provide a systematic study of this phenomenon, by probing for it across six language models (from four families and three sizes) and three metrics. More precisely, we examine whether (i) being under evaluation is linearly represented within the models' activations space, (ii) it is verbalized in their output tokens (as scored by an LLM-as-judge), and (iii) steering causally affects their behavior. For the open-checkpoint Olmo models, we further test these measures at every training stage. In doing so, we report that evaluation awareness is linearly decodable from the residual streams of every model (best AUROC $\geq 0.7$). By contrast, these representations align only in part with verbalization: their correlations and mutual information are nonzero in some settings, yet vary substantially across models, layers, and readout choices. Nevertheless, steering along probe-derived directions can shift the verbalization scores. Finally, a comparison across the Olmo checkpoints reveals that evaluation awareness is already present within base models, becomes amplified throughout the stages of supervised fine-tuning, and remains stable thereafter---unlike the effects of steering, that grow more pronounced at every successive training stage. These results show the need for evaluations to account for the disjunction between what models represent internally, what they verbalize, and their steering.

</details>


### [204] [No One Model Catches Every Harm: Benchmarking Content Moderation Across Safety Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21775)
*Afshin Orojlooyjadid,Hitesh Patel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文系统评估了53个大语言模型（LLM）在有害内容防护方面的能力，发现主流大模型在不同类型的有害信息面前表现不一，模型“越大越安全”的假设并不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM越来越多地应用于现实世界，但它们在面对恶意对抗、绕过安全规则、隐性仇恨言论等场景时仍有较高风险。目前用于安全防护的有专门内容审核器和通用LLM，但哪种适用于何种有害内容仍不明确，因此需要系统评估和对比它们在复杂性和实际场景下的表现。

Method: 作者对53个不同的LLM，在11个数据集（涵盖4类有害内容）下进行了系统的安全能力评估，按prompt-only和prompt-response两种场景分别测试，比较不同模型、不同规模和专业化程度的表现。

Result: 结果表明，在某些类型的有害内容上，领先的大规模通用模型明显落后于小型的、专门化的模型，各模型在真实对话场景下普遍存在安全漏洞。

Conclusion: 论文挑战了“大模型必然更安全”的普遍假设，并为模型选择和安全防护体系提供了更合理、结构化的建议框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in real-world applications, yet they remain vulnerable to generating harmful content. From adversarial jailbreaks that bypass safety filters to implicit hate that evades detection, the range of risks these models pose continues to grow. While both specialized content moderators and general-purpose LLMs are being used as safety layers, the question of which model is best suited for which type of harmful content remains unanswered. We present the most comprehensive evaluation of LLM safety capabilities to date, systematically testing \textbf{53} models across \textbf{11} datasets that we organize into four distinct categories. Our evaluation under both prompt-only and prompt-response settings uncovers critical blind spots: large frontier models that lead on one category fall significantly behind smaller, specialized alternatives on others, and real-world conversational safety remains largely unsolved across all model families. These findings challenge the assumption that scale alone ensures safety, and provide the community with a structured framework for informed model selection.

</details>


### [205] [Lexical Coupling in GUI Element Grounding: Sentence Embeddings Track Labels across Mobile and Web](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21794)
*Qijia Chen,Giulio Jacucci*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了GUI语义基础任务中，将UI元素转化为文本元数据时，嵌入相似度容易受到界面标签回忆的影响，导致评测结果混淆，提出了更科学的评测建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GUI grounding评测将高的指令与元素嵌入相似度当作语义对应的依据，但这种方法可能由于模型只是识别了界面上可见的文本标签（而非真正理解语义）而导致误判。

Method: 作者在三个移动和网页的数据集上，分别将每个操作作为同屏检索排序任务，用五种预训练单向编码模型，与基于词汇匹配的基线进行系统对比和分析，并细致考察模型在有/无显著标签情形下的表现。

Result: 词汇基线在Top-1指标上表现依旧出色，缺少文本标签的目标极大削弱文本方法效果，不同标签类型和候选池大小对结果影响显著。编码器有部分能力弥补词汇败笔，但实际可用的融合提升远弱于理想状况。

Conclusion: 基于嵌入的GUI grounding评测容易将标签匹配误作语义理解，因此需报告词汇基线、分类型标签分析，并做可落地的融合诊断。作者同时公开分析脚本与数据用于标准化社区评测。

Abstract: GUI grounding evaluations that expose UI elements as text metadata often treat high instruction-element embedding similarity as evidence of semantic grounding. Across three mobile and web benchmarks, we show that this interpretation is frequently confounded by visible-label recovery. Lexical baselines remain competitive at top-1, label-poor targets remain weak for text-only methods, and encoder top-1 hits are predictable from lexical rank, candidate-pool size, and label type. We evaluate each action as a same-screen ranking task, comparing five off-the-shelf single-vector encoders with lexical baselines. Encoders recover some lexical misses, but deployable fusion gains are much smaller than target-aware oracle gains. These findings show that embedding-based evaluations can conflate visible-label recovery with semantic GUI grounding. Embedding-based evaluations should therefore report lexical baselines, label-type stratification, and deployable-fusion diagnostics. Our released repository provides analysis scripts and detexted per-step panels: https://github.com/qijia123/lexical-coupling-release.

</details>


### [206] [More Computational Resources Do Not Ensure Higher Scholarly Impact: Evidence from Leading NLP Conference Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21806)
*Shuai Chen,Tong Bao,Jitong Peng,Chengzhi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了2020-2025年NLP顶会中GPU资源的使用情况及其与学术影响（引用、奖项等）的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来计算资源在NLP研究中变得日益重要，但GPU报告能力与学术影响之间的具体联系还未被系统性研究。

Method: 提取13921篇ACL、EMNLP、NAACL主会议论文的GPU型号与数量，将其标准化，结合引文、奖项、主题和机构数据分析GPU与学术影响的联系。

Result: GPU资源报告变得更普遍但仍不完整，资源主要集中在少量论文，但这些论文对总引用和奖项贡献有限。GPU数量与学术影响（引用、奖项）关联性高于硬件更新代数，但总体相关系数较低。

Conclusion: GPU资源与学术影响有关，但无法单独充分解释研究影响力。

Abstract: Computational resources are increasingly central to NLP research, but how closely reported GPU capability aligns with scholarly impact remains unclear. We analyze 13,921 ACL, EMNLP, and NAACL main-conference papers published between 2020 and 2025, using GPU resources as our operational measure of computational resources. From full texts, we extract GPU models and counts, standardize each paper's largest reported configuration into a comparable hardware-capability measure, and link these data to citation, award, topic, and institutional metadata. GPU reporting became more common but remained incomplete, while reported capability increased mainly through newer hardware generations and medium-scale multi-GPU configurations. Resource concentration substantially exceeded impact concentration: the annual top 20% of GPU-quantifiable papers accounted for 83.9%-89.9% of reported GPU capability, but only 27%-32% of citations and 20%-33% of paper awards. In adjusted models, a tenfold increase in aggregate reported GPU capability was associated with a 3.52-percentage-point increase in within-NLP topic-year citation percentile, but increased model R^2 by only 0.0042. GPU count showed more consistent positive associations with citation and award outcomes than newer hardware generation. Overall, reported GPU resources are associated with scholarly impact but provide little standalone explanation of research influence.

</details>


### [207] [MCite-RL: Towards Reliable Multimodal RAG via Citation-enhanced Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21808)
*Suifeng Zhao,Zida Liu,Xinyu Lei,Lei Sun,Jun Gao,Sujian Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了MCite-RL，一种用于多模态检索增强生成（RAG）视觉引用的强化学习框架，用于提升来源可追溯性及多模态问答的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态RAG系统，在处理视觉信息时，无法稳定实现跨模态推理，导致引用来源不精确或生成答案与引用脱节。本研究针对这一难题，希望提升多模态视觉引用的可靠性和答案质量。

Method: 提出了MCite-RL框架，引入Agentic Refinement模块，通过迭代检索、推理与递归裁剪，动态收窄搜索空间，将视觉引用过程转化为依据证据的动态推理过程。同时，设计了结合过程与结果反馈的奖励机制，在强化学习范式下同时优化答案准确性与引用可追溯性。

Result: 在Wiki-VISA、FinRAGBench-V和MMLongBench-Doc等多项基准测试上，MCite-RL实现了引用精度和答案质量的联合最优，效果优于现有方法。

Conclusion: MCite-RL框架能够显著提升多模态RAG场景下的视觉引用精准性与问答可信度，具有较强实用价值。

Abstract: Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) with visual citation is crucial for ensuring the traceability and verifiability of MLLMs. However, current RAG and SFT-based methods struggle to achieve robust cross-modal reasoning, causing imprecise visual citations or decoupling between the citation and the generated answers. To address these limitations, we propose MCite-RL, a citation-enhanced agentic reinforcement learning framework designed for reliable multimodal RAG. MCite-RL introduces an Agentic Refinement module for visual citation that employs iterative retrieval, reasoning, and recursive cropping to progressively narrow the search space, transforming citation into a dynamic, evidence-driven reasoning process rather than a static step. Furthermore, we incorporate a Citation-enhanced Reward mechanism that integrates both process-level and outcome-level feedback within a reinforcement learning paradigm to jointly optimize answer accuracy and source traceability. Extensive experiments on benchmarks such as Wiki-VISA, FinRAGBench-V, and MMLongBench-Doc demonstrate that MCite-RL effectively achieves the joint optimization of citation precision and answer quality.

</details>


### [208] [Convergence in Science, Divergence in Religion: Calibrated Framing Differences Across Wikipedia's Language Editions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21821)
*Hung-Hsuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究分析了多语种维基百科条目在描述相同概念时的表达差异，并提出了一种校准距离测量方法，以更准确衡量不同语言之间的 framing 差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究多关注不同语言维基百科条目的覆盖范围差异（coverage gap），但关于同一概念在不同语言下 framing（表述视角/立场）的差异性分析较少。本研究旨在弥补这方面的空白，更系统、定量地衡量和理解跨语言表达同一概念时存在的 framing difference。

Method: 选取了20个语言版本、150个基于Wikidata锚定的概念、涵盖4个领域，共2,799篇有效文章。利用三种多语种编码器（LaBSE、multilingual MPNet、CMLM）计算条目的向量嵌入距离。为去除语言自身差异带来的噪声，引入了以校准集（如化学元素、数字、颜色等跨文化稳定概念）定义的基线调整方法，即校准距离：实际距离减去相同语言对在校准集上的平均距离。

Result: 即使是在跨文化含义稳定的校准概念下，不同语言对的平均嵌入距离最大值也可为最小值的3.6倍，且同一语言家族内部距离普遍更小。校准距离方法能显著减少不同语言对间由于语言对齐造成的差异，并减弱语言家族效应。三种编码器下，科学类条目在不同语言间的对齐度比基准概念更高；宗教类条目的 framing 差异最大，科技类最小，政治领域内部在部分概念（如“审查”和“难民”）上差异突出，而“民主”和“人权”则非常一致。不同编码器对概念的排序具有高度一致性。

Conclusion: 该文提出的校准距离概念和测量流程，提供了跨多语言系统性、定量分析 framing 差距的新方法，对研究跨文化信息表述、编辑偏见与对齐具有重要意义，对相关NLP基准和社会科学研究具有参考价值。作者还开源了全部代码、数据及各语言对的基准数据，为后续工作奠定基础。

Abstract: When Wikipedia's language editions describe the same concept, how differently do they frame it? Prior work measures coverage gaps between editions; we measure framing distance for matched concepts. We analyze 2,799 valid articles from 3,000 possible concept-language observations, spanning 150 Wikidata-anchored concepts, 20 language editions, 4 domains, and a calibration set. Raw embedding distances reflect both content differences and how well the encoder aligns each language pair. Even among calibration concepts with stable cross-cultural denotations (e.g., chemical elements, numbers, colors), the largest language-pair mean distance is 3.6 times the smallest, and distances are typically smaller within language families. We define a baseline-adjusted distance (calibrated distance): the distance between two language versions of a concept minus the mean distance for calibration concepts in the same language pair. This adjustment substantially reduces pair-specific alignment differences and the language-family pattern. Across three multilingual encoders (LaBSE, multilingual MPNet, and CMLM), scientific articles align more closely than calibration articles, and all three rank religion first and science/technology last. Concept-level rankings are highly consistent across encoders (Spearman rho=0.75-0.79 for MPNet and CMLM relative to LaBSE). Religion lies significantly above the calibration baseline under LaBSE. Within politics, divergence concentrates on concepts such as censorship and refugee, while democracy and human rights are among the most aligned. Code, data, and per-language-pair calibration baselines are released.\footnote{https://github.com/hhchen1105/cross-linqual-concept}

</details>


### [209] [Do Large Language Models Perform Well on Comprehending Poetic Logic in Modern Chinese Poetry?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21827)
*Tian Lan,Shanshan Wang,Zehua Duo,Jiang Li,Guanglai Gao,Derek F. Wong,Xiangdong Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Peony，一个专为评估现代中文诗歌“诗性逻辑”理解能力而设计的基准，用以系统测试现有主流大语言模型（LLMs）。结果显示现有模型在该领域存在显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在NLP任务中表现出色，但它们对于现代中文诗歌等具有独特文学特征文本的理解力几乎未被探索。现代中文诗歌的“诗性逻辑”要求超越表层语义分析的整体性推理，现有模型和评价标准对此缺乏系统性方法。

Method: 作者提出了Peony基准，将诗性逻辑细分为四项任务，覆盖段落、诗句及意象三个层面，并用Peony系统评估六种主流大语言模型，分别在非推理和推理设置下对比性能。

Result: 实验结果显示，在理解现代中文诗歌的诗性逻辑方面，现有主流大语言模型表现有限，难以进行有效的整体性推理。Peony基准能有效揭示并分析这些局限。

Conclusion: 当前LLMs在现代中文诗歌领域的诗性逻辑理解上有显著缺陷，Peony基准的提出有助于推动该领域系统评估和模型改进。作者公开了数据和代码，利于后续研究。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved significant progress across a wide range of natural language processing (NLP) tasks, yet their ability to understand literary texts, particularly modern Chinese poetry, remains largely unexplored. The unique literary characteristics of modern Chinese poetry necessitate a distinct form of reasoning for effective comprehension. Unlike conventional texts that convey clear information, the unique "poetic logic" of modern Chinese poetry requires a holistic reasoning approach that goes beyond superficial semantic analysis to be understood. However, current evaluation paradigms largely ignore this critical dimension. To address this gap, we propose Peony, the first benchmark specifically designed for evaluating the poetic logic of modern Chinese poetry. We define poetic logic as four tasks across three levels, namely stanza, line, and imagery, and systematically evaluate and analyze six mainstream LLMs based on Peony. We evaluate these models under both non-thinking and thinking configurations. The experimental results reveal the limitations of current LLMs in understanding the poetic logic of modern Chinese poetry and validate the effectiveness and necessity of Peony. Our data and code will be available.

</details>


### [210] [Training a Knowledge Base: Supervised Structure Learning for Agent-Curated Document Stores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21829)
*Yu Pan,Hongfeng Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将知识库本身作为训练模型核心的反向检索增强生成方法，并系统性测量和利用基于监督问答对的知识库编辑对下游任务效率和准确率的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统检索增强生成方法通常将文档库视为静态输入，很少关注动态编辑和优化知识库本身对系统性能的具体影响。作者希望探索知识库结构被有监督方式主动优化时，能为下游问答任务带来多大提升。

Method: 训练代理通过对当前知识库回答有监督的问题，再基于正确答案（gold）编辑知识库，最终生成的知识库冻结后由reader在固定操作预算内评估查询表现。创新引入key-coverage gradient衡量训练题目对测试题目的覆盖程度，用覆盖度曲线刻画泛化能力，而非简单的训练测试二分。

Result: 在以标注问答对训练优化的知识库上，reader准确率和效率远超于无监督方法（分别提升1.6倍和1.8倍），动作节省Ablation高达31%。在新的语料PhantomWiki上亦表现优异。可见知识库结构通过简单问答对就能高效提升系统整体性能，并且覆盖扩展线性增长，远未饱和。

Conclusion: 有监督驱动的知识库编辑显著提升了基于该库的问答效率和准确率，覆盖-性能曲线展示知识库潜力未被挖尽，扩大训练集可进一步提升系统能力，这为动态知识库构建和检索增强生成奠定了坚实理论和方法基础。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation treats the document store as a frozen input, and the systems that instead let an agent curate one never measure what curation does to the store. We invert the framing: the knowledge base is the model. A training agent answers a supervised question against the current store, is shown the gold, then edits the store; an unchanged reader is later examined on a frozen snapshot under a fixed action budget. Where offline graph construction is unsupervised, (question, answer) pairs are our labels -- and that supervision is what makes the structure cheap. Per point of corpus indexed it returns 1.6x the action saving and 1.8x the accuracy of an unsupervised entity index covering everything, using 1,913 links against its 196,112. On questions the store trained on, an unchanged reader spends 31% fewer actions at higher accuracy, and the result reproduces on an official PhantomWiki generation whose questions we did not write. To measure how far this reaches we introduce a key-coverage gradient, a probe varying how much of a question the training set touched, replacing a train/test split's pass/fail with a decay curve. Generalization proves endpoint-dependent: accuracy carries to unseen questions (+0.167 F1 where both of a question's keys were indexed, +0.100 where one was, zero where neither) while the action saving stays on trained questions. Because that decay is indexed by coverage rather than by novelty, more training extends it -- and the store is undertrained, not saturated: coverage grows linearly in new questions and stops the moment training repeats them, so a hundred questions reach a quarter of the corpus and four times as many would close the gap.

</details>


### [211] [GUI-Primitives: Diagnosing Spatial Reasoning Failures in Vision-Language GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21832)
*Md Abrar Jahin,Md Rizwan Parvez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了GUI-Primitives基准，用于测试计算机使用代理是否能正确将自然语言指令中的空间关系绑定到界面元素上。研究发现当前视觉-语言模型在空间关系理解上表现有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准没能明确检测模型是否能理解并正确绑定界面中的关系性语言，阻碍了对计算机代理定位界面元素能力的评估。

Method: 作者设计了994对对比性指令及配套截图，每对变换空间关系表达但固定候选目标，通过人工注释验证并让19种视觉-语言模型进行测试，分析预测点落在候选区间内的情况，并与其它基准（ScreenSpot-Pro）做相关性分析。

Result: 模型在严格“点入框”准确率仅有32%以下。多数预测并未落在任何候选区，说明定位失败。若限定在区域内，部分位置/关系类型（如左右、上下等）准确率高达0.82-0.90，但包含、遮挡等类型则仅0.5左右，表明主要问题是候选定位而非关系理解。

Conclusion: 现有视觉-语言模型在界面元素空间关系上的绑定能力有限。新基准为诊断和提升模型的空间关系理解提供了工具，但实际落地还需进一步提升模型能力。

Abstract: Computer-use agents ground natural-language instructions in screenshots to locate interface elements, yet existing benchmarks do not isolate whether models bind relational language to the correct element. We introduce GUI-Primitives, a 994-item benchmark of contrastive instruction pairs over seven spatial relations in graphical user interfaces (left/right, above/below, containment, alignment, proximity, list ordinal, occlusion). Each pair holds the screenshot and anchor fixed while changing the relation expression, so the correct target moves between two designated candidates. Five annotators validate a 196-item subset ($κ= 0.94$ well-formedness; $κ= 0.79$ target selection). Nineteen vision-language models reach at most $32\%$ strict point-in-box accuracy. Because models emit unconstrained coordinates, we classify each prediction by the candidate region it falls within. Predictions fall outside both candidates on $60-92\%$ of items. Conditional on falling within a candidate region, target selection reaches 0.82-0.90 for horizontal position, vertical position, proximity, and list ordinal, but does not differ significantly from 0.50 for containment and occlusion: most failures reflect candidate localization rather than relation understanding. Across ten models, benchmark accuracy correlates with ScreenSpot-Pro accuracy (Spearman $ρ= +0.74$), an exploratory association at this sample size. Marking the two designated candidates raises selection accuracy by 35--57 percentage points, an oracle diagnostic that supplies the candidate set rather than a deployable method. We release the benchmark, predictions, and code.

</details>


### [212] [PUMA: A Polish Benchmark for Culturally Grounded Multimodal Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21853)
*Sławomir Dadas,Michał Perełkiewicz,Rafał Poświata,Małgorzata Grębowiec,Bartłomiej Jaworski,Izabela Woźniakowska*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出了PUMA——首个面向波兰语和文化的多模态模型评测基准，对多模态AI模型在本地语境下的能力进行了全面评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型多数以英语为主，缺乏在非英语、具体如波兰语及其本地文化下的系统性评测，导致模型在多语言、多文化背景下的实际应用能力成疑。

Method: 作者构建了包含900个手工设计任务的PUMA基准，涵盖文本、图像、音频以及视觉丰富文档的处理，重点评测模型的文化理解和实际应用能力。论文评估了商用前沿模型、开源权重模型以及专项小模型的表现，进行全面对比。

Result: 顶级商用大模型在视觉问答上表现优异，但大多数模型在复杂音频处理或文档理解方面力有不足。实验同时发现商用与开源或小规模系统在能力上出现了明显差距。

Conclusion: PUMA基准表明当前多模态模型在波兰语及文化背景下的多模态理解尚存明显不足，尤其是在复杂音频和文档任务中。作者开源评测工具，希望推动本地化多模态人工智能研究的发展。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly moving beyond text processing, adding support for other modalities such as images and audio. While text understanding and generation have been extensively studied, multimodal data processing capabilities, particularly in the context of cultures and languages other than English, have not yet been evaluated comprehensively. In this paper, we propose PUMA (Polish Unified Multimodal Assessment), a novel benchmark of 900 hand-crafted tasks designed to probe the limits of multimodal models in the Polish cultural and linguistic context. The dataset evaluates both cultural understanding and practical skill in processing text, images, audio, and visually rich documents. Our extensive evaluation of frontier commercial models, open-weights models, and specialized smaller systems highlights a significant performance gap. While top commercial models achieve high scores in visual question answering, most models struggle with complex audio or document understanding. We open-source our evaluation framework to advance localized multimodal AI research.

</details>


### [213] [HiDiffTIR: Hierarchical Difficulty-Aware Policy Optimization for Multi-Turn Tool-Integrated Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21863)
*Yucan Guo,Xiaohan Wang,Miao Su,Saiping Guan,Zhongni Hou,Jiajun Chai,Wei Lin,Guojun Yin,Xiaolong Jin,Jiafeng Guo,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层难度感知的策略优化框架 HiDiffTIR，用于提升大模型工具集成推理（TIR）能力，有效区分不同难度的推理步骤，从而提升多轮推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的方法对所有正确的工具调用等同对待，无法区分推理步骤和路径的难度差异，导致学习信号不精确，影响复杂任务的学习效果。因此，需要一种能够感知任务难度并更精准分配奖励的方法，提高推理效率和效果。

Method: 提出HiDiffTIR，一种分层（轨迹和轮次）难度感知信用分配的策略优化框架，无需额外人工标注，仅依赖于标准强化学习过程中产生的分组统计信息，对多轮TIR任务中的不同难度推理步骤进行精细化奖励分配，引导模型关注更重要的信息。

Result: 在三个工具使用基准任务上进行大量实验，HiDiffTIR在多轮TIR性能和工具调用准确率方面均优于现有强化学习强基线，证明了该方法在工具集成LLM智能体中的有效性。

Conclusion: 难度感知的信用分配对于提升工具集成大模型智能体的强化学习训练至关重要，HiDiffTIR无需额外监督下实现了更优的推理性能和调用准确性，为相关任务提供了新的有效训练范式。

Abstract: Tool-Integrated Reasoning (TIR) is a fundamental capability for LLM agents to solve complex tasks by interacting with external tools iteratively. Reinforcement Learning (RL) has become the dominant paradigm for enabling this capability. However, existing approaches typically assign uniform trajectory-level advantages and treat all correct tool calls equally, ignoring the varying difficulty and learning value across trajectories and reasoning steps. This can lead to imprecise learning signals that do not adequately distinguish between trivial and challenging tool-use patterns. To address this limitation, we propose HiDiffTIR, a Hierarchical Difficulty-aware policy optimization framework for multi-turn TIR. HiDiffTIR performs difficulty-aware credit assignment at both trajectory and turn levels, enabling the policy to focus on more informative trajectories and harder reasoning steps. Notably, this fine-grained optimization is achieved without additional supervision, relying solely on group-level statistics derived from standard RL rollouts. Extensive experiments on three tool-using benchmarks demonstrate that HiDiffTIR consistently improves multi-turn TIR performance and tool invocation accuracy over strong RL baselines, highlighting the necessity of difficulty-aware credit assignment for effective policy optimization in tool-integrated LLM agents.

</details>


### [214] [The Chase Is the Curriculum, the Capture Anchors the Credit: Pursuit-Evasion Self-Play for Zero-Data LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21871)
*Jing Yu,Shengchao Chen,Yiyun Tan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种新的零数据自对弈（self-play）方法LURE，用于在无需大量人工任务的情况下提升大语言模型的推理能力。通过把任务难度设计成追逐-逃逸游戏，显著提升了模型在零样本推理场景下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习与可验证奖励方法依赖大量人工设计任务，限制了模型扩展性。零数据自对弈虽可移除该依赖，但传统做法只通过事后拒绝难学任务，缺乏有效任务难度控制和稀疏奖励问题。作者旨在解决这一难题，实现自适应、自动化的任务难度分布与过程性奖励。

Method: 将自对弈过程建模为追逐-逃逸博弈：逃逸者（evader）在环境难度轴上设置任务，试图保持领先于追逐者（pursuer）；逃逸者的奖励设计为“捕获前沿”，即被追逐者在一半场景中正好捕获，形成最优难度分布；追逐者使用过程性密集奖励，将过程进展和终点捕获联合归一化，提升学习信号密度与对抗演化稳定性。

Result: 在三个可验证推理环境和三类模型骨干上，LURE在统一及专精任务下均明显优于先进对比方法。其中统一模型在九个未见的挑战基准的零样本推理任务上整体表现优于所有训练基线。

Conclusion: LURE实现了无监督、自动化的任务难度调节和密集奖励信号引导，有效提升了大语言模型的泛化推理能力，是零数据自对弈方法的一项重要突破。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has become the dominant recipe for improving large language model reasoning, yet it presumes large human-curated task collections. Zero-data self-play removes this dependency, but existing methods vet learnability only by probing candidates and rejecting post hoc, never learning where along an environment's difficulty axis to place a task, and credit the solver with sparse terminal rewards alone. We recast zero-data self-play as a pursuit-evasion game: in LURE, an LLM evader positions tasks along each environment's difficulty axis to stay one step ahead of a planner-executor pursuer that hunts it down through verifiable interaction. The evader is trained on a capture-frontier reward that peaks when the solver captures it on exactly half of its rollouts, turning barely catchable into a learned positioning strategy rather than a hand-tuned rejection band. The pursuer earns capture-anchored dense process credit, in which monotone verifier progress is group-normalized jointly with the terminal capture under a round-anchored KL that keeps the co-evolution stable. Across three verifiable reasoning environments and three backbone families, LURE outperforms advanced baselines under unified/specialist settings, while the unified model attains stronger aggregate OOD zero-shot accuracy than all trained baselines across nine held-out benchmarks from three task families.

</details>


### [215] [BanglaVeilGuard: Cross-Script Safety Benchmarking and Lightweight Guardrails for Bangla Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21880)
*Md. Rakibul Hassan,Muhammad Iqbal Hossain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了BanglaVeilGuard，这是首个专为孟加拉语（Bangla）设计的轻量级安全基准和提示防护工具，能够针对孟加拉语多种书写变体提升大语言模型的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM安全评测主要以英语环境和标准书写为主，难以适应真实孟加拉语用户常见的跨脚本、模糊拼写、混合语态以及方言等多种表现形式。缺乏能够支持这些变体的安全测评体系和有效防护措施，限制了孟加拉语LLM的安全应用。

Method: 1) 构建涵盖标准孟加拉语、罗马化、Banglish（语码混合变体）、Bangla-English混合、噪声化孟加拉语、方言等6种语言形式的安全基准（共2,366条高质量提示，另有354条测试分组）。2) 提出非侵入式多视图正则化、提示风险分类与门控机制组成的轻量级prompt guard，能无须修改目标模型权重地筛查危险提示。3) 在Claude Opus 4.8、BanglaLLama、TituLLM和TigerLLM-1B等多种模型上测试效果。

Result: 加装BanglaVeilGuard后，上述目标模型的恶意攻击成功率从93.8%-100.0%显著降低至6.3%；TigerLLM-1B结合防护后，准确率78.2%，攻击成功率8.8%；提示防护器对不安全提示的召回率达88.5%，明显优于基于提示的baseline。

Conclusion: BanglaVeilGuard显著增强了孟加拉语LLM多变体提示下的安全性，证明其在跨脚本和复杂语态下的适用性和有效性。但在方言和噪声良性提示上的拒绝率偏高，指出了未来安全与友好性权衡的改进方向。

Abstract: Bangla large language model (LLM) safety is difficult to evaluate with English-centric or standard-script benchmarks because Bangla users routinely write across scripts, spellings, code-mixed forms, and regional registers. This paper presents BanglaVeilGuard, a compact Bangla-first safety benchmark and lightweight prompt guard for six language forms: standard Bangla, Romanized Bangla, Banglish, code-mixed Bangla--English, noisy Bangla, and dialectal Bangla. The benchmark contains 2,366 quality-filtered prompts and a held-out 354-prompt evaluation split spanning unsafe, safe, and safe-sensitive requests. BanglaVeilGuard uses non-destructive multi-view normalization with a prompt-risk classifier and thresholded pre-generation gate, allowing it to screen prompts for heterogeneous target models without changing their weights. Across target-model families, guarded runs reduce attack success under deterministic response scoring from 93.8--100.0\% to 6.3\% for Claude Opus 4.8, BanglaLLama, and TituLLM; TigerLLM-1B with BanglaVeilGuard achieves 78.2\% accuracy with 8.8\% ASR. The prompt guard also attains 88.5\% unsafe recall, substantially above the evaluated prompt-only guard baselines. The main remaining cost is over-refusal on dialectal and noisy benign prompts, revealing a concrete safety-helpfulness frontier for Bangla LLM deployment.

</details>


### [216] [Modeling Claim Dependency Structure for Patent Litigation Prediction with Graph Attention Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21924)
*Takao Arai,Hiroyasu Inoue*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ClaimGAT模型，通过图注意力网络（GAT）结合每一项权利要求的依赖结构，实现对专利诉讼风险的准确预测，显著优于传统BERT模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专利诉讼成本高，影响R&D积极性。现有BERT模型未考虑权利要求间依赖结构，容易遗漏决定专利范围和法律效力的重要信息，亟需更有效的风险识别方法。

Method: 提出ClaimGAT模型，将每个专利权利要求独立编码，建立有向依赖图，利用图注意力网络（GATConv）处理，并通过Attentional Aggregation方式聚合，最终给出专利整体诉讼风险得分及各权利要求的贡献权重。实验在134万份美国专利数据上，通过六种消融实验验证各模块有效性。

Result: ClaimGAT模型在仅利用授权时可见信息条件下，AUC-ROC达0.818，前10%提升度达4.89倍，显著高于传统方法。模型还能揭示高风险专利通常编码结构选择和内容敏感性分离，符合防御性专利撰写特征。

Conclusion: ClaimGAT有效结合权利要求依赖结构，提升诉讼风险预测性能，并提供可解释的结构性分析，对专利风险管理和防御性撰写有实用价值。

Abstract: Patent litigation imposes substantial costs on firms and distorts R&D incentives, making early risk identification a practically important task. While prior work has applied BERT-based models to patent claim text, two fundamental limitations remain: flat sequence encoding loses the dependency structure between independent and dependent claims that legally determines patent scope, and feeding the entire claim set to a single encoder discards legally critical text. A six-model ablation on 1.34 million USPTO utility patents confirms that per-claim encoding, graph connectivity, attention, and Attentional Aggregation each provide independent, additive predictive value. We propose ClaimGAT, a Graph Attention Network that encodes each claim independently, constructs a directed claim dependency graph, processes it with GATConv layers, and aggregates independent claims via Attentional Aggregation to yield both a litigation risk score and claim-level gate weights that enable post-hoc structural analysis. ClaimGAT achieves an AUC-ROC of 0.818 and a lift of 4.89x at the top 10%, using only information observable at the time of patent grant. It reveals a tendency in high-risk patents for structural selection and content sensitivity to diverge, a pattern consistent with defensive claim drafting.

</details>


### [217] [EDGE: Experience-Distillation for Guided Exploration in Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21946)
*Can Xie,Yuyi Zhou,Wen Yang,Ziyi zhang,Siyao Song,Yingzhuo Deng,Shuo Ren,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了EDGE方法，通过在训练阶段利用经验作为临时支架，并逐步内化其益处，提升了LLM智能体的长期任务解决能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于经验增强的方法虽能在推理时进行检索，但高度依赖外部检索，且忽略了策略进化带来的能力提升，导致重复利用性差与外部依赖强。

Method: EDGE将检索到的经验在训练时作为支架，分组采样出有经验与无经验的轨迹，仅吸收经验带来的正向增益，并通过反向KL目标函数将经验行为蒸馏到基础策略中。同时，通过共进化经验库动态合成指引与剔除过时经验。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop任务中，EDGE分别比GRPO提升8.3和12.5成功率（在7B模型下），并在移除外部经验检索后依然保留96.0%的支架性能。

Conclusion: EDGE有效提升了大型语言模型智能体探索与长期任务能力，减少了对外部经验的依赖。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with outcome-based objectives such as GRPO enables LLM-based agents to solve complex, long-horizon tasks, yet the reusable exploration patterns embedded in interaction trajectories are largely discarded after a single policy update. Existing experience-augmented approaches retrieve historical guidance at inference time, but they apply experiences without accounting for the policy's evolving capability and create persistent dependencies on external retrieval. We propose EDGE (Experience-Distillation for Guided Exploration), a framework that treats retrieved experiences as temporary training-time scaffolds and progressively internalizes their benefits into the parametric policy. Concretely, EDGE partitions each rollout group into experience-conditioned and experience-free trajectories to estimate and admit only positive marginal gains without extra sampling, then distills the induced behavior into the base policy via a reverse-KL objective on its own empirical support. A co-evolutionary experience bank further synthesizes guidance from emerging failure modes and prunes obsolete entries as the policy evolves. On ALFWorld and WebShop, EDGE improves over GRPO by 8.3 and 12.5 success-rate points at the 7B scale and retains 96.0% of its scaffolded performance when external experiences are removed at inference time. The code is available at https://github.com/xvolcano02/EDGE.

</details>


### [218] [Bulbul: A Dataset for Dialectal Arabic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21950)
*Ahmed Ashraf,Aisha Alansari,Fadel Al Abbas,Nada Almarwani,Samah Aloufi,Saad Ezzini,Maged S. Al-Shaibani,Doaa Dalaq,AbdelRahim A. Elmadany,Muhammad Abdul-Mageed,Mohamed Mehdi Trigui,Dania Refai,Layan Refai,Mohamed Akrout,Mustafa Jarrar,Wasfi G. Al-Khatib,Alaa Dalaq,Darin El-Nakla,Samir Abdaljalil,Abdulrahman Al-Fakih,Nour El Imane Zeghib,Moussa Redah,Salmane Chafik,Mohamed El-Attar,Rima Grati,Sarah Kohail,Malak Alkhorasani,Khadijah Al Safwan,Ismail M. Mudhaffar,Ali Altam,Ahmed Al-Shaikh,Adnan Saeed,Hamzah Luqman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了BULBUL多方言阿拉伯语自动语音识别(ASR)数据集，覆盖11国多方言，并建立了基线，推动阿拉伯语ASR研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语ASR面临着双言制、方言多样性大和语音资源稀缺等挑战，现有数据集在多样性与标注质量间难以兼顾，严重制约ASR技术发展。

Method: 作者收集了来自11个阿拉伯国家275名说话者的语音，涵盖多种方言与子方言，并有结构化分布。同时，有结构化记录古典阿拉伯语和现代标准阿拉伯语。所有录音通过两级人工审核以保障质量。

Result: 作者构建了BULBUL数据集，并在其上对多种先进ASR系统进行了基准测试，给出阿拉伯语现代方言和口音敏感ASR的强性能基线。

Conclusion: BULBUL数据集为阿拉伯语多方言ASR研究提供了高质量、多样化的资源，有望推动相关研究和应用发展。

Abstract: Arabic automatic speech recognition (ASR) faces unique challenges due to diglossia, extensive regional dialect variation, and limited speech resources. Existing speech datasets often focus on single dialects or large-scale broadcast/web data, leading to trade-offs between linguistic diversity and annotation quality. We present BULBUL, a multi-dialect Arabic ASR dataset collected from 275 speakers in 11 Arab countries. BULBUL includes structured dialect and sub-dialect coverage, as well as recordings of classical Arabic and modern standard Arabic spoken by participants in their native dialectal accents to support accent-aware modeling. The quality of the recordings was ensured through a two-level human verification process. We further benchmark a range of recent ASR systems, establishing strong baselines for modern dialectal and accented Arabic ASR.

</details>
