KTC represents the candidate tokens involved in LLM generation as a positive semi-definite kernel that integrates both the LLM's conditional distribution and a token contradiction score. We then use the Von Neumann entropy to quantify the uncertainty of this kernel. To estimate token contradiction, we develop a new approach based on frequency statistics from the Wikipedia corpus. Although CPU-only, our approach achieves over an 8.2x speedup compared to state-of-the-art GPU-accelerated methods based on cross-encoders, and over a 65x speedup compared to CPU-only methods with comparable performance. Our evaluation spans two benchmarks across four European languages and 16 different models. KTC not only matches the average performance of existing methods but also outperforms them in high-precision regimes. This combination of computational efficiency and accuracy makes real-time monitoring of LLM outputs practical in production.

</details>


### [257] [From Diagnosis to Redesign: Using Quantitative Ethnography to Improve Multi-Agent LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22566)
*Vedant Khatri,Anthony Cusimano,Zachari Swiecki,Zhen Xu,Xiner Liu,Renzhe Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文采用定量民族志方法分析多智能体大语言模型系统中的代理间互动话语，通过模型改进提升了自动作文评分的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体大语言模型系统虽可分工协作为复杂任务推理，但多代理并不自动保证推理一致或结果符合目标。需要有诊断与优化该类系统的话语分析方法。

Method: 提出定量民族志(QE)方法，利用认知网络分析(ENA)对五代理多智能体辩论作文评分系统的对话模式建模，比较正确与错误评分决策的代理互动话语特征，进而用于提示词重设计。

Result: 初始系统中，正确评分以基于评分规则的举证、认同与深入讨论为特征，错误评分则以较松散且与评分标准关联弱的对话链为特征。依据分析结果优化代理提示词，系统准确率由27.78%提升至40.28%，不正确辩论的话语结构趋向于正确范式。

Conclusion: 定量民族志方法可实现AI多智能体系统的话语-表现联动分析与重设计迭代，优化代理互动模式与系统决策表现。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) systems are designed to improve reasoning by decomposing tasks across multiple agents with specialized functions, but the presence of multiple agents does not inherently guarantee coherent reasoning or outputs that align with task objectives. This paper introduces a quantitative ethnographic (QE) approach for diagnosing and redesigning multi-agent LLM systems based on the discourse produced through agent interactions. We test this approach using automated essay scoring as an example context, applying Epistemic Network Analysis (ENA) to model a five-agent multi-agent debate system and examine differences between debates that produced correct versus incorrect scoring decisions. Results show that, in the initial system, correct scoring decisions were characterized by rubric-grounded justification, agreement, and elaboration. Incorrect scoring decisions, in contrast, were characterized by extended proposition-challenge-response exchanges that were less consistently tied to rubric criteria. We then used the findings to revise the agents' prompts. The revised system improved exact scoring accuracy from 27.78% to 40.28% and shifted the discourse of incorrect debates toward the rubric-grounded pattern of correct ones, making the two nearly indistinguishable. Based on these results, we argue that QE can support a diagnostic-to-redesign loop for AI reasoning by tracing how patterns of agent interaction relate to system performance, informing prompt redesign, and evaluating whether those redesigns change both outcomes and interaction patterns.

</details>


### [258] [Hybrid Panels: Toward Human-AI Collaboration in Survey Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22582)
*Julia Romberg,Tobias Gummer,Gabriella Lapesa,Tanja Kunz,Claudia Wagner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出并展示了“混合型面板”（hybrid panel）的概念，将人工智能大模型与人类参与者结合用于大规模社会调查，以应对现有调查中的响应率下降和成本上升等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统大规模人口调查正面临响应率降低、成本增加、数据滞后和非响应偏差等问题。作者希望利用AI技术突破这些难题，提升调查的效率、质量与时效性。

Method: 提出了“混合型面板”基础设施：结合大语言模型（LLMs）和人类受访者于纵向的调查面板中，通过LLM模拟和修正与实际人口的偏差，反馈用于后续调查的设计与实施。首次实现了该混合型面板的试点研究，并建立了从数据收集到验证的完整框架。

Result: 试点研究验证了混合型面板的可行性，初步揭示了涉及人类与LLM协作调查过程中的若干挑战，如模型与真实人口之间的适应与误差、参与者招募与问题分配等。

Conclusion: 混合型面板为大规模调查探索出新路径，具备提升调查质量和效率的潜力，但仍需进一步研究以完善其框架和解决实际应用中的开放性问题。

Abstract: Large-scale population surveys are essential for generating robust social and scientific insights, yet they face significant challenges, including declining response rates, increasing data collection costs, long delays between data collection and data provision, and the risk of nonresponse bias. Advances in artificial intelligence (AI) have opened up new opportunities for AI-supported survey infrastructures where the goal is to overcome these challenges without limiting the data quality. A promising AI-enabled survey infrastructure for which we build a first pilot is a hybrid panel. A hybrid panel is a longitudinal AI-enabled survey which allows to iteratively improve the alignment between large language models (LLMs) and the population they aim to simulate and use the errors to inform the design and implementation of the next survey wave (e.g., inform the participant recruitment, assignment of questions to participants). It incorporates both human participants and LLMs as fundamental elements of its design. In this research note, we introduce the concept of a hybrid panel by providing a definition and outlining an overarching framework, spanning data collection to data validation. We detail results from a first pilot study to illustrate (open) challenges that we identify for hybrid panels.

</details>


### [259] [Teaching LLMs How ICU Physicians Approach Clinical Reasoning Through OMOP-Aligned Retrieval Improves Reasoning Across Clinical Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22622)
*Miguel Contreras,Scott Siegel,Subhash Nerella,Jessica Sena,Jiaqing Zhang,Heng Sun,Hruday Tej Akkaladevi,Peiyu Lu,Jordan Rosen,Sumit Kapoor,Sasank Desaraju,Grace R. Thompson,Jacob Purcell,Michael Petrauskis,Philip KW. Hong,Meghan Brennan,Sarah Chrabaszcz,Tierra Smith,Ronnie Ren,Michel S. Kabbash,Ceyhun Haziroglu,Rushi Patel,Gabriel Gomez,Charlotte Chaiklin,Randy Leung,Kenneth N. John,Whitman Wiggins,Philip Kayser,Vincent Bird,Maria Bruzzone,Tyler J. Loftus,Azra Bihorac,Parisa Rashidi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了ICU-REACT数据集，通过训练大语言模型（LLMs）掌握ICU中专家级的临床推理，显著提升临床推理能力，并优于现有通用及医学LLMs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在ICU等高数据密度和快速变化的环境中，临床决策对有效识别和推理解读病人信息十分依赖，现有LLM应用大多只支持表层检索而非真实的临床推理，因此有必要开发能学习深层推理能力的工具。

Method: 提出ICU-REACT数据集，邀请19名临床专家参与数据构建，采用人员循环训练流程，并以此精调多个参数级别、不同体系结构的Clin-REACT大模型，在五大临床推理基准任务和具体诊疗测试中进行评估。

Result: Clin-REACT模型在所有五项临床推理基准中，均超越其基础模型及主流的通用/医学大模型，且这种推理提升能力在多种任务（如诊断、脚本一致性测试等）上也有体现。

Conclusion: 通过专家推理监督训练出的LLM在ICU等关键环境下提升了泛化的临床推理能力，但在实际临床用前仍需进一步前瞻性验证。

Abstract: Clinical decision-making relies on identifying relevant patient information to guide diagnosis and treatment, a challenge that is especially difficult in the data-dense and rapidly changing intensive care unit (ICU). Large language models (LLMs) could support this task. However, existing applications and datasets mostly emphasize surface-level retrieval or factual recall rather than the inductive and deductive reasoning clinicians practice to select and reason over decision-relevant evidence. We hypothesized that training LLMs on expert ICU reasoning could yield clinical reasoning skills that generalize beyond critical care. Here we introduce ICU-REACT, a reasoning dataset developed with 19 clinicians through a clinician-in-the-loop framework to teach LLMs to perform information retrieval and context-aware clinical reasoning in the ICU. Using ICU-REACT, we fine-tuned Clin-REACT models spanning 8B-70B parameters and three model families. Across five clinical reasoning benchmarks, Clin-REACT consistently outperformed its backbone models and open-source general-purpose and medical LLMs. Gains extended to different tasks including script concordance tests, and downstream diagnosis and treatment tasks. These findings suggest that expert reasoning supervision in critical care can improve broader clinical reasoning, although prospective evaluation is needed before real-world clinical use.

</details>


### [260] [GeoRisk-RAG: A Hierarchy-Aware Risk Framework for Improving RAG Reliability through Selective Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22634)
*Meenu Ravi,Shailik Sarkar,Lulwah AlKulaib,Yordanos Tessema,Chang-Tien Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的地理有效性增强RAG方法GeoRisk-RAG，大幅降低了地理相关问题LLM自信但错误答案的比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提升LLM答案可靠性的RAG等方法多注重语义相似性，难以区分答案的地理有效性，在自然灾害管理等需高地理分辨率的场景中，错误答案风险极高，因此亟需提升地理判别能力。

Method: 提出GeoRisk-RAG框架：先利用有向无环图（DAG）度量地理位置距离，显式估算检索内容的地理适用性，仅选择地理匹配的信息进行回答，从而支持按需选择性回答，降低错误率。

Result: 在一个全新、与野火相关的QA数据集上，GeoRisk-RAG对应位置相关问题的假自信率由传统方法的大约0.09降至0.009，并获得更高人类偏好评分。

Conclusion: GeoRisk-RAG通过将地理有效性与选择性回答机制引入RAG流程，提升了地理AI决策安全性和系统实用性，为地理相关领域的RAG方案提供了更全面的评估视角。

Abstract: Current work on improving reliability in large language model (LLM)- generated answers has primarily leveraged Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), knowledge-graph augmentation, and reinforcement learning. While these methods are adept at enhancing and measuring reliability through semantic similarity and faithfulness, they often struggle to distinguish semantic similarity from geographic validity. This is especially critical in natural hazard management domains where geographic granularity (i.e., town vs. city vs. state) is significant for decision-making, as responses valid in one municipality may not transfer to another. In such domains, a confidently wrong answer carries greater risk than abstaining. We present GeoRisk-RAG, a novel hierarchy-aware framework that addresses this geographic-validity gap through selective answering. This framework explicitly estimates geographic applicability using a Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG)-based distance for context retrieval before response generation. Experiments on a novel held-out wildfire-related question-answering (QA) dataset show that GeoRisk-RAG significantly reduces false confidence rates for location-dependent questions, lowering the rate to 0.009 compared with ~0.090 for standard semantic similarity and reranking baselines, while consistently achieving higher human preference alignment. This work provides a more comprehensive assessment of end-to-end RAG pipelines by integrating geographic validity and selective-answering behavior for safer decision-making in geospatial domains.

</details>


### [261] [Iteration Without Elaboration: A Simple ReAct Architecture Suffices for Text-to-SQL Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22651)
*Jian Lu,Haiwei Yu,Raymond M Xiong,Anru Zhang,Danyang Zhuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了ReAct-SQL，一种基于迭代式推理与受限动作空间的零样本text-to-SQL模型，省略了复杂的多阶段模块，具备高效与易用性，在权威数据集上达到了高准确率并大幅提升推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有text-to-SQL系统为提升性能而结构复杂，涉及多个辅助模块，导致执行延迟及工程负担增加。因此，作者希望设计一种更简单、高效的方法，减少依赖复杂组件同时维持或提升准确率。

Method: 提出ReAct-SQL框架，基于ReAct思路，通过迭代推理与受限动作空间（15种关系型操作的DSL），逐步产生SQL查询，每步根据SQL执行反馈修正推理过程，无需自由生成完整SQL语句。

Result: 在经过修正的BIRD mini-dev和EHR-SQL数据集上，ReAct-SQL分别达到84.5%和73.9%的准确率，与更繁杂的基线模型效果相当，且速度提升最高达8倍。消融实验表明迭代推理提升了问题理解，DSL限制提升了组合可靠性。

Conclusion: ReAct-SQL以更简洁的设计实现了高准确性和推理效率，展示出简化text-to-SQL系统架构的巨大潜力，为实际应用降低了部署和维护难度。

Abstract: Modern text-to-SQL systems have become increasingly elaborate, relying on schema-linking modules, retrieval-augmented prompting, candidate generation, and multi-stage refinement pipelines. While effective, these additions introduce substantial latency and engineering overhead. To this end, we present \textbf{ReAct-SQL}, a simple yet effective zero-shot ReAct-style framework built solely on iterative reasoning and a constrained action space defined by a typed Domain-Specific Language (DSL) of 15 relational operations, rather than free-form SQL generation. The model incrementally issues DSL calls, observes compiled-SQL execution feedback, and revises its reasoning through interaction. On corrected BIRD mini-dev and EHR-SQL, ReAct-SQL achieves \textbf{84.5\%} and \textbf{73.9\%} accuracy, respectively, matching substantially more elaborate baselines while running up to $8\times$ faster. Incremental ablations further show that iteration primarily improves grounding, while the DSL improves compositional reliability.

</details>


### [262] [Enrich-Retrieve-Rank: Scaling Capability Discovery Beyond In-Context Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22695)
*Nazib Sorathiya,Daniel Zhang,Bardiya Akhbari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的能力发现方法，通过先离线强化注册表元数据，再在线检索并排序能力，提升大规模Agent生态系统下能力发现的效率和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Agent生态系统规模的不断扩大（包含数千个模型、Agent、工具和技能等MATS组件），现有基于上下文路由(registry routing)的能力发现方法在规模扩展时准确率大幅下降，因此亟需一种更有效的能力发现机制来应对注册表快速增长带来的挑战。

Method: 方法分为两步：首先，对注册表中的稀疏元数据进行离线处理，生成可检索的能力简介；其次，通过检索-排序（retrieve-then-rank）流程在线返回排序好的能力候选列表，无需在线调用候选组件。该方法与全上下文输入（Full-Ctx）以及引导LLM先搜索后选择（Search&Pick）两种基线方法进行了对比。

Result: 实验表明，当能力数提高（N=10到7278）时，传统上下文路由Match@1准确率由0.85跌至0.12，而检索-排序方法仅从0.81跌至0.39；后者在检索到正确能力后，排序器还能在70%-87%场合将其排第一。能力数约500时，两方法表现持平。最终，检索-排序法比Search&Pick高出6.5个百分点Match@1，成本约为一半；比Full-Ctx低70倍成本。

Conclusion: 所提检索-排序框架在大规模多Agent平台上作为默认能力发现层，具备高扩展性和显著成本优势，对Agent生态系统能力发现具有重要实际价值。

Abstract: Agent ecosystems now include thousands of MATS components (Models, Agents, Tools, and Skills), yet their discovery still relies on in-context routing. These systems read a registry (names, hints, or descriptions, as context budget permits), pick a candidate, invoke it, and retry on failure. This pattern degrades with scale, and registries are growing fast. We recast capability discovery as search over a registry by defining an offline enrichment step that turns sparse metadata into searchable profiles, and an online retrieve-then-rank pipeline that returns a ranked shortlist without invoking any candidates online. We show that from N=10 to 7,278 capabilities, in-context routing's top-1 accuracy (Match@1) collapses (0.85 to 0.12), while retrieve-then-rank degrades more gently (0.81 to 0.39) because its reranker still ranks the right capability first 0.70-0.87 of the time once retrieval finds it. In the Nova Micro sweep, the crossover is around N=500. We compare against two in-context baselines. Full-Ctx puts the whole registry in the prompt and asks the LLM to pick. Search&Pick gives the LLM a search tool to narrow candidates before it picks. At full scale the pipeline leads Search&Pick by 6.5 percentage points (pp) on Match@1 at about half the cost. It reduces cost 70x versus Full-Ctx. We use a fixed configuration (same enrichment, retriever, and scorer weights) across agent, tool, and skill registries. The pipeline runs in production as the default capability-discovery layer of a large-scale multi-agent platform.

</details>


### [263] [WnW: Waxing-and-Waning KV Cache for Long-Form Speech LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22704)
*Yiming Yao,Chenyang Lyu,Xuanfan Ni,Longyue Wang,Weihua Luo,Yazheng Yang,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出WnW（Waxing-and-Waning KV cache）方法，通过区分不同KV-head角色，实现长音频输入下的KV缓存高效管理，仅用20%的GPU缓存实现接近完整效果，显著优于传统Prefill-only方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长音频输入使得KV cache成为语音大模型（speech LLMs）推理的主要内存瓶颈。而现有Prefill-only KV压缩方法在evict操作时丢弃部分KV，导致无法召回和恢复，尤其在长音频输入时表现较脆弱。

Method: 提出WnW方法，将KV-head分为锚点（anchor）、潮汐（tidal）和固定（fixed）三种角色。锚点head始终驻留GPU，作为解码时重要性判断；潮汐head异地CPU保存，并依anchor评判分段召回；固定head只保留重要子集，其余永久丢弃。角色分配为离线校准。

Result: 在LibriSpeech-Long数据集及不同3B规模模型（Voxtral-mini-3b、Qwen2.5-Omni-3B）上，WnW用20%的音频token在GPU上实现了接近Full-Cache精度，而Prefill-only方法在长音频条件下无法正常结束。此外，WnW对语言、任务、领域迁移具有泛化性，CPU-GPU的召回只带来极小延迟。

Conclusion: WnW方法在节省GPU内存的同时，兼顾精度和效率，为长音频语音大模型KV cache管理提供了解决方案，显著优于传统Prefill-only压缩方法。

Abstract: Long-form audio inputs make the KV cache the dominant memory cost of speech LLMs. Prefill-only KV compression methods permanently discard audio KV positions once evicted, with no pathway to recover them during decoding. We show this is fragile on long-form audio: prefill attention concentrates near the audio start (an attention-sink effect), while decode-time attention distributes broadly, and the two rankings overlap weakly. We propose WnW (Waxing-and-Waning KV cache), which classifies KV-heads into anchor, tidal, and fixed roles via offline calibration. Anchor heads remain on GPU and serve as a decode-time importance observer; tidal heads keep a CPU-resident complement that is recalled chunk-by-chunk based on aggregated anchor-head scores; fixed heads keep only an on-GPU subset, with the rest permanently discarded. On LibriSpeech-Long with two 3B backbones (Voxtral-mini-3b and Qwen2.5-Omni-3B), WnW preserves near-Full-Cache accuracy while keeping only 20% of audio tokens on GPU, where prefill-only baselines fail to terminate. Results generalize across language, task, and domain shifts, and CPU-GPU recall adds little decode-time overhead in our measurements.

</details>


### [264] [A Source-Grounded Framework for Constructing and Evaluating Progressive Multimodal Diagnostic Dialogues from Clinical Case Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22713)
*Yufan Wang,Rui Yang,Yi Liu,Yi Lin,Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于来源的多模态诊断对话生成与评估框架，旨在更真实地评估MLLMs在医学诊断中的推理和解释能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前多模态医疗基准多只评估固定输入或最终答案，缺乏对推理过程与多源信息交互的真实模拟，无法全面评价诊断Agent的能力。

Method: 作者提出从病例报告自动构建进阶式多模态诊断对话的框架，并设计评估方法，对MLLMs的最终诊断结果、推理质量以及医学图像解释能力进行综合评测。

Result: 该框架在24个内科病例报告上，能高质量转换病例为参考对话（诊断F1=0.99，推理评分4.79/5），但前沿MLLMs（o4-mini、Claude Haiku 4.5）在推理和诊断F1、医学图像解释上远低于参考（推理评分分别为2.75和2.50）。

Conclusion: 流畅的模型答复不代表具备实证支撑的临床推理能力。作者的框架有助于真实评估和提升MLLMs在多模态医学诊断推理上的能力。

Abstract: Clinical diagnosis requires progressive integration of patient history, physical examination, laboratory findings, medical images, and diagnostic-informative tests. However, most multimodal medical benchmarks evaluate fixed inputs or endpoint answers, while fully interactive diagnostic agents conflate evidence selection with evidence interpretation. We present a source-grounded framework to construct progressive multimodal diagnostic dialogues from case reports and an evaluation strategy for assessing MLLMs on final diagnosis, diagnostic reasoning, and image-finding interpretation. Evaluation on 24 internal medicine case reports showed that our framework can accurately convert case reports into reference dialogues, achieving a diagnosis F1 of 0.99 and a reasoning-quality score of 4.79 out of 5. Evaluation on two frontier MLLMs (o4-mini and Claude Haiku 4.5) achieved reasoning-quality scores of 2.75 and 2.50, respectively, with substantially lower diagnosis, reasoning, and image-finding F1 scores. The results demonstrate that fluent responses do not necessarily reflect evidence-grounded clinical reasoning and highlight the utility of the proposed framework for evaluating multimodal diagnostic reasoning.

</details>


### [265] [DiaRelay: Relaying Dialogue Context with a Constant-Size Memory for Emotion Recognition in Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22745)
*Zihao Zhou,Bin Yang,Jinghui Qin,Kebing Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种高效适配器DiaRelay，用于提升大模型在多轮对话情感识别任务中的记忆能力和表现，突破了历史方法窗口固定、内存消耗大的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话情感识别方法常用固定窗口引入历史上下文，窗口过小会丢失关键信息，窗口过大则重复编码、增加资源消耗，还可能引入无关上下文。主流高效适配方式（如LoRA）未有效建模对话级状态，也未能动态感知对话上下文。为此，作者希望设计一种兼具高效性、灵活性的对话情感识别结构。

Method: 作者提出DiaRelay适配器，基于LoRA扩展，包含两个关键模块：（1）选择性交替内存转移模块，将对话历史中的关键信息压缩进有界的中继内存，并随预测过程层层传递，无需重复全部历史编码；（2）双轴内存读取模块，利用历史信息动态调节低秩特征变换，实现上下文相关的表征自适应，无需测试时反向传播。

Result: DiaRelay在MELD数据集上获得了SOTA（加权F1和准确率），在IEMOCAP上也取得了极具竞争力的表现，并且只增加了约7.1M参数，展现了方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: DiaRelay能有效利用长距离历史对话信息，提升LLM在对话情感识别任务的效果，同时保持参数量小和高效推理，适用于实际对话系统中的情感理解场景。

Abstract: Emotion Recognition in Conversation (ERC) requires models to identify subtle emotional cues that are often distributed across distant dialogue turns. Existing methods typically incorporate dialogue history through a fixed context window. However, short windows discard potentially useful long-range evidence, while enlarging the window repeatedly re-encodes overlapping utterances, increases computational and memory costs, and may introduce irrelevant context. Moreover, commonly used parameter-efficient adaptation methods, such as LoRA, mainly introduce fixed low-rank transformations in the feature space and do not explicitly maintain a dialogue-level state or condition their transformations on the evolving conversational context. To address these limitations, we propose a lightweight adapter, DiaRelay, to enable LLMs to explicitly maintain a dialogue-level memory for accurate ERC. Based on LoRA, DiaRelay introduces two extra tightly collaborative components, Selective Relay Memory Transition and Dual-axis Relay Memory Read. Selective Relay Memory Transition progressively aggregates useful historical evidence into a bounded relay memory and propagates it across successive utterance predictions. This allows earlier emotional cues to influence later predictions after they leave the local context window, without re-encoding the complete dialogue history or expanding the backbone context length. Dual-axis Relay Memory Read uses the propagated memory to dynamically modulate low-rank feature transformations, enabling context-dependent representation adaptation without test-time gradient updates. Extensive experiments show that DiaRelay can achieve SOTA weighted F1 and accuracy on MELD while obtaining competitive results on IEMOCAP with only an extra 7.1M trainable parameters, indicating the effectiveness and generalizability of our DiaRelay in enhancing LLM-based emotional understanding.

</details>


### [266] [Beyond Factual Knowledge: Benchmarking and Learning Step-Level Procedural Rule Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22753)
*Bohan Yu,Pengfei Cao,Chen Han,Chenxi Zhou,Zhiheng Zhang,Zhiyang Xie,Wenhao Teng,Xiangwen Liao,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了RuleWorld数据集和DynaRule框架，用于评估和提升大模型理解与应用抽象规则的能力，在大规模规则环境下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在文本理解与生成上表现突出，但在大规模应用外部提供的抽象规则时表现有限。为评测和促进模型规则运用能力，亟需新的基准和方法。

Method: 1. 构建了RuleWorld基准，将规则以可复用的抽象单元形式组织，设计包括单规则、多规则和多跳推理等多种场景。2. 提出DynaRule框架，将规则注入KV cache，将检索转化为模型可学习的逐步内在过程，引入特殊<search> token，采用叠加的逐步注意力训练，实现推理过程中的动态规则重参与更新。

Result: 实验表明，现有大模型在大规模规则池下表现不佳，而DynaRule将平均问答准确率提高了多达19个百分点，在1万条规则时Recall@1超过85%，显著优于强基线模型。

Conclusion: DynaRule框架和RuleWorld基准可以有效提升大模型对复杂抽象规则的推理能力，尤其在大规模规则场景下具备显著优势。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel at text understanding and generation, yet still struggle to reliably understand and apply externally provided procedural rules at scale. To evaluate this capability, we introduce RuleWorld, a large-scale benchmark that reformulates rules as globally reusable abstract units rather than instance-specific facts. In RuleWorld, several scenarios, including single-rule, parallel multi-rule, and multi-hop reasoning, are settled for comprehensive evaluation. We further propose DynaRule, an end-to-end framework that injects the given rules into the KV cache and turns retrieval into an internal, learnable, step-wise process. Specifically, DynaRule employs Stacked Step-Level Attention Training with a special <search> token to enable dynamic rule re-attention and updating during inference. In this way, the model can re-attend to the most relevant rules at each step, dynamically replacing outdated ones to support more stable multi-step reasoning. Experiments on RuleWorld show that existing LLMs face challenges under large rule pools, while DynaRule improves average QA accuracy by up to 19 points and achieves over 85% Recall@1 at 10K rules, outperforming strong baselines by large margins. We make our code and dataset available here: https://github.com/SharkSpicy-NLP/Beyond-Factual-Knowledge.

</details>


### [267] [XTC: Head-Aware Sampling by Excluding Top Choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22758)
*Philipp Emanuel Weidmann,Allen Roush,Judah Goldfeder,Sanjay Basu,Ravid Shwartz-Ziv*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的自动回归语言模型解码策略——XTC（Exclude Top Choices），能够在提升生成内容多样性的同时有效减少重复，且不影响流畅性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多样性解码方法主要通过重缩放或截断低概率token，但对于多种可选延续存在、概率仍高度集中于通用token的情况并不理想。这导致生成内容缺乏创造力和多样性。

Method: XTC是一种轻量级、兼容主流解码器的新型head-aware解码操作。具体做法为：首先检测出概率超过绝对阈值τ的token；若超过两个token满足条件，则以概率ρ去除主要的高概率token，仅保留最弱的可行选择（最大限度提高多样性），然后将分布重新归一化。该方法可与温度、重复惩罚等传统手段叠加。

Result: 在Gemma和DeepSeek等多种主流模型上测试，XTC显著提高了生成内容的多样性（Distinct-2指标提升11-15%，叠加温度最高提升38%），重复三元组下降27-47%（最高71%）；AMT主观人评偏好62.3%选择XTC（p<10^-4），流畅性无明显损失；GPT-4o评判也验证了结果；严格精度（IFEval）基本不变，仅略低1.7%。XTC策略兼容不同量化级别、多种模型架构，涵盖多类任务prompt，在主流社区框架中已被集成。

Conclusion: XTC针对多种合理答案可能的场景，有效拓宽多样性与重复度的折中边界，兼具实用性、广泛适用性和易用性，是提升大模型生成表现的重要新技术。

Abstract: Standard decoding rules for autoregressive language models promote diversity by rescaling the full next-token distribution or truncating its low-probability tail. These strategies overlook a common regime of open-ended generation in which several continuations are plausible but too much probability mass remains concentrated on the most generic choice. We introduce XTC (Exclude Top Choices), a lightweight head-aware decoding operator that targets this regime directly. XTC identifies tokens whose probabilities exceed an absolute plausibility threshold $τ$: when at least two qualify, it removes the dominant eligible choices with probability $ρ$ and retains only the weakest plausible alternative before renormalization. Across 60 experiments on Gemma 3 27B Q4, Gemma 3 12B Q6, and DeepSeek R1 14B Q6, with scaling validation on Llama 3.3 70B Q4, XTC improves the diversity-repetition Pareto frontier. On creative generation, Distinct-2 increases by 11--15% and repeat trigrams decrease by 27--47% across the four models. Combined with temperature scaling, gains reach 38% in Distinct-2 and 71% in repeat-trigram reduction over baseline. A blinded Amazon Mechanical Turk study with 150 Master raters yields a 62.3% creativity preference for XTC ($p<10^{-4}$) without reduced fluency, while a GPT-4o control judge reproduces the Anthropic-judge direction on every measure. On IFEval with Llama 3.3 70B Q4, XTC preserves prompt-level strict accuracy within 1.7 percentage points of baseline while recovering most of the diversity gain; a temperature setting matched on Distinct-2 reduces IFEval by 8.8 points. The effect is additive with temperature and repetition penalties, robust across quantization levels and model families, and consistent across twelve prompt genres. XTC has been adopted by llama.cpp, ExLlamaV2, and text-generation-webui.

</details>


### [268] [Don't Repeat Yourself: Stopping Verbatim Loops at Sampling Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22761)
*Philipp Emanuel Weidmann,Allen Roush,Judah Goldfeder,Sanjay Basu,Ravid Shwartz-Ziv*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种防止大语言模型生成文本时出现死循环（重复段落）的方法DRY (Don't Repeat Yourself)，可有效降低循环率且不损失文本多样性和生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型生成开放式文本时容易出现段落循环，现有防御方法效果不理想且往往损害文本流畅度和格式。需要新的机制既能防止死循环，又不影响文本质量。

Method: 提出DRY，在生成时对可能导致上下文段落重复的token增加惩罚，仅在生成同样的后缀序列时进行，且对格式化token进行保护。对多种模型、不同参数规模和多套模板进行了评测。

Result: DRY将后缀扩展重复率降低了47%，提升了词汇多样性，并且在多个大模型（包括量化模型）上，不影响主流评测基准下的性能。安慰剂实验证实效果确实来源于方法机制。

Conclusion: DRY方法有效解决了大语言模型生成文本时死循环重复的问题，在实际推理框架中得到广泛应用，具有实用价值和推广意义。

Abstract: Large Language Models generate text autoregressively, but open-ended generation is prone to verbatim looping, in which models repeat spans already present in context. Standard defenses such as repetition, presence, and frequency penalties and n-gram blocking act on token recurrence rather than the sequential structure of a loop, and often suppress looping only at strengths that also degrade formatting or fluency. We propose Don't Repeat Yourself (DRY), a sampling-time logit adjustment that penalizes a candidate token only when generating it would extend the current suffix into an exact continuation of a span seen earlier in the context. Sequence breakers protect chat templates and formatting tokens. Across models from 1.5B to 120B parameters, nine prompt families, and a 600-pair human study, DRY reduces suffix-extension rate by 47% while improving lexical diversity. An intervention-matched placebo produces no comparable reduction, identifying suffix matching as the operative mechanism. On AWQ-quantized 70B and 120B models, DRY reduces loop rate by roughly half while preserving MT-Bench, MMLU, and GSM8K performance, whereas standard alternatives lose measurable ground. DRY has been adopted by popular open-source LLM inference frameworks including llama.cpp, ExLlamaV2, and text-generation-webui, highlighting its practical impact on text generation.

</details>


### [269] [DelistBench: Evaluating Search-Enabled LLMs for Auditable Corporate-Event Database Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22770)
*Xuan Yao,Li Shuping,Dai Yang,Zhou Yi,Ke-Wei Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用大语言模型（LLM）结合网络检索能力，从公开信息复原金融事件记录的新方法，并建立了相应的基准数据集与评测体系，有效提升了金融数据库的准确性和自动化程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融机构需要一种独立的手段，检测商业数据库中的事件记录是否缺失、过时或分类错误，以降低因数据不全或不准带来的风险成本。

Method: 作者提出了名为“Search-to-Record”的任务，使用支持搜索的大语言模型，根据机构定义的事件标准，从公开网络数据中自动构建与验证上市公司事件记录。并构建了1,200条退市公告记录的评测数据集DelistBench，对5种模型在不连网和连网两种设置下进行对比实验。

Result: 实验显示，联网能力使事件公告日期的七天内识别准确率提高了34.0到48.0个百分点，事件状态识别提升约2.8到21.7个百分点。最佳系统可实现81.5%的七天内联合准确率。经济型系统以极低成本达到接近最佳系统的75.9-78.3%准确率。风险分层策略能筛选低错误子集，但广覆盖时仍需人工复查约27.3%的样本。

Conclusion: Web检索是提升事件识别时效和准确性的关键。低成本模型接近昂贵系统效果。部署时应根据本地事件分布特性调整风险分层策略，优先保证有正事件的召回率，并将正事件和不确定案例送审，以实现高效、可靠的数据库自动监测。

Abstract: Financial institutions need an independent way to detect missing, stale, and misclassified corporate-event records in vendor databases. We introduce Search-to-Record, a database-assurance task in which search-enabled large language models reconstruct institution-defined event records from public sources for a known security universe and historical cutoff, and DelistBench, a 1,200-record benchmark for security-level delisting announcements. We evaluate five models in paired closed-book and web-enabled conditions. Web access raises announcement-date accuracy within seven days by 34.0 to 48.0 percentage points and event-status accuracy by approximately 2.8 to 21.7 points; the best system achieves 81.5% overall joint accuracy within seven days. Economy web systems achieve 75.9-78.3% overall joint accuracy within seven days at 4.5-6.6% of the API cost of the most expensive web system. Risk-based triage identifies low-error subsets, although the highest-coverage operating point still sends 27.3% of the balanced test set to review. The evaluation identifies web retrieval as the main source of timing gains and shows that low-cost systems can approach the best system's accuracy. Together, Search-to-Record, DelistBench, and the evaluation provide concrete deployment guidance: calibrate triage to local event prevalence and market mix, preserve positive-event recall, and route positive and ambiguous cases to targeted review.

</details>


### [270] [SPOC-SQL: Stage-wise Preference Optimization for Controllable Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22772)
*Yingnan Chen,Chun Ding,Tianshi Xu,Xu Yang,Si Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SPOC-SQL将Text-to-SQL任务分解为四个阶段，针对每一阶段进行优化，提升SQL生成的可控性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-SQL方法无法针对中间决策点进行反馈，也缺乏中间过程可控和交互能力，导致生成SQL的过程不够精细和可控。

Method: 提出SPOC-SQL方法，将Text-to-SQL任务按照SQL执行逻辑分解为四个顺序子任务，针对每个阶段设计特定的优化策略和细粒度偏好优化机制，实现结构化决策和阶段干预能力。

Result: 实验结果显示，融合分阶段的人类知识后，模型性能持续提升，验证了阶段感知和可控生成方法的有效性。

Conclusion: SPOC-SQL通过分阶段分解和优化，提升了Text-to-SQL任务中SQL生成的可控性与可靠性，具有应用推广价值。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL aims to translate natural language questions into executable SQL queries over relational databases, requiring multi-stage structured reasoning over database schemas and query constraints. However, existing methods treat this task as single-step generation, where models optimize entire SQL sequences without targeted feedback at key decision points and lack support for interacting with and controlling the intermediate generation process. To address this issue, we propose SPOC-SQL, which decomposes Text-to-SQL into four sequential subtasks following standard SQL execution logic and designs stage-specific optimization strategies for the model to learn key decisions. Specifically, we propose the implementation of fine-grained preference optimisation at key decision points across SQL stages, with the objective of enhancing structured decision-making during query construction. Furthermore, a structured decomposition strategy is designed, facilitating stage-wise intervention and correction through explicit intermediate representations. This results in more controllable and reliable SQL generation. Experiments demonstrate that incorporating stage-wise human knowledge consistently improves performance, validating the effectiveness of stage perception controllable generation.

</details>


### [271] [TRACE: A Self-Evolving Skill Bank for Consistent, Limit-Aware LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22793)
*Wenhao Wu,Menghao Zhang,Xin Wang,Zhi Wang,Kun Shao,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种名为TRACE（TRAjectory-Contrastive Evolution）的方法，显著提升了大语言模型（LLM）作为车载助手的一致性与可靠性，并通过CAR-bench基准测试取得领先成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型在实际用户场景中，往往存在行为不一致和能力界限不清的问题，影响模型在如车载助手等产品中的可靠部署。研究的动机是解决仅依靠单次任务成功率无法反映实际一致性的问题，推动LLM在关键领域的可靠性提升。

Method: CAR-bench基准模拟用户提出不完整或含糊请求，考查模型通过多轮对话和工具使用解决不确定性的能力。提出TRACE方法，通过自进化迭代不断对技能模块（Skill Bank）进行对比和优化，提升模型表现的一致性。具体做法是：每轮评价后，按调用技能分组，比较成功/失败样本轨迹，进而改进相应技能规则，最终实现技能库的不断优化。部署阶段，模型根据当前状态选取合适技能进行多轮对话决策。

Result: 在GPT-5.5和GPT-5.6-Sol模型上，TRACE显著提升了一致性指标（Pass^3）：GPT-5.5从59.9%提升到94.5%，性能鸿沟由34.6个百分点缩小到4.0个百分点。在官方隐藏集上，TRACE以70%的Pass^3成绩拿到第一名，相较基线提升40%。

Conclusion: TRACE方法能有效提升LLM在复杂、多轮场景下的一致性和可靠性，将高潜力模型转化为稳定、实用的能力表现，对实际应用，尤其是智能助手等领域，有极高的推广价值。

Abstract: Reliable deployment of LLM agents in user-facing products depends not on raw task-solving ability but on consistency and limit-awareness: behaving the same way across repeated trials, and recognizing when a request cannot, or cannot yet, be safely fulfilled. CAR-bench exposes this reliability gap in the domain of in-car assistants: an LLM-simulated user issues incomplete or ambiguous requests, requiring the agent to resolve uncertainty through multi-turn dialogue and tool use while strictly adhering to domain policies. Even frontier models show a substantial gap between what they can solve at least once (Pass@3) and what they solve consistently across trials (Pass^k). We bridge this gap with TRACE (TRAjectory-Contrastive Evolution), which iteratively improves a skill-based agent's behavioral knowledge without modifying model weights. This knowledge is organized as a Skill Bank of modular, retrievable skills, each encoding a self-contained set of tool-use rules and behavioral guidelines. TRACE evolves this bank through an agentic self-evolution loop: after each evaluation round, it groups trajectories by the skills invoked and refines each skill by contrasting successful and failed behaviors. The updated bank then guides subsequent rounds, while during deployment the Actor performs state-conditioned skill orchestration at every turn. On GPT-5.5, TRACE improves consistency (Pass^3) by 34.6 points, from 59.9% to 94.5%, while shrinking the gap between potential and reliable performance to just 4.0 points. On the official hidden set, TRACE achieved first place using GPT-5.6-Sol, attaining a Pass^3 score of 70%-a 40% relative improvement over the baseline. These results show that TRACE converts high model potential into stable, consistent performance gain. Project homepage: https://darwin-agent.github.io/Car-bench-TRACE.

</details>


### [272] [SDoH-Aware Narrative Anchoring Bias in Medical LLMs for Trustworthy Clinical Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22802)
*Ahnaf Atef Choudhury,Ramkrishna Saha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文关注医疗大模型在不同病人叙述下，答题答案是否一致，提出NarrativeShield SDoH MedQA数据集并评测Qwen2.5系列模型各类表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对医疗大模型的评估只关注平均正确率，忽视了相同案例在不同患者口吻下模型答案是否稳定，可能对临床决策支持的可靠性构成实际风险。

Method: 构建NarrativeShield SDoH MedQA数据集，将医疗问答用不同患者叙述（persona narratives）重写，确保实际答案不变，随后对Qwen2.5家族（1.5B、3B、7B参数）模型在该数据集下评估，统计多项指标（正确率、一致性、敏感性等）。

Result: Qwen2.5 7B模型表现最佳，准确率为56.33%，正确一致性40.33%；与3B模型相比在所有提示下有统计学意义的提升。即便如此，最低的叙述敏感性错误率也达到了31.67%。

Conclusion: 单一平均正确率不足以评价医疗大模型的临床可用性，建议同时考察其在不同叙述下的稳定性，以提升医学决策支持系统的可信度。

Abstract: Medical large language models are often judged by how many clinical questions they answer correctly. That view is useful, but it misses a practical risk. A model may know the right answer and still change its response when the same case is written in a different patient voice. This paper evaluates that risk as SDoH aware narrative anchoring bias. We use NarrativeShield SDoH MedQA, a counterfactual medical question answering dataset in which each case appears in persona based narratives while the answer key remains fixed. The dataset is reshaped from wide format into case grouped persona rows. We evaluate three open source instruction tuned LLMs from the Qwen2.5 family: 1.5B, 3B, and 7B. The final experiment uses 300 clinical cases and produces 8,100 model responses across three prompting conditions. We report persona level accuracy, counterfactual consistency, correct consistency, and narrative sensitivity error. Qwen2.5 7B achieves the best accuracy at 56.33 percent and the best correct consistency at 40.33 percent. Paired McNemar exact tests show significant accuracy gains for 7B over 3B in all prompt settings. Even so, narrative sensitivity remains, with the lowest error still at 31.67 percent. These results suggest that trustworthy clinical decision support should be evaluated by both average correctness and stability across medically equivalent patient narratives.

</details>


### [273] [DIAG: Diagnostic Iterative Alignment and Generation for Data-Efficient Mathematical Preference Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22806)
*Guhan Chen,Songtao Tian,Bohan Li,Hejin Wang,YeXin Xie,Zixiong Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DIAG的自适应迭代对齐与生成框架，有效提升大模型在数学推理任务中的训练效率和推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在数学推理任务中的对齐常因有效监督信号稀缺而效率低下，尤其随着模型能力提升，现有题库逐渐无法提供有区分度的训练样本，导致有用的偏好对对数减少，制约了模型的进一步提升。

Method: DIAG包含两个阶段：（1）诊断阶段，通过经验贝叶斯收缩估计器评估并分配练习主题配额，优先选择能产生更多有效偏好对的知识点；（2）生成阶段，根据学生模型的失败轨迹，教师模型自动合成针对性的变体题目，从而动态调整训练分布。理论部分表明，DIAG本质上实现了有教师介入的KL正则化练习分布重加权，最大化有效偏好对的产出。

Result: 实验结果显示，DIAG能够在同等训练资源下持续提升有效偏好对的产出，显著强化模型的数学推理表现。

Conclusion: DIAG框架能够高效地为数学推理任务筛选和生成更具信息量的监督信号，在提升大模型能力和训练效率方面表现突出。

Abstract: Iterative preference optimization is essential for aligning Large Language Models on mathematical reasoning tasks, yet its efficiency is often throttled by signal scarcity: as the model improves, static problem sets become increasingly mismatched to the model's evolving competence, producing rollouts that are either too easy or too hard and therefore non-informative, which leads to a scarcity of valid preference pairs. We propose DIAG, a Diagnostic Iterative Alignment and Generation framework that adaptively reshapes the practice distribution to increase informative supervision and focus training near the student's current competence boundary. DIAG consists of two phases: (1) diagnosing valid preference-pair yield to calibrate the exploration-exploitation trade-off and allocate topic quotas via an Empirical Bayes shrinkage estimator, thereby prioritizing high-yield concepts; and (2) generating targeted practice, where a teacher synthesizes variants from the student's failure traces. We further provide a theoretical view interpreting DIAG as a teacher-mediated approximation to KL-regularized reweighting of the practice distribution toward the student's competence boundary, where valid preference-pair yield is maximized. Experiments show that DIAG boosts yield across iterations and delivers stronger reasoning performance under an iso-effective training budget, demonstrating that it can distill more informative preference supervision for mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [274] [Industrial-Instruction: An End-to-End Framework for Building Instruction-Tuning and Benchmark Datasets from Industrial Technical Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22817)
*Parsa Bakhtiari,Hassan Bashiri,Alireza Khalilipour,Masoud Nasiripour,Moharram Challenger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Industrial-Instruction项目，首次构建和公开了基于真实工业技术报告的QA数据集和端到端生成管道，旨在提升对复杂工业文档的检索与问答能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业技术报告包含大量有价值的知识，但由于其格式复杂（如密集文本、规范、表格），难以用于现有的检索和问答系统，且迄今无相关公开基准数据集。为支持工业应用与大模型微调，亟需高质量的工业文档QA数据集和生成方法。

Method: 作者利用906份松下公开工业文档（共7,525页），采用版面感知的文本抽取、语义检索索引等手段，自动合成多选QA样本，并覆盖五种典型的查询-文档关系。数据经过筛选与过滤，最终建立了两组13,600对左右QA样本。分别利用开源与闭源大模型（Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct与Claude-Opus-4.6）进行生成，以验证不同模型的生成能力及应用价值。

Result: 微调小型开源大模型（10B以下）在Panasonic基准集上，Set-Match Accuracy由28.5%提升至42%，F1由46.6%提升至63.5%。Claude-Opus-4.6生成的数据集带来了更高的模型提升和更干净的语料，但成本显著更高。MMLU评测显示，Claude-Opus数据集训练的模型保持了通用知识，而Qwen生成数据有小幅遗忘效应。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了两个高质量的工业技术文档QA数据集和完整管道，为相关模型评测与微调提供可复现、扩展性强的方法工具，推动工业领域大模型应用与基准体系建设。

Abstract: Industrial technical reports contain high-value knowledge for maintenance, troubleshooting, and product engineering, but their heterogeneous structure (dense prose, specifications, tables) makes them difficult to index and reason over with standard retrieval and QA pipelines, and no public instruction-tuning or benchmark datasets are built from such documents. We address this gap with Industrial-Instruction, contributing (i) two open QA datasets built from real industrial technical reports and (ii) the end-to-end pipeline that produces them. Using 906 public Panasonic documents (7,525 pages), we apply layout-aware extraction, build a semantic retrieval index, and synthesize multiple-choice QA grounded in retrieved evidence under five query-document relationships (irrelevant retrieval, single-/multi-document support, single-/multi-document answer). After filtering an initial 23.9k generated samples, each dataset provides approximately 13.6k QA pairs with source documents and a held-out benchmark split. Fine-tuning small open LLMs (under 10B parameters) improves Set-Match Accuracy from 28.5% to 42.0% and F1 from 46.6% to 63.5% on the Panasonic benchmark. We release two parallel versions built by the same pipeline: one generated with the open-weight Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct model and one with the closed, API-based Claude-Opus-4.6 model, enabling a direct comparison of open- versus frontier-model data generation. The Claude-Opus-4.6 dataset yields a cleaner raw corpus and larger fine-tuning gains, at roughly two orders of magnitude higher cost. MMLU evaluation shows models trained on the Claude-Opus-4.6 data retain essentially all general knowledge, versus a small but measurable forgetting effect for the Qwen-generated data. Together, these datasets and pipeline offer a practical, reproducible path toward scalable industrial benchmarks and training data from real-world documentation.

</details>


### [275] [SAVER: Selective Auditing of Verbal Evidence for Error Recovery in VLM Change Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22857)
*Youdi Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SAVER的轻量级规则方法，通过审查VLM输出中的口头证据并在证据缺失时触发结构化重新提问，显著提高视觉变化推理任务的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型在视觉变化推理任务上经常出现失败，即使其视觉编码器已经包含了足够信息。论文注意到，正确的模型输出倾向于提供支持其结论的明确证据，而错误输出往往缺这些证据，因此需要改进模型推理与表达相关变化的能力。

Method: 提出SAVER，一种基于规则的解析方法，自动检测VLM回答中是否包含关键证据（如目标名称、颜色、空间位置等），若证据缺失或不一致，则自动重新提问。并结合大语言模型生成证据模板以提升有效性。

Result: SAVER在三个变化检测基准和四个VLM中取得了显著准确率提升，特别是在CLEVR-Change数据集上提升可达25.8%。实验证明，证据检测机制是提升性能的主要因素。

Conclusion: 通过选择性审查和补偿VLM输出所需的证据，可以系统性提升VLM在视觉变化推理任务上的表现。SAVER是一种高效且通用的方法。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) frequently fail at visual change reasoning, even when their vision encoders contain sufficient information. We observe that correct VLM outputs tend to contain explicit verbal evidence (object names, colors, spatial locations) that supports the claimed change, while incorrect outputs often lack such evidence. We propose SAVER (Selective Auditing of Verbal Evidence for Error Recovery), a lightweight, rule-based method that parses VLM responses for this evidence and triggers structured reprompting only when evidence is missing or inconsistent. Across three change detection benchmarks and four VLMs, SAVER significantly improves accuracy on tasks where errors stem from the model failing to articulate what it saw (expression failures), with gains up to +25.8% on CLEVR-Change. The evidence patterns can also be generated by an LLM in a single call, matching the hand-tuned gate on CLEVR-Change. Ablation experiments confirm that the evidence gate, not reprompting alone, drives the improvement.

</details>


### [276] [Better Retrieval, Worse Robustness:How Multi-hop RAG Amplifies Upstream ASR Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22872)
*Zhenghua Bao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在语音识别（ASR）错误影响下，增强型生成（RAG）的两种扩展方案（实体图链接和迭代重述）对多跳问答任务性能的影响。结果显示，这两种扩展方案不仅未缓解，反而进一步放大了ASR带来的误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语音应用普及，ASR错误不可避免，但其对下游复杂检索任务的具体影响机制尚不清楚。作者希望分析和量化ASR误差对多跳问答中增强检索结构的影响。

Method: 在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和MuSiQue三个多跳QA数据集上，针对四种RAG配置，在四种英语口音（由神经TTS合成）下测试系统。在对比“干净文本”情境的同时，着重分析加入实体图链接和迭代重述两种结构时的性能损失。

Result: 在使用ASR输入时，结构更复杂的系统F1分数虽高于基线，但ASR误差带来的性能损失却被进一步放大。例如，最差口音的F1下降幅度，复杂结构比普通RAG高出36%-67%。其中，87%-96%的性能损失源于查询实体被ASR损坏。两种轻量的表层修正手段未能有效抑制该放大效应。

Conclusion: 增强型检索生成结构（如实体图、重述等）无法缓解，并且进一步放大了ASR引发的关键实体错误。应更加关注实体级鲁棒性改进。源码和数据已开放。

Abstract: Speech-based applications pass spoken queries through automatic speech recognition (ASR) before any retrieval module, so ASR errors enter the pipeline as a fixed upstream constraint. We empirically test whether two extensions to standard retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), entity-graph linking and iterative reformulation, absorb or amplify these errors. Using four English accents synthesized through neural TTS, we evaluate four RAG configurations on three multi-hop QA benchmarks (HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA and MuSiQue) against a clean-text oracle. Although the structurally richer configurations generally retain higher absolute F1 under ASR input, both extensions amplify the error: the F1 gap from clean text to the highest-WER accent is 36-67% larger under their combination than under naive dense retrieval, on all three benchmarks. The dominant failure mode is corruption of one or more query entities, accounting for 87-96% of degradation cases on 2WikiMultiHopQA across all four methods. Two lightweight surface-form mitigations leave most of the gap intact, indicating that downstream retrieval structure amplifies remaining entity errors. We release code and data at https://github.com/ZhenghuaBao/spoken-multihop-rag .

</details>


### [277] [AraDetox: A Multi-Dialect Arabic Detoxification Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22894)
*Mo El-Haj*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AraDetox数据集，这是首个覆盖多种阿拉伯方言的有害语言去除（detoxification）数据集，结合GPT-5和Gemini 2.5 Flash自动生成和人工评估，为阿拉伯语NLP提供了重要资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管针对阿拉伯语有害文本检测的研究较多，实际对阿拉伯文本进行去毒（detoxification）的研究和公开资源却非常缺乏，尤其是在不同阿拉伯方言文本层面。为此搭建了AraDetox数据集，以支持相关研究和模型开发。

Method: 作者收集了1.05万条阿拉伯有害社交媒体文本，涵盖现代标准阿拉伯语和3种主要方言。使用GPT-5和Gemini 2.5 Flash生成8.4万条去毒文本重写，并通过人工和自动化方法，从词汇变化、语义保持、情感和方言特征等角度评价文本质量和一致性。

Result: 结果显示，去毒本质上是保持语义的重写任务——大量词汇与结构变化，但语义高度相似。人工评测证实有害内容被有效去除且原意基本保留。自动化分析指出，生成文本与不同方言的语料风格高度吻合。还发现两种可行的去毒策略：最小编辑的词汇替换和较大范围意义保持的重写。

Conclusion: AraDetox证明了通过LLM生成并人工审核，可高质量构建阿拉伯语大规模多方言去毒数据集，为安全文本生成和多方言阿拉伯NLP研究提供了关键资源。数据集已公开供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Arabic harmful-language detection has received considerable attention, yet Arabic text detoxification remains underexplored. We introduce AraDetox, a multi-dialect Arabic detoxification dataset comprising 10,500 harmful social-media posts and 84,000 detoxified rewrites generated using GPT-5 and Gemini 2.5 Flash across Modern Standard Arabic, Gulf, Levantine, and Egyptian Arabic. The generated outputs were assessed through human evaluation and automatic analyses of lexical change, semantic preservation, sentiment, and dialectal style. Results show that detoxification is primarily a meaning-preserving rewriting task: substantial lexical and structural reformulation is accompanied by consistently high semantic similarity. Human evaluation confirms successful harmful-language removal while largely preserving the original meaning. Dialectal analyses further indicate that the generated variants exhibit measurable stylistic alignment with reference Arabic dialect corpora. Comparison with existing resources highlights two complementary approaches to detoxification: minimal-edit lexical substitution and meaning-preserving reformulation. Our findings demonstrate that large-scale Arabic detoxification resources can be constructed through LLM-assisted generation and human verification. The dataset is publicly available at https://github.com/ArabicNLP-UK/AraDetox to support future research on Arabic detoxification, safe text generation, and multi-dialect Arabic NLP.

</details>


### [278] [SelFusion: Self-distillation for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22898)
*Hyeongsoo Lim,Jinyoung Kim,Eunseo Seo,Minho Jang,Jiwon Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对扩散语言模型（DLMs）的新型自蒸馏框架SelFusion，显著提升了DLM的生成质量，优于常规知识蒸馏方法，且不依赖外部教师模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型虽然能缓解自回归大语言模型的生成延迟问题，但生成质量较差，限制了其实际应用价值。现有的知识蒸馏方法不能有效提升质量，有时甚至适得其反。因此亟需改进DLM训练方式以提高生成表现。

Method: 提出SelFusion自蒸馏方法，通过对同一数据分别以高和低掩码概率（分别对应困难和简单模式）前向推理，利用两者之间的生成结果差异进行双向知识蒸馏。蒸馏方向由每个token的正确性动态确定，从而避免模式间盲目迁移错误知识。整个过程无需外部教师模型。

Result: SelFusion方法在指令跟随类任务上明显优于传统外部教师指导的KD方法。多个配置下，经过该方法训练后，学生模型甚至超过原始大语言模型教师的性能。

Conclusion: SelFusion为提升扩散语言模型生成质量提供了实用路径，在不依赖外部教师的前提下能有效提升模型性能，为DLM的实际应用和研究打开更大可能。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) alleviate the inherent latency bottleneck of autoregressive (AR) large language models (LLMs), but their degraded generation quality limits practical applicability. Although knowledge distillation (KD) can be a promising direction for improving performance, we empirically find that naively applying conventional KD yields only marginal gains, or even degrades generation quality. Based on these observations, we propose a novel self-distillation framework for DLMs, namely SelFusion. To enable effective KD without an external teacher model, SelFusion performs two forward passes with different masking levels, defining the hard mode with a larger masking probability and the easy mode with a smaller masking probability. However, the easy mode is not always more accurate than the hard mode and can be overconfident on incorrect tokens. Thus, we introduce bidirectional KD between the two modes, which can dynamically determine the distillation direction based on token-level correctness. Experimental results on instruction-following tasks show that the proposed self-distillation substantially outperforms other KD methods with external LLM and DLM teachers. In many configurations, the student trained with SelFusion even surpasses the performance of the LLM teacher, providing a practical path toward improving DLM generation quality. Source code can be found at https://github.com/scai-research/SelFusion_official

</details>


### [279] [Do Spoken Language Models Hear Speech as They Read Text? Bridging Structural Gaps Between Speech and Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22908)
*Hyeonyu Kim,Hwayeon Kim,Youngwon Choi,Myeongkyun Cho,Huu-Kim Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新的框架提升口语语言模型（SLM）对语音和文本表示的对齐程度，从而改进多任务泛化和指令跟随能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管SLM能够直接从语音生成文本响应，对比传统级联系统有优势，但其在复杂任务上的泛化能力及指令理解仍弱于文本语言模型。研究发现，当前SLM的语音与文本表示对齐程度较弱，主因是语音的连续时变与文本的离散表示差异带来的结构性障碍。

Method: 作者提出了一个新颖的解耦框架，将语音-文本间的长度不匹配与语义对齐分离处理，并通过专门设计的训练目标鼓励它们在表示空间中的更紧密对应。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，新框架的表现达到及超越主流强基线，验证了解决语音与文本结构性差异对SLM训练价值的重要性。

Conclusion: 显式建模并弥合语音与文本表示结构差异，对于提升SLM的泛化和指令理解能力至关重要，该方法为今后SLM发展提供了新方向。

Abstract: Spoken Language Models (SLMs) generate textual responses directly from speech, offering an alternative to cascaded systems. Despite recent advances, existing SLMs still exhibit weaker instruction-following behavior and limited generalization across diverse tasks compared to text-based language models. Our analysis shows that speech and text representations in current SLMs remain weakly aligned despite strong downstream performance, indicating that structural differences between continuous, temporally varying speech and discrete text remain insufficiently addressed. To address this, we propose a simple framework that decouples length mismatch from semantic alignment and encourages closer correspondence between speech and text representations. Experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate competitive performance against strong baselines, underscoring the importance of explicitly addressing structural differences between speech and text in SLM training. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/jaykim9870/Do_SLMs_Hear_Speech_as_They_Read_Text.

</details>


### [280] [Exploring Dowker Homology for Sentence Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22909)
*Marius Huber,Juri Opitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用Dowker同调作为一种新的分析句子相似性的方法，将句子中的token嵌入视为点云，发现该方法确实能够捕获句子相似性信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的句子相似性测量方法多依赖池化等传统手段，本文旨在探索拓扑同调工具（Dowker homology）是否能提供新的视角和信息。

Method: 将句子中的token嵌入作为两个点云嵌入在Transformer模型的潜空间，然后用Dowker同调分析它们的相对位置，通过回归Dowker同调特征与真实相似性分数来评估效果。对比了未微调和已微调模型结果，并尝试从Dowker同调中导出可以直接作为句子相似性指标的单一数字特征。

Result: Dowker同调特征与句子相似性之间有较好的相关性，可用于数据和模型的可视化。导出的单一特征虽然效果不错，但不优于现有的基于池化的相似性指标。

Conclusion: Dowker同调可以有效捕获句子相似性，为相似性分析和可视化提供了新工具，然而在实际应用中，其性能尚未超越现有主流方法。

Abstract: Dowker homology is a topological tool that may be used to analyze the relative position of two point clouds living in a common space. We investigate whether Dowker homology captures sentence similarity information by treating the embeddings of the tokens that constitute a sentence pair as a pair of point clouds in the latent space of a transformer model, using both models that have and have not been fine-tuned for sentence similarity. We find that Dowker homology captures sentence similarity information, as measured by regressing Dowker homology features onto ground-truth similarity scores, and that it can be used for visual inspection of similarity data and models. In an attempt to make Dowker homology readily applicable, we derive from it single-number summaries that we expect to capture sentence similarity directly. These turn out to work reasonably well, but without outperforming standard sentence similarity measures based on established pooling methods.

</details>


### [281] [Knowing Isn't Always Saying: When Do Spatial Encodings Reach Answers in Vision-Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22916)
*Zeyu Wang,Xinming Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出使用定向插补（direction patching）的方法，研究视觉-语言模型（VLM）中空间信息如何在不同层级、位置和提示格式下影响最终答案，揭示了编码与推理之间存在条件性的传递机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然VLM能够编码空间信息，但在推理和作答时往往没有有效利用这些信息，目前尚不清楚信息在什么时候、哪些层级传递到答案，因此需要新的分析方法揭示其内在机理。

Method: 提出定向插补（direction patching）技术，即在不同的网络层、token位置和提示格式下，利用空间ID方向进行类条件因果干预，以分析空间信息在模型内部的传递路径和影响机制。

Result: 发现空间信息对答案的因果影响主要出现在网络的中至深层，文本推理链通常抑制信息的直接传递，而基于视觉提示的输入则保持其活跃；信息传输可能在部分token或答案步骤再次增强。分析了10个主要VLMs，并结合不同数据集、属性和编码强度，总结出了描述性的信息传递模式。

Conclusion: 这些发现将VLM中“编码-落地差距”问题重新表述为条件性信息传递问题，有助于进一步理解和提升VLM对空间信息的有效利用。

Abstract: Vision-language models are known to encode spatial information in their hidden states, yet often fail to use it when answering. However, it remains unclear when and where this encoded information reaches the answer. We address this with direction patching, a class-conditioned causal intervention applied across layers, token positions, and prompt formats. Using spatial-ID directions constructed following prior encoding evidence, we find that causal influence on answer logits emerges only at mid-to-deep depths. Text chain-of-thought suppresses immediate object-word argmax-level transport in most models, while visually grounded prompts keep it open. Positive target-logit gain can remain below the argmax threshold, and transport can re-emerge at the final prefix token or at the answer step in deeper layers. Across the ten VLMs we study, these local effects form descriptive transport patterns. Complementary experiments characterize how these patterns shift across datasets, attributes, and encoding amplitudes. Together, these results reframe the encoding-grounding gap as a problem of conditional transport in VLMs.

</details>


### [282] [TSWAP: A Multilingual Retrieval-Augmented Thai Wellness Advisor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22917)
*Pornthep Ukosaramig,Kobkrit Viriyayudhakorn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了TSWAP，这是一个支持八种语言的对话健康顾问系统，依托泰国传统医学认证知识库，通过检索增强生成技术实现问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏可靠的、可扩展的多语言传统健康问答系统，尤其是在泰国语境下，且传统医学信息尚未被系统整合用于现代人工智能应用。

Method: 使用开源大语言模型Qwen3.6-35B-A3B，结合稠密-稀疏混合检索与交叉编码器重排序，对泰传统医学知识库进行问答。采用规则安全层控制医疗适用范围与应急分流，通过‘先翻译后检索’支持零样本多语言服务。

Result: 系统在泰国传统医学检索基准上Recall@5达到0.88，实测质量问答通过率为91.1%。安全提示和强制检索显著提升系统可靠性和合规性。

Conclusion: TSWAP在多语言、传统医学领域具备良好性能。实际部署发现4-bit量化会损坏泰语音调标记，强制检索对系统可靠落地尤为关键。

Abstract: We present TSWAP, a deployed eight-language conversational wellness advisor grounded, via retrieval-augmented generation, in a verified knowledge base of Thai traditional medicine and certified wellness providers. An unmodified open-weight LLM (Qwen3.6-35B-A3B on vLLM) is grounded on a ~30.6K-chunk Thai index by a hybrid dense-sparse retriever with cross-encoder reranking; a first-turn query classifier forces tool-based retrieval for entity lookups; a rule-based safety layer enforces medical scope and Thai emergency routing; and all eight languages are served zero-shot with translate-then-retrieve. We release the first Thai traditional-medicine/wellness retrieval benchmark (50 questions with gold document IDs; Recall@5 = 0.88), production QA logs (91.1% test-retest pass over 259 cases), and a 71-question frontier no-retrieval probe showing what each grounding pillar contributes: without the safety prompt the backend model family produced a full drug-dosing schedule and complied with out-of-scope requests, and without the knowledge base it produced zero verifiable provider recommendations. We further report two transferable deployment findings: English-calibrated 4-bit AWQ quantization corrupts Thai tone marks, and forced-retrieval routing is necessary for reliable grounding.

</details>


### [283] [HelaBERT: Enhancing Sinhala Language Understanding with Dual Pooling Classification Head](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22922)
*Thisen Ekanayake,Nisansa de Silva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了HelaBERT，一组专门针对僧伽罗语（Sinhala）预训练的BERT模型，涵盖不同参数规模。在多个下游任务中，模型取得了良好的性能，并公开发布以促进僧伽罗语NLP发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前僧伽罗语自然语言处理资源匮乏，缺乏适用于复杂形态学及字符体系的高质量预训练语言模型。因此，针对僧伽罗语开发语言模型能够提升该语言相关NLP任务的研究与应用。

Method: 从零开始在约10亿个僧伽罗语标注的语料上预训练了两种规模（小型23.3M参数、6层；大型110M参数、12层）的BERT模型。采用SentencePiece Unigram分词器，词表为32000。模型在四类文本分类任务上进行评测，并尝试引入双池化分类头与传统的[CLS]-线性头进行对比。每个任务都做了多次独立分种评估，并采用分层8:2的训练/测试划分。

Result: 在情感分析任务中，双池化分类头带来了持续的性能提升，对新闻类别分类也有一定增益，在新闻来源分类（句首级短文本）上，常规[CLS]-线性头依然表现优异。

Conclusion: HelaBERT系列模型有效提升了僧伽罗语NLP任务性能，并作为公开资源有助于推动相关研究进展。

Abstract: We present HelaBERT, a family of two BERT-based masked language models pre-trained from scratch on approximately 1 billion tokens of Sinhala text sourced from MADLAD-400, CulturaX, and a custom corpus comprising news articles, Sinhala Wikipedia, and web crawl data. HelaBERT-Small (~23.3M parameters, 6 layers) and HelaBERT-Large (~110M parameters, 12 layers) both use a SentencePiece Unigram tokenizer (vocabulary size 32,000) tailored to Sinhala's agglutinative morphology and complex script. We evaluate both models on four downstream Sinhala text classification tasks: news category classification, news source classification, sentiment analysis, and writing style classification, using 5 independent seed runs with stratified 80/20 train/test splits. We additionally propose a dual pooling classification head and evaluate it systematically across all four tasks, finding consistent improvements on sentiment analysis and a moderate gain on news category classification for HelaBERT-Small, while the standard [CLS]-linear head remains competitive on news source classification, a headline-level task with short average input length. We release both models to support further research in Sinhala NLP.

</details>


### [284] [What Proves You Wrong: Benchmarking Language Models on Falsifiable Research Ideation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22948)
*Ziyue Wang,Aomufei Yuan,Yiran Yao,Linli Yao,Hongyao Zuo,Ziwen Gong,Yuanxin Liu,Shicheng Li,Yishuo Cai,Tong Yang,Xu Sun,Xiaohui Li,Haoli Bai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Lit2Test基准，用于客观评价大语言模型提出的研究想法质量，强调可证伪性，并开源相关基准和构建流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前用于评判大语言模型生成的研究创意的方法存在主观性，无一致评价标准，因此需要更客观、更有可操作性的评判体系。

Method: 作者设计了Lit2Test基准，将每个提案绑定到一个六字段契约，并明确指出使其被证伪的观察结果。选取200个真实论文领域，邀请四个前沿大模型生成提案，通过1200次盲评两两比较，辅以自诊断控制和多名人工标注员校验，明确了评判可靠性边界。

Result: 实验严格区分了四个模型的表现，并通过1万次bootstrap重采样验证了严格排名；区分度主要来源于测试和评价指标的质量，而非表达流畅性。

Conclusion: Lit2Test为评估大语言模型提出研究想法的质量提供了可靠、可复用的标准和工具，有助于推进该领域客观、公正的评价。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to propose research ideas, yet the prevailing ways of judging such ideas supply no shared decision rule: free-form judging sways with style and position, and scoring against a later paper rewards recovery of one realized trajectory. We introduce a benchmark that carries a proposal from Literature to Test: the Lit2Test benchmark centers on a six-field contract organized around a falsifying outcome, so that every proposal precommits the observation that would prove it wrong, making its quality decidable in the first place rather than merely arguable. Built prospectively from 200 real-paper neighborhoods, Lit2Test elicits proposals from four frontier models and compares them through 1,200 pairwise comparisons judged blind in both presentation orders. The protocol audits its own reliability through diagnostic controls and bounded human calibration, with three annotators corroborating the conclusions within explicitly stated reliability bounds. Lit2Test recovers a strict ranking of the four models in all 10,000 bootstrap replicates, and the separation comes from the quality of the proposed tests and metrics rather than from surface fluency. We release the benchmark, construction pipeline, and audit artifacts for public use.

</details>


### [285] [The Illusion of Control: Why Bare Classifier Inversion Silently Fails in Concept-Bottleneck Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22956)
*Qi Bing,Xiaowei Shao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于概念瓶颈的可控文本生成任务下，推理阶段如何从目标属性组合合成低维概念编码，并对三种编码获得方式进行了实验对比。结果发现简单的基于编码均值的后验先验法优于常用的分类器反演方法及其正则化变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多属性可控文本生成任务常借助概念瓶颈模型，其编码在推理阶段必须通过目标属性构成合成，如何实现有效合成并保证生成结果，亟需系统性研究。

Method: 对如何获得推理阶段概念编码进行了三种方法的比较：1）分类器反演法(以及无监督和标签约束的Mahalanobis正则、条件流密度正则多种变体)；2）参考文本编码；3）基于属性组合均值的后验先验法。跨三类主流大模型（124M-8B），系统对比了它们的语义表达能力和生成效果。

Result: 所有分类器反演法及其正则化变体的表现均不及基于编码均值的后验先验法。无正则反演法出现了退化至随机的表现，归因于编码落在训练分布之外。该结论在真实基准数据和外部评测下得到了验证。

Conclusion: 属性组合均值型后验先验法在多属性可控生成场景下效果最好；现有的分类器反演方法存在显著局限，不建议直接使用。研究为相关任务提供了新的思路和可靠的比较基线。

Abstract: Concept-bottleneck controllable generation routes multi-attribute control through a low-dimensional concept code that, at deployment, must be synthesised from a target attribute configuration. We study this problem in concept-bottleneck text generation under multi-axis compositional generalisation, comparing three ways to obtain the inference-time code: classifier inversion against the encoder heads, reference-text encoding, and a post-hoc label-conditioned prior. Since a concept code admits no direct LM-fluency term, regularising inversion must instead constrain the code toward the encoder's training distribution. We therefore test bare inversion and three regularised variants: label-agnostic and label-conditioned Mahalanobis penalties, and a conditional normalising-flow density baseline. Every inversion variant we test underperforms a simple post-hoc prior fitted to per-combination encoder means on the same checkpoints, across three backbone families spanning $124$M to $8$B parameters. The bare form of classifier inversion also silently collapses to chance, traceable to a directly measured off-manifold code. We validate this diagnosis on real-world benchmarks and under external evaluators, enabling fair comparison with published baselines.

</details>


### [286] [Closed-Loop Bayesian Molecular Inverse Design with Semantic LLM Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22967)
*Yaoyao Xu,Xinjian Zhao,Xiaozhuang Song,Lei Bai,Tianshu Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于分子逆向设计的闭环优化框架，将代理模型（而非生成器）作为优化决策的核心，并采用大型语言模型直接处理文本序列，实现了在实际药物和材料设计任务中比单次生成和传统高斯过程BO更优或更具竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子逆向设计流程多为闭环优化，实际操作中不能一次生成理想分子，需要多轮生成—评估—筛选以逐步富集候选池。标准的贝叶斯优化（BO）方法常用高斯过程代理模型，这些模型基于压缩的连续空间嵌入，忽略了化学家关注的分子子结构及与参考分子的相似性信息。为提升分子设计的实际可用性和决策透明度，需要更好地融合结构和历史信息。

Method: 作者提出了一种新方法，将代理模型作为优化的控制点，具体地，使用冻结的大型语言模型处理任务说明、SMILES优化历史及反馈，直接在文本层面进行推理。每轮迭代，代理模型输出结构化决策信号，依据探索与利用原则选择参考分子，且可生成精炼的指导语句。这一信号被转化为分子生成器的下一轮文本条件，整个流程具备可回溯的自然语言优化轨迹。

Result: 在药物分子和材料分子的MOlQA任务实验中，新方法较单次提示生成有明显提升，与或优于传统高斯过程贝叶斯优化基线。不同任务下，二分类药物目标更适合仅用参考分子转移，连续型材料设计问题则从额外简明摘要中获益更多。

Conclusion: 新提出的方法不仅优化了分子设计流程，提升了任务表现，还展现出良好的可解释性和灵活接口，适应了不同类型的分子设计任务，为化学分子生成和筛选提供了更实用的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Practical molecular inverse design is rarely a one-shot generation problem; it often takes the form of closed-loop candidate-pool enrichment, where under a limited oracle budget the goal is to \emph{increase the fraction of generated molecules that match a desired property profile}. Bayesian optimization (BO) offers a natural framework for this setting, yet standard Gaussian-process surrogates typically operate in compressed continuous embeddings, which discard the substructural and reference-similarity signals that chemists naturally use to decide where to look next. We propose \textbf{\method}, a closed-loop framework in which the surrogate, rather than the generator, is treated as the locus of design choice, and instantiate it with a frozen large language model that reasons directly over the task instruction, SMILES-level optimization history, and oracle feedback in their native textual form. At each iteration, the surrogate returns a structured decision signal that selects informative reference molecules under an exploration and exploitation principle, optionally with a concise guidance sentence. This signal is converted into next-round conditioning text for a frozen molecular generator, yielding an inspectable optimization trace in natural language. Experiments on MolQA drug and material design tasks show that \method improves over one-shot prompting, is competitive with or stronger than GP-based BO baselines, and reveals a domain-dependent interface: reference-only transfer works best for binary drug targets, while adding a concise surrogate summary is more beneficial for continuous material

</details>


### [287] [What Does Activation Steering Control? Attribution Across Answer Encodings and Output-Sensitive Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22985)
*Zhiwei Gao,Shaowen Peng,Shoko Wakamiya,Eiji Aramaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Cross-Encoding Steering Evaluation方法，用于分析激活操控（activation steering）在不同答案编码下的表现，并指出原先的提升往往反映的是与答案标识的兼容性而非模型实际理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往在评估LLM激活操控效果时，大多是在构造方向时用到的答案编码下进行，难以判断提升源自模型对语义的理解还是对答案格式的记忆。本文旨在区分这两类效果，提升对激活操控机制的理解。

Method: 提出Cross-Encoding Steering Evaluation：在保持干预不变的情况下，重新编码同一批样本的答案标识，并观察模型的输出变化。采用NormBank数据集，对答案选项（如A/B/C、X/Y/Z或1/2/3）重新赋值，比较激活操控对不同编码的影响，并在不同层、不同方法下进行实验。

Result: 实验发现，激活操控的效果更多跟‘抽取索引’（extraction index）相关，而不是和语义标签相关，且这种现象在网络后层更显著。此外，低秩、对输出敏感的分量虽只占方向范数的15.4%，却保留了96.3%的效果。不同任务上，MNLI倾向于索引跟随，而SC101更偏向于语义标签跟随。选择不同评测方法会导致不同的结论。

Conclusion: 不能只凭一个答案编码下的激活操控提升判断干预的具体控制对象。建议在不同答案编码下进行系统评估，以分辨模型对形式和语义的区别理解。

Abstract: Activation steering is often evaluated under the answer encoding used to construct the direction. A reported gain may reflect the intended judgment or compatibility with answer identifiers seen during construction. We introduce Cross-Encoding Steering Evaluation, which freezes an intervention while re-encoding answers to the same held-out items. On NormBank, after A/B/C identifiers are reassigned, contrastive activation addition (CAA) induces larger target-versus-source score changes for the extraction indices than for the semantic labels under the new mapping. We call this extraction-index following. Varying identifier vocabulary (A/B/C, X/Y/Z, or 1/2/3) and row order shows that the effect tracks extraction index rather than row position. After matching direction norms across layers, extraction-index following emerges mainly at later depths. A low-rank output-sensitive component containing 15.4% of the direction's squared norm retains 96.3% of this effect. An Inference-Time Intervention (ITI)-style method also favors extraction-index over semantic-label following on NormBank in three models. In aggregate, MNLI favors extraction-index following, whereas Social Chemistry 101 (SC101) favors semantic-label following. Multiple-choice and open-ended evaluations can yield different behavioral conclusions. Thus, a steering gain under one answer encoding does not by itself identify what the intervention controls.

</details>


### [288] [LLM Pedagogical Behavior in AI Tutoring Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22993)
*Suhyeon Lee,Juneha Baek,Jaehyeong Park,Donghyuk Shin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出并验证了一种用于评估大语言模型（LLM）作为辅导员时提供帮助程度的分级量表，分析了LLM在大学课程真实教学场景中的表现。研究发现LLM主要提供较高水平的直接帮助，并分析了此种帮助与学生行为及成绩的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被越来越多地用作学习和解题辅导工具，目前尚不清楚它们实际提供了多大程度的帮助。这对于设计有效的智能辅导系统具有重要意义。因此作者希望系统性地量化和评估LLM的辅导行为和帮助程度。

Method: 作者提出并验证了一个五级脚手架支撑（scaffolding level）量表，用以描述LLM对学生问题的响应直接性。对203名大学生、共14637条LLM响应进行了等级划分，并结合对话行为和后续考试成绩进行了统计关联分析。

Result: 绝大多数（>95%）LLM回复为高直接性帮助（解释或直接解答）。脚手架级别与学生后续对话行为有关，但对学生后续三次考试成绩预测力有限。

Conclusion: 本研究为LLM在真实教学辅导中的帮助程度提供了实证基线，并提出了评估LLM教学响应的新工具，有助于未来比较不同智能辅导系统的设计及效果。

Abstract: Students increasingly use LLMs as tutors for coursework and problem solving. Little is known about the level of assistance LLMs provide when students use them as tutors in authentic learning interactions. This matters because tutoring responses can differ substantially in how directly they help students complete a task. We operationalize this dimension as scaffolding level and develop a five-level scale, validated against human annotations, that characterizes responses according to the degree of direct assistance they provide. We apply the scale to 14,637 LLM responses from 203 students in a university AI course. Responses are overwhelmingly concentrated at high levels of assistance, with more than 95% classified as either Explaining or Solving. Scaffolding level is systematically associated with students' subsequent conversational behavior, but provides little additional predictive information about performance on three subsequent exams beyond prior achievement and dialogue behavior. These findings provide an empirical baseline for LLM assistance in tutoring interactions and a measurement framework for evaluating how alternative tutoring designs change that assistance.

</details>


### [289] [Unlearning Is Not Just Erasing: Temporal Decoupling via Generation Inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23020)
*Xunlei Chen,Qirui Ye,Yuang Li,Yi Gong,Zhaokun Wang,Wenyi Li,Shiyao Guo,Jinyu Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种细粒度的语言模型遗忘方法ADU，实现了高效遗忘敏感信息的同时，在TOFU和WMDP基准上取得最佳总成绩，并最大限度减少了对模型正常能力的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型要满足隐私与安全需求，需在不显著损害模型主功能的前提下，有效遗忘敏感信息。然而，现有方法容易影响语言结构或误伤无害知识，亟需更精细且副作用小的新机制。

Method: 提出ADU框架，通过训练attention-projection adapters，识别并切断特定的注意力路径，实现上下文相关的‘遗忘’。该方法保留局部注意力结构，确保模型主体知识不受影响，同时通过激活交换测试遗忘效果。

Result: 在TOFU和WMDP两大基准测试集上，ADU在遗忘质量与模型主效能上均超越对比方法。TOFU上Forget Quality达0.93，模型效用保留率在87-98%之间，平均（92.9%）远高于对比基准（81.9%），且误伤显著减少。

Conclusion: ADU方法能够精确、高效地遗忘目标信息，极大减小对语言模型其它功能的影响，为符合法规合规性与实际应用安全性需求提供了有效技术路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) require effective unlearning to address privacy regulations and safety concerns. However, achieving precise forgetting without compromising general utility remains challenging. Existing sequence- and token-level methods penalize target outputs without modeling their context-dependent retrieval paths, which can disrupt linguistic structure or suppress benign knowledge. We present ADU, a fine-grained, training-based framework that shifts unlearning from token erasure to contextual attention-pathway decoupling. Exploiting the functional distinction between local and global attention heads, ADU identifies preplan positions that retrieve persistent sensitive anchors and fixes their candidate paths under the original model. It then trains attention-projection adapters to suppress attention mass along these paths while preserving local-attention structure and retain-set language modeling. Post-training activation exchange tests whether the modified attention-output module transmits the learned forgetting effect. ADU achieves the strongest aggregate performance among evaluated baselines on the TOFU and WMDP benchmarks, including a Forget Quality of (0.93) on TOFU. It preserves 87--98% of model utility (92.9% on average versus 81.9% for baselines) while reducing side effects in benign contexts.

</details>


### [290] [Most of the LLM routing gap is task type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23023)
*Janghoon Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在多大语言模型（LLM）选择问题上的路由器机制，发现现有方法并未能有效提升整体性能，且较简单的静态分配已接近当前最佳路由效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于，尽管不同的语言模型在某些问题上各有优劣，如果能针对每道题选出最合适的模型，理论上能超过任一单独模型的能力。然而近期工作显示，尽管路由器的设计多样，实际提升有限，作者希望探明失败原因及可能的改进空间。

Method: 作者设定14个模型针对294道题（包括7种任务类型、3种语言），分别答题2轮，检测模型表现与一致性。考察静态（按任务、按语言分配最佳模型）和学习型路由器的极限表现，并将结果与最强单一模型对比。

Result: 路由提升空间有限，根据模型双轮均正确计为答对，静态按任务分配能提升大部分的表现，按任务和语言进一步提升，剩余能由学习路由器优化的空间很小，还小于模型自然波动。静态表格法在相同数据上已经接近理论最优表现，且成本远低于用最强模型。

Conclusion: 复杂的学习型路由器对提升多模型问答系统效果帮助甚微，简单的静态分配策略已接近理论最佳，进一步优化的空间很小。本文挑战了依赖复杂路由方法的价值，强调稳定性和成本收益。

Abstract: An LLM router picks which model should answer each query. The appeal is that models fail on different questions. Whatever single model is best overall still gets some wrong, and another model in the pool gets many of those right. Getting that choice right every time is the ceiling, and a router is an attempt to approach it.
  However, recent work reports that routers do not get close. Across 21 routing methods on five benchmarks, sharply different designs land within a fraction of a point of each other, and all of them stay far below that ceiling. Learned routers often fail to beat simply always calling the strongest model.
  We ask what those missed questions have in common. We set fourteen models to answer all 294 questions, with 7 task types across 3 languages: Korean, English and Hindi. We ran the whole matrix twice, changing nothing, but 5.37% of the 4,116 model-question pairs came out scored differently anyway. Run-to-run movement like that is normal, and we argue that a small win does not show that routing did anything, ours or anyone else's.
  Counting an answer correct only when the model got it right in both runs, 29 questions on this matrix can be improved with routing. Every correct-answer count here is on that rule. Task type accounts for most of them: assigning each task type one model in advance, chosen once and never updated, improves 21 of the 29. Splitting each task type by language improves 2 more and leaves 6 of 294 unoptimized. That handful is what a learned router would have been built for, and it is smaller than the run-to-run movement above, which is a share of pairs rather than of questions. The static table we adopted answers 262 of 294 questions at $3.33 per run, against the best single model's 245 at $7.69.
  All of this is fitted and scored on the same 294 questions with no holdout.

</details>


### [291] [Beyond Surface Cues: Disentangling Sociocultural Signals in Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23026)
*Yuanjun Feng,Tanzhou Liu,Stefan Feuerriegel,Yash Raj Shrestha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对多语言大模型（LLM）输出中的社会文化偏差和文化归因现象进行了详尽审计，发现现有模式易混淆真实文化理解与表面线索，提出并验证了用于剥离这两者的实用框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语种大模型常因输出内容涉及社会身份标签、语言特征等被认为有“文化归因能力”，但这些往往是通过显性或隐性线索推断的，可能掩盖了模型的真实文化理解和系统性偏差，需加以区分。

Method: 研究设计了由多人验证的、多代理审计流程，将输出中的三类问题分开考察：社会偏见再现、身份群体差别以及跨文化模式。分析了12种LLM在英语、法语、中文三种语言环境下89,253条输出，涵盖18种职业和3种任务设置，通过去除显性身份线索和名字等手段检验影响。

Result: 结果显示，偏见表现随语言和任务显著不同。去掉直接身份线索后，模型在英、中语境下的身份标签预测能力大幅下降，而法语变化较小。所有场景中，源语言背景文化始终得分最高，但在翻译和匿名处理后，辨认源语言能力明显降低。自动与人工评分有一定一致性。

Conclusion: 多语言模型的文化审计若未控制表面线索，极易将形式化特征误认作真实文化理解，造成对跨文化差异和偏见的误导性结论。该研究提出了分离表面捷径与深层跨文化模式的实证框架，为更科学地评估LLM文化能力与偏差提供了工具。

Abstract: Multilingual LLM outputs can vary across sociocultural contexts. However, evidence of cultural grounding can be misleading: identity labels may be inferred from explicit or indirect textual cues, while names and wording can reveal the source language. Treating all these signals as evidence of cultural grounding may obscure potential biases. We present a human-validated, multi-agent audit that separates three questions: whether outputs reproduce social biases, whether identity groups are represented differently, and whether outputs reflect cross-cultural patterns. The study analyzes 89,253 outputs from 12 LLMs in English, French, and Chinese, spanning 18 occupations and three task conditions.
  We find that bias representation varies systematically across languages and tasks. Removing direct identity cues sharply reduces identity-label prediction in English and Chinese, but has a much smaller effect in French. Across all language-genre settings, the cultural context associated with the source language receives the highest average relevance score, with moderate agreement between automated and human ratings. However, the ability to identify the source language drops substantially after translation and again after masking names. Without these controls, multilingual audits may mistake surface cues for cultural understanding, leading to misleading conclusions about cross-cultural variation and bias. Our audit offers a practical framework for separating such shortcuts from more meaningful cross-cultural patterns.

</details>


### [292] [Meta-Moderator: Empowering Multi-Agent Debate with Meta-Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23029)
*Wentao Hu,Zhuoyue Wan,Jinhao Shen,Chen Jason Zhang,Xiaoyong Wei,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新方法Meta-Moderator，通过动态调节多智能体语言模型的辩论流程，提升推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体辩论有助于提升大语言模型推理，但现有方法在仲裁（moderation）环节存在性能瓶颈，常用方法的裁决能力不足，导致推理低效与结果不稳定。

Method: 将仲裁建模为一个元认知流程，负责监控辩论效用、管理流程和裁定最终结果；提出可学习框架Meta-Moderator，用基于结果的策略优化独立训练，使系统主动调节辩论和决策终止点。

Result: Meta-Moderator在五个基准测试中的表现均超越了常用决策层，且具备良好的任务和系统迁移能力。同时，能够更高效地分配辩论资源，减少有效观点出现后的错误信息聚合。

Conclusion: Meta-Moderator将仲裁机制从被动转为主动，显著优化了辩论过程，提高推理效率和准确性，是多智能体语言模型系统的重要提升。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate can improve large language model reasoning by eliciting diverse hypotheses and critiques, yet its performance is often constrained by weak moderation. Common pipelines rely on fixed budgets, agreement-based stopping, or untrained judges, leading to redundant deliberation and unreliable evidence aggregation. We cast moderation as a meta-cognitive process, monitoring debate utility, controlling deliberation, and adjudicating a final answer, and introduce Meta-Moderator, a learnable framework that dynamically regulates debate and decides when to finalize an answer. Meta-Moderator is trained independently of the debaters via outcome-driven policy optimization, making debate regulation an explicit capability rather than an incidental effect of prompting. Across five benchmarks, Meta-Moderator outperforms widely used decision layers and transfers across tasks and system configurations.
