Further analyses show that it allocates debate more selectively and reduces mis-aggregation after informative hypotheses appear.

</details>


### [293] [The Multilingual FrameNet Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23037)
*Beatrice Fiumanò,Nicolas Lazzari,Simone Paolo Ponzetto,Valentina Presutti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了mFNC（多语言FrameNet语料库），通过整合10种语言的现有语料库扩展了英文FrameNet，显著提升了多语言和跨语言语义分析的性能，并开源了相关数据与模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FrameNet主要集中在英文，限制了多语言和跨语言的语义解析研究。而不同语言的FrameNet资源分散，缺乏统一的数据集，制约了模型的训练与评估。作者希望通过整合多语种数据推动语义理解技术在多语言环境中的发展。

Method: 作者收集并标准化了已有的九种语言FrameNet语料库，并与英文Berkeley FrameNet合并，形成了统一的mFNC多语言语料库。基于此，作者采用多种模型架构进行训练，对比分析了多语言和跨语言场景下的表现。

Result: 基于mFNC训练的框架语义解析模型在多语言和跨语言测试场景下均优于现有最先进方法，证明了丰富多语言数据对于提升性能的重要性。

Conclusion: mFNC作为开放多语言语义资源，推动了跨语种语义解析研究，为后续多语言NLP模型提供了重要数据支撑，其相关语料和模型已经开源，便于社区进一步研究与应用。

Abstract: This paper introduces the Multilingual FrameNet Corpus (mFNC), a novel resource that extends the English Berkeley FrameNet corpus by collecting and harmonizing existing language-specific corpora across nine additional languages: Brazilian Portuguese, Chinese, Dutch, French, German, Italian, Korean, Latvian and Swedish. By training models that rely on different architectures on the mFNC, we consistently outperform existing state-of-the-art Frame Semantic Parsers in both multilingual and cross-lingual settings, underscoring the importance of multilingual training data. The mFNC and our trained FSP models are openly available at https://github.com/beatrice-f/mFNC.

</details>


### [294] [Beyond Verdicts: A Graph-Based Analysis of Human and LLM Reasoning in Scientific Fact-Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23047)
*Abdul Ghafoor,Muhammad Arslan Manzoor,Yufang Hou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图的框架，用于比较人类专家和大型语言模型(LLMs)在科学事实核查时的推理路径，并在实际数据集上评估了多种主流LLM模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚假信息往往通过引用真实论文来误导大众，而现有的自动事实核查系统虽然可以判断真假并生成解释，但未必能体现出与人类专家一致的推理过程。因此，研究如何比较和分析模型与人类专家的推理路径差异对提升核查系统的可信度和解释性十分重要。

Method: 作者提出了一种“类型化推理图”(typed reasoning graph)的方法，将人类和LLM的解释建模为推理图。该图细致追踪虚假主张、相关研究背景、研究发现、支持谬误的前提以及谬误类型等，并实现了对齐比较。对于模型未对齐的人类推理路径，还对其与文献的关联、与主张的相关性以及判定的充分性进行验证。

Result: 利用MISSCIPLUS数据集中的84条虚假主张，评估了GPT-5、Claude Opus 4.7和Qwen3-32B三个模型。实验发现Qwen3-32B的判决错误率最低，GPT-5与人类推理路径对齐度最高，Claude Opus 4.7虽然判决准确率不高但在成功案例中常有有效推理。

Conclusion: 提出的推理图方法可以细致比较和分析LLM与人类专家的推理过程，不同模型在事实核查的准确性、人类对齐性和推理有效性上表现各异，为改进和评估自动事实核查系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Misinformation that cites legitimate papers can be especially harmful when it distorts what those studies actually report. While existing automatic fact-checking systems based on large language models (LLMs) can assess whether a model assigns an Incorrect verdict and can gen- erate explanations for that decision, they typi- cally do not indicate whether the model follows the same reasoning path as human experts or arrives at the verdict through a different but still valid path. In this work, we introduce a graph- based framework (typed reasoning graph) for comparing human and LLM reasoning paths in scientific fact-checking. Building on prior work on fallacious reasoning in biomedical misinformation, MISSCIPLUS (Glockner et al., 2025), we model each explanation as a rea- soning graph that links the false claim to the relevant study context, study findings, fallacy- supporting premises, and fallacy labels. This representation enables one-to-one alignment of human and LLM reasoning at the level of fallacy-specific sub-graphs. For non-human- aligned LLM paths, we validate grounding in the cited study, relevance to the claim, and suf- ficiency for the verdict. Using 84 false claims from MISSCIPLUS, we evaluate GPT-5, Claude Opus 4.7, and Qwen3-32B across prompt and evidence settings. Results show distinct perfor- mance dimensions: Qwen3-32B has the lowest verdict failure rate, GPT-5 the highest human alignment, and Claude Opus 4.7 weak verdict prediction but often valid reasoning in success- ful cases

</details>


### [295] [Signal or Noise? A Benchmark Study of Agent Skills in Web Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23067)
*Ziyue Yang,Fan Ding*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出WebDev-Skills-Bench基准，系统评估了开发代理（coding-agent）中技能（Skill）注入的利弊，发现注入技能往往增加代价且效果有限，评测与选择应考虑模型、项目、技能三元组的具体适配性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来，技能模块常被作为可重用编程子程序注入到智能代理中，用于提升代码生成效果和实现开发约定。然而，如何科学评测技能注入的真实有效性，尤其是考虑到注入后prompt长度膨胀带来的副作用（如性能下降和成本增加），尚无标准化方法。本文正是为了解决技能效果的评估难题。

Method: 作者提出了WebDev-Skills-Bench基准，对31个公开Web开发技能在50个项目、1000个有序任务中进行对比实验。实验分为四种对照组：包含目标技能、长度匹配的无关技能、逐个去除技能和空白对照，通过仅在prompt中插入技能描述文件，其他辅助文件直接挂载于工作空间，有效区分技能内容和长度影响。

Result: 结果显示，注入目标技能平均使Pass@2表现下降1.3%至4.2%、任务完成深度下降，但token消耗提升72%-394%。仅有17%至36%的技能-项目对组合出现性能提升。长度对照实验揭示两类失效模式：有的模型因prompt变长导致性能下降，有的模型则是被技能内容“误导”。损失集中体现在任务初期环节。同时，不同模型间技能效果排名相关性较弱，规则型技能优于举例型技能。

Conclusion: 本研究表明，技能注入并非通用加分项，而是需要针对模型、项目、技能三者具体适配。有效评估必须配备长度对照组和模型逐项审核，应将技能注入视为部署时需要精细决策的流程，不能一概而论。

Abstract: Agent Skills are reusable procedural modules that are increasingly injected into coding-agent sessions to encode framework conventions, anti-patterns, and reusable tools. However, because each injected Skill expands the prompt of every query, an effective Skill benchmark must determine not only whether an agent can solve a task, but whether the Skill should have been injected at all. We introduce WebDev-Skills-Bench and use it for a controlled empirical study of 31 public WebDev Skills on 50 Web-Bench projects and 1,000 ordered tasks. The benchmark compares four matched conditions, including a length-matched irrelevant control and leave-one-out component ablations. To isolate Skill effects from prompt-length artifacts, we place only SKILL.md in the prompt while mounting auxiliary files into the agent workspace. Across four models, target Skill injection reduces mean Pass@2 by 1.3% to 4.2%, lowers task completion depth, and increases token cost by 72% to 394%, with gains in only 17% to 36% of Skill-project pairs. Length-matched controls reveal two failure modes: some models are length-distracted, where an equally long irrelevant Skill reproduces most of the loss, while others are content-misled, where prompt length is neutral but Skill content still lowers Pass@2 by 1.1% to 1.4%. Further analysis shows that losses concentrate on easy early tasks, Skill rankings transfer weakly across models, and anti-pattern rules outperform example-heavy content within helpful Skills. These findings recast a matched Skill as a hypothesis about a particular Skill-project-model triple rather than a portable asset, reframing injection as a per-deployment routing decision and making length-matched controls and per-model audits a minimum standard for Agent-Skill evaluation.

</details>


### [296] [Definitional Sensitivity in Media Bias Detection: A Multi-Definition Dataset and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23095)
*Martin Wessel,Timo Spinde,Jürgen Pfeffer,Gianluca Demartini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了媒体偏见检测中偏见定义变化对模型和人工标注一致性的影响，并发布了新的多定义偏见检测数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有媒体偏见检测依赖于对“什么是偏见”的界定与示例，但这些界定往往因数据集而异或含糊不清，导致即使名称相同，训练模型也可能捕捉到不同概念，影响研究和应用的稳定性与可靠性。此前对此问题关注不足。

Method: 作者分别对354名真实参与者和4个大型语言模型进行对比实验。让他们基于不同概念框架与具体程度的偏见定义，对6篇新闻在4类偏见上进行评分，最终收集8496个人工评分和28800个大模型评分，系统分析定义变化对偏见标注的影响。

Result: 实验发现，偏见定义的概念框架（framing）对标注结果有显著影响，不同的定义导致评分系统性偏移。这种影响在人类和大模型上都存在，且在大模型上影响更为突出。相比之下，围绕同一概念的详细阐释对评分影响不大。

Conclusion: 作者强调了偏见定义选择对标注和后续模型分类结果的深远影响，建议标准化和透明化定义以提升公正性与可复现性。同时指出，定义敏感性问题可能波及其他需要主观定义的任务。最后，作者发布了一个支持多定义标注的新数据集MUDD，为相关研究提供资源。

Abstract: Media bias detection relies on definitions and examples that specify what counts as bias, yet these specifications often vary across datasets or remain implicit, even when given the same name. Such variation makes it unclear whether models trained for the same bias category learn the same construct or different phenomena, a problem largely overlooked in prior work. We examine how definition choice affects bias annotation in a between-subjects experiment with 354 participants and a parallel evaluation with four LLMs. Participants and models rate six news articles across four bias categories using definitions that vary in conceptual framing and elaboration. Across 8,496 human and 28,800 LLM ratings, we find that the conceptual target of a definition drives annotation divergence, while construct-preserving elaboration does not: conceptual framing significantly shifts annotations for humans and does so even more strongly for LLMs. We discuss implications for construct specification in annotation protocols and prompt-based measurement, and consider how definitional sensitivity may propagate to downstream classification beyond media bias. We also release MUDD, the Multi-Definition Bias Detection Dataset.

</details>


### [297] [Molecular LLM Agents: From Architectural Design to Scientific Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23104)
*Jiatong Li,Wengyu Zhang,Weida Wang,Yuxuan Ren,Wei Liu,Chenyang Mao,Yuqiang Li,Yatao Bian,Changmeng Zheng,Xiaoyong Wei,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了面向分子领域的大语言模型（LLM）智能体的概念和分层框架，旨在推动其在分子科学中的应用和发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子科学对LLM智能体提出了更高的能力要求，包括对分子对象的多种表示和数据（如分子图、三维构象等）的感知、推理和操作。本研究旨在为分子LLM智能体搭建系统性的理论和方法框架，促进其科学研究和实际应用。

Method: 提出了两个互补视角的框架：一是分子智能体架构视角，涵盖分子表征和感知、智能体框架、领域工具箱、学习与优化等；二是科学自主性阶梯，将分子智能体科学自主性分为四级：L1固定助手型，L2自适应计算型，L3反馈感知型，L4科学议程主导型。

Result: 建立了一套系统框架，用于对比现有分子领域LLM智能体、识别其能力缺失和潜在风险，并为未来智能体的设计、评估与应用指明方向。

Conclusion: 本工作为分子LLM智能体的发展打下了理论和方法基础，有助于指导其在分子发现等实际科学流程中的有效部署和创新。

Abstract: Molecular science represents an important frontier for LLM-based agents. Unlike general agents that mainly operate over natural language, code, or web environments, molecular LLM agents must perceive, reason about, and act upon chemical objects across symbolic strings, molecular graphs, 3D conformations, spectra, simulations, and wet-lab measurements. Their capabilities depend on chemically faithful molecular perception, an LLM-centered agent framework, domain-specific tool grounding, and computational or experimental feedback, in addition to planning and tool use. This work develops a conceptual framework for molecular LLM agents from two complementary perspectives. First, we introduce an architectural view of molecular-agent design, covering molecular representation and perception, the agent framework, domain-specific toolboxes, and learning and optimization. Second, we propose a scientific autonomy ladder inspired by staged autonomy in engineering systems, categorizing agents into four levels: L1 assistive or fixed workflows, L2 adaptive computational agents, L3 feedback-aware physical experiment agents, and L4 scientific-agenda agents. Together, these two perspectives establish a comprehensive framework for comparing existing molecular LLM agents, identifying missing capabilities and deployment risks, and guiding the design, evaluation, and deployment of future agents in molecular discovery workflows.

</details>


### [298] [Statistical Machine Translation Systems of English-Pnar Language Pair : Some Insights of the Emperical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23120)
*Edawanbiang Dhar Surmila Thokchom,Thoudam Doren Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次为英语-潘纳尔语（Pnar）语言对构建了双语平行语料库，并基于短语统计机器翻译（SMT）进行对比实验，建立了该语言对的机器翻译基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潘纳尔语缺乏数字语料和NLP资源，限制了该语言的信息获取及保存，因此需要构建基础的机器翻译和语料资源。

Method: 从Wyrta报纸收集并整理了10234句平行语料，使用Phrase-based SMT模型（Moses、GIZA++、KenLM），设置不同词汇重排序和MERT调优配置，并通过371句的测试集进行评估。

Result: Pnar到英文的最高BLEU为14.97，英文到Pnar为11.16，确立了该语言对的机器翻译基准。词汇化重排序在Pnar到英文方向效果显著（提升3.73 BLEU），MERT在低资源下反而降低性能。错误主要为形态OOV、长距离重排序和卡西语混杂等。

Conclusion: 本工作为Pnar语言对建立了首个机器翻译数据集和性能基线，推动了极低资源语言NLP研究，为今后基于神经和多语种方法的改进奠定了基础。

Abstract: Pnar, an Austroasiatic language spoken by approximately 0.4 million people in the Jaintia Hills of Meghalaya, lacks the digital corpora and natural language processing (NLP) resources. This paper presents the first machine translation study for the English and Pnar language pair. Using articles collected from the Wyrta newspaper, we built a parallel corpus comprising of 10,234 sentences and trained phrase-based statistical machine translation (SMT) systems the models using 9,563 parallel corpora under three configurations for each direction using Moses, GIZA++ , KenLM, varying lexicalized reordering and minimum error rate training (MERT) tuning. The models are evaluated on a held out test set of 371 sentences, the best performing system achieves a BLEU score of 14.97 (chrF2: 33.42, TER: 77.60) for Pnar to English and 11.16 (chrF2: 31.38, TER: 93.51) for English to Pnar, establishing the first quantitative benchmark for this language pair. Lexicalized reordering improves translation quality by 3.73 BLEU points for Pnar to English, reflecting the structural shift from the source language's SOV word order to the target language's SVO order, whereas MERT tuning degrades BLEU performance under low resource conditions. Finally, we analyze the remaining translation errors, including morphological out of vocabulary (OOV) words, long-distance reordering and Khasi code mixing and discuss future directions toward neural and multilingual machine translation for Pnar.

</details>


### [299] [LITERARYBIGFIVE: Author-Personalized Text Generation in a Unified Interpretable Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23124)
*Jinghui Zhang,Lang Gao,Ao Li,Mingzhe Li,Ruihong Zeng,Zirui Song,Kentaro Inui,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了文学五维人格模型（LiteraryBigFive），通过五维空间解释和控制文本个性化生成，提升了作家的表达性与模型解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有作者建模和个性化文本生成方法需要大量语料或单独微调，成本高，难解释，泛化能力弱。论文受到大五人格理论启发，尝试用统一的、可解释的多维空间表达作家写作风格。

Method: 构建五维的写作风格空间（比如古典性、情感性），每个维度由作家文本和中性文本在激活空间中的差异得出，实现作家文本或风格的空间定位。设计了可解释的引导机制，将文本生成定向到目标风格坐标，实现定制化生成。

Result: 实验显示，LiteraryBigFive显著提升了作家文本的表达性，语义保持良好。模型每个维度的分数与真实文学共识高度相关，同时生成过程具有高度可解释性。

Conclusion: LiteraryBigFive为作者个性化文本生成提供了统一、可泛化且可解释的解决方案，有效提升了文本个性表达和应用透明度。

Abstract: Personalized text generation for authors and literary writing is essential for applications such as adaptive writing assistants, creative support tools, and computational literary analysis. However, existing approaches to author modeling and personalization often represent writing behavior as independent labels, requiring large-scale corpus collection or fine-tuning for each author or stylistic category. Such formulations are costly, difficult to interpret, and poorly suited for generalizing across authors. Inspired by the Big Five model's dimensional view of personality, we propose LiteraryBigFive, a framework that reframes authorial writing characteristics as coordinates within a unified and interpretable space. In this space, we derive each interpretable axis (e.g., Classicism, Emotionality) from activation-space contrasts between author-written and neutral passages, yielding distinct stylistic dimensions that allow texts or authors to be positioned within a five-dimensional system. Beyond localizing different authors, we further introduce an interpretable steering mechanism, which adaptively guides text generation toward target coordinates to perform author-personalized writing. Experimental results show that LiteraryBigFive improves authorial expressiveness while preserving semantic fidelity. The derived author per-axis scores strongly correlate with real-world literary consensus, offering transparent and interpretable explanations of author-specific generation behavior: https://github.com/Znull-1220/LiteraryBigFive.

</details>


### [300] [Language Chain in Alignment: Cross-Lingual Ranking Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23149)
*Seungyoon Lee,Minhyuk Kim,Jungseob Lee,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CRPO方法，以英语为锚点联合优化英语与目标语言间的偏好，实现了大语言模型在多语言环境下的偏好对齐提质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前大语言模型主要依赖高质量的英文偏好数据进行对齐，导致模型在其他语言上的效果较差。

Method: 设计CRPO框架，通过建立英语与目标语言之间的层次化平行偏好对，将intra/inter-lingual偏好联合优化，并基于LambdaLoss扩展为多候选应答的排序学习。

Result: 在5种不同资源规模的语言实验中，CRPO在指令跟随与知识利用方面均明显优于标准方法，且多种权重配置均表现稳健。

Conclusion: CRPO能有效提升多语言偏好对齐的稳定性和效果，对跨语言LLM发展具有重要意义。

Abstract: The alignment of Large Language Models heavily relies on English-centric high-quality preference data, which often leads to suboptimal performance in other languages. In this paper, we propose Cross-Lingual Ranking Preference Optimization (CRPO), a novel framework that leverages robust preference knowledge from English to facilitate preference alignment in the target language. We design a hierarchical structure within parallel preference pairs across the target language and English to jointly optimize intra- and inter-lingual preferences, thereby enhancing language adaptation and output quality. Building on the LambdaLoss framework, CRPO goes beyond the binary comparison based optimization by providing a relative ranking signal across multiple candidate responses. Our experiments across five languages with varying resource scales demonstrate that CRPO consistently outperforms standard approaches in both instruction-following and knowledge utilization capability. Notably, the robust performance gains observed across various weighting schemes further validate the empirical effectiveness of our hierarchical design in a multilingual setup. Furthermore, our findings highlight that CRPO significantly improves both reward margins and the log-probability of desirable responses, contributing to a more stable preference manifold for cross-lingual alignment.

</details>


### [301] [Counter with Evidence! A Multi-Agent Memory Efficient Reasoning Framework for Hate Category Informed Counterspeech Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23152)
*Sujoy Nath,Aswini Kumar,Tanmoy Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了FIRE框架，结合事实性判断和多智能体推理，根据仇恨言论的不同类型生成有针对性的反制言论，并构建了配套数据集，实验证实显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动反制言论生成的研究大多只关注文本风格控制，忽视了不同类型的仇恨言论需要不同的反应方式，且通常把仇恨言论视为同质化问题。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的Factuality Informed Multi-Agent Reasoning Framework（FIRE），首先将仇恨言论分为五类（错误信息、刻板印象、阴谋、去人性化、非事实类），再针对每种类型选择最有效的反制风格。同时，作者构建了FactualCS数据集，包含4784条标注了类别、推理链及证据的样本，用于支持模型训练和评估。

Result: FIRE在28组对比实验中，无论在事实准确性还是类别针对性的准确性上均优于现有最强基线，提升幅度分别达到约12%和11%，同时生成内容的毒性降低约11%。人类评价也表明FIRE生成的内容更受欢迎。

Conclusion: 将仇恨言论细分为不同类型，并结合事实性和多智能体推理，可大幅提升自动反制言论生成的安全性、有效性和上下文相关性，有潜力应用于现实世界中。

Abstract: Counterspeech effectively neutralizes the impact of online hate. Although prior work explores automated counterspeech generation, it largely emphasizes stylistic control while treating hate speech as homogeneous, overlooking that distinct forms of abuse require fundamentally different counterspeech strategies. To address this gap, we introduce FIRE (Factuality Informed Multi-Agent Reasoning Framework) that first decomposes hate speech into one of the five distinct categories (misinformation, stereotype, conspiracy, dehumanizing, non-factual), and then maps it to a targeted counterspeech style. To facilitate FIRE, we curate FactualCS, a novel dataset of $4,784$ instances that provides the annotations regarding hate categories, reasoning traces, and evidence mappings, which are critical elements for grounded generation that are missing in prior work. A comprehensive evaluation across $28$ baseline configurations demonstrates that FIRE significantly surpasses existing methods, despite using compact agents ($<$2B). FIRE achieves a $\sim$ $12 \%$ and $\sim$ $11 \%$ improvements in factual and category-specific accuracy respectively, while simultaneously reducing toxicity by $\sim$ $11 \%$ relative to the strongest baselines. Further human evaluation confirms that responses generated by FIRE are significantly preferred over the strongest baselines, underscoring its effectiveness for real-world deployment. These findings show that decomposing the underlying intent of hate speech is essential for generating safe, effective, and contextually precise counterspeech.

</details>


### [302] [Accelerating Diffusion Language Models via Structured Suffix Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23167)
*Zifeng Cheng,Keda Li,Zhiwei Jiang,Cong Wang,Fei Shen,Qing Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对Diffusion Language Models（DLMs）并行解码需要高计算开销的问题，提出了一种高效的结构化后缀建模推理方法，在不需重训练的情况下大幅提升推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前DLMs虽然支持多token并行解码，但每步都需计算所有后缀，大大增加了开销。现有方法仅用局部后缀窗口替代全后缀，未考虑后缀内部结构异质性，且后缀token每步都是相同初始化。为此，作者提出需要更精细的后缀token处理。

Method: 作者将后缀分为局部、中间、尾部三个区域，根据其结构角色分别保留不同数量的token；同时将上一步解码结果融入当前步后缀token表示中，使后缀token跨步携带信息演化。该方法无需训练，可结合多种加速技术（如并行解码、KV缓存）。

Result: 在三种DLM、多项基准测试上，该方法进一步加速了DLM推理，并在多数情况下提升性能。在长序列任务中结合现有加速技术，推理速度最高提升72.81倍。

Conclusion: 论文提出的结构化后缀建模方法能有效缓解DLM推理中的计算瓶颈，提升推理速度和整体性能，并且无需模型重训练，通用性和实用性较强。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) exhibit strong parallel decoding capabilities by denoising multiple tokens in a single generation step. However, this parallelism comes with substantial computational overhead, as each step requires interactions with all suffix tokens. Existing methods typically reduce this cost by retaining only a local suffix window as a substitute for the full suffix. Despite their effectiveness, these methods overlook the structural heterogeneity across suffix regions and re-initialize suffix tokens with identical representations at each timestep. To this end, we propose a structured suffix modeling method for efficient DLM inference. Specifically, we divide the suffix into three regions, i.e., the local, middle, and tail regions, and retain different numbers of suffix tokens in each region according to their structural roles. Moreover, we incorporate the decoding results from the previous step into the suffix token representations at the current step, allowing them to carry evolving denoising information across generation steps. Notably, our method is training-free and orthogonal to several existing acceleration techniques, such as parallel decoding strategies and KV cache. Empirical results across multiple benchmarks on three DLMs demonstrate that our method can further accelerate DLM inference and improve performance in most cases. In particular, in long-sequence inference, our method achieves up to a \(72.81\times\) speedup when combined with other acceleration techniques. Our code is available at https://github.com/zifengcheng/SSM.

</details>


### [303] [CaRGo-T: Causal Reasoning Graph-of-Thought improves Multimodal Humor Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23172)
*Abhilash Nandy,Rahul Seetharaman,Aman Bansal,Rounak Saha,Manav Nitin Kapadnis,Millon Madhur Das,Pawan Goyal,Niloy Ganguly*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了CaRGo-T（Causal Reasoning Graph-of-Thought）框架，能更好地让视觉-语言大模型理解多模态幽默，并在多个数据集上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前大规模视觉-语言模型在多模态任务中展现出卓越能力，但幽默理解依赖于复杂的因果关系及上下文互动，传统推理方法难以捕捉。

Method: 设计了CaRGo-T推理框架，以轻量级的图结构串联并编码幽默内容因果及上下文关系，由大模型生成并解读代码化表示，实现零样本和上下文学习。同时，在四个含有不同幽默类型的数据集（如讽刺、讽喻、表情包）上进行评估。

Result: 使用主流的商业和开源视觉-语言模型进行实验，相较于推理基线方法，在幽默理解上提升1-20%，在幽默检测上提升1-3%。互信息分析显示，CaRGo-T生成的推理表示包含了更多相关输出的信息。

Conclusion: CaRGo-T框架能够有效提升视觉-语言大模型在幽默理解和检测领域的推理能力，对多模态幽默推理具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: Large-scale vision-language models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable versatility across a wide range of multimodal tasks. However, understanding humor remains challenging because humorous content often depends on subtle interactions among entities, events, context, and implicit relationships across image and text modalities. These interactions can involve complex chains of reasoning that are difficult to capture through conventional prompting or linear chain-of-thought reasoning. In this work, we propose CaRGo-T (Causal Reasoning Graph-of-Thought), a reasoning framework that represents the causal and contextual relationships underlying multimodal humor as a lightweight graph-based reasoning structure. The graph is serialized into a code-based representation generated by a VLM, which can subsequently be interpreted by the same or a different VLM to produce the final prediction in zero-shot or in-context learning settings. We evaluate CaRGo-T on humor understanding and humor detection across four datasets spanning diverse forms of comedic content, including satire, sarcasm, and memes. Experiments with state-of-the-art commercial and open-source VLMs show that CaRGo-T consistently improves performance over existing reasoning-based baselines, achieving gains of approximately 1-20% on humor understanding and 1-3% on humor detection. Further analysis using mutual information indicates that the reasoning representations produced by CaRGo-T contain more information relevant to the target output than those generated by baseline reasoning approaches. Code is available at https://github.com/abhi1nandy2/CaRGo-T.

</details>


### [304] [LongWoF-Bench: Evaluating EvoMap Genes for Verifiable Long-Workflow Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23200)
*Xiao Zhang,Qumeng Sun,Jihao Li,Yiming Ren,Xiang Liu,Haoyang Zhang,Junjie Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出通过外部化和复用大型语言模型（LLM）在复杂工作流中获得的成功执行经验，显著提升了LLM在长流程任务中的表现，并引入了新的基准与数据结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在完成复杂工作流任务时，每次执行后经验无法被保留和复用，导致模型在后续任务中需要从头摸索成功策略和应对失败，效率低且浪费资源。作者希望解决经验遗失与重复探索的问题，让模型可以共享和积累成功经验。

Method: 作者提出了EvoMap框架，通过将验证器确认过的执行轨迹整合为结构化的Gene，实现经验的外部化与复用。为评估这种方式，作者设计了LongWoF-Bench基准，涵盖778个机器可验证任务，包含代码生成、代理-环境合成、数学推理和规则遵循等领域。随后在252个验证通过的任务上，比较了EvoMap Gene与Skill和参考蒸馏Gene的效果。

Result: 实验显示，使用EvoMap Gene（来源于成功执行经验）在七种模型上的表现比Skill高8.7-15.5个百分点，对不同模型家族均有效。相比之下，参考蒸馏Gene没有这种优势。对于Claude Opus模型，Gene的复用使其多完成39个任务，并减少9.9%的Token消耗。

Conclusion: 经验证的成功执行经验可以被结构化外部化为可复用资源，大幅提升LLM在长流程复杂任务中的表现，同时减少重复探索的资源消耗，有助于提升模型效率和泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly expected to execute complex workflows whose success depends on maintaining interdependent constraints and producing artifacts that satisfy strict end-to-end verification. Yet successful execution experience is typically lost after a single run, forcing subsequent models to rediscover strategies and failure modes from scratch. We study whether such experience can instead be externalized and reused through EvoMap, where verifier-confirmed execution trajectories are consolidated into structured Gene. To evaluate this setting, we introduce the Long-Workflow Benchmark (LongWoF-Bench), comprising 778 machine-verifiable tasks across code generation, agent-environment synthesis, mathematical reasoning, and rule following. On the 252 tasks with verifier-confirmed Opus trajectories, evolved EvoMap Gene outperform Skill across all seven evaluated models by 8.7-15.5 percentage points, with the gains extending to consumer models from different model families. In contrast, reference-distilled Gene do not exhibit the same advantage, indicating that compact representation alone is insufficient and that Gene utility is closely associated with verified experience provenance. For Claude Opus, Gene reuse also completes 39 more tasks than Skill while reducing solve-time token consumption by 9.9%. Together, these results show that verified execution experience can be retained and shared as a reusable external resource, enabling models to improve long-workflow completion without repeatedly paying the full cost of experience discovery.

</details>


### [305] [Aligning Biomedical Texts and Knowledge Graphs: A Systematic Comparison of Lightweight Alignment Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23214)
*Artem Bisliouk,Elizaveta Nosova,Heiko Paulheim,Andreea Iana,Rita T. Sousa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的对比学习方法，将生物医学自由文本和知识图谱（KG）进行高效对齐。通过系统性实验，发现简单的设计选择即可实现优异表现，为文本和KG的桥接打下了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以将自由文本证据与知识图谱三元组进行显式对齐，这限制了知识落地、证据检索和KG补全等关键任务的发展。因此亟需高效、系统性的方法来对齐生物医学文本与KG。

Method: 在固定文本编码器和知识图谱嵌入模型参数的基础上，只训练一个轻量级投影头，通过对比损失学习文本与KG空间之间的映射。设置六个设计维度，并基于22K对一一对应的化学-基因三元组及其文献对（CTD-Align）进行评测。

Result: 实验显示，三元组组成和训练方向（即是否共享检索空间）对结果影响最大，而文本编码器和困难负采样影响较小。最优方案是简单地将三元组编码拼接并用线性投影将文本映射到KG空间。

Conclusion: 简单、轻量的对比对齐方法即可高效桥接生物医学文本与知识图谱，适合作为后续研究和应用的基础。

Abstract: Biomedical knowledge exists in two complementary but distinct forms: unstructured scientific literature and structured knowledge graphs (KGs). Aligning them is essential for knowledge grounding, evidence retrieval, and KG completion, yet existing methods do not explicitly align free-text evidence with KG triples. We present a unified framework for systematically studying design choices for aligning biomedical text and KGs. With a text encoder and a KG embedding model both frozen, we learn only a lightweight projection between their spaces via a contrastive objective. This enables a fair comparison across six design dimensions: text encoder, KG embedding model, projection head, triple composition, training direction, and hard-negatives sampling. We construct CTD-Align, a corpus of over 22K one-to-one tripledocument pairs linking chemical-gene interactions from the Comparative Toxicogenomics Database to supporting PubMed passages. We evaluate alignment on it in two retrieval settings: document-to-triple and triple-to-document. We find that the triple composition and the training direction (i.e., shared retrieval space) have the greatest impact, whereas the text encoder and hard-negatives sampling matter little. Overall, simple choices win: projecting text into the KG space with a linear head over concatenated subject, predicate, and object embeddings performs best. These findings establish lightweight contrastive alignment as an effective, practical foundation for bridging biomedical text and KGs.

</details>


### [306] [A Multi-Domain and Multi-Task Generative Framework with Explicit Task and Domain Conditioning for Cross-Domain Event Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23235)
*Siting Liang,Omar Adjali,Daniel Sonntag*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的多领域多任务事件抽取训练框架，通过领域条件信号和任务提示，实现了灵活的跨领域泛化和优异的抽取性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件抽取模型尽管在同域数据表现良好，但面对领域变化和不同事件架构时，泛化能力不足。特别是不完整事件标签集推理和领域差异，限制了模型实际应用。

Method: 提出利用领域条件信号和任务特定提示的多领域多任务统一训练框架，使单一模型能够适配不同领域和异构事件架构。无需完整标签集，支持流水线及端到端抽取，并促进行业和任务层面转移。

Result: 在多个事件抽取公开基准上，模型取得了有竞争力的性能，展现强跨域泛化和良好可扩展性，同时保持领域内高精度。

Conclusion: 该方法有效缓解了跨领域事件抽取的泛化难题，具备实际推广价值，并为后续领域自适应事件抽取研究提供新思路。

Abstract: Event extraction aims to identify event triggers, classify event types, and extract arguments to construct structured event representations. Despite strong in-domain performance, developing models that generalize robustly across domains remains challenging due to variations in contextual expressions and event schemas. Prior unified and multi-task approaches improve in-domain accuracy but exhibit limited flexibility when applied to unseen domains. Even large language model-based methods that provide full event ontologies at inference time often underperform compared to smaller, task-specific fine-tuned models. We propose a unified multi-domain and multi-task training framework that models heterogeneous event schemas within a single model. Our approach introduces domain conditioning signals, jointly with task-specific prompts, enabling dynamic adaptation to dataset-specific schemas without requiring complete event label sets at inference time. The framework supports both pipeline and end-to-end extraction settings, facilitating efficient task- and domain-level transfer. Experiments on diverse event extraction benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves competitive performance, strong cross-domain generalization, and practical scalability, while preserving domain-specific precision.

</details>


### [307] [Credal Large Language Models for Semantic Commitment under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23244)
*Shireen Kudukkil Manchingal,Sofiia Nikolenko,Fabio Cuzzolin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种新的大语言模型框架Credal LLM（CLLM），通过集成多个LoRA适配器来显式建模预测不确定性，从而更好地区分模型无知与真实模糊。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型（LLMs）在预测时将所有不确定性压缩为单一的分布表达，难以区分模型知识的不足与问题本身的多解性，容易出现错误但自信的回答（幻觉）。有必要提升模型对预测置信度的表达和甄别能力。

Method: 作者提出了Credal LLMs（CLLM）方法：利用LoRA适配器的集成，得到多组概率分布，即credal set。由此推导出两类承诺度指标：Credal Token Commitment（CTC）用于标记级别分析，无需额外生成；Semantic Commitment Consistency（SCC）用于语义空间评估，基于采样扩充，SCC-Gap衡量两者不一致性。

Result: 在Gemma-2-9B等多模型和OpenBookQA等多数据集上测试，CLLM在问答准确率和校准误差上表现最优。CTC在大部分设置下幻觉检测AUROC指标领先，而且无需额外生成。选择性预测任务中，OpenBookQA上SCC方法准确率达99.0%，ARC-Challenge上Csem置信度校准误差低于0.6%。

Conclusion: CLLM方法显著提升了大语言模型在未知情境下的稳健表现和不确定性建模能力，对提升幻觉检测、预测准确性和模型置信度校准具有实际价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often produce fluent but incorrect answers with unwarranted confidence. A central limitation is that standard LLMs represent uncertainty through a single predictive distribution, conflating epistemic ignorance with genuine ambiguity. We introduce Credal Large Language Models (CLLMs): an ensemble of LoRA adapters induces a credal set whose lower and upper probabilities expose the spread of plausible predictive distributions rather than collapsing to a single softmax output. From this representation we derive two complementary commitment scores. Credal Token Commitment (CTC) is a token-space score that combines lower-bound support, credal width, and intersection entropy, computed without additional generation. Semantic Commitment Consistency (SCC) extends commitment to semantic space using sampled completions, with SCC-Gap measuring the mismatch between token-level and semantic-level support. We evaluate hallucination detection, calibration, selective prediction, and reasoning on Gemma-2-9B, Llama-3.1-8B, and Qwen2.5-7B across OpenBookQA, CoQA, TriviaQA, and ARC-Challenge. CLLM is the best method on QA accuracy at competitive expected calibration error, and CTC tracks the best hallucination AUROC within 1.5 pp on most settings without additional generation. On selective prediction at 80% coverage, CLLM with SCC reaches 99.0% accuracy on OpenBookQA, and on ARC-Challenge CLLM with Csem confidence achieves <= 0.6% ECE across the three backbones.

</details>


### [308] [Future Querying: Can LLMs Serve as Implicit Medical World Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23248)
*Siri Willems,James Butterworth,Lore Goetschalckx,Peter Vrancx,Philippe Modard,Elke Giets,Ludovic Denoyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种称为“未来查询”的新范式，将大型语言模型（LLMs）用于预测临床任务，不再依赖结构化数据和特定流水线，直接处理非结构化医疗文本。实验显示，小型的本地微调开源模型也能达到或接近大型专有模型的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的临床预测模型对结构化、人工整理数据依赖较大，难以扩展和充分利用丰富的非结构化医疗文本，因此需要一种新方法利用这些未结构化文档进行临床预测。

Method: 提出“未来查询”范式，通过让LLM模型根据非结构化临床文本，回答关于患者未来的、带有时间索引的临床查询。该方法无需手工特征工程和针对具体任务的重新训练。训练在不同终点通用的数据集上，评估在合成医疗报告和MIMIC-IV ICU真实笔记上。

Result: 结果显示，本地微调用的小型开源模型性能能够匹配或接近更大、更昂贵的专有系统，验证了LLM捕捉临床动态的能力、有实际部署价值。

Conclusion: LLM不仅能较好地进行医学预测，且兼顾隐私和易部署性，为基于非结构化文本的医疗场景下多样化临床预测提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Traditional clinical prediction models rely on task-specific pipelines and curated, structured data, which scale poorly and underutilize unstructured text. To address this, we introduce future querying, a paradigm that probes whether large language models (LLMs) can function as implicit medical world models by evaluating their ability to answer time-indexed clinical queries about a patient's future. Our framework operates on unstructured clinical documentation using endpoint-agnostic training, enabling a single model to answer diverse clinical queries over patient trajectories without manual feature engineering or task-specific retraining. We show that small, locally fine-tuned open-weight models can match or approach larger proprietary systems, making the framework suitable for privacy-preserving, on-premise deployment. Evaluated on a new synthetic medical reports dataset and real ICU notes from the MIMIC-IV dataset, our results provide encouraging evidence that LLMs can capture aspects of clinical dynamics.

</details>


### [309] [A Scalable Cross-Domain Event Extraction System via a Unified Generative Training Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23261)
*Siting Liang,Omar Adjali,Omair Shahzad Bhatti,Daniel Sonntag*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的生成式序列到序列框架，实现了事件抽取任务的联合建模，并支持多种配置，提升了跨领域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往事件抽取方法将事件检测和要素抽取分离，或依赖特定数据集的设计，导致可扩展性和跨领域泛化能力受限。

Method: 提出基于生成式序列到序列的统一框架，联合进行事件抽取各子任务。通过微调预训练语言模型，兼容多种领域的事件数据集。该系统支持流水线和端到端两种模式，并开发了支持文档上传、模式感知事件抽取、可视化和跨域配置比较的网页应用。

Result: 单一模型能够同时保留领域特定语义，并在大规模和动态变化的标签空间上实现泛化。实验表明，所提方法跨领域表现优良，且系统已实现可用的线上工具。

Conclusion: 所提统一生成式框架提升了事件抽取的可扩展性和跨域适应性，并为实际应用提供了便利的技术支持。

Abstract: Event extraction is fundamental to information extraction. Prior approaches often separate event detection and argument extraction or depend on dataset-specific designs, limiting scalability and cross-domain generalization. We propose a unified generative sequence-to-sequence framework that performs event extraction subtasks jointly and supports both pipeline and end-to-end configurations. We fine-tune pretrained language models on multiple event datasets across diverse domains, enabling a single model to retain domain-specific semantics while generalizing over large and evolving label spaces. We demonstrate these capabilities through a web-based application tailored for researchers and practitioners. The platform supports document upload, schema-aware event extraction, visualization of triggers and arguments, and comparison of different extraction configurations across domains.

</details>


### [310] [EvoWiki: Incremental State Overwriting and Traceable Question Answering for Cross-Meeting Knowledge Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23265)
*Dongsheng Chen,Tianyu Wang,Wenhui Que*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EvoWiki提出了一种新架构，解决多轮会议动态知识演化下的信息检索与问答问题，实现更准确和可追溯的答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文和RAG、结构化记忆方法不能有效处理知识的生命周期，导致同时保留新旧冲突状态或丢弃历史，检索过时，难以验证答案。

Method: 提出EvoWiki系统，将离线增量构建（BUILD）与在线结构化读取（READ）解耦。BUILD用实体版本链与状态覆盖协议明确区分当前有效状态与被取代历史，并保留溯源。READ则基于确定性实体寻址与跨实体多跳聚合，无需传统的相关性Top-K检索，实现可追溯问答。此外引入CrossMeet数据集，用于模拟长时知识演化测试。

Result: 在六个数据集和两个阅读模型中，EvoWiki在Judge Accuracy上分别比最强基线高9.72和10个百分点。

Conclusion: EvoWiki在动态状态频繁变化下更鲁棒且事实准确，证明以有效状态为中心的阅读是处理跨会议知识演化的可靠方案。

Abstract: In long-term collaboration spanning multiple meetings, factual states such as decisions and risks are continually revised, overturned, and replaced. Existing long-context methods typically stack the entire history, while many RAG and structured-memory methods organize knowledge as static or append-only facts and rely on semantic relevance at read time. Without explicit modeling of knowledge lifecycles, these approaches may retain conflicting old and new states simultaneously or discard history, leading to stale retrieval and answers that are difficult to verify. We present EvoWiki, an incremental question-answering architecture for dynamic long-form text. EvoWiki decouples offline incremental construction (BUILD) from online structured reading (READ). BUILD captures the intra-meeting micro-evolution from proposal to decision and uses entity version chains and a fine-grained State-Overwrite Protocol to explicitly distinguish current valid states from superseded history while preserving meeting-level provenance anchors. READ bypasses relevance-based Top-k retrieval and performs deterministic entity addressing, temporal resolution, and cross-entity multi-hop aggregation over the complete Wiki to produce grounded and traceable answers. We further introduce CrossMeet, a high-fidelity bilingual benchmark designed to simulate long-term state evolution, covering factual consistency, temporal reasoning, and cross-meeting multi-hop reasoning. Across six datasets and two reader models, EvoWiki improves macro-average Judge Accuracy over the strongest baselines by 9.72 and 10.00 percentage points, respectively. Human evaluation shows that EvoWiki is more robust and factually faithful under frequent state flips, validating valid-state-oriented reading as a reliable approach to cross-meeting knowledge evolution.

</details>


### [311] [Dynamic Topic Modeling for Cross-Corpus Temporal Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23284)
*Ruoxuan Li,Bruce Kogut*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的动态嵌入式主题模型（D-ETM），能够同时保持跨语料库主题比对的可解释性和各自的语料特化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的动态嵌入式主题模型在进行跨语料库主题演化比较时，存在模型独立训练、后期对齐稳定性差的问题，难以保证主题一致性。因而需要模型内建主题对齐机制以提升跨库对比的准确性和稳定性。

Method: 作者提出在多语料库合并后，先学习一个共享动态主题空间（backbone），再对各语料库基于该backbone进行残差适应（residual adaptation），从而保证主题索引一致，并允许词汇细节差异，无需单独后期对齐过程。

Result: 在三个跨97年历史的时序语料库上评估，残差适应法在保持统一主题轨迹的同时，有效提升了各语料库模型适配度，主题对齐能力远优于传统独立训练+后期对齐及全量微调方法（例如轨迹检索准确率提升至97.5% vs 17.9%）。

Conclusion: 模型内在的主题对齐机制支持了更稳定、可比较的跨语料库时序主题演化分析，同时保留各自的词汇特化能力，优于现有后处理或微调对齐方法。

Abstract: Dynamic Embedded Topic Models (D-ETM) provide an interpretable framework for modeling temporal semantic evolution, but cross-corpus comparison remains difficult because topics are often learned independently and aligned only after training, a process that does not guarantee stable topic correspondence across corpora and time. To address this problem, we propose a D-ETM framework that first learns a common dynamic topic space over a merged multi-corpus collection, which we call the shared backbone, then introduces corpus-specific residual adaptation around the frozen backbone without creating separate latent topic spaces. This design preserves a shared topic index for cross-corpus comparison while allowing each corpus to specialize lexically. We evaluate the framework on three temporally structured corpora spanning 97 years: the Corpus of Historical American English, Harvard Business Review, and International Labour Review. Residual adaptation improves corpus-specific fit relative to the shared backbone while preserving the same-index cross-corpus topic trajectories, achieving substantially stronger alignment than full fine-tuning from the same backbone, with $97.5 \pm 0.7\%$ versus $17.9 \pm 1.1\%$ trajectory Retrieval@1, as well as stronger alignment than independent training with post-hoc Hungarian matching. These results suggest that incorporating topic alignment into the model can support more stable over-time cross-corpus comparisons while retaining corpus-specific lexical variation.

</details>


### [312] [Beyond the Stability-Exploration Dilemma: Environmental Regularization for LLM Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23311)
*Xianlei Zhou,Xiangdi Meng,Yu He,Tianyu Qi,Shuyan Guan,Xianli Zhang,Jian Zhang,Xin Li,Qika Lin,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新的大模型策略优化方法ERPO，用输入侧的Query-KL正则替代传统输出侧的Policy-KL，实现更好的训练稳定性和探索性平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大模型策略优化普遍采用Policy-KL作为正则，但该方式会导致模型探索性受限或缺乏漂移控制，难以兼得训练稳定性和探索效率。作者希望突破这一困境。

Method: 提出Environment-Regularized Policy Optimization (ERPO)方法，在损失函数中加入Query-KL正则项限制输入分布漂移，并且按参照分布为每个query赋予静态权重。此方法可无缝集成进主流策略优化框架，并且对探索性保持友好。

Result: 在6个数学推理任务上，ERPO替代传统Policy-KL后，能有效控制query分布漂移，提升模型准确率，并在高温采样和长序列训练下表现出更高训练稳定性。

Conclusion: ERPO通过输入侧正则创新性地解决了策略优化的稳定性与探索性矛盾，可直接扩展到现有RLHF训练流程，具有显著实用价值。

Abstract: Policy optimization (PO) for Large Language Models faces a stability--exploration trade-off, currently mediated by an action-side Policy-KL regularizer. This puts practitioners in a double bind: keeping Policy-KL constrains response behavior and consumes the action-side exploration budget, while dropping it leaves the optimization without an explicit drift control. We argue for an alternative that breaks the dilemma by moving regularization to the input side. As training progresses, the distribution over training queries induced by the current policy drifts unchecked from its pre-RL reference distribution.
  Concretely, Environment-Regularized Policy Optimization (ERPO) introduces a Query-KL (QKL) term that bounds this query distribution shift, together with a dataset-static reference-derived per-query weight that biases each per-query update toward queries typical under the reference. The QKL gradient flows strictly through the query likelihood; the response score function used by policy-gradient estimators does not appear in the QKL term, so QKL exerts no direct gradient pressure on the response distribution---exploration is preserved. ERPO plugs into GRPO/PPO/REINFORCE-style pipelines without additional forward passes. On six mathematical reasoning benchmarks, ERPO replaces the standard Policy-KL regularizer while achieving effective control over query distribution drift, delivering stronger accuracy and substantially more stable behavior under high-temperature decoding and long-horizon training.Our source code are available at https://github.com/alibaba/ERPO

</details>


### [313] [Flesch-Kincaid Readability Depends Only on the Topic Distribution in Long Texts under Topic Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23327)
*Yo Ehara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了FRE与FKGL这两种英语可读性指标在长文档下的不变性，并证明其分数变化主要受主题分布影响，而非真正的可读性信号。实验证明，文档主题向量能很好预测FKGL，但对提升已有特征后的预测能力提升有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FRE与FKGL是常用的英语可读性计算方法，但它们的分数如何受文档主题结构影响，学界尚不清楚。本文旨在揭示这两指标在长文本中的真正变异来源。

Method: 理论上，基于带显式句边界的主题模型，分析长文档极限下FRE和FKGL的收敛性质。实证上，在Brown和BNC语料库中，通过主题向量预测分文档FKGL，并与其他特征（如体裁、音节数）联合验证其补充价值。

Result: 在Brown和BNC语料库上，主题向量对另一半文档FKGL的预测相关性分别达到0.779和0.884，但在控制体裁和平均音节数基础上提升的R^2值很低，增益有限。

Conclusion: FRE与FKGL长文档极限下几乎全由主题组成决定，对剩余的可读性特征不敏感。因此不建议将其作为人类可读性、因果效应的直接替代。

Abstract: Flesch Reading Ease (FRE) and the Flesch-Kincaid Grade Level (FKGL) are widely used readability scores for English computed from the same two document statistics, yet their stability on long documents need not imply invariance to lexical composition. Surprisingly, under a topic model with an explicit sentence-boundary token, both scores converge almost surely to deterministic functions of the document topic distribution through just two scalar rates: in the long-text limit, all score variation is mediated by topical composition rather than any residual readability signal. The theory covers both formulae, while the experiments evaluate FKGL. In a fixed admixture with rank[1, q, s] = 3, fibres through interior topic vectors are locally (K-3)-dimensional, whereas regular iso-score level sets are locally (K-2)-dimensional and curved. In out-of-fold evaluation on two balanced corpora, Brown and the written BNC, a topic vector inferred from one document half's content words predicts the other half's FKGL at r = 0.779 and 0.884, respectively. On Brown, adding the topic prediction to genre and mean content-word syllable count yields $ΔR^2$ = 0.002, with a confidence interval spanning zero; on the BNC, the corresponding split-half increment is 0.024, positive in four of five K = 100 fits (median 0.021). Because inferred topics may also absorb genre, register, and style, we do not interpret these results as evidence about human readability or causal effects.

</details>


### [314] [The Emergence of Relevance Through Axiomatic Attention Patterns During LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23338)
*Matthew Perlman,Atharva Nijasure,James Allan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了对大语言模型（LLM）进行LoRA微调于重排序任务时，网络中哪些区域学到了与相关性相关的特征，以及注意力机制发生了怎样的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA已广泛用于微调LLM以适应重排序任务，但尚不清楚具体网络位置和注意力层级特征的学习规律和细节，因此需要明确不同网络位置LoRA更新对任务表现和模型行为的影响。

Method: 作者通过消融实验和注意力分析，分别对模型中不同注意力层应用LoRA微调，考察其对RankLLaMA表现的提升，并分析不同区域的注意力模式（如词汇匹配、稀有性敏感度、查询-文档交互）与性能的关系。

Result: 发现仅在中间部分的注意力层进行LoRA微调就能恢复一半以上的性能增益，且该部分受影响最大。提升性能最明显的区域，其注意力模式也最充分体现了IR相关性特征（如稀有词敏感、查询-文档交互等）。

Conclusion: 相关性取向行为主要在LoRA微调网络的特定区域涌现，且其可解释性特征与性能提升高度相关；为今后更高效、可解释地适配重排序器提供了方向。

Abstract: LoRA fine-tuning is standard for adapting LLMs to reranking, but it remains unclear where in the network task-specific relevance behavior is learned and what attention-level changes accompany that learning. Through ablation and attention experiments, we identify where LoRA attention updates to RankLLaMA improve performance and whether those gains coincide with interpretable relevance-oriented attention patterns such as lexical matching, rarity sensitivity, and query-document interaction. We find that given LoRA fine-tuned MLPs throughout the network, restricting LoRA attention updates to a compact mid-network region is sufficient for recovering over half of the performance gained by applying LoRA to all attention layers, and that omitting attention fine-tuning in this region hurts performance more than elsewhere in the network. Additionally, we show that regions where applying LoRA affects performance the most overlap with regions where fine-tuning increased attention to axiomatic IR features. Rarity sensitivity, document-query interaction, and several compositional features are highly correlated with gains in ranking performance. Our results support an interpretable, correlational account of how relevance-oriented behavior emerges during LoRA fine-tuning and point toward improved strategies for adapting rerankers.

</details>


### [315] [FormuEvo: LLM-Guided Evolution for Discovering Solver-Efficient Mixed-Integer Programming Formulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23353)
*Haofeng Yuan,Jianing Peng,Jieyi Bi,Ni Zhang,Shiji Song,Zhiguang Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种由大语言模型（LLM）引导的进化式框架FormuEvo，可自动发现对求解器高效的混合整数规划（MIP）建模表达方式，显著提升优化效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM虽能自动从自然语言生成MIP模型，但存在只重语义正确、忽略建模表达强度的弊端，导致优化求解效率低下。为突破此瓶颈，作者致力于开发一种能提升MIP建模表达质量的自动化方法。

Method: FormuEvo将MIP表达设计视为在符号空间上的进化优化。首先通过LLM生成多种可执行建模程序（候选表达），随后借助进化操作（交叉、变异、修复）对表达进行迭代更新。为增强进化方向性，引入基于求解器统计数据的诊断机制，作为“语言梯度”指导候选表达改进。此外，设计结构化记忆机制，将过往经验抽象为可复用策略，实现不见问题的零样本迁移及助力小模型训练。

Result: 在多种线性与非线性问题上实验表明，FormuEvo能发现优于专家设计和现有LLM自动建模的MIP表达，求解器加速高达5.5倍，并且抽象知识能有效迁移到新问题与不同模型规模上。

Conclusion: FormuEvo不仅提升了自动化MIP建模的表达质量和优化效率，还推动了大语言模型在运筹优化领域的实用性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Mixed-integer programming (MIP) lies at the core of operations research and industrial optimization. While large language models (LLMs) have recently shown promise in automated MIP modeling from natural language, they prioritize semantic correctness but overlook formulation strength, severely bottlenecking the efficiency of downstream solvers. We propose FormuEvo, an LLM-guided evolutionary framework for automated discovery of solver-efficient MIP formulations. FormuEvo frames MIP formulation design as evolutionary optimization over the symbolic space of MIP formulations, represented as executable modeling programs, by iteratively generating, evaluating, and selecting stronger candidates via LLM-driven crossover, mutation, and repair operations. To move beyond blind exploration, FormuEvo introduces a solver-informed diagnosis mechanism that exploits fine-grained solver statistics as verbal gradients for targeted refinement. Additionally, a structured memory abstracts prior experience into reusable modeling strategies, avoiding redundant exploration while enabling zero-shot transfer to unseen problems and bootstrapping smaller LLMs. Experiments across diverse linear and non-linear problems demonstrate that FormuEvo discovers formulations that significantly outperform both expert-designed formulations and existing LLM-based approaches, accelerating solvers by up to 5.5$\times$, with distilled knowledge transferring effectively across problems and model scales.

</details>


### [316] [The Geometry of Low-Resource Language Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23358)
*Francois Meyer,Jan Buys*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文研究大型语言模型（LLM）在高、低资源语言上的性能差距，发现内部表示几何结构与数据量密切相关，并提出通过几何正则化缓解低资源语言的表示退化，实现性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大家已知大型语言模型在高、低资源语言上表现有明显差距，但导致这个差距的模型内部机制尚不清楚。本文旨在揭示造成这种差距的关键内部因素，并找到改善低资源语言表现的方法。

Method: 作者从“表示几何”这一角度，比较了30种语言在LLM内部隐藏层的表示特征，重点分析了表示退化的现象。进一步，论文使用几何正则化项（如cosine similarity-based regularisation）在继续预训练（CPT）阶段惩罚表示退化，并在9个基础LLM上针对10种非洲语言进行了单语适应实验。

Result: 结果发现低资源语言在模型最终层表现出明显的表示退化。应用几何正则化后，可以有效减少CPT阶段的表示退化。对于大模型，基于余弦相似度的正则化相较于常规CPT带来了一定的性能提升，尤其在最具挑战性的任务上效果更稳定。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统地证明了高、低资源语言在LLM中的表示几何是有显著区别的，并验证了通过几何干预（如正则化）是一种提升低资源语言CPT表现的有效策略。

Abstract: The performance gap between low- and high-resource languages in LLMs is widely known, but it remains unclear which internal model factors drive these disparities. In this paper, we characterise this gap through the lens of representational geometry. Comparing the geometric properties of hidden representations across 30 languages reveals that LLM geometry is systematically related to language data availability. The most consistent effect is in final layers, where low-resource languages exhibit representational degeneration. To counter this, we investigate the effectiveness of regularisation terms to penalise degeneration during continued pretraining (CPT). Experiments monolingually adapting 9 base LLMs to 10 African languages show that geometric regularisation successfully reduces representational degeneration during CPT. For larger models, cosine similarity-based regularisation marginally improves performance over vanilla CPT, with more consistent gains on the most challenging tasks. We establish that the representational geometry of low- and high-resource languages in LLMs is measurably distinct, and that targeted geometric intervention is a viable strategy for improving CPT for low-resource languages.

</details>


### [317] [Cross-lingual Biography Enrichment via Claim Extraction and Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23390)
*Yifei Song,Ziyang Chen,Emil Sayilov,Claire Gardent*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种利用非英语维基百科丰富英语传记内容的方法，重点关注低资源语言背景下的女性人物。作者构建了CLAW-4L基准，验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 英语维基百科经常被作为权威资料库，但对于某些长尾人物（尤其是来自非英语国家的人物），非英语维基百科往往包含更丰富的本地信息。提升这些英语传记的完整性和多样性具有现实和学术意义，尤其对于女性等代表性不足群体。

Method: 作者提出了一种基于事实声明的丰富框架：首先从英语和非英语传记中抽取声明，然后进行对齐，识别那些可用于补充或丰富英语传记的新证据，最后用这些声明重写英语传记。作者还构建了CLAW-4L数据集，包括300对英语—法语/中文/阿塞拜疆语传记及声明对齐注释。

Result: 实验结果显示，非英语维基百科传记能够为英语传记补充有价值的信息，在丰富英语传记覆盖度方面效果明显。但是，对于低资源语言背景的人物，完善信息仍具挑战。

Conclusion: 非英语维基百科是提高英语传记覆盖度和信息准确性的宝贵资源，尤其对于长尾和代表性不足群体。未来方法还需进一步突破低资源环境下的限制。

Abstract: English Wikipedia is often treated as the default encyclopedic source, yet non-English Wikipedia editions can contain richer locally grounded information for long-tail figures. We study cross-lingual biography enrichment: enriching an existing English biography with facts supported by a non-English biography about the same person. Focusing on women from non-English-speaking contexts, we introduce \textsc{CLAW-4L}, a benchmark consisting of 300 Wikipedia biography pairs linking an English biography with its French, Chinese or Azerbaijani counterpart, along with claim annotations and a fine-grained claim-pair relation corpus. We propose a claim-based enrichment framework that extracts English claims from both biographies, aligns them to identify enrichment evidence from the non-English biography, and rewrites the English biography using the selected claims. Our results show that non-English Wikipedia biographies provide valuable evidence for improving English biography coverage, while lower-resource settings remain challenging.

</details>


### [318] [Cross-Domain, Multi-Task Data-to-Text Generation without In-Domain Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23391)
*Yifei Song,Kun Efimov-Zhang,Claire Gardent*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了将结构化数据跨领域生成文本（D2T）的问题，提出了结构保持的数据增强，并证实小模型经过知识蒸馏后在多个领域效果优于大型微调模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往的结构化数据到文本生成任务主要针对特定任务或数据集，方法依赖于特定领域数据或大模型零样本推理，缺乏通用性。本文旨在解决无领域特定训练/测试数据、领域变化大时的跨领域生成问题。

Method: 对比了数据驱动知识蒸馏（DDKD）、零样本推理及在非本领域微调，并提出结构保持的数据增强方法（结构子采样和扰动）以提升跨领域泛化能力。

Result: 在1.7B参数规模下，DDKD方法在五个基准测试上均优于零样本和微调，且小模型在两个领域超过了更大规模的微调模型，并在另外三个领域取得了可比结果。数据增强策略明显优于简单拓展真实输入。

Conclusion: 结构保持的数据增强结合知识蒸馏是进行跨领域结构化数据文本生成的有效且高效方案。

Abstract: Structured data exists in many forms (tables, knowledge graphs, charts, and time series), and converting it into text may involve different generation tasks. However, most prior work on data-to-text (D2T) generation has focused on specific tasks and datasets, relying either on task-specific training data or on the zero-shot capabilities of large language models. We study cross-domain D2T generation in a setting where neither in-domain training text nor test references are available, and where domains, generation goals, and input structures vary substantially. We compare data-driven knowledge distillation (DDKD) against zero-shot inference and fine-tuning on out-of-domain D2T data, and introduce structure-preserving augmentation via structural subsampling and perturbation. Experiments on five benchmarks show that, at constant model size (1.7B parameters), DDKD consistently outperforms both fine-tuning and zero-shot inference. Moreover, the resulting small models outperform a much larger finetuned model on two of the five domains, achieving comparable performance on the remaining three. We further construct QUINTD-5, a fivefold extension of QUINTD-1, and show that simply scaling real target-domain inputs yields only modest gains, whereas our augmentation strategy remains more effective and more cost-efficient for cross-domain distillation.

</details>


### [319] [STONIC: A Layered Measurement Contract for LLM Value Profiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23411)
*Andrei Chetvergov,Stepan Ukolov,Timofei Sivoraksha,Alexander Evseev,Danil Sazanakov,Mikhail Solovev,Sergey Bolovtsov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文系统检验了通过问卷评分、配对选择和生成文本推断值得到的偏好是否可以合并为LLM一致性的统一偏好配置。发现模型在行为上表现出一定重现性，但并不完全支持三种方式的偏好一致性假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大模型相关偏好研究常将不同偏好测量方式（问卷、对比选择和文本推断）合并为统一的个体偏好画像，但缺少对其假设一致性的系统验证。

Method: 作者采用STONIC评测，覆盖4个数据bank、35种模型配置和5144个情境，比较了模型在隔离评分、冲突条件下选择、自发回答以及对自身与人工答案后续对比偏好的表现，并通过人工三重标注和模型判别等方式分析一致性。

Result: 在含有足够行为数据的配置中，多数模型能保持选择与评分的一致性，并且模型普遍倾向于选择自己早前的答案。不过问题选项顺序对选择率有影响，不同测量方式得到的偏好画像转移程度有差异：评分到对比选择转移最强，自发文本转弱。

Conclusion: LLM模型展现出一定的行为连续性，但无法证明三种评价方式下的偏好可完全合并为单一、量表无关的价值画像。

Abstract: LLM value studies often merge questionnaire ratings, pairwise choices, and values inferred from generated text into one profile. That merge assumes that the three observations describe the same stable preference. STONIC tests this assumption on 5,144 situations from four banks and 35 fixed model configurations. It compares responses rated in isolation, choices made under counterbalanced conflict, spontaneous answers, and later choices between a model's own answer and authored alternatives. 10 of 17 configurations with usable behavioral data preserve the endorsement-choice relation across banks. Every one of the 17 eligible configurations prefers its own earlier answer (median effect 0.790), although option position changes the choice rate in every eligible configuration. Profile shape transfers most strongly from ratings to conflict choices and weakens for spontaneous text. Three-way annotation of 200 L3 responses provides a task-local check of the semantic audit: FULCRA agrees most closely with the human majority, while DeBERTa retains useful rank information after calibration. Hidden states encode the completed decision more clearly than the prompt alone. Thus the models show reproducible behavioral continuity, but the evidence does not support one scorer-independent value identity across interfaces.

</details>


### [320] [A Comprehensive Analysis of Arabic Natural Language Processing Research: Trends, Topic Evolution, and Research Gaps -- A Bibliometric and Topic-Based Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23421)
*Mullosharaf K. Arabov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文对1960-2026年间7120篇阿拉伯语NLP论文进行了大规模的计量和主题分析，揭示了领域发展趋向和研究空白，并提出改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管近年来阿拉伯语自然语言处理（NLP）领域迅速发展，但缺乏全面的定量元分析。研究旨在填补该空白，系统梳理领域发展图谱、研究热点以及存在的不足。

Method: 作者收集了六大数据库中7120篇阿拉伯语NLP论文，利用BERTopic进行主题建模，用回归分析探索被引量的影响因素，并用社会网络分析和地理映射揭示合作结构和分布。

Result: 发现2020年后受Transformer模型和大语言模型推动，论文数量激增；主题建模识别出19个子领域，最强为文本、语音、翻译与识别；论文被引与发表时间正相关，paper被OpenAlex、Semantic Scholar收录、及所在机构对被引量影响显著；沙特、美国、埃及产出最多；总结/摘要任务在马格里布、伊拉克、苏丹等方言下严重缺乏研究。

Conclusion: 本项工作补全了阿拉伯语NLP领域定量分析空白，建议未来关注欠资源方言、发展本土化的能力评测基准，从而推动领域均衡和可持续发展。

Abstract: Natural Language Processing (NLP) has grown rapidly over the past decade, driven by digital transformation in the Arab world, social media, and large language models (LLMs). Despite this growth, a comprehensive quantitative meta-analysis of the field remains absent. This study presents a large-scale bibliometric and topic-based analysis of 7,120 Arabic NLP papers published between 1960 and 2026, sourced from six collections. We employ BERTopic for topic modeling, regression analysis to identify citation predictors, social network analysis for co-authorship structures, and geographic mapping. Our findings show a significant publication surge after 2020, driven by transformer models and LLMs. Topic modeling identifies 19 substantive themes, the largest centered on text, speech, translation, and recognition. Citation analysis reveals a positive correlation between paper age and citations (r = 0.245, p < 0.001); regression shows that indexing in OpenAlex or Semantic Scholar and institutional affiliation are associated with higher citation counts. Saudi Arabia, the United States, and Egypt lead in research output. A task-dialect gap matrix identifies critical understudied areas, including summarization for Maghrebi, Iraqi, and Sudanese dialects. The largest topic has the highest H-index (87), followed by sentiment analysis (54). Our quantitative approach complements existing qualitative surveys and offers recommendations to prioritize under-resourced dialects and develop culturally aligned benchmarks for Arabic NLP.

</details>


### [321] [How Useful are LLMs for Grammar Engineering? Cantonese ParGram Resources and Controlled Experimental Evaluation with English Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23448)
*Chit-Fung Lam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文介绍了新的粤语ParGram语法资源，并评估了大型语言模型（LLMs）在知识驱动语法工程中的能力及局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于知识的语法工程需要高效、自动化的工具来辅助专家工作，特别是在资源较为稀缺的粤语领域，因此探索LLMs在该领域的应用潜力具有重要意义。

Method: 以新开发的粤语ParGram资源为金标准，并以英语为对照，考察OpenAI的gpt-oss-120b和GPT-5.4模型在多种提示条件下，能否根据句子或目标形式结构生成可机器处理的语法规则。实验系统评估了两种模型在不同输入条件下的表现。

Result: GPT-5.4总体表现优于gpt-oss-120b，从目标形式结构生成的语法一般比从原始句子生成的语法效果更好。两种模型虽然能生成局部合理的短语结构规则、词汇条目和模板，但在多构式设置下难以协调各类形式约束。

Conclusion: 当前LLMs可辅助语法开发中间阶段，但其生成内容仍需人类语言学专家分析、验证和完善。此外，本文贡献了新的粤语符号语法资源。

Abstract: This paper presents new Cantonese ParGram resources and evaluates LLMs for knowledge-driven grammar engineering within a controlled experimental paradigm. Using Cantonese ParGram resources as gold standards, with corresponding English baselines, we investigate whether OpenAI's gpt-oss-120b and GPT-5.4 can generate machine-processable grammars from sentences and target formal structures under systematically varied prompting conditions. GPT-5.4 outperformed gpt-oss-120b, while grammars generated from target formal structures generally outperformed those generated from sentences. Although both models could generate locally plausible phrase-structure rules, lexical entries, and templates, they often struggled to coordinate interacting formal constraints, especially in multi-construction settings. The results characterize both the capabilities and limitations of current LLMs for potential integration into AI-assisted expert workflows: LLMs may support intermediate stages of grammar development, but human linguistic expertise remains central to analysis, validation, and refinement. The study also contributes new Cantonese symbolic grammatical resources.

</details>


### [322] [What's the Catch? Evaluating Temporal Consistency in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23474)
*Marek Hradil,Danae Sánchez Villegas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对视觉-语言模型（VLMs）时序一致性能力的评测方法，并发现模型对单帧异常检测有效，但对时序异常几乎无感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前VLMs在图像/视频任务中表现优异，但其是否具备对时序结构的把握能力尚不明确。

Method: 将时序定位问题转化为异常检测，通过交换相邻帧和引入高斯噪声制造两种异常（时序与帧级），提出TimeCatch基准，并在人类和VLMs测试的多个数据集上评测。

Result: VLMs能较好检测和定位帧级异常，但对时序异常检测几乎等同随机水准，在定位上仅略优于随机；而人类在两项任务上表现几近完美。模型容量、感知、召回等都难以完全解释VLMs的不足。

Conclusion: 当前VLMs的时序结构建模能力较弱，难以整合多帧信息进行时序一致性推断，TimeCatch为此类能力评估提供了新的可控基准。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong performance on video and image-sequence benchmarks, yet it remains unclear whether they capture temporal structure. To study this question, we formulate temporal grounding as an anomaly detection problem, providing a simple and controlled evaluation that directly tests sensitivity to temporal consistency. We introduce TimeCatch, where temporal anomalies are created by swapping consecutive frames and frame-level anomalies by replacing a frame with Gaussian noise. Models are evaluated on anomaly detection and localization tasks across four synthetic and real-world datasets, alongside a human study. Our evaluation reveals a substantial gap between frame-level and temporal anomaly detection. While VLMs consistently detect frame-level anomalies and often localize them accurately, they perform near chance on temporal anomaly detection and only modestly above chance on localization. Humans, in contrast, achieve near-ceiling performance on both tasks. Additional analyses across model scales, prompting strategies, sequence lengths, and visual similarity suggest that these failures cannot be explained solely by limitations in perception or model capacity. Together, these findings indicate that current VLMs can identify anomalies within individual frames but struggle to integrate information across frames to reason about temporal consistency. TimeCatch provides a controlled benchmark for evaluating temporal grounding in vision-language models.

</details>


### [323] [On the Threat Model of Weird Generalization and Emergent Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23476)
*Miriam Wanner,Mark Dredze,William Walden*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 细粒度微调小型领域数据集会导致模型出现广泛且出乎意料的行为变化，被称为“奇异泛化”（WG）。本研究探讨了引发WG所需的数据特征及评价敏感性。结果显示WG与数据构成、语言等因素关系密切，对新颖性及评价集敏感，推断WG更像可控的对抗性风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究发现小规模、定制微调能在某些场景下极大改变大模型行为，但尚不清楚哪些数据特征会导致“奇异泛化”现象，因此作者试图揭示造成WG的关键数据因素，并评估WG测度的可靠性。

Method: 作者系统性考察了数据集规模、构成、语言、表现风格、新颖性等特征，并分析了不同评测集对WG测度的敏感性。通过在三种开源模型和四个数据集上实验，比较各种情况下WG的表现差异。

Result: 实验发现：（1）WG主要受数据集构成和语言影响，规模影响较小；（2）微调数据越接近于预训练语料时，WG体现越明显；（3）WG测度高度依赖于所选评测集。

Conclusion: WG现象高度依赖于训练和评价集的脆弱属性，因此WG更可能是在精心设计的数据攻防中出现的对抗性风险，而非常规微调过程中的普遍隐患。

Abstract: Narrow fine-tuning on small, domain-specific datasets can produce broad and surprising changes in model behavior-a phenomenon called weird generalization (WG). Yet, it remains unclear what features of the fine-tuning data are necessary for WG to arise. Here, we address this question by investigating a range of plausibly relevant features, including dataset size, composition, language, presentation style, and novelty relative to a model's parametric knowledge. Further, since WG evaluations rely on small question sets that assess the extent of the generalization, we also analyze how sensitive this measurement is to the set of questions used. Experiments with three open-weight models on four datasets show that the degree of WG (1) depends heavily on dataset composition and language (more than on size); (2) is greater for data familiar from pretraining than for novel data; and (3) is sensitive to the set of evaluation questions used. Collectively, these results indicate that WG is a product of quite fragile properties of both training and evaluation data. As such, we argue that WG is more plausible as an adversarial threat-requiring careful data engineering-rather than as a significant hazard inherent to routine fine-tuning.

</details>


### [324] [When Names Cross Scripts: A Source-Grounded Benchmark for Historical Entity Reconciliation in the Mongol World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23507)
*Xiang Chen,Zeyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MHER基准，用于蒙古历史人物名的配对消歧任务，发现正确的证据溯源能极大提高识别准确率，并揭示历史身份消歧不仅依赖于名字表面相似，还要结合上下文和证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史人物名字跨语言、跨书写系统并存，同名异人问题突出，单纯字符串匹配或音译难以准确识别，急需更精准的历史身份消歧方法与评测基准。

Method: 作者构建了MHER数据集，包括只含人名的核心集（396对，84人）及更严格基于来源证据的子集（160对），开发与测试各自独立。对五种生成模型测试不同证据输入（仅名、上下文、溯源证据）的识别效果，分析不同信息对配对准确率的影响。

Result: 引入正确的证据溯源信息比只用人名大幅提升对测准确率（提升12.96到94.44个百分点）；对五组同名异人案例，模型仅凭名字全部失效，但有溯源证据时几乎都能准确区分。同样发现，只加入名字对个别模型反而导致错误匹配。

Conclusion: 历史人物身份消歧不仅要比对姓名，还要结合可靠的历史证据。MHER为研究证据利用、模型失效与拒判提供了高质量的受控框架，对推动历史自然语言处理具有重要意义。

Abstract: Historical people may appear under different languages, scripts, and transcription traditions, while distinct individuals may share highly similar or even identical names. This makes historical identity reconciliation more than a problem of string matching or transliteration. We introduce MHER, a provenance-controlled benchmark for pairwise reconciliation of person-name attestations from the Mongol world. MHER contains a balanced 396-pair Name-only core over 84 primary historical persons and a stricter 160-pair Source-grounded subset constructed from mention-by-source evidence, with entity-disjoint development and test splits.
  Across five generative systems, correctly Source-grounded evidence improves paired TEST accuracy by 12.96 to 94.44 percentage points relative to Name-only input. On five identical-surface different-person cases, all models fail under names alone (0/25 model-item decisions), whereas Source-grounded evidence yields 24/25 correct resolutions, with the remaining output an abstention. Context-only ablations show that historical descriptions often carry substantial identity information, while explicitly signaled misgrounding controls produce substantially lower performance. We also find that names are not uniformly beneficial: for Qwen3-8B, restoring surface forms converts ten otherwise correct Context-only distinctions into false identity merges.
  These results show that historical entity reconciliation depends not only on surface correspondence, but on whether identity judgments respond appropriately to provenance-controlled historical evidence. MHER therefore provides a controlled framework for studying evidence use, abstention, and failure modes in historical NLP.

</details>


### [325] [ConvergeFlow: Language Flow with Provable Convergence to Token Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23551)
*Na Li,Yuchen Jiao,Changxiao Cai,Gen Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的基于流的语言模型ConvergeFlow，通过约束数据预测器在token embedding的凸包内，有效解决了以往连续模型不能直接输出有效token embedding的问题，在无需交叉熵（CE）训练解码器的情况下实现了直接预测token。实验证明其与已有方法表现竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续扩散和流式LM虽性能提升，但token生成需依赖用CE训练的解码器，因为流轨迹可能不能落在有效embedding上，限制了纯连续方法的端到端能力。为解决这一限制，作者提出新范式。

Method: 提出ConvergeFlow，通过将数据预测器局限于所有token embedding组成凸包，直接用flow matching下的均方误差(MSE)目标训练预测器，无需CE损失。此外设计三种采样机制调控生成困惑度与熵之间的平衡。并从理论上证明流过程最终将收敛到有效token embedding。

Result: 在OpenWebText数据集上，ConvergeFlow取得了与当前最优秀的连续和离散扩散语言模型相当的生成性能，并且无需CE训练的解码器。

Conclusion: ConvergeFlow充分展现了全新流式范式在语言建模方向的潜力，绕开了以往方法流轨迹不落在有效token embedding上的瓶颈，并实现了直接高效的token生成。

Abstract: Recent advances in continuous diffusion and flow-based language models (LMs) have achieved performance competitive with discrete LMs. However, existing continuous frameworks still rely on decoders supervised with cross entropy (CE) because the flow trajectories are not guaranteed to terminate at valid token embeddings. Motivated by this limitation, we introduce \textbf{ConvergeFlow}, an embedding-space flow-based LM, which constrains the data predictor to the convex hull of token embeddings and trains it solely with the mean squared error objective induced by flow matching. Under suitable regularity conditions, we prove that the resulting flow converges to valid token embeddings despite errors in the data predictor, enabling direct token prediction without a CE-supervised decoder. We further develop three sampling mechanisms for controlling the trade-off between the generative perplexity and entropy. Experiments on OpenWebText demonstrate that ConvergeFlow achieves performance competitive with existing continuous and discrete diffusion LMs. These findings demonstrate the potential of the flow-based paradigm for language modeling. Our code is available at https://github.com/Na-Li66/ConvergeFlow.

</details>


### [326] [SWE Refactor Bench: Can Coding Agents Complete a Long-Horizon, Whole-Repository Stack Migration?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23564)
*Deyao Hong,Yizhe Chi,Wenyi Li,Xiaoqiu Wang,Mingju Gao,Kaisen Yang,Bingxiang He,Youjie Zheng,Calvin Xiao,Qinhuai Na*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了SWE Refactor Bench基准，用于系统性地评估编程代理在全库迁移技术负债任务上的能力，结合了对迁移完成度和行为正确性的严谨考核。研究发现现有智能体尚无法实现完美迁移，并揭示迁移能力与行为正确性是不同维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件系统长期发展过程中积累了大量技术债务，使得全库迁移变得复杂、耗费巨大。目前的代码智能体在修复bug方面进展明显，本文关注它们是否能自主实现高质量、自动化的迁移。但现有基准只关注功能正确性，容易被简单地“复制原实现”作弊，这种局限需要通过新的基准加以解决。

Method: 作者提出了SWE Refactor Bench，包含20个实际全库迁移任务，覆盖4类技术债务。构建了三阶段评价标准：（1）迁移审核，检查是否真的完成迁移；（2）固定测试集上的行为测试；（3）由六个独立代码智能体生成针对性测试，发现隐藏的不同行为。评估涵盖了8个前沿模型、不同配置，共520次实验。

Result: 在520次实验中，仅有28次（5.4%）通过所有考核，20个任务中13个无人完成。最佳模型（claude-opus-5）得分为47/100。在340个通过迁移审核的实验中，58%能通过99%的测试，但仅26%能全部通过。不同迁移类型上，智能体表现差异明显，如构建工具重构平均得分31.4，而语言重写仅为5.6。

Conclusion: 迁移任务中的行为正确性和迁移完整性并非同一能力，当前的代码智能体尚难胜任高质量的自动化全库迁移。SWE Refactor Bench为代码智能体的研发和评估提供了严格的标准和测试平台，对推动可靠自动化迁移工具的研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: Modern software systems accumulate technical debt over decades of development, which makes migration expensive and largely manual. As coding agents become increasingly capable at bug fixing, can they autonomously perform such migrations? Existing benchmarks cannot answer this question because they evaluate only behavioural correctness, not whether the migration actually occurred. This leads an easy hack: agents copy the original implementation to make tests pass. We call this Blindness. To address this problem, we introduce SWE Refactor Bench, a benchmark comprising 20 whole-repository migrations, covering 4 kinds of technical debt. A three-stage evaluation protocol measures both migration completeness and behavioural correctness. (1) Migration Audit verifies that the migration occurred. (2) Behavioural Tests measure correctness with a fixed test suite. (3) Agentic Verification uses 6 independent coding agents to generate targeted tests for hidden behavioural differences. Across 520 runs from 8 frontier models and 26 model-effort configurations, only 28 of 520 runs ($5.4\%$) pass all three stages, 13 of the 20 tasks receive no accepted solution, and the best model (claude-opus-5) scores $47.0/100$. Migration completeness and behavioural correctness are distinct abilities: a few runs preserve behaviour by skipping the migration and are stopped at Migration Audit; most attempt it and break behaviour, and are stopped at Behavioural Tests. Agents cannot deliver a perfect migration: among the 340 runs that pass Migration Audit, $58\%$ reach $99\%$ of the fixed checks, yet only $26\%$ reach $100\%$. Agent capability differs across migration categories: agents score $31.4$ on build toolchain rewrites but only $5.6$ on language rewrites. Together, these findings position SWE Refactor Bench as a rigorous testbed for developing coding agents for reliable whole-repository migrations.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [327] [RoboShape: Information-Theoretic Point Cloud Representations for Privacy-Aware Robot Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21380)
*Oguzhan Baser,Mirac Sozen,Kaan Kale,Sandeep Chinchali,Sriram Vishwanath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种能够兼顾压缩率和隐私保护的新型点云编码方法RoboShape，可高效保留必要信息同时抑制敏感属性泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人在现实环境中应用日益广泛，采集并共享的点云数据常常意外暴露空间隐私（如房间功能等），目前缺乏对点云中敏感信息有原则性的保留/抑制方法。

Method: RoboShape是在冻结的Sonata编码器后端添加的压缩头，通过信息论中的相互信息指标进行投影，使得嵌入向量最大化与物体理解相关的信息，同时最小化与隐私属性相关的信息；技术上采用了Donsker-Varadhan公式化的互信息约束。

Result: RoboShape大幅压缩嵌入（减小87.5%），几乎不损失对象分类能力（保留98.7%），但对敏感属性的预测能力降低39.3%，实验在三个现实室内LiDAR数据集上有效。

Conclusion: RoboShape实现了高效、隐私保护的点云嵌入表达，便于网络传输和下游任务训练，并已开源，是构建紧凑、隐私感知机器人感知系统的实用工具。

Abstract: With the increased adoption of robotic agents operating in human environments by scanning and sharing 3D representations (e.g., for fleet learning, cloud-based planning, or collaborative mapping), collected point clouds reveal not just the objects in a scene but also sensitive spatial context, such as room function or information that occupants never consented to disclose. Traditional point cloud encoders offer no principled control over this: either all is preserved, or none. Hence, we introduce RoboShape, an information theory guided compression head following the frozen {\tt Sonata} encoder. We project voxel-level embeddings using the Donsker-Varadhan formulation of mutual information (MI). Specifically, we maximize the MI between embeddings and object-level understanding while minimizing it for private attributes. RoboShape leads to 87.5\% smaller embeddings that retain 98.7\% of object classification utility while collapsing sensitive attribute predictions by 39.3\% across the three real-world indoor LiDAR datasets. Its privacy-preserving embeddings are cheaper to transmit over the network or to train a model for any downstream tasks. We release the RoboShape codebase to give the robotics community a practical, encoder-agnostic tool for building perception pipelines that are compact, privacy-aware, and deployment-ready.

</details>


### [328] [Multimodal-Language-Model-Driven Interaction and Companionship for Service Robots in Elderly-Care Facilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21387)
*Ching-Chieh Liu,Cong-Thanh Vu,Yen-Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成多种功能的智能陪护机器人系统，能够实现连续陪伴、自然交互以及安全监测，既能追踪陪护老人，又能通过大模型进行语音交互和跌倒检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着服务机器人在养老机构的应用增长，现有方案多只关注单一任务，缺乏对持续陪伴、自然交流以及安全监测等多功能的深度融合，难以真正减轻护理人员负担、提升护理质量。

Method: 系统分为感知层和交互层。感知层利用视觉跟踪和主动云台保持用户可见；交互层通过大语言模型（LLM）理解语音请求并执行相应任务，如陪同、导航等；同时安全代理基于视觉-语言模型（VLM）实时检测跌倒和异常姿态并进行紧急响应。

Result: 实验结果显示，该系统能够可靠跟随和与人交流，并有效检测跌倒等潜在危险，提升使用者安全保障。

Conclusion: 本研究实现了服务机器人多功能融合，突破了以往只单一关注服务任务的局限。系统验证了在实际养老环境下的有效性和安全性，为智能陪护机器人的应用推广提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: Service robots are increasingly deployed in elderly-care facilities to alleviate caregiver workload and enhance the quality of daily care. However, most existing studies focus on isolated service functions and lack integrated capabilities for continuous companionship, natural interaction, and safety monitoring. In this paper, we present an intelligent companion robot system that unifies active visual human-following, real-time LLM-driven speech interaction for intent understanding and task execution, and VLM-based safety monitoring for fall detection and abnormal posture assessment. The perception layer ensures robust human tracking and uses an active gimbal to maintain the user in view during occlusions or abrupt movements. At the interaction layer, a Large Language Model interprets spoken requests and maps them to robot actions, enabling escorting and semantic navigation. Simultaneously, a VLM-based safety agent continuously analyzes visual observations to detect fall-related or abnormal postures and triggers emergency responses when necessary. Experimental results demonstrate the system's ability to reliably follow and interact with humans, while effectively detecting potential falls to ensure user safety.

</details>


### [329] [Gimbal-Based Human Tracking for Companion Robots Using Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21388)
*Cong-Thanh Vu,Ching-Chieh Liu,Yen-Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于云台摄像头的移动机器人实时、连续的无穿戴标签人体跟踪方法，并通过持续学习提升ReID性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人体跟踪方法依赖穿戴标签或固定摄像头，受视野和应用场景限制，易因目标离开感知范围而跟踪失败，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 采用安装在移动机器人上的可控云台摄像头主动保持目标在人视野内，并引入持续学习方案以提升人物重识别对外观和环境变化的适应能力，实现稳定、长期的人体跟踪。

Result: 实验显示该系统可显著提升跟踪的稳定性和连续性，支持从步行为至奔跑等多状态的人体重识别和实时反馈。

Conclusion: 所提方法无需穿戴标签，增强互动自然性和用户舒适度，可为伴侣机器人等场景提供更可靠的人体跟踪支持。

Abstract: Reliable and continuous human tracking is essential for natural human-robot interaction, particularly for companion robots. However, many existing approaches rely on wearable tags or fixed cameras with limited fields of view, which reduces system flexibility and often causes tracking failures when the target moves outside the sensing range. In this paper, we present a human tracking approach based on a gimbal-mounted camera integrated into a mobile robot.
