By actively controlling the gimbal mechanism, the camera can dynamically adjust its viewing direction to maintain the target within the field of view, even under substantial relative motion between the robot and the human. Furthermore, a continual learning strategy is applied to the person re-identification (ReID) task to adapt to changes in appearance and environmental conditions during long-term tracking. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed system significantly improves the stability and continuity of human tracking, enables real-time re-identification, and provides responsive feedback for reliable tracking of human motion from walking to running. User studies further indicate that the proposed approach enhances user comfort by eliminating the need for wearable tags.

</details>


### [330] [On the Optimized Use of Non-Orthonormality Constraints for the Quasi-Static INS Alignment of Autonomous Underwater and Surface Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21390)
*Carlos Renato C. Durao,Felipe O. Silva,Itzik Klein,Vinıcius M. G. B. Cavalcanti,Adriano Frutuoso,Ettore A. de Barros,Jay A. Farrell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了提升惯性导航系统初始对准速度与精度的新方法，将传统的粗略与精细对准方法加以优化，显著缩短对准时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统惯导系统初始对准流程存在收敛速度慢与惯性传感器偏置补偿能力有限的问题，对于水下及水面自主航行器的快速启动和高精度工作形成制约。

Method: （1）将新提出的带粗略偏置估计的TRIAD方法(TRIAD-CBE)用于粗略对准阶段；（2）在精对准阶段，提出加入非正交误差约束（NON）的新型扩展卡尔曼滤波(EKF)观测模型，使非正交误差可用于直接估算惯导传感器的偏置。

Result: 通过大量蒙特卡洛仿真和不同等级IMU实测，验证了新方法可将系统对准与偏置估计的收敛时间由原来的数分钟级大幅缩短到秒级，同时保持与传统方法相当甚至更优的精度。

Conclusion: 提出的方法有效提升了惯性导航系统初始化效率和传感器偏置估计速度，为水下及水面自主航行平台的快速高精度应用提供了技术支撑。

Abstract: Inertial navigation systems are specialized navigation apparatuses that equip almost all autonomous underwater and surface vehicles. They require precise initial alignment, i.e., determination of their initial attitude, which is typically achieved: (a) in quasi-static conditions (whenever possible); and (b) in two stages: Coarse Alignment (CA), using methods like TRI-axis Attitude Determination (TRIAD), and Fine Alignment (FA), via Zero Velocity Update (ZVU)-based Extended Kalman Filtering (EKF). However, conventional methods suffer from slow convergence and limited bias estimability. In response, this paper introduces: (a) an optimized version of a recently proposed CA method, namely, TRIAD with Coarse Bias Estimation (TRIAD-CBE); and (b) a novel FA EKF observation model that incorporates Non-Orthonormality (NON) error constraints derived from TRIAD, directly linking these errors to the inertial sensor biases. As validated through extensive Monte Carlo (MC) simulations, as well as real-world experiments using two Inertial Measurement Units (IMUs) of different grades, our approaches substantially accelerate the convergence of misalignment and bias estimates (from minutes to seconds), while maintaining accuracy/precision comparable to traditional techniques.

</details>


### [331] [ODG-NoMaD: Overhead-Camera Direction-Guided NoMaD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21395)
*Blossom Treesa Bastian,Keerthi S. Shetty,Manish Kolachalam,Rani Malhotra,Ashish Dutta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ODG-NoMaD方法，在无需重新训练的情况下，为NoMaD视觉导航算法的探索模式提供全局引导能力，实现更加高效的无目标探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有的NoMaD只能在未知环境中无指导地随机探索，缺乏全局感知，导致导航效率低且难以有效到达目标。本文旨在为其探索提供全局路径引导，提高探索效率和安全性。

Method: 在机器人部署时，使用一次性的顶视深度摄像头建立占用地图并规划全局路径，然后分段获得期望方向。机器人自身的逐帧可通行性地图进一步细化为无碰撞方向。最终在采样轨迹的去噪步骤中注入方向梯度，引导探索轨迹向目标旋转，同时保留多样性。无需对原导航策略重新训练。

Result: 在有/无随机障碍的模拟办公环境中，ODG-NoMaD实现了比无引导探索低一个量级的剩余目标距离，优于NaviDiffusor的点目标成本引导，并且是唯一在每次实验中都能避免碰撞的方法。

Conclusion: ODG-NoMaD显著提升了NoMaD的导航效率和安全性，为无目标探索提供了高效且无需重新训练的全局引导方案。

Abstract: NoMaD [31] is a learned vision-navigation policy that unifies goal-conditioned navigation and exploration in a single goal-masked diffusion policy. In an unseen environment, however - where neither a goal image nor a topological map is available - it can only explore undirectedly, wandering without global awareness. We present ODG-NoMaD, which gives NoMaD's exploration mode a global sense of where to proceed, without retraining the policy. An overhead depth camera is used once on deployment to build an occupancy map and plan a global path, which is segmented to yield a desired heading; a per-frame traversability map from the robot's onboard depth then refines this into a collision-free direction. The gradient of a cosine direction cost is injected into the final denoising steps, rotating sampled trajectories toward this direction while preserving the multimodality of exploration. In simulated office environments with and without random obstacles, ODG-NoMaD reduces the residual distance to the target by up to an order of magnitude over unguided exploration, outperforms the point-goal cost guidance of NaviDiffusor [37], and is the only configuration that remains collision-free on every trial.

</details>


### [332] [Active Interaction-Aware Model Predictive Path Integral via Ego-Conditioned Generative Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21400)
*Khaled A. Mustafa,Mohamed-Khalil Bouzidi,Christian Schlauch,Ahmad Gazar,Nadja Klein,Joerg Reichardt,Javier Alonso-Mora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合自车行为预测与模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制的主动交互式自动驾驶规划框架，有效提升了车辆在复杂交通场景下的安全性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集交通环境中，自动驾驶车辆与周围交通参与者存在持续的相互影响，仅依赖被动预测难以保证安全与效率。因此，提出能主动推测“如果...会怎样”的交互感知规划方法。

Method: 方法上，将条件生成自回归模型用于预测自车每个备选动作下周围目标的多模态随机轨迹，并嵌入进MPPI控制框架中，通过嵌套采样方式，评价不同动作下的期望风险与碰撞概率，使车辆能“主动试探”并选择更优动作。

Result: 闭环仿真结果显示，该方法在安全性和效率上明显优于传统的“先预测后规划”与被动交互感知方法。

Conclusion: 主动交互式行为规划，通过集成条件生成模型与高效控制算法，为自动驾驶在复杂场景下带来了更高的决策能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Dense traffic is inherently interactive. The ego vehicle and surrounding agents continuously influence each other's reactions, making "what-if" reasoning essential for safe and efficient driving. To enable such an active interaction-aware behavior, we propose a planning framework that integrates an ego-conditioned generative autoregressive prediction model within Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control. The generative prediction model outputs stochastic, multi-modal predictions of surrounding agents conditioned on each of the ego's considered future actions. A nested sampling scheme enables tractable evaluation of expected cost and collision risk under the induced distribution. This formulation allows the ego to actively probe how different candidate actions shape the interaction outcomes and to identify actions that reduce ambiguity in uncertain interactions. Closed-loop simulations demonstrate improved safety and efficiency compared to conventional predict-then-plan and passive interaction-aware approaches.

</details>


### [333] [Selective Cross-View Consistency for World Action Models: Held-Out Viewpoint Robustness Without Test-Time Camera Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21402)
*Bingqi Huang,Bingchuan Wei,Yingkai Cai,Zhaokui Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的训练方法Selective cross-view consistency（SCVC），用于提升World Action Models (WAMs) 在不同相机视角（viewpoints）下的泛化和鲁棒性，尤其针对控制策略输出的一致性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAMs能够同时预测未来的视频帧和机器人动作，但在遇到相机视角变化时鲁棒性较差。因此，亟须研究如何通过合理的训练损失函数（consistency loss）设置，提升其在未见过视角下的泛化能力。

Method: 作者研究了在训练时使用跨视角配对图片施加consistency loss的作用点：WAM输出分为与视角相关（covariant）的未来场景、与视角无关（invariant）的动作等。理论证明将损失错误地施加在与视角相关的内容上，会抑制特有内容，故SCVC方法只约束不变部分，不需要额外的相机信息。文中还提出新的评价方法，并进行了控制实验。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus任务和超过训练范围的视角上，SCVC比常规配对训练方法在机器人闭环控制上提升成功率12.2~15.5个百分点。同时，该方法并不影响训练分布内和内插情况下的表现，验证了其选择性的一致性损失对泛化有效。

Conclusion: Selective cross-view consistency（SCVC）是提升基于视觉输入的机器人控制模型泛化能力的有效方法，无需额外标注，也不会损害已有能力，能显著改善新视角下的表现。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) jointly denoise future video frames and robot actions, and the video prior is expected to generalize their control. Camera viewpoint change remains one of their hardest perturbation axes. We study a question specific to this model class: when training with same-state cross-view image pairs, on which output coordinates should a consistency loss be imposed? The WAM denoising target mixes view-covariant coordinates, namely the predicted future scene, with view-invariant coordinates, namely the action chunk, future proprioception, and value. We show that consistency applied to the covariant block is provably harmful, shrinking legitimate view-specific content to a fraction $1/(1+4λ)$ of its true value, and we verify this shrinkage law in controlled experiments. Selective cross-view consistency (SCVC) therefore constrains only the invariant block, requires no camera labels, extrinsics, depth, or view synthesis at training or test time, and leaves the deployment interface unchanged. We introduce a carve-and-hold-out evaluation protocol on the LIBERO-Plus camera track that separates a distribution-matched ceiling from genuine interpolation and extrapolation to held-out viewpoints, with a matched pair-trained control isolating the effect of the consistency term from pair exposure. On held-out orbital viewpoints beyond the training envelope, SCVC improves closed-loop success over the matched control by 12.2 points (95% CI [7.4, 17.0]; +15.5, CI [11.7, 19.4], under an independent second seed) -- an effect two further camera axes replicate -- while interpolation within the envelope shows no gain in either seed (-1.2 and -4.3 points) and in-distribution competence is preserved (-0.6, -0.2). We also report a cross-backbone audit showing that published camera-robustness numbers are confounded by wrist-camera pose stability.

</details>


### [334] [Tolerance-Dependent Inspection Disagreement Between a Fixed CMM and a Portable Articulated-Arm CMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21404)
*Md Manjurul Ahsan,Hamidreza Samadi,Shivakumar Raman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文对比了固定式CMM与便携式关节臂CMM在测量同一零部件任务时的差异，发现两者之间测量结果的平均差异不足以评估替换风险，提出了容差图用于识别需要重点验证的量测情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然固定式CMM和便携式臂式CMM都会用于相同的检测任务，但它们各自的精度规格并未说明更换仪器是否会影响零件的合格判定，因此需要研究实际替换对检验结果的影响。

Method: 作者对圆柱体、立方体和球体的特征，在20°C与30°C两种环境下分别用两类CMM进行多次测量，去除无效和信息不足的数据，重点分析了几何尺寸与形状误差数据的比较，并提取出两种仪器平均值距离名义值的区间及其与容差关系。

Result: 固定式CMM测量平均值比便携式关节臂CMM高约11.2μm，且四个形状误差数据均跨越10μm的容差界限。两者间的不一致区间平均宽度于20°C为6.573μm、30°C为4.995μm，说明在部分量测情形下两仪器会判为不同结果。

Conclusion: 仅凭两仪器的平均误差无法决定能否直接替换。文中提出的容差图方法，有助于识别哪些特征/容差组合在更换仪器后容易导致合格/不合格标签变化，强调实际替换前需针对这些情形开展等效性验证和特定测量不确定度评估。

Abstract: Fixed coordinate measuring machines (CMMs) and portable articulated-arm CMMs are often assigned to the same inspection task, but their nominal accuracy specifications do not show whether a change of instrument will preserve the disposition of a part. The question is not simply how far the two results differ, but whether that difference crosses the tolerance boundary. We examined this issue with recorded measurements of cylindrical, cubic, and spherical features under nominal 20 °C and 30 °C conditions. Repeated records and two roughness profiles without sufficient acquisition information were removed, leaving six dimensional and four form profiles. For each dimensional feature, the distances of the two system means from nominal define the exact tolerance interval in which the systems receive opposite direct labels. The fixed-CMM stream was approximately 11.2 μm higher than the articulated-arm stream at both conditions. All four form profiles fell on opposite sides of the recorded 10 μm upper limit. The dimensional disagreement intervals also overlapped strongly; their mean widths were 6.573 μm at 20 °C and 4.995 μm at 30 °C. The results clarify why an average difference between instruments is not, by itself, a measure of substitution risk. The proposed tolerance map identifies the feature-tolerance combinations for which instrument choice can change the recorded inspection label and, therefore, where a controlled equivalence study and a task-specific uncertainty budget are needed before substitution.

</details>


### [335] [Mamba-based Selective State Space Modeling Improves the Accuracy-Complexity Tradeoff of SmolVLA Vision-Language-Action Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21407)
*Farida Mohsen,Thowayba Elkaffash,Mohammad Reza Chalak Qazani,Mohamed Mabrok,Nader Meskin,Ali Safa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中，提出以Mamba的选择性状态空间建模替代SmolVLA模型中的自回归自注意力机制，以优化模型在动作推理频率与任务成功率之间的权衡，实验验证其在不同推理步长下的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在每次推理执行动作数目与计算复杂度之间存在权衡：单步推理保证准确，但计算开销大，难以实时部署；多步推理可实时，但成功率下降。作者希望突破这一瓶颈，提升长期推理下的系统表现。

Method: 将Mamba的选择性状态空间建模方法集成到SmolVLA模型的动作专家组件中，并与传统Transformer专家进行比较。分别在LIBERO基准任务下设定N=1、25、50三个动作推理步长，考察不同复杂度下的表现。

Result: Mamba专家在长推理步长（N=50、N=25）下明显优于Transformer，分别提升7.8%、3.7%的任务成功率。在每步重规划（N=1）场景下，与Transformer持平，但参数量减少24%。

Conclusion: Mamba专家能在减少模型复杂度的基础上，有效提升多步推理下VLA模型的成功率，实现更优的准确率与计算复杂度权衡，有助于VLA系统的实际实时部署。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models face a crucial tradeoff between their task success rate and the policy-call frequency. Executing a single action per inference ($N=1$) enables accurate robot control but comes at the cost of huge compute time overheads, making real-time implementation infeasible. On the other hand, executing longer action horizons before replanning ($N\gg1$) reduces compute complexity, but inevitably degrades the system's success rate. In order to improve the VLA accuracy-complexity tradeoff, this paper investigates Mamba's selective state-space modeling as an alternative to causal self-attention within the action expert of the popular SmolVLA model, widely used as a reference model for its highly accurate yet low complexity nature. We evaluate both the Mamba- and Transformer-based experts on the widely-adopted LIBERO benchmark suites across three execution horizons $N\!\in\!\{1,25,50\}$, respectively corresponding to high, moderate and low compute complexities. Our results remarkably show that the advantage of the Mamba expert increases with the execution horizon, indicating significant success retention under long execution horizons $N = 50$ and $N = 25$. When $N = 50$ actions are executed before replanning (i.e., corresponding to feasible real-time deployment), the Mamba expert outperforms the Transformer baseline by $7.8\%$. In addition, when $N = 25$ actions are executed before replanning, our Mamba expert outperforms the Transformer baseline by $3.7\%$. Finally, under per-action replanning ($N=1$), our Mamba variant matches the Transformer-based mean success rate while significantly reducing the overall model parameter complexity by $24\%$ thanks to Mamba's compute-efficient nature.

</details>


### [336] [Position: Robot Privacy as Embodied Boundary Work. Connecting Capabilities, Contexts, and Design Responses in Everyday Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21410)
*Liwen He,Shuning Zhang,Chengwen Zhang,Xin Yi,Chun Yu,Jihong Jeung,Xin Tong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了“具身边界隐私”作为分析机器人如何在实际互动中重塑隐私边界的理论框架，系统分析了机器人的七种能力和五种部署环境下的隐私风险，并探讨了相应的设计与治理建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人逐渐进入日常环境，其对隐私的影响已不仅限于数据收集和处理，还涉及空间、身体、社交与关系边界。然而，现有隐私理论多聚焦于数据或界面层面，无法完全解释机器人带来的新型隐私挑战。因此亟需有新的理论框架以理解和应对此类问题。

Method: 作者综合和扩展了以往关于机器人隐私的研究，结合不同的机器人具身能力和应用场景，提出了“具身边界隐私”框架，系统梳理了相关隐私风险，并从能力与语境两个维度分析机器人如何影响隐私边界。

Result: 基于该框架，作者总结了机器人在运动、朝向、接近、物体访问、远程在场和社会表达等行为方面对隐私的潜在影响，并提出了具体的隐私机制设计建议，如边界检查点、视点感知控制、远程在场披露、物体及身体级访问规则等。

Conclusion: 文章呼吁HRI（人机交互）领域在设计和治理中高度重视机器人的具身行为及其在不同语境下对隐私的影响，倡导将机器人行动纳入隐私相关考量，从而更有效地保护个人与群体隐私。

Abstract: Robots are increasingly entering everyday environments where privacy is shaped not only by data practices, but also by spatial, bodily, social, and relational boundaries. Their embodied capabilities allow them to reshape these boundaries through situated action, challenging privacy framings centered on data flows, interface settings, or one-time consent. Prior work has examined robot privacy through sensing, data collection, telepresence, transparency, consent, bystander awareness, and multi-stakeholder governance. Building on this work, we propose embodied boundary privacy as a capability-by-context framing for examining how physically present robots may reshape privacy boundaries in situated interaction. Specifically, this framing organizes privacy risks across seven robot capabilities and five deployment contexts, asking how embodied capabilities enable boundary crossings and how situated contexts shape who is affected, how these crossings are interpreted, and when they become contested. We use this perspective to outline design and research implications for embodied privacy mechanisms, including boundary checkpoints, viewpoint-aware sensing control, remote-presence disclosure, object- and body-level access rules, constraints on socially persuasive privacy influence, and local interruption rights. We encourage HRI research, design, and governance to treat robot movement, orientation, proximity, object access, remote presence, and social expression as privacy-relevant actions whose meaning depends on context.

</details>


### [337] [Social Graph Mamba: Forecasting Pedestrian Movements Based on Social Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21411)
*Hong-Son Nguyen,Yen-Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的行人运动预测方法 Social Graph Mamba (SGM)，以高效建模复杂人群中的社交互动，在标准预测基准和真实机器人实验中取得了优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有依赖 attention 机制的方法虽然预测效果好，但计算量大，难以扩展，同时在群体层面难以模拟真实场景下的人群自然行为，特别是群体结构。

Method: 方法上，用可动态构建的人群交互图替代传统的 attention 机制，用选择性状态空间模型（SSMs）在图结构上对交互进行推理；提出社交三元组分解机制序列式建模人群互动，并通过可微分 MinCut 优化发现群体结构，结合群体成员关系调整特征空间和多模态解码。

Result: 在 ETH/UCY、SDD 等标准数据集上，SGM 在维持线性复杂度的同时在表现上与 attention 方法相当甚至更优。此外，将 SGM 预测结果结合社交力模型应用于物理机器人实验，验证了其实用性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 SGM 能以更高效的计算实现复杂人群中真实运动行为的预测，兼顾精度与可扩展性，并可直接服务实际应用，比如机器人导航。

Abstract: Forecasting pedestrian motion has always been fundamental for autonomous navigation in crowded environments. While attention-based methods achieve strong performance, they suffer from quadratic computational complexity in modeling social interactions, limiting scalability. Additionally, the existing methods often achieve high accuracy on prediction benchmarks at the individual level, but fail to fully capture the natural movement behaviors of crowds in real-world scenarios, particularly group structures. In this study, we propose Social Graph Mamba (SGM), a novel architecture that replaces attention-based social reasoning with Selective State Space Models (SSMs) operating on dynamically constructed interaction graphs. SGM introduces a dynamic interaction graph with social triplet factorization to decompose crowd interactions sequentially, and a community-aware module to effectively discover group structures via differentiable MinCut optimization and conditions both the embedding space and multi-modal decoder on group membership. Our experiments on standard benchmarks (ETH/UCY, SDD) demonstrate competitive performance with linear sequence complexity compared to quadratic attention-based methods. We further validate SGM in physical robot experiments by integrating predicted trajectories into a Social Force Model (SFM) for real-world implementation.

</details>


### [338] [RiskWorld: Object-Centric Latent World Modeling for Autonomous Driving Risk Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21414)
*Jingzheng Li,Yufei Ge,Qianren Mao,Zhijun Chen,Bing Li,Xingyu Peng,Baochang Zhang,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了RiskWorld，一种面向对象的潜在世界模型，通过模拟各候选对象与自车的互动演变，直接定位自动驾驶场景中的风险来源，显著提升了风险识别准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶风险识别方法多为场景级事故预测，或通过自车行为间接推断风险对象，缺乏对预测自车-对象关系的直接利用，导致风险定位不够精确，因此需要更有效的风险对象识别机制。

Method: 作者提出RiskWorld模型，结合预训练视频表征和结构化的自车-对象历史数据，利用RSSM风格的潜在动力学模型，推演各对象相对于自车的未来状态，并输出对象级风险评分，同时辅以辅助的未来关系预测和时间风险预测，训练时利用已记录的未来数据进行监督，推理时仅用当前观察。

Result: 在RiskBench基准上，RiskWorld获得了63.0%的F1值和2.1%的最低虚警率。分析显示，该模型的推演能力可以在关键事件发生前捕捉到对象级风险演化，其风险对象筛选还能在观测被过滤时保留对规划关键的信息。

Conclusion: RiskWorld能更加精确和前瞻性地识别并定位自动驾驶过程中的高风险对象，提升了自动驾驶系统的安全性和决策有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous driving risk identification aims to determine which observed object is likely to become safety-critical to the ego vehicle. Existing approaches typically predict scene-level accidents, infer risk objects indirectly from ego behavior, or apply geometric checks after trajectory forecasting, without directly using predicted ego--object relations for risk-source localization. We propose RiskWorld, an object-centric latent world model that identifies risk from the imagined evolution of each candidate relative to the ego vehicle. RiskWorld combines pretrained predictive video representations with structured ego--object histories, contextualizes observed interactions, and rolls relation-aware object states into the future using RSSM-style latent dynamics. It decodes the rollout into object-level risk scores, supported by auxiliary future-relation and temporal-risk predictions. Inference uses only observations up to the current time, while logged futures provide training supervision. On RiskBench, RiskWorld achieves the best overall F1 of 63.0\% and the lowest false-alarm rate of 2.1\%. Further analyses show that the learned rollout captures the evolution of object-level risk before critical events, while RiskWorld's selections preserve planning-critical information under filtered observation.

</details>


### [339] [Operational digital twin clinics enable task-based evaluation of embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21416)
*Xinyuan Wu,Jingrao Zhang,Mengdi Xu,Henry K. Chu,Mingguang He,Danli Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用普通临床照片快速创建可编辑、可供机器人测试的数字孪生环境，实现对体感人工智能（embodied AI）在真实医院环境中的有效评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体感人工智能需在真实医院环境中评测，但传统建模方法造价高且难以扩展。如何低成本、高效率构建高度还原且可与机器人互动的虚拟临床场景，是推广AI医疗应用的关键难题。

Method: 用39张眼科门诊照片，将单张照片转换为可编辑、适合机器人仿真测试的环境。对重建场景在结构还原、设备交互、多机器人可行性和对扰动敏感性的适应等多个维度进行定量与定性分析，并比较不同机器人在同一场景中的操作表现。

Result: 重建出的场景结构准确、支持设备位置灵活变更。通过引入设备网格和碰撞检测技术，使得视觉重建场景也可进行物理接触仿真。三种机器人在同一任务下的可达性、接触可行性存在显著差异，场景微调对机器人的操作边界有具体影响。同时，数字孪生环境可支持策略学习和闭环测试。

Conclusion: 提出并验证了临床数字孪生用于体感AI评估的“操作有效性”原则。为AI医疗应用由线下开发到真实部署提供了关键的中间测试层，为机器人与复杂医疗环境的高效对接奠定基础。

Abstract: Embodied artificial intelligence (AI) must be tested in the clinical environments where it will operate, but building realistic, robot-testable settings is costly and difficult to scale. Here we show that routine clinic images can be transformed into operational digital twins for task-based evaluation of embodied AI. Using 39 ophthalmic clinic scenes, we converted single photographs into editable, simulator-ready environments and assessed reconstruction quality, room-scale geometry, mesh grounding, multi-robot feasibility, perturbation sensitivity and closed-loop policy performance. The reconstructed scenes preserved workspace structure, while local editing enabled controlled device reconfiguration. Device meshes, collision proxies and semantic anchors converted visual reconstructions into contact-aware simulation scenes. Across three robot embodiments, shared task targets showed different patterns of reachability and contact feasibility. Small device translations and rotations produced task-specific changes in contact margins that were not captured by visual similarity alone. Digital-twin trajectories also supported local policy learning and closed-loop evaluation. These findings establish operational validity as a key principle for clinical digital twins and provide an intermediate layer between offline development and physical deployment of embodied AI in healthcare.

</details>


### [340] [Evaluating Human and LLM-Generated Thematic Analysis in HRI for Vulnerable Populations: A Comparative and Ethical Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21420)
*Alva Markelius,Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Julie Bailey,Hatice Gunes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文比较了人工和大语言模型（LLM）在以弱势群体为对象的人机交互（HRI）研究中的主题分析（TA）结果，并探讨LLM生成的分析在伦理和有效性上的潜在风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在各类文本分析中的应用，研究者亟需评估其在敏感领域（如弱势群体相关HRI研究）中的适用性、安全性和伦理合法性。

Method: 本文对同一HRI弱势群体数据集，分别采用人工和LLM生成主题分析，并从客观和语义层面比对两者的主题一致性，进一步分析这些差异背后的系统性解释与伦理影响。

Result: 发现人工与LLM生成的主题在一定程度上存在分歧，并揭示了这些分歧可能源于LLM固有的解释框架，且部分偏差涉及对弱势参与者经验的误读或边缘化。

Conclusion: 在HRI敏感领域中，LLM协助下的主题分析需谨慎使用，研究者需关注其可能带来的伦理风险，避免弱势群体经验被曲解或忽略。

Abstract: Thematic analysis (TA) has long been regarded as an inherently human, reflexive, and interpretive process. However, the extent to which LLM-generated TA is appropriate for Human-Robot Interaction (HRI) research involving vulnerable populations remains largely unexamined and raises critical questions about validity and ethics, particularly in sensitive research contexts. This paper presents a comparative study of human- and LLM-generated TA in an HRI context with a focus on vulnerable populations. We evaluate both objective and semantic agreement between human- and LLMgenerated themes, and examine whether observed divergences reflect systematic interpretive patterns with ethical significance. Our analysis investigates whether LLM-generated TA risks marginalising or misrepresenting the experiences of vulnerable participants, with implications for researchers employing LLM-assisted TA in HRI.

</details>


### [341] [The Setting of IMU Parameters in Kalman Filtering-based Information Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21433)
*Qiang Hu,Yanhua Zou,Shuaiyi Huo,Haibo Ge,Wei Ouyang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Allan方差校准的惯性测量单元（IMU）参数设置方法，以提升在卡尔曼滤波框架下的参数调优准确性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IMU在传感器融合中的参数设置难以兼顾多变的工作环境，单纯的静态校准方法往往难以应对动态扰动，导致噪声和偏置参数选择依赖经验，难以系统化和自动化。

Method: 作者结合Allan方差和功率谱密度之间的关系，将Allan方差校准结果引入到卡尔曼滤波的过程噪声建模，提出了基于Allan方差的参数设置方法，并在两类典型IMU融合系统中进行了实验验证。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的方法能够实现更合理和有效的IMU参数设置，改善系统下的过程噪声建模，提高融合系统的实际性能。

Conclusion: 基于Allan方差的IMU参数设置方法在实际卡尔曼滤波融合系统中表现良好，有助于降低对经验的依赖，为IMU噪声和偏差参数的科学设置提供了新思路。

Abstract: The setting or tuning of specifications for the inertial measurement unit (IMU) is tricky in sensor fusion. The underneath conundrum is caused by the fact that the working condition of IMU is more complex than the stationary calibration scenario. Since the noises and biases instabilities calibrated under static condition cannot accommodate other cases, the effective tuning of IMU parameters largely hinges on the experience or profound understanding of the system. In the current work, the setting method of IMU parameters based on Allan variance calibration is delved into within the Kalman filtering framework. Specifically, the relationship between the power sepctral density and Allan variance is leveraged in formulating the process uncertainty in continuous-time filtering. Two typical IMU-based sensor fusion systems are considered to show the feasibility and effectiveness of this parameter setting process.

</details>


### [342] [Geo-VLA: Geometry-Aware Vision-Language-Action Planning via Internalization of Map Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21440)
*Ran Chen,Jiaxing Ren,Zhikun Zhang,Yunhao Hou,Junbao Zhuo,Bochao Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Geo-VLA框架，通过学习具备几何感知能力的视觉表示，提升视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在自动驾驶场景下的规划表现。引入了Geo-QA数据集，通过对比学习与指令微调方式，将道路几何信息注入到视觉-语言表示中。实验结果在NAVSIM v1上取得了92.1 PDMS，刷新了单摄像头VLA规划模型的最新记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型在复杂道路环境中由于仅依赖图像难以充分获取道路的几何与拓扑信息，导致决策与规划性能有限。为增强模型对道路结构的理解能力，需引入更具几何语义的视觉表示。

Method: 提出Geo-VLA框架。在训练阶段，融合几何地图语义，增强视觉特征中的道路结构表示，同时不需要高精地图或推理阶段的额外车道信息。支持方法的Geo-QA数据集，设计为注入道路几何信息到VLA表示的几何类问答数据，通过对比学习和指令微调优化模型。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1基准测试中，Geo-VLA显著提升了各种架构的VLA自动驾驶系统的决策与行动表现，获得92.1 PDMS分数，优于以往单摄像头VLA规划方案。

Conclusion: Geo-VLA有效强化了VLA模型对道路几何结构的建模能力，促进了更安全、准确的自动驾驶规划。无需高精地图等额外信息，实现了端到端、低成本部署，推动了自动驾驶VLA模型的实用性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have advanced end-to-end autonomous driving by leveraging foundation models for semantic reasoning and long-tail generalization. However, their planning performance remains limited in complex driving environments because image-only representations inadequately capture planning-relevant road geometry and topology. In this paper, we propose Geo-VLA, a plug-and-play framework that enhances VLA models by learning geometry-aware visual representations. During training, Geo-VLA internalizes geometric map semantics to strengthen road-structure representations, while requiring no HD maps or additional lane information during inference. To support this approach, we introduce Geo-QA, a geometry-focused question-answering dataset that injects road geometry into vision-language representations through contrastive learning and instruction tuning. Experiments on NAVSIM v1 demonstrate that Geo-VLA consistently improves VLA planners with distinct action-generation architectures, achieving 92.1 PDMS and establishing a new state-of-the-art among single-camera VLA planners.

</details>


### [343] [Constructing Predictive Surgical Path for AI-based Capsulorhexis Skill Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21441)
*Mohammad Javad Ahmadi,Hamid D. Taghirad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个利用深度学习平台，通过AI优化外科手术培训中学员操作路径，从而提升培训效果，并引入了新数据集和评价指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化外科医生培训能够减少手术培训的风险与成本。随着AI和外科数据的发展，AI辅助外科培训的前景广阔，为更好地支持学员提升，亟需建立AI平台优化其学习路径。

Method: 提出并构建了一个基于深度卷积神经网络的平台，使用JIGSAWS和新发布的ARAS-Farabi数据集，分析工具尖端运动轨迹。该系统通过专家轨迹特征建立参考模型，并引入双损失函数优化学员路径，既提升技能，又保留学员原有意图。

Result: 实验结果表明，通过AI辅助，学员的运动路径至少可提升20%，同时保持其操作意图。此外，论文还开发了用于验证学员进步程度的新指标。

Conclusion: 引入AI平台用于外科手术培训能有效提升学员技能，是自动化外科培训的重要一步。新平台和数据集为后续相关研究与临床应用奠定基础。

Abstract: Automated training of surgeons is one of the most crucial factors that significantly minimize surgical training risks and expenses. With recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI) knowledge and available data from various surgeries, AI's involvement in surgical training is becoming very promising. It is recommended that at the early stages of AI development, it interferes in the surgery as a third agent alongside the trainer. As trust in AI increases, this process will lead to an AI agent acting as a trainer in the future. The first phase in which AI can intervene in the training process is to suggest an improved surgical path to the trainer. A platform must be constructed in the first step, to accomplish this task and to enhance the movement path of trainee surgeons. This paper introduces this platform along with an annotated capsulorhexis surgery dataset called the ARAS-Farabi dataset. In this research, a deep convolutional neural network is pre-trained with JIGSAWS and ARAS-Farabi surgical datasets that can extract surgical skill characteristics from surgery tool tip motion data. The proposed platform develops a reference model from the feature space of an expert surgeon's movement trajectory and proposes an improved path to enhance the skill of a novice surgeon. An optimization with two loss functions is utilized to create a path that raises the skill level of the novice surgeon's path while simultaneously predicting and preserving his/her intent. The results of this study reveal that, with the assistance of an AI agent, the trainee surgeon's movement path can be enhanced by at least 20 percent while maintaining his intentional objective. In addition to the recommended deep network, various tangible indicators have also been developed in this research to verify the level of trainee improvement.

</details>


### [344] [Model-Free Adaptive Parameter Tuning for Efficient Multi-Robot Warehouse Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21533)
*Pratap Tokekar,Mouhacine Benosman,Rahul Chandan,Alexandre Ormiga Galvao Barbosa,Michael Caldara,Joseph W. Durham*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于极值点寻优控制（ESC）的自适应参数调优框架，可实时优化仓储机器人调度策略，提升出库效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多机器人调度在仓库操作中需权衡机器人路径、通道拥堵和存储效率，不同策略对吞吐量和拥堵的影响难以提前静态优化，且随需求变化而变化，手动调参繁琐且效果有限。

Method: 采用极值点寻优控制（ESC），通过对调度参数施加周期性微扰，根据实际吞吐量反馈，估算参数-性能梯度，实时调整参数以持续优化性能，无需依赖系统具体模型，适应多分钟级时延及复杂因果关系。

Result: 仿真验证表明，所提方法在多种地图和机器人数量情况下平均提升吞吐量5.0%，在动态运营条件下提升8.4%，优于静态参数策略。

Conclusion: 该方法免去了耗时的手动参数设置，实现了仓储调度参数的实时自适应，推动了仓库自动化运营的自调优发展。

Abstract: Robotic Fulfillment Centers (FCs) store inventory on shelves (pods) arranged in dense blocks. Retrieving a target pod that is buried deep in a block requires moving obstructing pods out of the way (i.e., digout). Multi-robot planners use parameterized cost functions to control digout behavior, producing a spectrum of strategies: at one extreme, obstructing pods are sent to other blocks (using more robots in travel lanes); at the other, pods are shuffled within the block (avoiding lane congestion but increasing extraction time). Each point on this spectrum has different downstream consequences for floor congestion and throughput. The optimal operating point depends on the specific facility configuration and shifts with operational conditions such as varying station demand and congestion patterns, making offline tuning impractical. We present an adaptive parameter tuning framework based on Extremum Seeking Control (ESC) that continuously adjusts planner parameters in response to measured throughput. ESC performs model-free optimization by perturbing parameters with sinusoidal dither signals and correlating perturbations with performance changes to estimate gradients, making it robust to the multi-minute delayed effects and credit assignment challenges inherent in large FC operations. Simulation studies demonstrate that the adaptive policy improves upon fixed policies across several conditions. We observe an improvement in throughput by an average of 5.0% across map and robot fleet size variations, and by 8.4% under dynamic operating conditions. This work eliminates manual parameter provisioning and enables real-time adaptation, providing a self-tuning paradigm for FC storage operations.

</details>


### [345] [GOLEM: Modular Humanoid Autonomy Towards Electric Vehicle Battery Disassembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21550)
*Max Conway,William Xie,Allen Devaraj,Yutong Zhang,Niraj Pudasaini,Mateo Feit,Adam Abid,Zachary Allen,Chen Liu,Xuan Tan,Jensen Lavering,Jason Chen,Lyle Antieau,Anthony Von Pischke,Alessandro Roncone,Zachary Sunberg,Nikolaus Correll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了GOLEM，一个用于电动汽车（EV）电池包拆解的开源系统架构，通过模块化设计，提升了人形机器人自动拆解EV电池的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前EV电池包的拆解工作主要由人工完成，过程枯燥且危险，急需自动化和标准化的系统来解决安全、高效拆解问题，降低人工成本与安全风险。

Method: 构建了GOLEM系统架构，将运动、操作、动态稳定、导航和空间记忆等功能分为独立模块，各自拥有抽象接口，便于方法开发、互换和对比。系统通过Docker和ROS 2实现，穆乔科和IsaacLab的数字孪生与真实机器人接口一致，支持模块组合和自定义，涵盖仿真到实际应用。

Result: GOLEM实现了模块化的人形机器人EV电池拆解任务，模块性能可逐步评价。实验表明，LiDAR-惯性导航能精准定位；学习型站立控制器优于基于采样的MPC；真实电池包抓取成功率随场景复杂性下降（固定97%，自由87%，带导航波动时37%）。

Conclusion: GOLEM证明了模块化、开放的人形机器人架构在复杂EV电池拆解场景下的实用性和公平性能对比能力，推动了机器人自动化拆解解决方案的研发。源码已开放，便于业界采纳与进一步研究。

Abstract: Disassembling end-of-life electric vehicle (EV) battery packs is dull and dangerous work, performed almost entirely by humans. We present GOLEM (Generalized Open Library of Embodied Modules), an end-to-end, open-source system architecture for EV battery disassembly with the Unitree H1-2 humanoid robot in which walking, manipulation, dynamic stability, navigation, and spatial memory are independent modules with abstract interfaces, so that methods are easily developed, interchanged, and compared. GOLEM is deployed as a Docker-based ROS 2 abstraction in which MuJoCo and IsaacLab digital twins expose interfaces matching the physical robot. GOLEM's composability and per-module customization enable development and demonstration of humanoid EV battery disassembly, from simulation to reality. GOLEM provides fair comparison between humanoid modules, enabling evaluation as a capability ladder, in which one module is characterized at a time and added as a rung: LiDAR-inertial navigation places the robot within 13.0cm of a 6m goal; a learned standing controller recovers from external disturbances that sampling-based lower-body MPC does not; and grasping loosened fasteners from a real Hyundai Ioniq 5 pack degrades from 97% tethered to 87% free-standing to 37% under navigation-induced pose variance. Source code is available at the project page https://golem-humanoid.github.io

</details>


### [346] [Model-Based Reinforcement Learning for Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Task Assignment Under Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21554)
*Daniel Garces,Sara Castro,Adrian Haimovich,Byron Crowe,Stephanie Gil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向异构多机器人任务分配的预测感知自适应rollout框架，兼顾计划内及实时到达请求。通过动态规划整合历史数据与实时分配，实现对未来需求与分配鲁棒、高效的调度。实际医疗场景测试表明该方法在服务完成率和响应时间上优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构多机器人服务系统需持续分配到达请求，历史数据可辅助预测未来需求，但过度依赖错误预测会导致性能下降，因此需要设计对预测误差鲁棒的调度方法。

Method: 将问题建模为有限时域随机动态规划，考虑机器人与任务兼容性、顺序服务要求、路径约束等。采用基于采样未来请求情形的动态rollout分配，在做即时决策时限制在已观测到的请求。为在线应用提升效率，引入剪枝候选、等待动作和基于交互的估价策略，并通过动态重加权应对预测误差及选择性重优化。还提出了基于历史数据的异构机器人队伍配置选择程序。

Result: 在真实医院护理任务分配实例中，该框架服务完成率接近100%，平均等候时间及尾部延迟显著优于现有反应式、令牌传递、基于预测位置和贪婪方法。改进最为显著体现在极端尾部等候时间指标。

Conclusion: 该框架在多机器人异构服务任务分配中兼顾了预测利用率和鲁棒性，有效提升了待服务响应和整体服务质量，尤其适合需求分布有变动的现实环境，可为相关智能调度系统设计与应用提供重要参考。

Abstract: Heterogeneous multi-robot service systems must assign requests to compatible robots, construct feasible schedules, and adapt as new tasks arrive online. Historical data can help anticipate future demand, but relying too heavily on inaccurate predictions can degrade performance under distribution shifts. We develop a prediction-aware adaptive rollout framework for heterogeneous multi-robot task assignment with scheduled and real-time requests. The problem is formulated as a finite-horizon stochastic dynamic program incorporating robot-task compatibility, ordered service requirements, routing constraints, service windows, and end-of-horizon return requirements. The proposed policy evaluates current assignments using sampled future request scenarios while restricting immediate commitments to requests already observed. To enable online use, the framework combines pruned candidate controls, wait actions, and an interaction-aware base policy for efficient future-cost estimation. Robustness to forecast error is provided by adaptively reweighting predicted requests based on recent prediction mismatch and selectively re-optimizing assigned but unstarted requests. We also introduce a historical-data-driven procedure for selecting the heterogeneous fleet composition before deployment. In a case study using real nursing-task requests from hospital inpatient floors, the proposed approach achieves near-complete service and reduces serviced-request wait times relative to reactive, token-passing, prediction-positioning, and myopic greedy baselines, with the largest improvements in tail-delay metrics.

</details>


### [347] [Betting for Sim-to-Real Performance Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21572)
*Yujia Chen,Bowen Weng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于赌注机制的模拟到真实（sim-to-real）性能证明框架，实现通过大量模拟实验指导对有限真实实验结果的性能区间估计，大幅收紧性能证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统的性能测试常受限于真实试验样本数量有限，导致性能区间（性能证书）往往非常宽泛，难以反映真实能力，亟需利用丰富的模拟资源提升区间估计精度。

Method: 提出以大规模模拟实验结果为“赌注”，在真实试验揭示前对其结果下注，再根据累计的赌注收益动态调整对模拟器的信任度，算法设计关联模拟器库、赌注方案与性能证书，并给出保证任何时刻都有效的概率证明及证书宽度优化原则。

Result: 实验涵盖合成分布和真实机器人，在标准复现与在线评估场景下，所提方法相比经典及最新方法将性能证书收紧51.6%±16%；在极小样本（≤30）条件下收紧32.26%±8%。

Conclusion: 提出的赌注式sim-to-real框架能高效利用模拟结果，理论上保障区间覆盖率，并显著提升有限样本下性能证书紧致性，为机器人性能评估带来新的高效解决思路。

Abstract: Consider a typical test of a robot system: one observes a sequence of outcomes concerning some aspect of interest (crash or no crash, tracking error, time to completion), and reports a mean (crash risk, average error, mean time to completion) and, more importantly, an interval guaranteed to contain that mean at a prescribed confidence, referred to as a performance certificate. Given expensive real-world trials, the sample size is therefore small, and the certificate is often loose. Now consider the same procedure, except that before each real outcome is revealed, the operator ``peeks'' at a large bank of simulated results, and places a bet on where the real outcome will land. As the real outcomes settle the bets, the operator gains or loses wealth. One's ``trust'' over simulators also shifts within the portfolio. This paper develops that idea into a sim-to-real betting certificate framework with three contributions: (i) An algorithm that links a scalable bank of simulators to effective bets, and the accumulated betting wealth to the certificate. (ii) A proof that the returned certificate is anytime valid, covering the true mean with the prescribed probability, using any simulator bank. (iii) The guaranteed wealth-regret bounds yield configuration principles for the proposed algorithm and simulator bank design to deliver tight certificates. Experiments across synthetic distributions and real-world robot tests, covering both replayed standardized testing outcomes and online runtime evaluation, show the proposed method narrows the certificate by $51.6\%\pm16\%$ against classic and state-of-the-art baselines, and by $32.26\%\pm8\%$ in the extremely limited-sample regime ($\leq30$ samples).

</details>


### [348] [Force/Torque-Based Kinematic Adaptation for Robotic Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21592)
*Carl Glen Henshaw*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种能够在线自适应估计未知工具运动学关系的方法，利用关节角和腕部力/力矩传感器数据，且无需对工具末端进行外部测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际的接触丰富型机器人操作任务中，机器人本体、手、工具之间的运动学关系经常未知且易变（如切换工具或抓取点），精确建模难度大。作者希望机器人能在执行任务时自动适应这种未知且变化的关系。

Method: 提出一种自适应在线估计算法，仅依赖关节角和腕部力/力矩传感器。推导了能保证稳定性的运动学自适应更新律，并分析了刚性和柔顺控制场景下的系统稳定性。进一步，将控制与估计问题表述为二次规划（QP），分析了QP的优缺点。最后，通过仿真peg-in-hole插入任务验证方法有效性。

Result: 理论推导证明估计算法的稳定性，并指出仅能识别被运动激发的方向（如刚性插入无法识别工具长度，柔顺控制能部分激发）。QP方法能够再现预测项但无法再现跟踪自适应项。仿真结果验证了方法在典型任务下的实用性。

Conclusion: 该方法为机器人操作任务中的在线运动学建模提供了新思路，尤其适用于工具和抓取点频繁变化的场景，是将操作任务学习与运动学自适应相互解耦的重要一步，为后续结合强化学习等自适应控制方法奠定了基础。

Abstract: Contact-rich robotic manipulation requires an accurate model of the kinematic relationship between a robot's joints and the task features it senses. This relationship is rarely known exactly: it changes with each tool the robot picks up and shifts, sometimes almost instantaneously, as contact modes change --- especially for multi-fingered hands that make and break contact at points that are not exactly prescribed, as in full-hand grasping. This paper develops an adaptive scheme that estimates that relationship online, using only joint-angle sensing and a wrist-mounted force/torque sensor, with no exteroceptive measurement of the tool tip. We derive a provably stable kinematic update law that identifies the kinematics of an unknown tool from force/torque feedback alone, and prove stability of both the rigid case and the case with a compliance controller as an inner loop. We show that identification is confined to the directions the motion excites --- so that, for example, a tool's length is unobservable under a rigid insertion push, while a compliant loop's passive yielding partially excites it; and that with a second-order admittance the compliant certificate holds unconditionally in continuous time. We also pose the combined control and estimation problem as a Quadratic Program (QP): the formulation yields the prediction term of the update law exactly but, instructively, cannot reproduce the tracking adaptation term. We validate the scheme in simulation on a peg-in-hole insertion. This work is the first step in a research program aimed at factoring manipulation learning into a task policy which can be learned in isolation of the robot, for instance by reinforcement learning, and an adaptive kinematic component that adapts online to the particular robot, hand, or tool in use.

</details>


### [349] [Why Personalization Matters: Cross-Subject Challenges in EMG-IMU-based HRI Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21620)
*Ruan Rithelle Chagas de Faria Carminati,Giovanni Braglia,Luigi Biagiotti,Ronnier Frates Rohrich,Andre Schneider de Oliveira,Mikael Nedel Hartmann,André Eugenio Lazzaretti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了用于人机协作场景下活动与手势识别的新数据集MAGIC-HRI，并评估了多模态信号的分类效果，强调了个性化适应的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人机交互（HRI）中，识别和理解人的动作和意图尤为关键，尤其是在物体交接或装配任务等实际应用中。现有数据集和方法对于动作类别的覆盖面有限，且缺乏面向实际HRI场景的定制化研究。因此，作者动机是提供高多样性、实用性强的数据集，并评估动作识别系统的泛化与个性化能力。

Method: 利用Myo臂环分别采集EMG（肌电）和IMU（惯性测量单元）信号，涵盖53类精细动作（包含手势、工具操作、装配动作等），共11名受试者、每人每类动作10次。采取EMG能量包络进行信号分割，用滑动窗口提取时域和频域特征，实验对比多种经典分类器（以随机森林效果最佳），采用LOSO（留一主体法）检验泛化性，并测试注入少量新用户数据后对个性化模型的提升。

Result: 随机森林分类器在本任务中效果最好，但LOSO实验发现泛化性较弱，即不同主体之间性能差异大。个性化实验发现，只需注入较少新用户样本就能大幅提升识别能力。

Conclusion: 该文贡献了复杂多样、贴合HRI的动作识别数据集和标准基线，强调了当前方法在泛化上的局限性，并实验验证了面向实际部署时个性化自适应的必要性。

Abstract: This paper investigates wearable-based recognition of human activities and gestures to support Human-Robot Interaction (HRI) in object-handover and assembly-like scenarios. Electromyography (EMG) and Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) signals were collected using a Myo armband, culminating in a novel dataset introduced as MAGIC-HRI (Multimodal Activity, Gesture and Intention Collection) with a large taxonomy of 53 movement classes, including Brazilian Sign Language (LIBRAS) numbers (0-9), hand gestures, object/tool handover actions (pick up/give/hold), tool-manipulation tasks, and generic assembly/idle motions, collected from 11 participants with 10 samples per class (530 samples per participant). Signals are segmented by detecting muscle activation via an EMG energy envelope, then processed using sliding windows; time- and frequency-domain features are extracted. Multiple classical classifiers are tuned via cross-validated grid search, with Random Forest as the strongest baseline. A Leave-One-Subject-Out (LOSO) protocol reveals a large generalization gap, indicating substantial subject dependence. A personalized adaptation experiment suggests that injecting a small number of samples from a new user can markedly improve recognition. Overall, the study contributes a broad, HRI-driven multimodal dataset, a rigorous evaluation emphasizing generalization, and practical evidence that personalization is likely required for robust deployment in practical HRI.

</details>


### [350] [OpenSCvx: An Open-Source Modular and Extensible Nonlinear Trajectory Planning Package](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21631)
*Christopher R. Hayner,Griffin J. Norris,Fabio Spada,Samet Uzun,Avi Mittal,Behcet Acıkmese,Karen Leung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenSCvx是一个开源的Python框架，简化和自动化了轨迹优化问题的建模与求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹优化在自主系统实现复杂任务时需要满足多种约束，但传统方法要求用户手动推导数学公式，使用门槛高、工作量大。因此需要一种工具，能让用户专注于问题建模，而无需深度了解数值优化细节。

Method: OpenSCvx提供符号建模接口，让用户通过模块化描述目标、动力学和约束自动生成轨迹优化问题。此外支持连续时间约束建模、时序及逻辑规范、自动向量化批量求解，并采用模块化架构，方便扩展新算法、模型和求解器。

Result: 用户可以更快捷地原型开发、求解并扩展最先进的轨迹优化方法，降低了轨迹优化的技术门槛和开发难度。

Conclusion: OpenSCvx极大简化了轨迹优化的建模与求解过程，为研究者和工程师提供了一种灵活、可扩展且高效的工具，有助于推进自主系统相关领域的发展。

Abstract: Trajectory optimization computes dynamically feasible motions that enable autonomous systems to accomplish complex tasks while satisfying operational and environmental constraints. This tutorial presents OpenSCvx, an open-source Python framework that bridges the gap between high-level problem specification and efficient numerical optimization. Rather than requiring users to derive solver-specific mathematical formulations, OpenSCvx provides a symbolic modeling interface that automatically constructs and solves trajectory optimization problems from modular descriptions of objectives, dynamics, and constraints. Beyond simplifying problem formulation, OpenSCvx supports (i) continuous-time constraint modeling, (ii) temporal and logical specifications, (iii) automatic vectorization for scalable and batched optimization, and (iv) a modular architecture that enables new algorithms, models, and solver backends to be incorporated with minimal effort. These capabilities allow researchers and practitioners to rapidly prototype, solve, and extend state-of-the-art trajectory optimization methods.

</details>


### [351] [Lifelong Robot Recomposition via Persistent Categorical Modeling for Unified Task-Driven Co-Design, Verification, and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21676)
*Steven Swanbeck,Mitch Pryor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种能够在机器人全生命周期实现硬件、软件和行为同时综合的组合式框架，使机器人在面对自身、任务或环境变化时能够自适应地重新配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人系统的设计-部署范式导致机器人只能适应狭窄的工作条件，对突发变化缺乏弹性。本文旨在突破这一局限，使机器人能在各阶段动态调整配置。

Method: 将机器人系统形式化为严格对称幺半范畴中的抽象电路，通过SMT求解器实现硬件、软件与行为的同步组合，并利用幺半函子实现生命周期视角转化和参数—组件双重推理。此外，该模型支持生命周期内的各类查询与自适应恢复机制。

Result: 新方法与现有最优数值、基于流的和SMT规划器在实际部署机器人上进行了评测。在搜索与救援场景中，该系统能够自主发现自身功能失效并合成全新配置以恢复运行。

Conclusion: 本文方法显著增强了机器人系统的适应性和可恢复性，实现了机器人在全生命周期内的自我调整和最小化变更恢复。相关的软件与求解器已开源。

Abstract: Robotic systems are traditionally designed and deployed in static configurations, with assumptions made at design-time becoming immutable constraints during runtime. This design-then-deploy paradigm produces performant systems under narrow operating conditions, but renders robots brittle when qualities of themselves, their tasks, or their environments unexpectedly change. We address this challenge with a compositional framework that formalizes robotic systems as abstract circuits within a strict symmetric monoidal category, in which design and runtime composition of hardware, software, and behavior are synthesized simultaneously via an SMT-based solver, with monoidal functors projecting the system into lifecycle-specific views and free symbolic variables simultaneously solving for parameters and entire component specifications within larger compositions. This persistent model also supports queries a long-lived system needs beyond plan existence across its entire lifecycle, including mapping Pareto fronts over candidate compositions, diagnosing why a composition has become infeasible, finding its minimal restoration, and reconfiguring with limited change to the deployed system. We evaluate against official implementations of optimal numeric, stream-based, and SMT-based planners all measured onboard a deployed robot and demonstrate the approach end-to-end in a search-and-rescue scenario in which the robot recognizes when it has become unfit and synthesizes and assumes new holistic configurations to restore operation. We release our solver and supporting software open-source.

</details>


### [352] [In-Situ Reconstruction of the International Space Station Using 3D Gaussian Splatting and Astrobee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21685)
*Hudson Kim,Ryan Soussan,Brian Coltin,Jordan Kam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文利用Astrobee机器人收集的灰度图像，通过3D Gaussian Splatting方法实现了国际空间站（ISS）日方舱（JEM）内部的高保真3D重建，优于Nerfacto和TensoRF等主流方法，提升了重建质量与渲染速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISS等空间站内部复杂，需高效准确的3D建模以支持机器人导航、任务规划和虚拟现实等应用。现有3D重建方法在空间站环境下存在渲染效率低或重建质量不足的问题。

Method: 作者采用3D Gaussian Splatting（3DGS）方法，基于Astrobee机器人在空间站内部导航时采集的灰度图（约500张），完成日方舱内部的3D点云与表面重建，并与Nerfacto和TensoRF方法作了对比。

Result: 实验结果表明，3DGS方法在渲染速度和重建精度上均优于Nerfacto、TensoRF等主流方法，仅需500张图像即可达到高保真、可用的空间舱内部3D地图效果。

Conclusion: 基于3DGS的重建系统非常适合空间站等密闭环境的快速、高质量地图创建，可为机器人自主导航、维护等任务提供支持，未来可用于空间站等载人航天系统的舱内环境重建。

Abstract: This article presents a novel 3D reconstruction and mapping of the interior of the International Space Station (ISS) using 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS). Using existing grayscale images from the Astrobee free-flying robot dataset, we construct a full 3D splat of the ISS' Kibō or Japanese Experiment Module (JEM). 3DGS has in recent years shown promise in providing novel view synthesis of scenes captured from many images or videos, this article applies this approach to human spaceflight systems. We compare our 3DGS architecture to existing methods such as Nerfacto and TensoRF and show that reconstruction improves the state-of-the-art in both scene quality and rendering speed. We show that with as little as 500 in-situ images, a high-fidelity map can be constructed using Astrobee's Navigation Camera (NavCam) during free-flight in the JEM. These reconstructions could enable free-flyers to rapidly create and update interior maps for intra-vehicular habitats like the ISS.

</details>


### [353] [Towards insect-like distributed proprioception in actuators and appendages for flapping-wing insect-scale aerial robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21699)
*Alexander Hedrick,Arvind Gupta,Kaushik Jayaram*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出并验证了两种适用于昆虫级扑翼飞行机器人的嵌入式本体感受传感器，可直接在机器人机翼动作中精确追踪拍打幅度和仰角，为实现机器人自主飞行铺平道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有昆虫级扑翼机器人在灵活性上与真实昆虫相当，但高度依赖外部传感系统（如光学跟踪相机），限制了它们的自主能力。为实现更智能、独立的微型机器人，需要发展更高集成度的机载本体感受传感器。

Method: 作者分别在驱动执行器和机翼转轴（pitching hinge）集成了薄膜压电聚合物传感器，利用层压堆叠制造技术实现结构集成，对机器人机翼的拍打角度和仰角进行实时测量。通过chirp测试实验验证了传感器的精度（RMSE分别为0.44度和2.44度）。

Result: 实验证明集成传感器的微型扑翼机器人能实现对机翼运动参数的高精度监测。作者分别展示了传感器在碰撞检测和异步扑翼控制中的应用，证明其可提升机器人自主性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 嵌入式本体感受传感器有望成为提升昆虫级机器人自主性的关键技术，推动微型机器人和机器人物理学的进步。其高度集成、仿生特性将为实现闭环飞控、自主定位等多种应用提供重要支持。

Abstract: Modern flapping-wing insect-scale air vehicles display agility similar to that of their insect counterparts; however, these impressive maneuvers are only possible with off-board sensors like optical tracking cameras. In this manuscript, we introduce two embedded proprioceptive sensors for insect-scale aerial robots: thin film piezoelectric polymers integrated directly into a driving actuator and a pitching hinge which track stroke and pitch angle, respectively. We fabricate the aforementioned size-agnostic mechanically intelligent structures (sensor-actuator, sensor-flexure) using laminate stack fabrication methods. Chirp experiments with our sensors integrated into an insect-size flapping-wing robot show accurate tracking of stroke (RMSE = 0.44 deg) and pitch (RMSE = 2.44 deg) angles in the relevant frequency range. As the first step towards demonstrating the utility of these sensors for enabling numerous onboard autonomy applications, including closed-loop wingbeat control and sensor fusion with existing insect-scale sensor suites for more accurate proprioception and localization, we show one application for each sensor. The proprioceptive hinge enables collision detection, reducing the chance of permanent damage if the robot's wing collides with an object. The proprioceptive actuator enables asynchronous flapping, which is hypothesized to increase adaptability and efficiency in insects and robots alike. A microrobot equipped with our proprioceptive actuator allows us to test these hypotheses with potential for improving flapping aerial robot performance. We foresee proprioceptive sensors having an important role in progressing both the fields of insect-scale aerial robots and robo-physics due to the bio-inspired nature and high integration level of our sensors.

</details>


### [354] [Safety-Critical Bilateral Teleoperation for Omnidirectional Aerial Manipulation Using Force-Sensorless Haptic Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21735)
*Yubin Kim,Jinwoo Lee,Yongjun You,H. Jin Kim,Jeonghyun Byun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种应用于全方位空中操作机械臂的安全关键双边遥操作框架，实现了无需力/力矩传感器的视觉与无传感器触觉反馈，提升了遥操作的安全性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中操作机械臂遥操作方法主要依赖板载力/力矩传感器或模型相关的力反馈估算，但这些方法在模型不确定或空载飞行时存在可靠性和反馈误差问题。此外，当前系统普遍忽视了推力饱和对于飞行安全的影响。

Method: 提出基于控制障碍函数的分层安全过滤器，对操作员指令进行调整以避免机械臂与环境交互时的跟踪误差和推进器饱和。同时，将调整后的指令映射为直观的触觉反馈，既提示操作者物理交互信息，也预警电机推力接近极限。

Result: 通过六旋翼全向空中机械臂的实验证明，该方法在空载飞行中不会产生误触觉反馈，实际物理交互中能提供一致的方向反馈，且适用于多种操作任务。消融实验也表明饱和过滤器显著提升了交互稳定性。

Conclusion: 分层安全过滤器不仅有效保障了空中操作的安全性，也通过触觉反馈提升了操作者的操作体验和系统稳定性，适用于多任务空中机械臂操作。

Abstract: This paper presents a safety-critical bilateral teleoperation framework for omnidirectional aerial manipulators that integrates visual and force-sensorless haptic wrench feedback. Unlike existing approaches that either rely on onboard force/torque sensors or use model-dependent wrench estimates, which may become unreliable under model uncertainties or induce unintended feedback during free-flight, our method implements a hierarchical safety filter based on control barrier functions to avoid such limitations. The safety filter, being the key contribution, explicitly accounts for tracking errors arising from physical interaction between the aerial manipulator and its surroundings while enforcing thrust limits, a factor overlooked despite its critical importance for flight safety. This safety filter adjusts the command from the operator to ensure safe and stable aerial manipulation and avoid motor saturation. The adjustment made by the filter is mapped to haptic feedback, which is intuitive to the operator and conveys information on physical interaction and impending motor saturation. By actual experiments with a hexarotor-based omnidirectional aerial manipulator, we demonstrate that the proposed method avoids haptic feedback during free-flight, provides directionally consistent feedback under physical interaction, and can be operated for diverse manipulative tasks. Moreover, an ablation study further shows that the saturation filter improves interaction stability by explicitly preventing motor saturation and informing the operator of corrective actions.

</details>


### [355] [CounterAlign: Counterfactual Supervision for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21740)
*Haru Kondoh,Kei Ota,Asako Kanezaki,Yueh-Hua Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出在仅有专家演示的情况下，通过“指令重标”生成反事实监督信号，为视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型和机器人任务提供更丰富、鲁棒的训练方式，无需额外的非专家数据或奖励设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的行为克隆（BC）方法只能提供正向监督，缺乏对错误或不合理动作的负反馈，导致模型泛化和鲁棒性不足。而强化学习（RL）虽能提供负向监督，但需要高成本收集指定奖励信号或非专家数据，实际应用受限。

Method: 论文提出采用“指令重标”技术，即将专家演示中的动作与非匹配的指令进行配对，合成反事实的指令-观测-动作样本，并通过对抗判别器训练无监督获得面向指令的奖励模型，实现高效的离线强化学习，无需额外收集数据或注释。

Result: 在LIBERO-PRO基准上，该方法在应对物体位置变化和任务扰动方面优于当前最先进基线。在真实机器人TX-G2（兼容AGIBot G2）实验中也优于其他竞争方法。

Conclusion: 从每条专家演示中发掘更加密集的监督信号，有助于提升数据受限场景下VLA模型的学习效果和鲁棒性，是对传统仅靠扩展数据量的有力补充。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are typically trained with behavior cloning (BC) on expert demonstrations. However, BC provides only positive supervision for expert actions, without explicit negative supervision indicating which actions are instruction-inconsistent or otherwise inappropriate. Reinforcement learning (RL) can provide such corrective signals, but often relies on externally specified rewards or curated non-expert data, both of which are costly to obtain in robotics. We show that offline RL for VLA models need not rely on curated non-expert trajectories: successful expert demonstrations alone can be transformed into dense corrective supervision through instruction relabeling. Specifically, by pairing expert actions with mismatched alternative instructions, we synthesize counterfactual instruction-observation-action tuples from the dataset and combine them with adversarial discriminator training to learn an instruction-grounded reward model for offline RL, without collecting additional rollouts or annotations. On the robustness-focused LIBERO-PRO benchmark, our method improves robustness to object position and task perturbations over a strong state-of-the-art baseline. It also outperforms competitive baselines in real-robot experiments on the TX-G2 (compatible with AGIBot G2). More broadly, our results suggest that, for data-constrained VLA learning, extracting denser supervision from each demonstration can complement collecting additional data.

</details>


### [356] [Vision Guided Target Conditioned Control for Autonomous Excavation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21778)
*Shuai Zhao,Ji-An Pan,Junwei Li,Xun Tang,Fansen Xi,Qing Xu,Keqiang Li,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合视觉目标与动作分块的智能控制框架，实现自动挖掘任务在仿真环境下的高效控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化挖掘需要将空间工作的意图转换为协调的铲斗动作，并应对土壤的复杂交互。现有控制方法在精确执行目标区域挖掘方面存在限制，特别容易忽略目标信息。

Method: 提出了一种以目标掩码为条件的智能控制框架，使用图像对齐的目标掩码作为视觉空间指令，通过多视角RGB观察和本体感知，利用Action Chunking Transformer（ACT）输出延展时间的操纵杆命令，并采用成对演示（paired-condition supervision），同一场景下设置不同目标掩码及相应动作分块，提升学习针对性。

Result: 在操纵任务中，配对掩码ACT达96%目标完成率，远超无条件或非配对条件的对照组。顺序堆清除任务配对掩码ACT可移除76.8%的土方，比两组基线显著提升，且实现91%的人类效率。

Conclusion: 结合视觉目标、成对演示和动作分块策略，可以构建出在虚拟仿真中具备高效执行力的自动化挖掘控制管道，有望提升实际挖掘自动化水平。

Abstract: Autonomous excavation requires an intelligent control system that can convert spatial work intent into coordinated bucket motion under contact-rich soil interaction. This paper presents a target-conditioned intelligent control framework for autonomous excavation in a physics-based deformable-soil simulation workflow. An image-aligned target mask serves as a visual spatial command for the desired digging region, while a mask-conditioned Action Chunking Transformer maps multi-view RGB observations, proprioception, and the target mask to temporally extended joystick commands. To reduce target-ignoring behavior, demonstrations are organized with paired-condition supervision, where the same or closely matched scene is demonstrated with different target masks and corresponding action chunks. The framework is evaluated through both a diagnostic manipulation task and an excavation simulation benchmark with single-scoop and sequential pile-clearing protocols. In manipulation, target success is 4\% for no-condition ACT, 63\% for non-paired mask-conditioned ACT, and 96\% for paired-condition mask-conditioned ACT. In sequential pile clearing, paired-condition mask-conditioned ACT removes 76.8\% of the pile versus 27.4\% and 15.7\% for the two baselines, with 91.0\% human-normalized efficiency. The results show that visual target conditioning, paired demonstration structure, and action-chunk control form a practical cyber-physical simulation pipeline for excavator automation.

</details>


### [357] [Vision-Guided Morphing Quadcopter for Multi-Geometry Payload Transport through Narrow Passages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21879)
*Aashish Sahu,Shriram Hari,R. Prasanth Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种具有形态变换能力的四旋翼无人机，能够通过视觉引导，实现多种形状载荷的抓取和运输，并适应狭窄通道的通过需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多旋翼无人机在空中运输不同几何形状载荷时，受限于传统机械手无法灵活调整抓取形态，难以适应载荷或通道宽度的变化，制约了无人机的应用场景。

Method: 设计了一种四臂可同步伸缩的混合结构无人机，每个结构既能作为落地支撑又能作为抓取部件。中置单驱动执行器拉动腱索实现四臂的同步收放，结合机载视觉系统识别载荷/通道尺寸，并利用末端力反馈确认抓取。结合任务规划、PID飞控和自适应抓取控制，并通过仿真（MuJoCo）验证性能。

Result: 该系统在箱体、圆柱体和球体三种载荷情况下完成了抓取—运输—释放流程。最高RMS定位误差0.31米，掉落区误差低至0.18米，紧凑抓取占地0.09-0.21平方米，形态收缩率达75.0-89.7%。

Conclusion: 单执行器变形无人机能够自适应调整抓取结构和整体占地，应对不同载荷几何形状的运输任务，并能缩小自身占地，顺利通过狭窄通道，展现出较高的灵活性和实用价值。

Abstract: Aerial payload transport using multirotor unmanned aerial vehicles is challenging because payload geometry, contact interaction, grasp stability, flight control, and narrow-passage traversal are strongly coupled during pickup and transport. Object-specific grippers often cannot adapt their footprint or grasp geometry when the payload shape or passage width changes. This paper presents a vision-guided morphing quadcopter for multi-geometry payload transport through narrow passages. The proposed platform uses four hybrid arm-leg structures that function as both landing supports and grasping members. A centrally placed actuator drives a tendon-based morphing mechanism, enabling all four arms to synchronously retract or expand for object grasping, footprint reduction, and post-transport release. Onboard vision estimates the payload geometry and passage width, while endpoint force feedback is used to confirm grasp contact during payload engagement. A phase-wise mission planner, PID-based flight stabilization, and morphology-adaptive grasp controller are implemented in a MuJoCo simulation environment. The framework is evaluated using box, cylindrical, and spherical payloads, representing flat-faced, rolling-curved, and fully curved contact conditions. Across the three cases, the simulated system completes the pickup-transport-release sequence with a maximum RMS position error of 0.31 m, a final drop-zone error below 0.18 m, a compact grasp footprint of 0.09-0.21 m2, and a footprint reduction of 75.0-89.7 percent. The results demonstrate that a single-actuator morphing quadcopter can adapt its grasp footprint for the transport of payloads with different geometries while reducing its overall footprint for narrow-passage traversal.

</details>


### [358] [An Interpretable Deep Learning Framework for Material Perception and Classification from Multisensory Tactile Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21894)
*Li Zou,Dave Hogendoorn,Yasemin Vardar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于深度学习的多模态触觉信号到材料感知的计算框架，探索了从触觉信号到知觉表征的路线，并揭示了关键的感知线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类触觉感知依赖复杂的多感官信息，但触觉信号与知觉表征之间的关系尚不清楚，这限制了触觉在数字环境和机器人系统中的应用。本文旨在揭示触觉信号如何被处理成材料类别感知。

Method: 研究建立了三个互相关联的深度学习模型：（1）从低层触觉信号到感知属性分布；（2）从感知属性分布到材料分类；（3）直接从触觉信号到材料分类，并结合Integrated Gradients方法解释模型决策来源。

Result: 该框架在材料分类上取得高准确率。直接端到端的模型可实现接近完美的分类，而引入知觉属性等中间阶段后，性能逼近人类却更加困难。热感知信号在所有模型中尤为重要，对材料区分有重要贡献。

Conclusion: 本文为触觉信号到材料感知的计算过程提供了解释，展示了可解释深度学习既能逼近人类水平，也能揭示机器人和触觉系统应重点开发的感知线索。

Abstract: Human tactile perception relies on complex multisensory cues. Yet the relationship between tactile signals and perceptual representations remains poorly understood, limiting the integration of touch in digital environments and human-like robotic perception. To address this gap, we developed a computational framework comprising three interconnected deep learning models that map multisensory touch data to material perception, without relying on hand-crafted features. The models represent progressively different routes from tactile signals to material class: from low-level interaction signals to perceptual attribute distributions (Model 1), from predicted attribute distributions to material classification (Model 2), and directly from tactile signals to material categories, bypassing intermediate representations (Model 3). By combining deep learning with Integrated Gradients, the framework achieved high accuracy while offering interpretability, revealing which sensory modalities most strongly drive its decisions. Our results show that deep learning can approach near-perfect material classification when unconstrained by intermediate perceptual stages, but matching human-like performance is harder once those stages are modeled explicitly. Notably, thermal cues emerged as particularly informative across all models, providing robust signals for material differentiation. The results offer a computational account of how tactile signals lead to material perception and show how interpretable deep learning can both approach human-level performance and reveal cues that robotic and haptic systems need to incorporate.

</details>


### [359] [CIDER: Continual Interactive Distillation for Embodied Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21899)
*Houlin Li,Minghui Xu,Guo Xu,Xuan Du,Xiaohan Yan,Chun Wang,Yuxiang Yan,Shukai Yang,Yongcheng Liu,Wei Shan,Maoqing Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新的人在环真实环境强化学习持续学习框架CIDER，并结合互动蒸馏和梯度路由，有效解决了持续学习过程中的遗忘问题，在多个实际机器人任务上显著超过现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有真实世界的持续强化学习方法难以在无遗漏旧技能的情况下快速学习新任务，普遍受到灾难性遗忘影响。论文旨在解决在强化学习中单一策略如何在不断引入新任务的同时保留旧行为的问题。

Method: 提出CIDER持续强化学习框架：每学习新任务前，将历史叠加的旧策略冻结为教师，结合交替的任务学习与蒸馏保持旧行为。同时引入梯度路由机制，明确区分学习新任务与保持旧行为的梯度流，以减少遗忘。

Result: 在六个真实的家庭与工业机器人操作任务中，CIDER方法能在每个新任务中10-20分钟内学习完成，并能持续保持对旧任务的高成功率，而所有对比方法均会出现对某些旧任务的遗忘。消融实验揭示了稳定性与可塑性的权衡关键因素。

Conclusion: CIDER能显著提升真实机器人持续强化学习的能力，在不牺牲旧任务表现的前提下高效习得新技能，为实际应用提供有效持续学习途径。

Abstract: Human-in-the-loop real-world reinforcement learning enables rapid acquisition of effective robotic manipulation policies for individual tasks, often within tens of minutes. Yet it remains unclear how to extend this paradigm to continual learning, where a single policy must acquire new skills without losing previously learned behaviors. Existing real-world continual learning methods do not explicitly constrain prior behaviors, leading to severe catastrophic forgetting. We introduce Continual Interactive Distillation for Embodied Reinforcement Learning (CIDER), a continual reinforcement learning framework that freezes the accumulated historical policy as a teacher before learning each new task and interleaves task learning with distillation-based retention. We further introduce gradient routing to separate the gradients used for acquiring new tasks from those used for preserving prior behaviors. We evaluate our method with a single shared actor on six real-world household and industrial manipulation tasks. Interactive Distillation maintains high measured success on previously learned tasks across our six-task real-robot sequence while acquiring each new task in 10 to 20 minutes, whereas every baseline forgets at least one previous task. Additional ablations reveal the key design choices that govern the tradeoff between stability and plasticity in real-world continual reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [360] [Stakeholder Insights for Designing In-Home Social Robots for Dementia Disorientation Detection and Caregiver-Aware Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22008)
*Emmanuel Akinrintoyo,Nicole Salomons*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究关注痴呆患者在日常生活中迷失定向的问题，探讨社交辅助机器人如何检测和干预该问题。通过对照护者及相关从业者的访谈，总结了行为特征和设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 居家痴呆患者常出现迷失定向，既影响情绪又增加安全风险，但目前对辅助机器人在此方面的支持研究不足。研究旨在填补这一技术和社会结合的空白。

Method: 研究采用半结构化访谈法，面向14位痴呆照护相关人员，包括家庭与专业照护者、作业治疗师、心理健康从业者、护士以及技术负责人，收集实际场景下的经验与需求。

Result: 研究发现迷失定向具有反复和波动性，表现为重复提问、时间感紊乱和作息异常。直接纠正常引发情绪问题，受访者更接受机器人提供支持性、非纠正性帮助。

Conclusion: 社交辅助机器人需具备情境感知和日常整合能力，并以支持性方式协助痴呆患者及照护者，减少迷失定向带来的不良影响。

Abstract: Disorientation is a common and distressing experience for people living with dementia. It often manifests as confusion about time, place, or personal context. These episodes can increase anxiety, agitation, and safety risks, especially for persons with dementia (PwDs) who live independently at home. While assistive technologies have explored reminders, monitoring, and activity support, little research has explored how socially assistive robots can support the detection and management of disorientation in everyday living contexts. Hence, disorientation detection and intervention remain under-examined as socio-technical challenges. We conducted 14 semi-structured interviews with dementia caregivers and practitioners, including family and professional caregivers, occupational therapists, mental health practitioners, dementia nurse practitioners, and well-being and technology leads. The findings reveal that recurrent and fluctuating. It often emerges through behavioural cues such as repeated questioning, inappropriate activity timing and disrupted daily routines. Caregivers described orientation as emotionally charged, and direct correction may increase distress. The participants were generally receptive to robotic assistance when framed as supportive rather than corrective. Based on the insights, we identify essential design implications for socially assistive robots that provide context-aware orientation support, integrate into daily routines and support caregivers through timely escalation.

</details>


### [361] [Design of a Human-Assistance Robot System with Contextual Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22028)
*Amanuel Ergogo,Teresa Zielińska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种能够进行上下文感知和主动响应的人类辅助机器人系统设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着服务机器人逐渐进入人类生活，机器人如何更好地理解和辅助人类变得尤为重要。传统系统通常被动响应，业务响应智能高度依赖于场景和上下文的适应能力，因此有必要设计可主动辅助的人类辅助机器人系统。

Method: 系统架构结合上下文人类行为识别（HAR）、行为树（BTs）以及ROS框架，以Spot机器人为例展开设计。通过HAR理解人的动作，在不同情境中进行准确识别；利用行为树定义机器人的动态、可解释行为；讨论了如何通过改进的上下文HAR提升决策能力，并弥补传统HAR局限。

Result: 提出的系统能更好识别人类复杂活动场景下的人类动作，并通过行为树进行合理、主动的响应。针对不同场景，系统展现了较强的主动辅助能力。

Conclusion: 基于上下文的人类行为识别结合行为树控制方法可显著提升人机协作时机器人的主动服务和辅助能力，对未来服务机器人智能化发展具有重要价值。

Abstract: This paper presents a conceptual design for a proactive human assisting robot system capable of recognizing human activities and responding proactively. The system leverages contextual human activity recognition to interpret human actions across diverse contexts, while behavior trees are utilized to define dynamic and interpretable robot behaviors. We outline the system architecture, incorporating contextual human action recognition (HAR), behavior trees (BTs), and ROS, using the Spot robot platform as a representative example. We explain how HAR enables the robot to provide proactive assistance, discuss its limitations, and introduce methodologies for contextual HAR to address these limitations, thereby enhancing the robot's decision-making in complex human activity scenarios.

</details>


### [362] [DELTA: Deformable Elevation-Based Local Terrain Attention Encoder for Sparse-Terrain Quadrupedal Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22033)
*Sanghyun Park,Moonkyu Jung,Jemin Hwangbo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型地形注意力编码器DELTA，用于增强四足机器人在稀疏复杂地形上的稳定移动能力，同时大幅降低计算开销，实现高分辨率下的高效足端落点规划与端到端强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型的足端规划方法依赖精确的模型假设，而最新基于注意力机制的地图编码方法虽可通过RL实现隐式引导，但随着地图分辨率提高，计算成本大幅增加，限制了其在细粒度稀疏地形上的应用。迫切需要一种在保证性能的同时计算成本与分辨率无关的新编码机制，提升四足机器人的实际落点选择与跨域泛化能力。

Method: 提出DELTA编码器：通过预测与状态相关的采样位置，自适应选取局部高程小片段构成固定规模的地形特征token，并仅对这些token进行注意力计算，令编码开销与分辨率无关。端到端强化学习用于训练足端选择，且不依赖足端标签或监督信号。通过模拟和实际机器人（RAIBO2）开展对比与迁移实验。

Result: 在标准分辨率下，DELTA展现出与传统AME方法可比拟的通行能力，同时学习效率显著提升。基于固定的编码开销，DELTA可处理更高分辨率的地形地图，在更精细、稀疏地形上表现更优。此外，在未见过的混合地形（连续+离散）评测场景中也展现出良好的泛化能力。DELTA能无缝实现从仿真实验到真实硬件的迁移。

Conclusion: DELTA有效解决了稠密注意力编码在高分辨率下的计算瓶颈，提升了四足机器人在复杂稀疏地形下的足端选择能力和学习效率，并具备良好泛化和仿真到现实迁移能力，为机器人地形感知与运动控制领域带来新进展。

Abstract: Stable quadrupedal locomotion on sparse terrain requires selecting state-relevant terrain evidence for precise foot placement. Model-based foothold planners provide precise foothold selection but rely heavily on explicit model assumptions. Recent attention-based map encoding (AME) studies show that end-to-end reinforcement learning (RL) can learn implicit foothold guidance. However, the computational cost of dense AME encoding grows with map resolution, limiting its scalability to fine-grained sparse terrain. We propose DELTA, a Deformable Elevation-Based Local Terrain Attention encoder. DELTA predicts state-conditioned sampling locations, forms terrain evidence tokens from adaptive local elevation patches, and attends only to a fixed-size token set. With fixed sampling and patch settings, DELTA's encoder cost is independent of map resolution. Experiments show that DELTA achieves final traversal performance comparable to AME at the standard resolution while improving learning efficiency. This fixed encoder cost enables the use of higher-resolution terrain maps, improving traversal on fine-grained sparse terrain. DELTA also demonstrates strong generalization to unseen mixed evaluation courses composed of continuous and discrete terrain elements. Beyond simulation, DELTA demonstrates successful sim-to-real transfer on RAIBO2. Analysis of the learned sampling offsets and attention weights shows that DELTA samples steppable regions and attends to terrain evidence relevant to future touchdowns without foothold labels or attention supervision.

</details>


### [363] [Ludi${}_{\scriptscriptstyle 0.1}$: An Agentic System for Socially Intelligent Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22035)
*Wooseong Chung,William Cong,Jakub Dworakowski,Ethan Ewer,Tri Wahyu Guntara,Yeonwoo Jeong,Tianchong Jiang,Chaewon Kim,Hyunseo Kim,Jinwoo Kim,Jinyeon Kim,Yea-Seul Kim,Jack Kunde,Kangwook Lee,Sangheon Lee,Robert Nowak,Junha Roh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了Ludi_0.1系统，让机器人具备更自然的社交智能，能够跨多轮对话进行推理、保持上下文、表达意图和灵活调整行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基础模型虽在感知和控制上有进步，但在人机自然协作中仍缺乏持续对话、上下文保持、意图透明表达与动态调整能力。

Method: Ludi_0.1系统集成了交互语音、多模态推理、记忆、导航和学习操控。核心为微调后的视觉-语言模型，基于包含模糊请求、澄清、修正、打断和多步任务等多轮交互语料进行训练；同时，采用专门设计的管理框架协调模型与具体导航、操作策略之间的协作。

Result: Ludi_0.1能够在实际人机协作场景中实现流畅对话、复杂任务理解和物理技能执行，并能持续生成多模态交互数据，有助于后续模型优化。

Conclusion: Ludi_0.1为实现更加自然的人机协作提供了可行路径，并为未来深度融合的人机基础模型奠定了交互数据基础。

Abstract: Robot foundation models have substantially advanced perception and control, but natural human-robot collaboration requires more than executing isolated commands. A robot must recognize ambiguity, maintain context across turns, communicate its intentions, and revise ongoing behavior as the user's intent changes. We present $\scriptstyle\mathsf{Ludi}_{\scriptscriptstyle 0.1}$, an agentic system for socially intelligent robots that integrates interactive speech, multimodal reasoning, memory, navigation, and learned manipulation. Its decision-making core is a fine-tuned vision-language model trained on multi-turn interaction traces spanning ambiguous requests, clarifications, corrections, interruptions, mixed social and task dialogue, and multi-step tasks. A purpose-built harness manages the model-tool interaction loop, while specialized navigation and manipulation policies execute physical skills. Ludi${}_{\scriptscriptstyle 0.1}$ demonstrates a practical path toward fluid human-robot collaboration today while producing the multimodal interaction traces needed to develop a more deeply integrated foundation model for robots and people.

</details>


### [364] [Inferring Action from Future Latent State for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22067)
*Fenghao Lei,Zhixiong Huang,Long Yang,Jiabao Chen,Jie Cheng,Peilin Huang,Han Fu,Zhuo Li,Xiaoxue Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需视频生成过程的机器人控制方法DELE-w0.5，利用低维未来状态推理动作，实现更高效的世界-动作建模，并在多项真实机器人操作任务中显著超越传统基于视频生成的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界-动作模型（WAMs）常依赖视频生成预测未来，但视频逐帧重建既浪费计算资源，也未能直接服务于机器人操作任务的本质目标，即准确预测执行动作后的世界状态。因此，作者提出不必再用繁重的视频生成任务作为中间目标。

Method: DELE-w0.5放弃视频生成，只预测与动作相关的未来紧凑状态，然后由这一潜在状态推理出动作序列。这样，世界建模和动作生成之间由“未来状态”这一桥梁相连，大大减少了建模和推理的高维冗余。

Result: 在四项长时序真实机器人操作任务的480次实验中，DELE-w0.5在整体任务成功率（62.5%）和分阶段任务进度（81.3%）方面均远优于其他对比方法，分别领先最强的基线方法47.5和30.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 去除无用的高维视频生成后，用紧凑未来状态实现动作与世界建模的高效联动，可以显著提升机器人操作任务的性能和效率。DELE-w0.5为机器人世界模型及动作建模提供了更合理、更经济、且效果更佳的新范式。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) build robot control on video-generation backbones, which jointly predict dense future visual trajectories and robot actions. We argue that video generation is an unnecessary intermediate objective for world-action modeling. For robotic manipulation, the goal of a world model is not to reproduce how the world looks at every intermediate moment, but to predict the state that the world will reach after an action is executed. The intermediate frames only describe the visual transition between physical states, which consumes substantial model capacity and computation, but do not directly specify the physical outcome that the robot action is intended to produce. In this paper, we propose DELE-w0.5, which infers robot actions from predicted future states without relying on video generation. Concretely, DELE-w0.5 infers the action sequence from its corresponding compact future latent state. The future latent state captures the action-relevant physical outcome of robot interaction and serves as an explicit bridge between world modeling and action generation. The core design principle of DELE-w0.5 is to model how the physical world changes under robot actions, rather than how its visual appearance evolves frame by frame. This formulation removes the high-dimensional visual redundancy introduced by dense video representations, and it therefore enables cheaper training and low-latency inference. Across 480 real-robot trials on four long-horizon manipulation tasks, our DELE-w0.5 achieves the best performance among all compared policies, attaining 62.5 overall full-task success and 81.3 macro ordered-stage progress, outperforming the strongest baseline by 47.5 and 30.7 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [365] [EndoNav: Semantic-to-Geometric Grounding for Language-Guided Robotic Endoscopic Examination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22093)
*Jecia Z. Y. Mao,Hisashi Ishida,Kathryn Jung,Masaru Ishii,Russell H. Taylor,Manish Sahu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了EndoNav系统，实现了基于自然语言的自动内镜可视化导航，可自动响应外科医生的高层级语音指令，在患者专属解剖结构内进行自主可视化操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前机器人内镜系统虽能稳定/调整内镜，但无法理解环境语境，难以根据外科实际需求进行协助。为优化狭小解剖空间的手术视觉，亟需基于解剖场景和语音指令的智能导航方案。

Method: EndoNav以患者专属三维解剖模型为基础，采用自然语言处理，将医生语音转化为结构化的可视化目标，再借助受限于解剖几何的路径规划和关节空间控制，实现内镜的自主导航和视域调整。

Result: 在3例CT模型和1例尸体标本的鼻窦三步检查中，EndoNav系统实现的自主可视化与两位住院医师的检查结果IoU分别为87.04%和84.37%，与医师间的一致性87.44%相当，且可重现92.91%、93.20%的医生观察区域。

Conclusion: EndoNav能有效将外科医生的高级解剖指令转化为患者特异的内镜导航目标，实现安全、精确并具解释性的自主视野管理，证明了该类系统在手术机器人辅助中的可行性与应用潜力。
