Abstract: Minimally invasive procedures performed within confined anatomical spaces depend on continuous endoscopic visualization. Current robotic endoscope systems can stabilize or reposition an endoscope, but they do not possess relevant context to provide effective visualization assistance. We present EndoNav, an anatomy-grounded natural-language framework that translates high-level surgeon commands into autonomous endoscopic visualization behaviors within patient-specific sinonasal anatomy. Spoken surgeon commands are transcribed and interpreted by an endoscopic viewpoint agent conditioned on a patient-specific anatomical scene representation. Rather than generating robot motion directly, the viewpoint agent generates structured visualization objectives that are converted into target viewpoints and inspection trajectories, which are then executed through geometry-constrained endoscope motion planning and joint-space control. We evaluate EndoNav using a structured three-pass sinus examination across three CT-derived anatomical models. For one cadaveric specimen, autonomous visualization is compared with sinus examinations performed by two resident surgeons. EndoNav achieved mean visualization IoUs of 87.04% and 84.37% relative to the two surgeon examinations, compared with an inter-surgeon IoU of 87.44%, while recovering 92.91% and 93.20% of surgeon-observed anatomical surfaces, respectively. These results demonstrate the feasibility of grounding high-level anatomical commands into patient-specific geometric objectives and translating them into anatomically constrained robotic visualization behaviors.

</details>


### [366] [Contact-Rich Robotic Manipulation in Construction via Zero-Shot Learning: A Diffusion Policy-Guided Adaptive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22100)
*Roman Ibrahimov,Salma Mozaffari,Arash Adel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合扩散策略与自适应控制的新型机器人自动化装配框架，实现了更高效、稳定的复杂建筑组装，能够直接将仿真训练迁移到真实场景，减少数据采集成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在建筑业中，提升生产力、缓解劳动力短缺以及降低工人劳动强度都是当前的重要需求。但实际复杂装配任务（尤其是需严格公差与复杂接触动态时）仍面临机器人可靠性挑战。

Method: 框架采用在仿真环境中训练的扩散策略（结合位姿与力矩数据），并引入L1风格的自适应控制器用于实时修正策略输出，以应对未知接触动态，实现仿真到真实装配的无缝迁移。

Result: 在木工榫卯、管道对接以及全尺度桁架子装配等实验上，新框架在单任务下装配成功率100%，多子任务装配成功率在90-100%之间，且接触力更低更稳定，优于对照组。

Conclusion: 该方法有效减少了真实数据采集需求，提升了多阶段复杂装配的自动化泛化能力和鲁棒性，为建筑业更广泛的复杂操作自动化奠定基础。

Abstract: Construction robotics and automation offer promising means of improving productivity, alleviating workforce shortages, and reducing workers' exposure to physically demanding tasks. However, reliable contact-rich robotic assembly remains challenging under tight tolerances, fabrication inaccuracies, and uncertain contact dynamics. To address this challenge, we present a framework coupling diffusion policies trained on simulation-generated pose and force/torque data with an L1-inspired adaptive controller that corrects policy-predicted actions online to compensate for unmodeled contact dynamics. We benchmark the framework against baselines in timber joinery, pipe fitting, and sequential full-scale truss assembly. It achieves 100% success on single-task assemblies and 90-100% success across sequential truss assembly subtasks, with lower, more stable contact forces than the baselines. By enabling zero-shot sim-to-real transfer for force-aware contact-rich assembly, the framework reduces costly, labor-intensive real-world data collection for policy training and advances scalable, robust automation of multistage assembly, motivating extension to broader contact-rich manipulation tasks in construction.

</details>


### [367] [Meta-Ctrl: Guaranteed Plan Generation by Decoupling Syntactic and Semantic Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22149)
*Gwen Yidou-Weng,Edward Sun,Tianyi Ma,Metin Alp Dogan,Benjie Wang,Allen Peng,Guy Van den Broeck,Yuchen Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新型的约束解码框架Meta-Ctrl，能在保证执行约束的同时，保留大型语言模型优质的机器人规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的机器人规划易违反必要语法和语义约束，虽有软/硬纠正手段，但软方法无可行性保证，强约束则失去常识推理。本研究旨在高效、精确地融合约束与LLM能力。

Method: 设计Meta-Ctrl框架，引入用于动作抽象的meta-token，在token级别实现语法约束、在动作级别确保语义（前置条件、目标、顺序）约束，并极大压缩内存使用（从TB级降到GB级）。

Result: 小型开源LLM在任务基准LoTa-Bench上达到当前最高的子目标成功率，超越GPT-4，并在真实机器人桌面任务中所有生成方案都严格满足预期条件。

Conclusion: Meta-Ctrl有效结合了LLM的规划能力和符号规划的严格性，为实际机器人规划提供了高性能且有约束保证的新范式。

Abstract: LLMs generate fluent plans for robots but routinely violate the syntactic and se8mantic constraints they must satisfy to execute, and existing remedies trade formal guarantees against plan quality: soft methods (affordance scoring, grounded decoding) give no guarantee, while symbolic planners (LLM+P) discard the LM's commonsense. We propose \textbf{Meta-Ctrl}, a constrained-decoding framework that guarantees the encoded constraints while preserving the base LM's plan quality. Meta-Ctrl introduces \emph{meta-tokens}---a compact vocabulary of grounded actions---enforcing syntax at the token level and semantics (preconditions, goals, ordering) at the action level, an exact factorization that cuts the memory of constrained decoding from over 107TB to under 2GB. With it, a small open-weight LM becomes competitive where it otherwise sits at the bottom of the leaderboard: on WAH-NL under the LoTa-Bench protocol it reaches the highest reported subgoal success rate, exceeding GPT-4's, with consistent gains across the Embodied Agent Interface. We further demonstrate it on a real tabletop robot, where every generated plan satisfies its preconditions and goals by construction. Project website: https://meta-ctrlg.github.io/.

</details>


### [368] [BehaviorWorldGen: Closing the Loop between Action Models and World Simulators via Controllable Behavior-Aware Structured World Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22187)
*Jiaqi Wang,Zhuo Zhang,Haining Guan,Tingguang Zhou,Haowen Cui,Zhongyang Zhu,Yulong Zheng,ChuanYe Wang,Xuefeng Chen,Zhen Yang,Tianchen Deng,Feiyang Tan,Hangning Zhou,Bo Dai,Lixia Shen,Xiwu Chen,Xiyang Wang,Jiajun Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了BehaviorWorldGen框架，通过行为可控的结构化世界生成方法，加强了自动驾驶模型在模拟环境中的自我提升能力。核心组件BehaviorFlow可产生多智能体、高度互动的交通场景数据，用于提升驾驶行为模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶仿真器无法生成具有行为合理性的交互数据，导致模拟数据在行为互动和分布上不真实，从而限制了自动驾驶决策模型的提升空间。

Method: 提出BehaviorWorldGen框架，通过行为条件约束和结构化轨迹生成(BehaviorFlow)，实现对多车辆行为的可解释调控。产生产景轨迹后，经世界模拟器渲染为多视角观测，再与修正后的互动轨迹配对训练决策模型。该方法以结构化轨迹为模块接口，兼容不同的驾驶模型和仿真器。

Result: 在世界生成、场景外推和政策完善方面取得了一致改进，尤其在高交互复杂场景中提升效果最为显著。

Conclusion: 通过行为可控的结构化世界生成，实现了更真实的多车交互数据模拟，显著提升了自动驾驶行为模型性能和泛化能力。

Abstract: Modern driving action models are increasingly improved in a self-improvement loop, where a learned world simulator imagines future observations and the resulting data is fed back to refine the action model. However, the bottleneck of this loop lies in the simulators' inability to generate behaviorally plausible responses by surrounding agents, making generated data both unrealistic in interaction and imbalanced in distribution. We introduce BehaviorWorldGen, a framework that closes the loop between action models and world simulators through controllable behavior-aware structured world generation. Its core component is BehaviorFlow, a meta-action-conditioned traffic-flow model that injects interpretable behavior controls and jointly generates multi-agent rollouts. BehaviorFlow realizes the specified agent behaviors while allowing surrounding vehicles to respond to the ego and to one another. The resulting rollouts are rendered by a world simulator into realistic multi-view observations, which are paired with corrected interaction-aware trajectories for action-model refinement. Since BehaviorWorldGen uses structured trajectories as the interface between its modules, it is compatible with diverse action models and world simulators. Experiments on world generation, scene extrapolation, and policy refinement demonstrate consistent improvements, with the largest benefits concentrated on difficult interactive scenarios.

</details>


### [369] [DreamMimic: Learning Visuomotor Whole-Body Loco-Manipulation via World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22278)
*Jie Yin,Xingyu Lai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DreamMimic框架，将特权知识的高性能老师策略通过世界模型辅助的蒸馏方法迁移至基于视觉的仿人机器人控制，实现更好的全身运动操控能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿人机器人基于视觉完成全身操控面临部分可观测、高维视觉输入、动态复杂（频繁接触）和长期行为学习难等挑战，现有方法效果受限。

Method: 采用Dreamer风格的可复现状态空间模型（RSSM）辅助蒸馏老师策略，不用于规划而是作为特征空间与多步动作监督信号，并增加额外的预测头（如接触、奖励、对象状态），强化表征学习。提出基于奖励驱动的表现条件引导机制（PCG），自适应调整蒸馏进程，平衡探索与老师指导。

Result: 在OMOMO和BEHAVE等平台上，相比基线视觉方法，DreamMimic能更好地实现跟踪性运动操控，在部署时无需在线特权状态。模拟实验也显示了算法对形态和仿真器变化的适应性。

Conclusion: 世界模型在复杂接触场景下能有效提升视觉策略蒸馏的稳定性和性能，为仿人机器人基于视觉的全身操控带来新思路。

Abstract: Vision-based whole-body loco-manipulation on humanoid robots is challenging due to partial observability, contact-rich dynamics, and the difficulty of learning long-horizon behaviors from high-dimensional visual inputs. We present \href{https://github.com/DreamMimic/DreamMimic}{DreamMimic}, a framework that distills privileged teacher policies into vision-based humanoid controllers via world-model-assisted distillation. Instead of using a Dreamer-style RSSM for planning, we repurpose it to learn predictive latent dynamics that serve as both a representation space and an action-conditioned multi-step supervision signal, while exposing compact predictive features to the student policy to reduce long-term drift. Beyond standard reconstruction objectives for proprioceptive and visual observations, we add auxiliary prediction heads for privileged state, contact, object state, and reward estimation. These heads provide additional supervision related to agent--object interaction and task progress, encouraging the latent representation to retain signals that are useful for contact-rich loco-manipulation. We further introduce Performance-Conditioned Guidance (PCG), a reward-driven adaptive distillation schedule that computes performance scores for both teacher and student to dynamically balance guidance and exploration. PCG prevents both premature teacher annealing and excessive teacher interference in challenging visual settings. Experiments on OMOMO and BEHAVE show improved tracking-based loco-manipulation performance over strong vision-based baselines, without exposing online privileged interaction states to the student at deployment. Qualitative simulations further examine morphology and simulator changes. These results suggest that world models can provide a useful mechanism for stabilizing visual policy distillation in contact-rich humanoid behaviors.

</details>


### [370] [Beyond Instance Slots: Semantically Rich World Models for Physical Interaction Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22294)
*Juntao Cheng,Jingkai Wang,Yijun Shen,Xiansheng Chen,Zhiwei Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了语义丰富世界模型（SR-WM），通过五种功能角色（如夹持器、目标、关系等）将物理交互中的对象和任务结构结合，支持更强的下游规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理交互世界模型多注重预测，而真正用于规划的模型需判断候选动作是否能实现一致的任务目标。传统状态表达方式无法区分实体功能角色，导致模型对于任务决策支持不够直观。

Method: SR-WM模型包含视觉实体编码器，由预训练特征提取软实体假设，并通过角色绑定器将其映射到具体任务角色。基于角色的动态模型则用于预测实体功能及语义变化，如动作中的抓取、关系保持等。

Result: SR-WM通过对多候选动作生成、分阶段再排序、违反约束后的采样等下游任务表现出色。模型在四种LIBERO仿真套件、跨套件迁移、感知诊断和动作敏感性等评测中进行了全面验证。

Conclusion: SR-WM将对象中心预测转化为语义任务接口，显著提升了视觉动态与规划型决策间的衔接能力。

Abstract: World models for physical interaction are typically trained to predict future observations or latent features; however, a planning-oriented model must answer a fundamentally different question: whether a candidate action produces a task-consistent future while preserving essential relations.Monolithic state representations obscure the underlying entities, while standard instance-level object slots merely identify \emph{what} is present without specifying \emph{what role} each entity plays in the task context. To bridge this gap, we present the Semantically Rich World Model (SR-WM), a task-conditioned world model structured around five functional roles: gripper, target, goal, relation, and phase.Within SR-WM, a visual entity encoder extracts soft entity hypotheses from pretrained patch features, allowing segmentation masks to serve as optional proposal priors without mandating them as required state representations or inference inputs.A role binder subsequently maps these hypotheses to task-specific roles, while an action-conditioned dynamics model predicts role transitions alongside fine-grained semantics, including grasp/contact, predicate establishment, relation preservation, fixture state, and phase change.Crucially, this unified role state grounds downstream multi-candidate action generation, stage-aware reranking, and violation-aware suffix resampling.Our comprehensive evaluation protocol spans all four LIBERO simulation suites, cross-suite transfer, perception diagnostics, and action-sensitivity analysis.Ultimately, this formulation transforms object-centric prediction into a semantic interface linking visual dynamics with planning-oriented decision making.

</details>


### [371] [TONAV: Task-Oriented Navigation and Action-Velocity Chunk Learning for Articulated Object Quadrupedal Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22296)
*Haoran Lin,Mingyu Yang,Pengfei Qi,Kehan Chen,Qiang Diao,Liangji Zeng,Wenrui Chen,Yaonan Wang,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了TONAV系统，一个结合任务导向导航和动作-速度片段学习的四足移动操作框架，实现了导航与操控的高度一体化，从而提升连续接触操作的成功率和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足机器人移动操作方法在移动到目标点后，常难以直接进入可操控状态，且存在轨迹跟踪滞后、动作抖动和接触不稳定等问题，阻碍了高效的连续物体操作。

Method: TONAV包含三大技术：1）引入位置-速度耦合的远程操控框架，提升操作一致性并采集平滑示范数据；2）结合视觉-语言推理，将高层指令分解为可执行子目标，并自主优化基座位置以达到操控准备状态；3）动作-速度片段学习，联合建模关节位置和速度变化，实现平滑、稳定的持续接触操作。

Result: 在多种现实场景下，TONAV实现了更高的任务导向导航和整体移动操作成功率，显著缩小了导航-操控之间的落差，并提升了连续接触操作的平稳性。

Conclusion: TONAV为四足机器人提供了一套端到端、一体化的导航与操控方案，有效提升了复杂环境下的连续物体操作能力，为未来机器人自主操作任务奠定了基础。

Abstract: Quadruped mobile manipulation requires two tightly coupled capabilities: reaching manipulation-ready configurations and maintaining stable contact throughout articulated-object interaction. However, existing methods often terminate navigation near the target, leaving a gap between reachability and manipulation readiness, while tracking lag, motion jitter, and contact instability limit continuous interaction. To address these challenges, we present TONAV, a unified framework integrating task-oriented navigation with action-velocity chunk learning. First, we introduce a position-velocity-coupled teleoperation framework that explicitly captures motion dynamics to improve master-follower consistency and collect smooth, temporally consistent demonstrations. Next, task-oriented navigation leverages vision-language reasoning to decompose high-level instructions into executable subgoals and adaptively refine the robot base toward a manipulation-ready configuration. Finally, action-velocity chunk learning jointly models joint positions and their temporal transitions under velocity supervision, enabling smooth and stable sustained-contact manipulation. Real-world experiments across diverse articulated-object tasks demonstrate that TONAV achieves higher success rates in both task-oriented navigation and complete mobile manipulation, mitigating the navigation-manipulation gap and improving continuous-contact interaction. The project page is at https://haochen611.github.io/TONAV.

</details>


### [372] [The Imitator Game: Benchmarking Robot Imitative Ability Beyond Action Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22301)
*Xunzhe Zhou,Yiyang Cai,Fengyi Wang,Ran Ju,Hanxiang Ren,Ruizhe Liu,Yu Zhang,Qian Luo,Feng Chen,Pei Zhou,Yi Ma,Yanchao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了The Imitator Game基准和IG-10K数据集，用以评测机器人的意图级模仿能力，并指出当前主流模型在应对环境泛化和意图转化方面存在显著瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够通过观察演示推断任务意图并灵活模仿，而当前机器人多以轨迹复现为主，难以跨场景完成任务。研究目的在于推动机器人从仅模仿动作到理解和实现上层意图，提升其泛化能力。

Method: 作者设计了一个包含四个难度层级（L0-L3）的The Imitator Game基准，通过逐步增加机器人场景与人类演示差异，测试从轨迹复现到意图推理的能力。同时发布了IG-10K大规模人机配对数据集及Imitator Arena评测平台，覆盖大量任务和领域，对多种SOTA模型进行大规模评测。

Result: 实验发现，各模型在低难度（L0-L2）下表现稳定，但在L3难度，即需要实现功能替代时（不同物品实现同一意图），性能大幅下降。基于视频的模型优于基于语言描述的模型，但在新任务上的零样本成功率均不足13%。通过在IG-10K上预训练后再用极少人机配对样本微调，可显著提升模型泛化能力，且增益随预训练规模扩大。

Conclusion: 现有机器人模仿学习方法对于“意图级模仿”仍远未解决，未来需进一步突破功能替代等泛化难题。IG-10K和Imitator Game为该方向的标准评测与数据资源提供了支撑，有望促进意图推理方法的发展。

Abstract: Humans imitate at the level of intent: given a demonstration, we infer its goal and carry it out with whatever tools, objects, and layouts are at hand. Current robot policies instead learn observation-to-action mappings from visual inputs and language instructions, without explicitly inferring the demonstrated task. Learning from human video thus remains largely trajectory-level: models can replay motions in near-identical scenes, but still struggle to imitate what the demonstrator intends rather than merely what they do. We introduce The Imitator Game, a four-level benchmark (L0-L3) that progressively widens the gap between the human demonstration and the robot's own scene, isolating where trajectory replay ceases to suffice and task understanding becomes necessary. We pair it with IG-10K, the largest environment-aligned paired human-robot dataset to date and the only one instantiated across all four levels in both real and simulated settings (20,000+ paired episodes, 50+ tasks, 6 domains), and Imitator Arena, an open platform for blind A/B human evaluation. Across nine state-of-the-art models, performance is stable from L0 to L2 but collapses at L3, identifying functional substitution - achieving the same intent through a different object affordance - as the decisive barrier to intent-level imitation. Human-video-conditioned models outperform caption-conditioned ones, yet every model falls below 13% zero-shot success on unseen tasks; fine-tuning IG-10K-pretrained models with only $10$ paired human-robot demonstrations yields large gains that grow with pretraining scale. The project website and access to Imitator Arena are available at https://imitator-game.github.io.

</details>


### [373] [GCS-Bridging: Restoring Connectivity of Disconnected Convex Sets for Graph-of-Convex-Sets Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22326)
*Xiaokai Zhou,Baoshi Cao,Yang Liu,Kui Sun,Boyu Ma,Zhengpu Wang,Zongwu Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为GCS-Bridging的新方法，通过在不可达的凸集之间建立无碰撞路径并扩展凸集，实现原本不可行的轨迹优化。实验显示该方法几乎总能恢复起点到终点的可达性，并在真实硬件上验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于凸集图（GCS）的轨迹规划，假设所有凸集间有足够连接，未处理起点与终点分属不同连通分量的情形。这一问题在复杂环境下较为常见，导致规划不可行，因此亟需解决断开时段的可达性恢复。

Method: 提出GCS-Bridging方法：先在断开的凸集之间以点路径方式寻找无碰撞连接，再通过膨胀这些路径生成额外凸集，使原先不连通的区域变为连通。此方法可嵌入现有多种GCS/IRIS类算法。

Result: 在多种仿真场景下测试，GCS-Bridging可将原本断开的GCS映射恢复可达性，成功率高达99.8%。此外还在单臂Franka机械臂真实实验中验证了其实用性。

Conclusion: GCS-Bridging可高效恢复GCS轨迹规划不可达情形的可行解，且实际硬件测试效果良好，显著提升真实场景中的应用范围。

Abstract: Graph-of-Convex-Sets (GCS)-based trajectory optimization represents collision-free regions in configuration space as a finite collection of convex sets and directly performs collision-free trajectory planning over these sets, substantially simplifying the planning process. However, existing GCS-based trajectory planning methods generally assume sufficient connectivity among the convex regions and do not explicitly address cases in which the start and goal regions belong to different connected components of the initial GCS map. To address this limitation, we propose GCS-Bridging, which reconnects disconnected convex regions through collision-free point paths followed by convex region inflation, thereby recovering the feasibility of otherwise disconnected GCS planning problems. Extensive simulations across multiple IRIS-related algorithms and scenarios demonstrate that GCS-Bridging restores missing start-to-goal connectivity in the initial GCS map with a 99.8% success rate. In addition, a hardware experiment on a single-arm Franka platform in a real-world scenario with initially disconnected start and goal regions validates the effectiveness of the proposed method in practical motion planning. Project website: https://zhouxk1997.github.io/GCS_Bridging/

</details>


### [374] [MotionDLO: Hybrid Event- and Frame-Based Tracking of Deformable Linear Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22398)
*Annalena Hartmann,Priyamvada Ajithkumar,Patrick Bründl,Jörg Franke*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出MotionDLO，一种专为追踪变形线状物体(DLO)设计的实时跟踪框架，融合了事件相机与帧相机的数据，兼顾精度、速度和时序一致性，在动态操作场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人感知中，对移动且易变形的线状物体（如电缆、绳索）进行精确、连贯、快速地追踪仍存在显著挑战。传统基于事件的跟踪方法虽然高效但精度有限，基于帧的方法则速度受限且难以保证事件稀疏性，二者之间存在明显取舍。

Method: 提出MotionDLO框架，融合高时空分辨率的事件相机信息与传统帧相机图像。利用图像分割结合CPD（Coherent Point Drift）算法，实现基于运动连贯性理论的时序一致形状估计。事件流实现高频率运动更新，帧信息提升空间精度及目标身份的稳定性。

Result: 该方法在实验中获得了12毫秒更新速率，形状跟踪误差低至0.43毫米，可动态适应操作轨迹，满足实际机器人操作任务的感知需求。源代码与数据公开。

Conclusion: MotionDLO兼顾了实时性、鲁棒性与高精度，有效弥补了现有方法的短板，为移动变形体的机器人操作提供了有力的感知基础。

Abstract: Reliably tracking moving deformable linear objects (DLOs) while simultaneously ensuring robustness, accuracy, and temporally consistent state estimation remains a fundamental challenge in robot perception. We introduce MotionDLO, a real-time tracking framework specifically designed to overcome these limitations in temporal continuity and latency. The method exploits the high temporal resolution and sparsity of event-based cameras and combines segmentation with the Coherent Point Drift (CPD) algorithm under the principles of Motion Coherence Theory. This integration enables temporally consistent shape estimation while maintaining a low computational overhead. Existing event-based tracking methods are typically computationally efficient but exhibit reduced accuracy compared to frame-based approaches, or alternatively compromise event sparsity to achieve competitive performance. To resolve this trade-off, we propose a hybrid event- and frame-based tracking architecture that preserves the complementary strengths of both sensing modalities. The event stream ensures high-frequency motion updates, while frame-based information stabilizes spatial accuracy and object identity. We demonstrate that the proposed framework reliably associates DLO instances across video sequences, enabling robust perception for robotic manipulation tasks. Experimental results validate real-time performance at 12 ms update rates and accurate shape tracking with an point-to-curve error as measurement of accuracy of up to 0.43 mm, supporting dynamic path adaptation during manipulation. The source code and demonstration datasets are publicly available.

</details>


### [375] [LD4WAM: Learning Latent Dynamics from Human Videos for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22403)
*Zhenhao Shen,Jiaqi Liang,Jasper Lu,Feng Jiang,Yuran Wang,Chuanbo Wei,Jiayi Liu,Jianchun Yang,Qize Yu,Jiadi You,Ce Hao,Guanqi He,Chen Xie,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的人类视频到机器人动作的模型——LD4WAM，可以更好地将视频中的动作知识迁移到不同类型的机器人上，实现动作泛化和更强的实际执行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的WAM（World Action Models）要么只能预测像素级未来帧，缺乏直接执行性的动态信息；要么如动作重定向存在“视觉鸿沟”，无法跨不同机器人平台泛化。提升模型的泛化能力（跨人-机、不同手型、不同环境）和可行动性是核心动机。

Method: 作者提出了“motion-aligned latent dynamics”（动作对齐的潜在动态）作为一种与实体本身无关的通用表示，用于桥接视频知识和机器人的低层动作。LD4WAM模型结合了带有语义重建和动作对齐的潜在动态模型，以及基于transformer的世界动作模型（MoT）。模型可以同时进行视频生成和动作决策。

Result: 在超5000小时的人类和机器人数据上预训练后，LD4WAM模型在RoboTwin仿真和实际机器人手（包括抓取器和灵巧手）上均表现优异，能适应未见过的物体与背景，展示出良好的泛化和执行能力。

Conclusion: LD4WAM通过引入动作对齐潜在空间，较好地将人类视频经验迁移到了各种机器人上，大幅度提升了WAM的泛化与实际操作能力，是视频驱动机器人动作学习的有效新途径。

Abstract: Human video is playing an increasingly central role in training World Action Models (WAMs), owing to its diversity and low collection cost relative to teleoperated robot data. However, most WAMs learn from such video only by predicting pixel-level future frames, giving dynamics that are not directly actionable, whereas motion retargeting recovers directly actionable actions but leaves a large visual gap across embodiments. We therefore propose motion-aligned latent dynamics as an embodiment-agnostic representation to bridge video priors and low-level actions. We further present LD4WAM, which pairs a Latent Dynamics Model trained with semantic reconstruction and real motion alignment with a World Dynamics Action Model built as a mixture-of-transformers (MoT), which preserves full future-video generation and uses learnable queries to distill these latent dynamics from generated futures for action conditioning. Pretrained on our curated unified dataset of over 5{,}000 hours of human and robot data, LD4WAM performs strongly in RoboTwin simulation and on real robots equipped with both grippers and dexterous hands, while generalizing well to unseen objects and backgrounds.

</details>


### [376] [Robust Bimanual Vision-Language-Action Models via Embarrassingly Simple Modality Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22419)
*Dongzhou Cheng,Ziang Li,Yixiao Zhou,Haojuan Li,Jinghao Zhang,Lei Lei,Minjing Dong,Jie Gui,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于双臂机器人操作的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的鲁棒性提升方法 —— 模态遮蔽机制（M3），并证明该方法可显著提升多任务中的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然基于查询的VLA模型在推理时具有低延迟优势，然而在复杂双臂任务中仍容易发生动作不连贯和执行失败，怀疑多视角与语言融合不稳是主要原因。

Method: 提出模态遮蔽机制（M3），即在训练时随机遮蔽部分模态通道，让模型在部分缺失信息下完成任务，不改变网络结构，也不需大规模机器人预训练。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0平台的10项双臂任务和3个真实长时序任务评估M3，相比基线方法平均提成成功率21.7%（Clean设置）、11.4%（Clean2Rand设置），真实场景下全任务成功率提升30%以上。

Conclusion: 训练时结构化的遮蔽策略是一种实用的方法，可以提升基于查询的VLA策略在双臂操作场景下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Query-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer low-latency inference that is attractive for bimanual robotic manipulation, but we observe that they can still exhibit discontinuous actions and execution failures in complex dual-arm tasks. We hypothesize that unstable multi-view and language fusion is one contributing factor in these failures, often coinciding with attention spreading to distracting regions. To improve robustness, we introduce the Modality Masking Mechanism (M3), an embarrassingly simple, training-only strategy that requires no architectural changes or large-scale robot pretraining. M3 stochastically masks subsets of modality channels during training, exposing the policy to controlled partial observations and encouraging it to rely less on distracting cues and more on evidence that remains reliable. We evaluate M3 on ten bimanual tasks from RoboTwin 2.0 and on three long-horizon real-world tasks. Compared with the Adapter baseline, M3 improves average success by 21.7% in the Clean setting and 11.4% in Clean2Rand, where policies are trained on clean demonstrations and evaluated on randomized scenes, while also improving averaged real-world full-task success by over 30%. These results suggest that structured training-time masking is a practical way to improve the robustness of query-based VLA policies for bimanual manipulation.

</details>


### [377] [EMPIRE: Explicit Manipulation Planning as a Learnable Intermediate Representation for Egocentric Hand-Motion Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22449)
*Wen Wang,Ruibing Hou,Hong Chang,Shiguang Shan,Xilin Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了EMPIRE框架，在自我视角下预测灵巧双手运动，设立显式操作规划作为中介表示，并构建了大规模数据集EMPIRE-651K，实现了当前最优的预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言模型（VLM）方法直接将观察映射至未来手部动作，忽略了手与物体交互的操作过程，且端到端训练导致操作学习与动作生成耦合，影响操作感知表征的学习效果。

Method: 提出EMPIRE两阶段框架。第一阶段：从多模态上下文学习显式操作规划，建模手与物体交互进程；第二阶段：动作生成器基于已冻结的操作规划表征合成未来双手动作，避免梯度干扰。并构建了大规模双手运动预测数据集EMPIRE-651K。

Result: 在相同的训练与评估协议下，EMPIRE取得当前最优的预测精度：平均关节点误差（MPJPE）为84.53毫米，指尖相对误差为38.97毫米。

Conclusion: EMPIRE通过将显式操作规划引入手部动作预测，有效分离规划与生成过程，提高了预测精度，并提供了新数据集和代码资源，推动领域发展。

Abstract: Forecasting dexterous hand motions from egocentric observations is fundamental to intelligent interactive systems. Existing VLM-based methods typically map observations directly to future motions, overlooking the underlying manipulation process that governs hand-object interactions. Moreover, end-to-end optimization couples manipulation learning with motion synthesis, causing motion-generation gradients to interfere with the pre-learned manipulation-aware representations. To overcome these limitations, we propose EMPIRE, a two-stage framework that introduces Explicit Manipulation Planning as an Intermediate Representation for Egocentric hand-motion forecasting. Stage I: Learn to Plan. EMPIRE first learns explicit manipulation plans from multimodal context to capture the progression of hand-object interactions. Stage II: Learn to Act. A motion generator synthesizes future bimanual hand motions conditioned on frozen planner representations, preventing motion-generation gradients from affecting manipulation planning. To support our method, we further construct EMPIRE-651K, a bimanual hand-motion forecasting dataset comprising 650,910 training windows across 111 tasks, each paired with an explicit per-hand manipulation plan. Under identical training and evaluation protocols, EMPIRE achieves state-of-the-art forecasting accuracy, with an MPJPE of 84.53 mm and a finger-relative error of 38.97mm. We release the code and dataset at https://github.com/wangwen-banban/EMPIRE.

</details>


### [378] [A Unified Neural-Aided Alignment and Calibration Method for AUVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22496)
*Guy Damari,Zeev Yampolsky,Itzik Klein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经网络的全新DVL初始化流程，用于水下自主航行器的精确导航，大幅提高初始化效率和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的DVL初始化（对准和标定）依赖模型驱动算法，需要复杂操作和高质量传感器，受限较大且耗时。因此，亟需更高效、鲁棒性更强的方法，降低对操作与传感器的要求。

Method: 作者提出以两个互补神经网络（ResAlignNet和DCNet）取代传统的对准与标定阶段，可直接在单条匀速航迹、实地实时执行，输入与传统方法一致。通过端到端神经网络智能处理，优化初始化过程。

Result: 在5组真实传感器误差案例测试下，新管线将速度均方根误差（RMSE）平均降低了68.7%，且初始化仅需25秒数据，显著优于模型驱动基线。

Conclusion: 基于神经网络的DVL初始化方法不仅大幅加快了初始化速度，还显著提高了精度，减少了环境、操作与硬件限制，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) rely on the fusion of inertial navigation systems (INS) and Doppler velocity logs (DVL) for accurate navigation. Before deployment, this fusion requires a DVL initialization pipeline consisting of two stages: alignment, which estimates the rotation between the INS and DVL frames, and calibration, which estimates the DVL error terms. Conventionally, both stages are solved with model-based algorithms that demand complex vehicle maneuvers, surface-level satellite reference measurements, and simplified error models, making initialization time-consuming, trajectory-dependent, and sensitive to sensor quality. In this work, we propose a fully neural- aided DVL initialization pipeline that replaces both stages with two complementary neural networks: ResAlignNet for alignment and DCNet for calibration. The unified pipeline operates in situ on a single nearly constant-velocity trajectory and uses the same inputs as the model-based baseline. Using real-world data recorded across five distinct sensor error-term combinations, the proposed pipeline reduces the velocity root mean squared error by an average of 68.7% over the model-based baseline, using only 25s of data for initialization.

</details>


### [379] [What is the effect of running-specific prostheses on long jumps? Optimization-based prediction and analysis using biomechanical models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22507)
*Anna Lena Emonds,Johannes Funken,Wolfgang Potthast,Katja Mombaur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出并验证了一个用于比较有无小腿截肢（BKA）运动员远跳动作的动力学模型，通过最优控制方法分析两者的跳远差异。结果显示，健全运动员的理论跳远距离比BKA运动员长6.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来，使用专用假肢（RSP）的BKA运动员跳远成绩显著提升，但其跳跃生物力学与健全运动员存在差异。人们关注RSP的弹性是否帮助其获得更远的跳跃距离。

Method: 建立了健全运动员与BKA运动员的多刚体模型，并通过最优控制问题（OCP）求解跳远动作，保障动力学合理，能用于动作重建与动作合成。

Result: 提出的模型能计算出真实的跳远动作。合成结果显示，健全运动员的跳远距离比BKA运动员多64cm（6.9%），同时为两类运动员指出了性能提升的方向。

Conclusion: 健全运动员在当前模型下仍具备相对优势，但模型揭示了影响差异的具体原因，并为BKA运动员跳远性能改进提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Long jumpers with below the knee amputation (BKA) that take off from their running-specific prosthesis (RSP) improved performances significantly over the last years. The long jump biomechanics differs compared to athletes without BKA and the question arises whether the spring-like properties of the RSP facilitate achieving long jumping distances. The aim of this work is to propose a long jump model for athletes with and without BKA, to evaluate it and to apply it for comparing long jump motions with and without RSP. We establish rigid multi-body system models of one athlete with and one athlete without below the knee amputation (BKA). Long jump motions are computed by solving a specific optimal control problem (OCP) with constraints enforcing a physically correct dynamics, both for motion reconstruction or motion synthesis. With the proposed long jump model, we are able to compute realistic long jump motions. We discuss the causes of differences in measured long jumps and show directions for eliminating them. For both athletes, the synthesized solutions reveal potential for performance improvement. The jumping distance of the athlete without BKA is 64cm (6.9%) longer than the one of the athlete with BKA in the synthesized solutions.

</details>


### [380] [WorldToken: Time-First Sequence Modeling for Robotic Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22591)
*Chunkai Yang,Andong Yang,Chao Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WorldToken，一种新的时间优先机器人决策策略，将多模态观察信息（多视角图像、本体感知、任务条件）在每一步融合为单一token，由时序Transformer处理，并用扩散模型生成动作片段。实验证明在RoboCasa任务上取得较好效果，并系统探究数据量、模型大小和历史窗口长度对性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人策略模型如何组织多模态输入顺序不一，影响决策效率与时序建模能力。为更高效整合和建模多模态观测，探索时间优先的输入组织形式。

Method: 设计WorldToken，将每步的全部多模态观测融合为单一世界token，通过因果时序Transformer建模token序列，再用扩散动作头生成动作块。对数据量、模型规模、历史窗口长度做系统实验。

Result: 85.3M参数模型在23个RoboCasa任务上平均闭环成功率为59.45%；更多数据带来稳定提升，但中等模型规模后效益递减，减少可见历史步大幅降低成功率。在RMBench评测历史窗口缩短任务中，表现显著下降。

Conclusion: 实验证明WorldToken架构在时序和数据扩展性上具有可行性和潜力，但未与其它序列组织策略直接比较，也尚未明确性能来源于哪些组件。

Abstract: Robot policies receive heterogeneous observations at each decision step, yet sequence models differ in how they organize these inputs over time. We introduce WorldToken, a time-first policy instantiation that fuses multiview images, proprioception, and task conditioning within each policy timestep into one world token. A causal temporal Transformer models the resulting world-token sequence, and a diffusion action head generates action chunks. On 23 RoboCasa tasks, an 85.3M-parameter policy trained from scratch apart from a frozen pretrained CLIP text encoder achieves 59.45% mean closed-loop success using 2,900 generated demonstrations per task. A complete factorial sweep over five dataset sizes, five model sizes, and two training seeds shows consistent gains from additional target-domain data and diminishing returns beyond moderate model size. Under same-checkpoint history truncation, reducing visible history to one or two policy timesteps lowers closed-loop success for all 50 RoboCasa policies. On RMBench Blocks Ranking, reducing visible history from 146 to 8 seconds lowers evaluator success from 95% to 28%, while an exploratory extended rollout sustains the reference swap sequence for over 850 seconds. These results establish the empirical feasibility of the complete WorldToken instantiation and characterize its data-scaling and temporal-context behavior under the tested recipes. They do not establish superiority over alternative sequence organizations or isolate which components of the complete implementation drive the observed performance.

</details>


### [381] [Enhancing Sim2Real Transfer for Torque-Controlled Robots through Real2Sim Dynamics Estimation and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22629)
*Davide Bargellini,Alex Pasquali,Andrea Govoni,Riccardo Zanella,Gianluca Palli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Real2Sim2Real的新流程，结合了轨迹匹配、遗传算法参数优化和域随机化，有效提升了基于力矩控制的机器人从仿真到现实的策略迁移能力。经实验验证，参数校准后策略在多个目标到达任务中表现出更高精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强化学习策略在从仿真转移至实际机器人时，特别是低层力矩控制下，常因建模不精确导致行为不稳定甚至不安全。为了解决力矩控制精度和策略可迁移性面临的挑战，作者提出改进方法。

Method: 作者提出Real2Sim2Real流程，先通过轨迹匹配和遗传算法优化摩擦、惯量和重力补偿等动力学参数，使仿真与实际机器人运动轨迹误差最小化。然后利用校准后的动力学模型，用TQC算法在仿真中训练强化学习策略，并分别在Gazebo、MuJoCo和真实机器人上进行测试。

Result: 经过动力学参数优化后，策略在仿真和现实环境中的轨迹跟踪精度显著提升，多个目标到达任务中的迁移过程更平滑，策略鲁棒性增强。

Conclusion: 准确的物理建模和参数校准对于实现稳定、可泛化的基于力矩控制的强化学习策略至关重要，本文提出的Real2Sim2Real方法有效提升了仿真到现实的策略迁移表现。

Abstract: Transferring reinforcement learning policies from simulation to Real-World robots remains a major challenge, particularly when dealing with low-level torque control, where even small modelling inaccuracies can lead to unstable or unsafe behaviours. In this work, we propose a Real2Sim2Real pipeline that improves Sim2Real transfer for torque-controlled robotic arms by combining trajectory matching, parameter optimization via genetic algorithms, and domain randomization. Using the 7-DOF Franka Emika Panda robot, we first identify friction, inertia, and gravity compensation parameters by minimizing the error between real and simulated joint trajectories. These calibrated dynamics are then used to train a TQC-based reinforcement learning agent in simulation. The trained policy is evaluated in both Gazebo and MuJoCo environments, and finally deployed on the real robot. Our results demonstrate a significant improvement in tracking accuracy and policy robustness after parameter tuning, with smooth policy transfer from simulation to the Real-World across multiple target-reaching tasks. This work highlights the effectiveness of accurate physical modelling in enabling stable and generalizable torque-based reinforcement learning policies.

</details>


### [382] [Physical Agentic AI: An Architecture for Orchestrating a Robot Crew with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22657)
*Xinyuan Liu,Eren Sadikoglu,Riana Chatterjee,Ransalu Senanayake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Physical Agentic AI框架，通过在多机器人系统的规划与执行之间引入明确的架构接口，实现任务分解、技能验证与安全调度，显著提升了跨机器人任务执行的可行性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agentic AI框架虽可将复杂任务分解为多步执行，但因缺乏对机器人实际执行约束的细粒度校验，容易产生不可行、时机错误或不安全的操作，现实物理机器人团队需要弥合语义规划与真实执行之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出了一套将技能库、机器人状态、命名位置及流程约束与Mission Planner分离的物理代理AI架构，并通过Robot Orchestrator逐步验证与授权技能执行，保证每一步行动均与实际能力匹配。实验在无人机－地面机器人搜救与人形－四足协作搬运任务上，通过仿真与物理实验，检验了规划知识、在线校验的独立作用。

Result: 技能检索将技能对接率从51%提升至96%，但有知识的规划器仍有23-29%的步骤出错，逐步校验则可将错误调度降为0%，且不会错误阻断合法行动。注入8个故障时，无校验则全部穿过界面并6次导致实际动作，有校验则全部被拦截。

Conclusion: 逐步、基于技能的物理调度接口能有效防止机器人团队中规划－实际脱节导致的不安全操作，是实现跨机器人物理任务安全执行的关键架构。

Abstract: Agentic AI frameworks interpret open-ended task goals and decompose them into multi-step plans. Richer information about embodiment-specific capabilities, physical preconditions, and cross-robot coordination improves grounding, but does not eliminate infeasible, mistimed, or unsafe physical actions. Physical robot crews therefore require an explicit architectural interface between semantic planning and execution, where every planned action is verified against robot capabilities, system state, and workflow constraints before actuation. This paper introduces Physical Agentic AI, a framework for skill-grounded robot agent orchestration, in which each robot exposes a typed library of executable skills while a foundation model planner decomposes a task into phases and assigns each phase to a robot-skill pair. A Robot Orchestration layer exposes the skill library, robot state, named locations, and workflow contracts to a non-actuating Mission Planner, while a deterministic Robot Orchestrator validates and authorizes one skill at a time. We evaluate on a drone-UGV search-and-dispatch mission, where every mission in every condition is executed live in Gazebo, and on a humanoid-quadruped transportation task using hardware-equivalent skill interfaces plus two physical trials on a Unitree G1 and Go2. Varying planner knowledge and runtime enforcement independently, we find that retrieval raises skill grounding from 51% to 96% yet leaves informed planners dispatching 23-29% of faulted steps. Per-dispatch enforcement reduces false dispatch to 0% with no false blocks, and a held-plan ablation confirms that the gate, not plan variation, is responsible. Live execution makes the difference physical: without enforcement all eight injected faults crossed the orchestration boundary and six produced robot motion; with enforcement all eight were refused before motion.

</details>


### [383] [Exact Finite-Length Theory of Uniform Car Parking: Spatial Laws, Absorption, and Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22671)
*Ganesh P Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维有限长度区间上均匀汽车停车问题，从随机顺序吸附理论出发，给出了有限区间情形下的精确理论及计算方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车泊车问题是随机顺序吸附（RSA）类模型中的经典问题，解析有限区间情况下相关统计量具有理论与应用意义，但其精确解相对较少，递推关系与积分方程分析难度较高。本文旨在发展对有限长度区间的精确分析理论，填补该领域空白。

Method: 作者将泊车型的联合概率密度在有限区间内分解为jamming cells（阻塞单元），在每个阻塞单元上表达为有理函数，并采用子集递归算法进行高效计算（运算复杂度O(2^n n)）。此外，边际分布和间隙有序统计量通过超对数积分（hyperlogarithm）得出；整体吸附数量及聚合量则归结为一个源于Rényi的积分方程求解。

Result: 得到了有限区间一维泊车过程的联合密度精确表达式，边际分布和间隙分布通过超对数函数完整刻画，且给出泊车数和相关总量的精确计算方案。通过高效子集递归算法，实现了复杂度可控下的精细精算。

Conclusion: 本文全面解决了有限长度一维泊车过程的精确理论问题，提出的方法为相关统计量的计算提供了理论基础和高效工具，有助于进一步理解有限系统下的随机顺序吸附模型。

Abstract: The uniform car-parking process is the one-dimensional random sequential adsorption of unit cars on a segment of finite length $s$: cars arrive at uniformly random positions and park wherever they fit, until no gap admits another. This paper develops the exact finite-$s$ theory. The joint density of the parked positions is resolved into jamming cells, on each of which it is a rational function, and evaluated by a subset recursion in $O(2^n n)$ operations; the marginal and gap order statistics are obtained as hyperlogarithms whose weight is fixed by the number of coordinates integrated out; and the absorption count and the aggregate quantities are treated through the integral equation descending from Rényi.

</details>


### [384] [VikPath: A Vision Kansformer Framework for Effective Obstacle Avoidance in Self-Supervised Pathfinding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22675)
*Junyao Wang,Yulin Xu,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自监督路径规划框架Design，能有效提升障碍物规避能力与轨迹平滑性，同时显著降低推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统启发式方法（如A*）在复杂障碍环境下效率低，学习型方法受限于标签质量且大多只优化路径长度，忽视了障碍物规避与轨迹平滑等实际执行关键因素。

Method: 提出了自监督路径规划框架Design，核心是Vision Kansformer模块，无需人工标签学习障碍物分布特征，并引入急转惩罚项，联合优化路径平滑性与障碍距离。

Result: 在多组实验中，Design比SOTA方法平均提升3.28%的障碍物规避距离，推理延迟降低87.07%，同时保持路径平滑性。

Conclusion: Design能在无需监督标签的条件下，根据环境自适应生成既安全又平滑的路径，并大幅提升推理速度，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Pathfinding is a fundamental problem in artificial intelligence and autonomous systems. Traditional heuristic-based algorithms, such as A*, rely on predefined heuristic functions to guide the search process. Although effective in structured environments, their search efficiency can degrade substantially in complex, obstacle-rich scenarios, where handcrafted heuristics may provide limited guidance. Recent studies have explored learning-based approaches to improve pathfinding efficiency; however, most existing methods rely on supervised learning and require labels generated by conventional planners or obtained through manual annotation. As a result, their performance is inherently influenced by the quality of the underlying supervision and may degrade when the labeling heuristics fail to capture complex environmental structures. Moreover, existing methods primarily optimize for path length while paying limited attention to obstacle clearance and trajectory smoothness, which can lead to paths that are difficult or unsafe to execute in real-world environments. To address these limitations, we propose $\Design$, a self-supervised pathfinding framework that jointly considers obstacle proximity and path smoothness. At its core, our novel \textit{Vision Kansformer} module learns representations of obstacle distributions without relying on labeled trajectories, enabling the model to better adapt to complex environments. We further introduce a sharp-turn penalty to encourage smoother and more practically executable paths. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, compared with state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches, $\Design$ achieves an average of 3.28\% greater obstacle clearance and 87.07\% lower inference latency while maintaining smooth path generation.

</details>


### [385] [RACO: Reliability-Aware Coarse-Goal Optimization for Inspection-Oriented UAV Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22678)
*Sen Wang,Yiming Sun,Jiaxuan He,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对无人机视觉-语言导航(UAV-VLN)在实际巡检应用中的不足，提出了LG-UVI评测设置和RACO导航框架，显著提升了巡检到达和结果可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAV-VLN方法侧重目标到达，而实际巡检任务要求无人机进入有效巡检区域并避免误报相似干扰物。以往粗到细导航策略在粗定位可靠性不足时，局部修正难以弥补，影响整体性能。

Method: 作者提出LG-UVI新评测方法，引入目标、干扰物、类型感知巡检区及更细致的诊断指标。针对巡检需求，进一步设计RACO框架，将初步粗目标视为假设，通过多候选锚定点检测和修正，提高粗定位可靠性，同时自适应精细化终点修正，提高靠近终点时的判别能力。

Result: 在统一线上评测下，RACO框架在验证集和测试集中的到达率相比基线方法分别提升了9.53和7.98个百分点。RACO还有效提升了巡检区抵达率并降低了误确认风险。

Conclusion: RACO显著提升了UAV-VLN在巡检场景下的可靠性和准确性，验证了粗目标可靠性优化是提升现有UAV-VLN方法的重要补充。

Abstract: UAV vision-language navigation (UAV-VLN) is commonly evaluated as goal reaching, but inspection-oriented deployment requires the agent to stop within a valid inspection region and avoid falsely confirming visually or semantically similar distractors. This requirement exposes a key weakness in existing coarse-to-fine UAV-VLN policies: the coarse goal predicted before local refinement is often treated as reliable, although it may drift toward plausible but incorrect object regions and limit the ability of the local stage to recover. To systematically evaluate this problem, we introduce LG-UVI, an object-centric inspection evaluation setting derived from CityNav/CityRefer. LG-UVI extends standard UAV-VLN episodes with target objects, hard distractors, type-aware inspection regions, and diagnostics for inspection-region arrival and object-level confirmation. To address this inspection-oriented setting, we further propose RACO, a reliability-aware adaptive coarse-to-fine navigation framework. Instead of treating the predicted coarse goal as a fixed waypoint, RACO views it as a runtime hypothesis and uses object-level candidate anchors to check and correct coarse localization before Stage 1 and at the Stage 1-to-Stage 2 boundary. RACO also applies scale-adaptive terminal refinement to handle terminal near-miss cases using runtime-observable geometric and anchor-based evidence. Under a unified online evaluation protocol, RACO improves SR over the reproduced HETT baseline by 9.53 and 7.98 percentage points on validation-unseen and test-unseen, respectively. It also improves inspection-region arrival and reduces false verification risk, showing that coarse-goal reliability optimization is an effective complement to existing coarse-to-fine UAV-VLN policies.

</details>


### [386] [Physics Filtering Favors the Generalization of Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22701)
*Jindou Jia,Shixuan Han,Meng Wang,Gen Li,Zihan Yang,Sicheng Zhou,Kexin Guo,Jianfei Yang,Xiang Yu,Wei Wang,Lei Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一个名为PhyFilter的反馈机制，可以在有限训练数据下提升机器人在动态不确定性环境中的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实机器人学习受限于数据量，获取大规模真实世界演示数据成本高昂。如何通过其他方式实现机器人的泛化与适应能力成为亟需解决的问题。

Method: 设计了物理过滤反馈模块PhyFilter，能够对学习输出进行调整纠正。该模块轻量、模型无关，并可利用自学习算法自动调优参数，无需人工调整，可无缝集成进不同的机器人策略中。

Result: 在四类典型机器人上进行了验证。包括四足机器人应对未见地形、负载及速度变化，无人机对抗突发风扰动，空中机械臂精确捕获，以及加速度辨识器在分布漂移下的鲁棒表现。

Conclusion: 利用物理过滤反馈机制，可以有效提升机器人在缺乏大量数据情况下的泛化能力，是大数据方法之外的有力替代方案。

Abstract: Living organisms exhibit extraordinary adaptability to unseen environments through their intrinsic physical structures and lifelong feedback-driven learning. Endowing robots with comparable generalization is critical for reliable operation in the real world. While recent approaches attempt to improve generalization by scaling training data, such strategies remain impractical for robotics, where collecting real-world demonstrations at the scale of large language models is prohibitively costly and slow. Contrary to this reliance on massive datasets, we show that robots can generalize effectively under dynamics uncertainties even with limited training data by leveraging a feedback mechanism, namely PhyFilter, that corrects learning outputs with physics-filtered learning residuals. PhyFilter operates as a lightweight, model-agnostic module whose parameters can be automatically optimized through an auto-learning algorithm, eliminating manual tuning and enabling seamless integration with diverse robot policies. We validate PhyFilter across four representative robotic systems, demonstrating that it enables quadruped robots to generalize to unseen terrains, payload variations, and speed ranges; drones to flight under unseen wind disturbances; aerial manipulators to achieve centimeter-level in-air capture despite wind and mass uncertainties; and acceleration differentiators to remain robust with distribution shift. These results show that physics-filtered feedback can serve as a powerful alternative to massive data scaling.

</details>


### [387] [Reproducible Vision-Guided 6-DoF Robotic Manipulator with a Mixed Stepper-Driver Architecture and Browser-Native Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22799)
*Lasan Perera,Deneth Priyadarshana,Dulana Pitiwaduge,Isitha Dinujaya,Mokshan Colambage*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了一款由本科生团队开发的开源低成本6自由度机械臂NeuralNexus Arm，包括详细的设计、硬件及固件问题排查经验，旨在为复制与改进此类项目提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高性能机械臂价格高昂、难以复制，阻碍了机器人教育与研究的普及。因此，作者动机是提供一个易于复现、低成本且性能良好的机械臂平台，推动相关领域的发展和实践。

Method: NeuralNexus Arm采用单一STM32H743微控制器搭载定制PCB板，创新性地在一个控制器上整合推挽输出（控制手腕TMC2209步进驱动）与开漏输出（控制大扭矩外部步进驱动CL57T及DM542），并结合中断驱动固件、MATLAB/Simscape逆运动学、基于Web Serial API的浏览器操作界面和轻量化视觉管线。文中还详细记录了自开发板到定制PCB过程中遇到的硬件与固件异常，并公开所有设计文件。

Result: 该平台实现了六轴协调控制，控制更新频率达2kHz，能够通过浏览器界面手动或自动执行预设的运动任务，实现了目标定位和自主抓取放置。文中还总结了硬件与固件调试经验。

Conclusion: 论文展示的NeuralNexus Arm兼具低成本、开源、功能完善和高复现性，为教育与初学者科研场景下的机器人应用提供了强大实践平台。公开的硬件与软件资源将促进该领域的进一步发展与创新。

Abstract: We present the NeuralNexus Arm, an open, low-cost 6-DOF robotic manipulator built by an undergraduate engineering team, together with the design decisions and debugging experience needed to reproduce it. The arm is driven by a single STM32H743 microcontroller on a custom printed circuit board (PCB) and combines two stepper-driver strategies on one controller: push-pull 3.3 V step/direction outputs for onboard TMC2209 drivers on the three wrist joints, and open-drain outputs for external CL57T and DM542 drivers on the three high-torque proximal joints. We describe the mechanical design, mixed-driver electronics, interrupt-driven firmware, a MATLAB/Simscape-based inverse-kinematics pipeline, a browser-native control interface using the Web Serial API, and a lightweight vision pipeline for object localisation and autonomous pick-and-place tasks. We also document non-obvious hardware and firmware failure modes encountered during the transition from a development board to the custom PCB as reproducibility guidance. All design files and firmware are released openly. The platform actuates all six axes under coordinated control at a 2 kHz update rate and executes both manual and pre-recorded motions from the browser interface.

</details>


### [388] [Triplet2Track: A Hierarchical System with Object-Centric Representations for Reliable Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22800)
*Jianxiang Liu,Gaojing Zhang,Chuan Wen,Qipeng Liu,Yuxuan Zhao,Ning Guo,Wenzhao Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种称为Triplet-to-Track System (TTS)的长时序机器人操作系统，通过引入闭环模仿学习和观察驱动的在线重规划，显著提升了复杂任务的成功率和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往的长时序操作方法要么数据需求量大且不透明（端到端视觉-语言-动作模型），要么虽易于解释但缺乏与实时观察的结合和反馈（层次化方法），容易导致执行失控或计划幻觉，因此亟需更可靠、可泛化的管道。

Method: TTS通过使用人类演示视频以减少对机器人自采数据的依赖，将高层子目标以实例相关的三元组形式表达，并转化为连续的执行轨迹先验，还可从实时观察中检测任务进度以便动态重规划，实现了从高层抽象到低层动作的闭环控制。

Result: 在多种真实世界的长时序操作任务上，TTS取得了平均74.8%的成功率，展示出较强的泛化能力，可支持物体级和组合级的任务迁移。

Conclusion: TTS系统有效提高了机器人在复杂、不可预知环境中的操作可靠性，减少训练数据需求，兼具可解释性和自适应性，是长时序机器人操控任务中的有力方法。

Abstract: Ensuring reliability in uncertain environments remains difficult for long-horizon robotic manipulation. End-to-end VLA models are data-heavy and opaque, making diagnosis and verification difficult. Hierarchical pipelines are more interpretable, but their plans are often weakly grounded in observations, weakly aligned with low-level actions, and computed without online feedback, leading to open-loop behavior and hallucinations. To address these issues, we introduce the Triplet-to-Track System (TTS), a closed-loop long-horizon imitation learning system that uses human videos to reduce reliance on robot-collected data. TTS represents high-level subgoals as instance-grounded triplets, translates them into continuous track priors for execution, and monitors task progress from observations for online replanning. Across diverse real-world long-horizon tasks, TTS achieves a 74.8\% average success rate and supports object-level and compositional generalization.

</details>


### [389] [UniMem: Unifying Multimodal Memory and Control for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22869)
*Lars Osterberg,Maggie Wang,Mac Schwager*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了UniMem框架，将多模态高层次记忆与低层控制统一在同一模型骨干下，在需要记忆的视觉-语言-动作任务上显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在处理需要长期记忆的非马尔可夫任务时表现不佳，且大多通过额外的VLM管理记忆，导致训练流程复杂并产生性能瓶颈。

Method: UniMem框架通过事件分类器更新记忆，关键帧编码器进行密集空间记忆，并引入关键帧缓存技术减少策略执行时的开销。

Result: UniMem在五个仿真与四个硬件任务中，顺序和空间记忆测试上均显著优于固定间隔取帧与分层基线（如93.4%对68.2%，80.0%对43.5%），且推理更快，训练流程简单。

Conclusion: UniMem统一了记忆与控制，减少了技术链条和延迟，提高了任务完成效率，可方便推广应用于实际多模态长时序任务。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have leveraged internet-scale pretraining and task-focused finetuning to achieve strong performance on long-horizon tasks, they often struggle with non-Markovian tasks that require memory. Existing approaches to memory typically involve additional Vision-Language-Models (VLMs) for long-term memory management, introducing a memory bottleneck and a fractured training pipeline. Conditioning on multiple historical frames can provide the VLA with access to more descriptive features of past scenes, but can degrade performance if frames are chosen at arbitrary, fixed intervals. To address these limitations, we present UniMem, a framework that unifies high-level, multimodal memory and low-level control under one backbone. UniMem employs an event classifier for memory updates, a keyframe encoder for dense spatial memory, and a keyframe caching technique to minimize overhead during policy rollouts. We evaluate UniMem across five simulation and four hardware tasks targeting sequential and spatial memory, demonstrating that our unified, single-model system outperforms fixed-interval image sampling baselines (93.4% vs. 68.2%) in simulation and hierarchical baselines (80.0% vs. 43.5%) in hardware, while offering faster inference and a simple training pipeline for easy adoption. Project website: https://losterberg3.github.io/unimem-vla/

</details>


### [390] [SuperMap: A Spatio-Temporal SLAM System for Visual-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22896)
*Shibo Zhao,Guofei Chen,Honghao Zhu,Zhiheng Li,Changwei Yao,Nader Zantout,Seungchan Kim,Wenshan Wang,Ji Zhang,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了SuperMap，一种用于语言引导导航的4D时空语义建图框架，通过结合高频几何SLAM与异步的开放词汇感知，解决现有方法中的目标漂移和过时语义问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人在真实人类环境中的导航依赖于具备开放词汇和可适应环境长期变化的时空语义表示。然而，直接应用基础模型存在语义漂移、目标识别间断等问题，限制了机器人的感知和导航能力。

Method: SuperMap框架通过高频几何SLAM获取场景结构，同时引入异步开放词汇感知模块。其核心是一个基于一致性的建图引擎，能够根据3D感知结果进行实例关联和重激活，并通过合理的存在与标签置信度更新算法，维护稳定的目标身份，对被遮挡或环境变化导致的陈旧目标进行自动剔除。最终生成可查询的4D场景图，可与视觉语言模型无缝集成。

Result: SuperMap在公开基准和真实机器人平台上进行了评估，表现出对动态场景下出现、消失、位置变动等情形的良好适应性。论文同时提供了消融实验和运行时分析，验证了方法有效性。系统已开源。

Conclusion: SuperMap为开放词汇时空语义建图提供了可落地的框架，有助于推动机器人在复杂多变人类环境中的自主导航能力。

Abstract: Robotic navigation in human environments requires a spatio-temporal semantic representation that can rec- oncile open-vocabulary perception with long-term environmental changes. While foundation models provide strong zero-shot recognition, their predictions are intermittent and view-dependent, and naively integrating them into mapping pipelines leads to identity drift and stale semantics over time. We present SuperMap, a 4D spatio-temporal mapping framework for language-guided navigation that integrates high-frequency geometric SLAM with asynchronous open-vocabulary perception. Our core contribution is a consistency-driven mapping engine that combines 3D-aware instance association/re-activation with a principled existence-and-label confidence update to maintain stable object identities and prune outdated map content under occlusions and scene changes. SuperMap produces a queryable 4D scene-graph representation that interfaces naturally with Vision-Language Models by supporting compositional queries over object semantics, relations,
  We demonstrate SuperMap on benchmarks and real robots, including dynamic scenes with appearance/disappearance and relocation, and provide ablations and runtime analysis. We release the full system as open-source to provide the community with a deployable baseline for open-vocabulary spatio-temporal mapping. Project website: superodometry.com/supermap.

</details>


### [391] [Privileged Critic Training Enables Sensor-Free Thruster Fault Adaptation in End-to-End RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22976)
*Ricard Marsal I Castan,Miguel A. Olivares-Méndez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新方法，使推进器驱动的机器人在不需要专用故障传感器的前提下，实现对推进器复杂故障（如降级、卡死、失效）自适应导航。通过引入特权评论者训练，训练阶段加入真实故障状态，部署时无需任何故障检测传感器，即可显著提升容错能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统故障检测方法依赖于专用传感器或假定可观测到所有故障状态，这在实际部署中难以实现。推进器的故障表现复杂且不总是可完全观测，亟需一种无需专用传感器的容错导航方案。

Method: 采用特权评论者训练框架，在训练期间让PPO价值函数获取真实故障状态信息，而actor仅接收常规任务观测。提出带有循环记忆的RAFT策略，采用不对称训练方式提升容错能力。

Result: 在8个推进器1个反作用轮组成的浮动平台机器人上，面对多达4个推进器同时故障，RAFT方法达到70.2%的任务成功率，显著优于无容错基线（4.8%），接近理想的oracle（82.4%）。

Conclusion: 特权评论者训练能显著提升机器人在不可见复杂故障下的导航能力，不依赖额外感知硬件，方法通用性强；代码及数据开源，有望加速实际应用。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant navigation for thruster-actuated robots requires online adaptation to failures that are neither binary nor fully observable: thrusters may degrade continuously, fail dead, or jam stuck-open. Classical fault detection pipelines require dedicated sensors unavailable at deployment; oracle controllers that observe the true failure state are equally impractical. We show that privileged critic training is sufficient for sensor-free fault adaptation: giving the PPO value function access to the true degradation state dgt during training, while the actor receives only standard task observations, shapes a policy that compensates for failures at deployment without any dedicated fault sensing. We propose RAFT (Recurrent Asymmetric Fault Tolerant), a policy with recurrent memory trained with a privileged asymmetric critic. Evaluated on a floating-platform robot (8 thrusters, 1 reaction wheel) under up to four simultaneous thruster failures, RAFT achieves 70.2% success at four concurrent failures, closing 84% of the gap from a failure-naive baseline (4.8%) to an oracle policy that sees the full degradation state at deployment (82.4%). All code, checkpoints, and data are open-source.

</details>


### [392] [CSymPlan: Certified Symbolic Planning and Control for High-DOF Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22983)
*Aditya Narendra,Ashok Kumar Saini,Mahathi Anand,Mahmoud Khaled,Fares J. Abu-Dakka,Abdalla Swikir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CSymPlan是一种用于高自由度机械臂的认证符号规划与控制框架，能在已知和动态环境中安全地实现到达-避障任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的机械臂运动生成通常采用规划和控制分离的方式，但实际环境中由于执行器极限、跟踪误差、模型不准确和障碍物距离小等问题，这种分离方法会导致难以执行的轨迹和潜在安全风险。为提升在复杂操作环境中的执行效率与安全性，提出了新的规划与控制一体化框架。

Method: 提出了CSymPlan框架，包含离线和在线两种互补实现。离线方式通过反馈线性化，将机械臂动力学简化为采样的扰动双积分器模型，并预先计算得到认证的到达-避障反馈策略，实现为量化-查找-力矩实现流水线。在线方式利用相同建模与细化接口，实时根据任务和感知更新符号策略，通过并行化处理实现快速决策。

Result: 在随机仿真基准测试和实际Franka FR3机械臂的感知驱动实验中，两种实现均能安全完成到达-避障任务，无安全违规；当无认证动作时，机器人将暂停、重新规划或安全停止，避免执行未认证命令。

Conclusion: CSymPlan能显著提升高自由度机械臂在存在扰动和不确定性的复杂环境中的安全性和任务完成率，是对传统分离式运动生成方式的重要补充。

Abstract: Robot manipulators are commonly engineered around a decoupled motion-generation stack: a planner computes a collision-free path and a lower-level controller tracks the resulting reference. This separation is computationally convenient, but it can produce references that are difficult to execute under actuator limits, tracking error, model mismatch, and small obstacle clearances. We present CSymPlan, a certified symbolic planning and control framework for high-DOF manipulators with two complementary implementations: an offline implementation that precomputes certified reach-avoid feedback policies for known workspaces; and an online implementation that synthesizes or updates symbolic policies at runtime from changing task and perception information using parallelization. The offline implementation reduces the manipulator dynamics to a sampled perturbed double-integrator model in operational space through feedback linearization, treats torque-realization errors, modeling inaccuracies, and measurement uncertainty as bounded disturbances, and refines the synthesized symbolic policy to the Franka FR3 through a quantization--lookup--torque realization pipeline. The online implementation uses the same abstraction and refinement interface, but replaces the precomputed policy table with a runtime pFaces request--synthesis--execution loop. In randomized simulated benchmarks and perception-driven Franka FR3 experiments, both implementations complete reach-avoid tasks with zero safety violations; whenever no certified action exists, the robot holds, replans, or stops safely instead of executing an uncertified command.

</details>


### [393] [InstructMove: A Text-Indispensable Benchmark for Instruction-Following Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22990)
*Mengao Zhao,Ziang Li,Chaodong Huang,Mengchen Ma,Haoyi Jiang,Yiwei Jin,Xinjie Wang,Yun Du,Xuewu Lin,Taojun Ding,Hongyu Xie,Jackson Jiang,Chunlei Yu,Kaihua Zhang,Lichao Huang,Liu Liu,Tianwei Lin,Zhizhong Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了适用于机器人操作的新基准InstructMove，用以评估多模态模型在指令遵循上的真实能力，强调测试必须要语言信息不可或缺。实验结果表明，该基准可以有效暴露现有模型的视觉捷径问题，且模拟数据能提升实际任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型虽然实现了泛化的机器人操作，但很多基准未能准确评估其对指令的依赖性，容易让模型仅凭视觉知识猜测正确动作。本文希望通过设计语言信息不可或缺的基准，促使模型真正“听懂并执行”自然语言指令。

Method: 作者提出InstructMove基准：在每个任务场景中，多种操作在视觉和物理上都可能合理，只有一个与文本指令完全匹配。任务包含类别识别、属性区分、空间推理和组合操作等维度，并采用语义“干扰项”测试。设计了专门的训练和评测protocol，并提供诊断指标检测模型的语言依赖度。

Result: 利用代表性VLA策略在InstructMove上实验，证明该测试集能有效揭示模型利用视觉捷径的行为。实验还显示，在模拟环境中用InstructMove数据训练有助于提升真实世界中的机器人指令遵循操作水平。

Conclusion: InstructMove为研究和提升机器人理解以及执行自然语言指令提供了严格、控测的基准，未来有助于推动更加可靠、泛化的视觉-语言-动作模型发展。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have made general-purpose robot manipulation increasingly plausible by conditioning robot actions on natural-language instructions. A key test of such generality is whether policies actually follow language instructions. Yet many manipulation benchmarks leave this ability underdetermined: the intended object or destination is often visually salient or uniquely feasible, allowing policies to succeed without grounding the instruction. We argue that instruction-following evaluation should be text-indispensable: multiple actions should be visually and physically plausible, while only one should be consistent with the language instruction. We introduce InstructMove, a text-indispensable benchmark for instruction-following manipulation. InstructMove instantiates this principle in pick-and-place scenes with semantic distractors, decomposing instruction following into category identification, attribute discrimination, spatial reasoning, and compositional pick-and-place. InstructMove supports a train-eval protocol with InstructMove training data and held-out evaluation tasks, with additional diagnostics for language dependence. Experiments with representative VLA policies show that InstructMove provides a controlled testbed for diagnosing visual shortcuts and that InstructMove simulation data can improve real-world instruction-following manipulation performance. Code: https://github.com/HorizonRobotics/RoboOrchardSim

</details>


### [394] [Free-Energy-Gated Plasticity for Real-Time Online Motor Learning in Physical Human--Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23000)
*Hiroki Sawada,Jun Tani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于预测编码的可变递归神经网络（PV-RNN）扩展方法，实现了在线自适应权重调整，并引入了“自由能门控可塑性（FEGP）”机制以提升人机持续互动中的行为学习与保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在持续的人机交互任务中，神经网络需要不断适应新行为，同时不遗忘已学到的动态，但传统方法存在遗忘旧知识的问题。作者希望解决在线学习中保持已获得行为的难题。

Method: 作者在PV-RNN的基础上，提出让突触权重能够在持续互动过程中实时适应（在线学习），并设计了根据变分自由能调节有效学习率的FEGP机制。通过对比不同学习率设置与门控机制，在无预训练、重放或任务边界信号的物理人机实验下，检验了方法的有效性。

Result: 在三种周期运动模式的学习中，FEGP方法无需预训练即可让网络自发获得全部行为。控制实验表明，FEGP显著提升了行为范围覆盖和过去行为的保持能力，而单纯调整学习率或重放相同的增益数值（打乱时间顺序）均无法达到这种效果。

Conclusion: 结果表明，塑性调整的时序分配（与模型-环境不匹配时相关）对持续在线学习期间行为保持至关重要，仅依赖平均数量级或分布无法实现同样的性能。

Abstract: Fully online embodied learning requires synaptic adaptation to acquire new behaviors while preserving previously learned dynamics during ongoing interaction. We extend the Predictive-Coding-inspired Variational Recurrent Neural Network (PV-RNN) to continuously adapt its synaptic weights and propose Free-Energy-Gated Plasticity (FEGP), which regulates the effective learning rate according to variational free energy. In real-time physical human--robot interaction, a randomly initialized network acquired three cyclic motor patterns without offline pretraining, replay, or task-boundary signals, with all three patterns emerging in autonomous rollouts. Controlled experiments over ten randomized teaching streams and five network initializations per stream showed that FEGP substantially improved repertoire coverage and retention of previously acquired patterns after they left the recent observation window. Neither a constant learning rate matched to the gate's time-averaged effective rate nor replay of the same gain values with disrupted temporal organization reproduced these improvements. These results indicate that the temporal allocation of plasticity relative to model--environment mismatch, rather than simply its average magnitude or distribution, is critical for maintaining previously acquired behaviors during continued online learning.

</details>


### [395] [RoboRacer Arena: Scaling High-Fidelity Autonomous Racing in Isaac Sim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23040)
*Mihaela-Larisa Clement,Agnes Poks,Ezio Bartocci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出RoboRacer Arena系统，能从多种来源自动生成3D赛车仿真环境，实现规模化与标准化仿真测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小型自动驾驶车辆研究平台虽然有标准硬件，但赛道多样制作困难。2D占用格仿真器不能模拟物理接触，3D模拟器赛道又需手工资产制作，难以扩展。

Method: RoboRacer Arena采用泛洪填充算法从占用图中提取可行驶区域，计算距离场建立碰撞边界，结合表面材料等组装为USD场景，实现Isaac Sim里自动生成3D环境。输入地图支持SLAM生成、F1赛道缩放或自然语言描述，利用大型语言模型Gemma 4 31B生成赛道规范。通过几何筛查、过程生成与光栅校验保障一致性和可重现性。

Result: 仿真环境初始化时间随地图尺寸线性增长（1.18-2.48秒），验证21张地图全部通过。目前系统含130条赛道，并能按自然语言生成赛道。多车辆并行下可达8707步/秒（不含渲染和策略）。

Conclusion: RoboRacer Arena突破了以往赛道生成和物理仿真难点，为小型自动驾驶研究提供了高效、可扩展、标准化的仿真平台，支持多种数据来源，便于后续算法开发和公平对比。

Abstract: RoboRacer offers a standardized platform for research using 1:10-scale autonomous vehicles, but the variety of available tracks hinders the process of acquiring policies. Although existing occupancy-grid simulators allow for the quick addition of new maps, they fail to include physical contact, while 3D simulators require each circuit to be implemented as a separate asset, thus limiting their scalability. In order to overcome this issue, we have developed RoboRacer Arena, a system that creates 3D racing environments directly from occupancy maps. Our method starts by using a flood fill algorithm to extract the drivable corridors and to identify the track boundaries, which are then used to establish the barriers. A distance field is calculated to define the collision boundaries. The track surfaces, collision properties, and materials are assembled into a USD stage, which allows for the automated and reproducible generation of the environment in Isaac Sim. The input maps can be obtained from SLAM sessions, from rescaled Formula 1 circuits, or from natural-language descriptions. When the input is based on natural language, we use Gemma 4 31B to generate a track specification without specifying any coordinates or geometry. To guarantee consistency and reproducibility, we apply geometric screening, procedural generation, and raster-level validation. The simulation environments are initialized in a time range of 1.18 to 2.48 seconds, with the initialization time increasing linearly as the raster size increases. In 30 matched trials involving 10 tracks and 3 seeds, 21 maps were generated and all passed validation. RoboRacer Arena currently contains 130 tracks and supports the generation of tracks from natural language. In benchmark tests, the system attains 8,707 vehicle-steps per second when using 256 parallel rigid-body vehicles, excluding the time taken for rendering and policy execution.

</details>


### [396] [Switched Turn-based Adaptive Source Seeking Strategy using Estimation and Information-driven Direction of Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23068)
*Shubhra Banerjee,Satadal Ghosh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）与基于Fisher信息矩阵（FIM）方向选择的循环式源头搜寻新框架，能够提升静止和移动信号源的定位准确性并降低估计误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有源头寻迹方法多为环形几何路线设计，仅保证路径可行性，未能充分利用估计不确定性来优化运动决策，因此定位准确度存在提升空间。

Method: 本方法将EKF用于实时更新运动过程中的信号源位置信息，并在每个循环节点以源位置估计的不确定性和预测增益（由FIM决定）共同决定行进方向。此外，依据测量数据动态设定终止条件，实现无需先验信息即可判断收敛。

Result: 仿真结果显示，在静止和移动信号源场景下，该方法的追踪表现和估计误差均优于仅依赖信息增益或估计驱动的传统策略。

Conclusion: 作者的融合策略充分结合了状态估计与信息驱动的优点，在无需提前知道源头位置的前提下，实现了更高效和可靠的源头定位，对实际环境监测和搜索任务具有良好应用前景。

Abstract: Source seeking arises in applications such as gas leak localization, radiation monitoring, and environmental surveillance, where the origin of an unknown signal field must be estimated from spatial measurements. In practice, the source location is not directly observable and must be inferred from noisy scalar measurements collected during motion.In robotic source seeking, estimation and motion are closely linked: measurements improve the source estimate, while the chosen trajectory affects the quality of future measurements.Existing loop-based geometric strategies generate feasible motion but do not explicitly use estimation uncertainty to regulate direction updates.This paper presents a loop-based source-seeking framework that combines Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) estimation with Fisher Information Matrix (FIM)-based direction selection. The source estimate is updated during motion, and the heading is changed at loop boundaries using both estimation uncertainty and predicted information gain. A measurement-based stopping condition is used to detect convergence without requiring prior knowledge of the source location.Simulation results under stationary and moving source scenarios demonstrate improved tracking performance and reduced estimation error compared to purely information-driven or estimate-driven strategies.

</details>


### [397] [Shaping the Evolutionary Dynamics of Robot Morphology via Adaptive Control Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23100)
*Junru Song,Yang Yang,Yaqing Xu,Ying Wen,Wei Peng,Guozhen Li,Wei'en Zhou,Wen Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了机器人协同设计中的脑-体协同进化问题，提出新的适应度评估方法（AdaControl），提升了设计效率和多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人协同设计方法通常只关注形态进化对控制学习的促进作用（即形态智能），而忽视了控制学习反作用于形态进化的机制。进一步，现有的适应度评估可能过早，导致设计空间探索受限和偏见。

Method: 首先将形态对控制学习的贡献拆解为两个正交维度：收敛速度（形态智能）和性能上限（真实潜力）。通过分析个体的学习曲线，建立了两者的函数关系，并在群体层面刻画进化过程。实验在体素软体机器人仿真平台上，揭示了过早评估的偏见，并提出自动化控制学习分配方法AdaControl，动态调整学习过程防止选择偏见。

Result: 实验表明，传统方法过早评估会系统性低估个体潜力，使选择偏向于学习快但潜力低的形态，减弱多样性和进化效率。AdaControl通过动态平衡学习资源分配，提升了高性能、多样性的发现效率，且计算成本大幅下降（可节省80%计算量）。

Conclusion: AdaControl能实现无偏见的适应度评估，让简单的遗传算法在多样性和性能上媲美复杂的生成模型方法，为协同设计和脑-体共同进化提供了更科学高效的工具。

Abstract: Robot co-design via bi-level optimization couples within-lifetime controller learning for fitness evaluation with cross-generational morphological evolution. Prior work has established that well-adapted morphology facilitates faster control learning, a property termed morphological intelligence. Yet how control learning reciprocally shapes morphological evolution remains unexplored. This paper examines both directions for a holistic account of brain-body interplay. We first show that morphological contributions to control learning decouple into two orthogonal dimensions. We formalize the convergence speed as morphological intelligence and identify the performance ceiling as a complementary quantity termed true potential. A concise functional relation is then established to jointly characterize both quantities from individual learning curves, which, when aggregated at the population level, capture evolutionary profiles. Through extensive experiments on simulated voxel-based soft robots, we reveal that premature fitness evaluation systematically underestimates true potential and biases selection towards fast learners. This restricts design space exploration, compromising both optimization efficiency and morphological diversity. Notably, the widely recognized morphological Baldwin effect emerges as an artifact of this bias rather than a general evolutionary tendency. We therefore propose AdaControl, which monitors disproportionate selection for morphological intelligence during evolution and allocates minimally sufficient control learning for unbiased fitness evaluation. With AdaControl, a simple genetic algorithm rivals state-of-the-art generative-model-based co-design methods in discovering diverse high-performing designs while cutting computation by up to 80% versus exhaustive control.

</details>


### [398] [Pointing-VLA: Typed Spatial Grounding Interfaces for Vision-Language-Action Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23138)
*Xiwen Chen,Zelin Li,Zhiruo Zhou,Huiming Chen,Chenwei Wang,Xiaojun Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型的视觉-语言-动作模型Pointing-VLA，通过类型化空间读出机制，提升了机器人多模态推理与执行的衔接效率与透明性，实现了新的性能高度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型通过文本坐标或不透明动作标记进行空间定位，导致多模态推理与机器人执行之间接口不稳定、效率低、难以明确检查；亟需更直接、高效、可调试的空间信息输出。

Method: Pointing-VLA基于Embodied-R1，设计了几何类型专用的空间读出头，分别预测归一化点、对象功能定位（OFG）热图和视觉轨迹，摆脱了传统坐标序列化为文本方式。针对PICK和PLACE任务，分别采用OFG和Pointing作为空间目标，明确任务阶段与空间输出的对应。该方法在Bridge/WidowX等物理装置和NORA-1.5等跨数据集上进行了测试。

Result: Pointing-VLA无需Bridge专属微调，在四项任务集上平均表现达到72.9%，超越SOTA。在OFG/Pointing输出互补下，模型在原生及跨数据集测试中均优于对比方法；OFG/Contact输出在NORA-1.5上显著减少了控制器时间（20倍+）。空间读出头比文本解码快6.68-6.9倍。内嵌到π_0.5动作策略后，真实机器人自主成功率从52.7%提升到80.7%。

Conclusion: 类型化空间读出为多模态推理与机器人执行之间提供了高效、透明、可检查的接口，是提升真实机器人感知-决策-执行闭环性能的有效方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models often expose spatial grounding through autoregressive text coordinates or opaque action tokens, creating brittle interfaces between multimodal reasoning and robot execution. We present Pointing-VLA, a typed hidden-state spatial readout built on Embodied-R1. Geometry-specific heads predict normalized points, object-functional grounding (OFG) heatmaps, and visual trajectories without serializing geometry as text. For the evaluated Bridge/WidowX and physical pick-place deployments, an explicit execution contract assigns PICK to source-conditioned OFG and PLACE to Pointing, providing direct stage-aligned spatial targets. Pointing-VLA achieves SOTA performance on Bridge/WidowX, averaging 72.9\% across the evaluated four-task set without Bridge-specific finetuning under collision-enabled CuRobo execution. Pointing and OFG show complementary strengths across native and cross-dataset evaluations. The OFG/contact readout transfers to NORA-1.5, preserving or improving success while reducing recorded controller time by more than 20$\times$; typed heads are also 6.68--6.90$\times$ faster than Embodied-R1 text decoding on a shared external suite. When integrated as spatial guidance for a $π_{0.5}$ action policy, Pointing-VLA raises autonomous real-robot success from 52.7\% to 80.7\% across three visual contexts. These results establish typed spatial readouts as an efficient, inspectable interface between embodied reasoning and robot execution.

</details>


### [399] [Spinning Quadrotor: Hover Thrust Augmentation with Passive Lifting Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23163)
*Aniketh Parkala,Harikumar Kandath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型旋转四旋翼飞行器，通过主动维持持续偏航（自转），从而提升能效，实验验证所需升力推力减少22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多旋翼飞行器在悬停时需主动抑制偏航旋转，以保持机头方向，导致能耗增加，而偏航控制本身对悬停升力和高度无直接作用。作者质疑该传统，探讨能否将偏航能量用于提升飞行效率。

Method: 提出让四旋翼持续以一定速度自转，并开发了其动力学模型。在低雷诺数（Re）工况下分析并选取适合的翼型用于升力面。并进行了初步的硬件实验以验证设计思路。

Result: 硬件实验结果表明，持续偏航旋转可使所需的推力降低约22%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，有意引入偏航自转不仅不会影响多旋翼飞行，还可显著提升能效，为多旋翼飞行器的结构及控制带来新的设计思路。

Abstract: Conventional multirotor aerial vehicles actively suppress yaw rotation during hover, expending power to maintain a fixed heading despite the fact that yaw regulation is not required for force balance or altitude control. This paper challenges that paradigm by proposing a spinning quadrotor architecture that intentionally operates at a sustained yaw rate, converting power traditionally spent on yaw regulation into useful aerodynamic effects. A dynamic model of the spinning quadrotor is developed, analysis for low Re range is conducted to choose an airfoil for lifting surfaces. Preliminary hardware tests show a 22% reduction in thrust required. These findings suggest that intentional yaw rotation, rather than being suppressed, can be exploited as a design mechanism for efficient and robust multirotor flight.

</details>


### [400] [Guided Riemannian Optimization (GuRO): Bridging Model Predictive Control and Decision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23204)
*Hossein Abdi,Satya Prakash Dash,Mingfei Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一个结合MPC和RL的新决策框架，利用曲率感知优化方法，有效提升在高维机器人任务中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维、非线性系统中决策问题复杂，MPC虽高效但受模型误差影响，RL采样复杂度高且不稳定，二者各有缺陷。结合两者并提升优化过程表现是当前亟需解决的问题。

Method: 提出将MPC与RL纳入序列决策框架，MPC预测本地最优轨迹以指导决策transformer，无需大量的离线训练。采用曲率感知（Riemannian）优化方法，在参数空间高效训练策略，以应对非凸损失带来的训练困难。

Result: 在高维四足机器人控制任务中，与TRPO、SAC及Online Decision Transformer等方法相比，该框架表现出更高的收益与更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效结合了MPC与RL的优势，并用曲率感知优化解决了训练不稳定问题，在复杂机器人任务中表现优异并有望推广。

Abstract: Decision-making in high-dimensional, nonlinear systems remains a central challenge in robotics. While model-based methods like Model Predictive Control (MPC) offer sample efficiency and interpretability, their performance degrades when the dynamics model is inaccurate or long-horizon predictions are required. Conversely, model-free reinforcement learning (RL) learns policies directly from interaction but suffers from high sample complexity and unstable optimization. Recent advances in sequence modeling have inspired transformer-based decision-making frameworks that can unify MPC and RL, but their training typically faces significant optimization challenges due to highly non-convex loss landscapes. In this work, we propose a novel framework that integrates MPC with RL in a sequence decision-making framework and leverages a curvature-aware optimization to efficiently tackle non-convex loss landscapes. MPC provides predictions of locally optimal trajectories that guide the decision transformer, removing the need for extensive offline pretraining. To address the slow and unstable convergence of traditional optimizers, we train the policy in a Riemannian parameter space using an efficient Riemannian (curvature-aware) method, leading to faster and more robust optimization. We evaluate our framework on high-dimensional quadruped control tasks and demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines, including TRPO, SAC, and Online Decision Transformer, achieving higher returns and faster convergence.

</details>


### [401] [Think Only When Needed: Prompt-Authority Control for Selective Slow-Path Intervention in Vision-Language-Action Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23224)
*Zhiruo Zhou,Zelin Li,Xiwen Chen,Jiazhuo Li,Chenwei Wang,Huiming Chen,Xiaojun Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究发现，在视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略中，直接追加文本会极大削弱模型成功率，并提出TOWN-VLA接口，通过分离提示生成与策略输入权限，实现更高效、安全的提示增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提高已冻结VLA模型在下游任务中的表现，通常采用检索增强。然而，添加检索文本对行为决策产生强干扰，本研究旨在分析原因并提出改进方法。
